ūm̐ namaḥ śrīmapamātmane ||
atha sāṃkhyapravacanabhāṣyam|
vidvadvaraśrīmadvijñānabhikṣuviracitam|
eko'dvitīya iti vodavacāṃsi puṃsi
sarvābhimānavinivarttanato'sya muktyai||
vaidharmyalakṣaṇabhidāvirahaṃ vadanti
nākhaṇḍatāṃ kha iva dharmaśatāvirodhāt|| 1||
tasya śrutsaya mananārthatāvirodhāt||
sadyuktijālamiha sāṃkhyakṛdāvirāsīt||
nārāyaṇaḥ kapilamūrttiraśeṣaduḥkha -- 
hānāya jīvanivahasya namo'stu tasmai|| 2||
nānopādhiṣu yannānārūpaṃ bhātyanalārkavat||
tat samaṃ sarvabhūteṣu citsāmānyamupāsmahe|| 3 ||
īśvarānīśvaratvādi cidekarasavastuni ||
vimūḍhā yatra paśyanti tadasmi paramaṃ mahaḥ|| 4 ||
kālārkabhakṣitaṃ sāṃkhyaśāstraṃ jñānasudhākaram||
kalāviśiṣṭaṃ bhūyo'pi pūrayiṣye vaco'mṛtaiḥ|| 5||
cidacidgranthibhedena mocayiṣye cito'pi ca ||
sāṃkhyabhāṣyamiṣeṇāsmāt prīyatāṃ mokṣado hariḥ|| 6 ||
tat tvameva tvamevaitadevaṃ śrutiśatoditam||
sarvātmanāvaidharmyaṃ śāstrasyāsyaiva gocaraḥ|| 7 ||
" ātmā vā are draṣṭavyaḥ śrotavyo mantavyo nididhyāsitavyaḥ" ityādiśrutiṣu paramapuruṣārthasādhanasyā''tmasākṣātkārasya hetutayā śravaṇāditrayaṃ vihitam| tatra śravaṇādāvupāyākāṅakṣāyāṃ smaryate -- 
śrotavyaḥ śrutivākyebhyo, mantavyaścopapattibhiḥ,|
matvā ca satataṃ dhyeyaṃ, ete darśanahetavaḥ|| iti |
dhyeyo yogaśāstraprakāreṇeti śeṣaḥ| tatra śrutibhyaḥ śruteṣu puruṣārthataddhetujñānatadviṣayātmasvarūpādiṣu śrutyavirodhinīrūpapattīḥ ṣaḍadhyāyīrūpeṇa vivekaśāstreṇa kapilamūrttibhagavānupadideśa| nanu nyāyavaiśeṣikābhyāmapyeteṣvartheṣu nyāyaḥ pradarśita iti tābhyāmasya gatārthatvaṃ , suguṇanirguṇatvādiviruddharūpairātmasādhakatayā tudyuktibhiratratyayuktīnāṃ virodhenobhayorapi durghaṭaṃ ca prāmāṇyamiti| maivam| vyāvahārikapāramārthikarūpaviṣayabhedena gatārthatvavirodhayorabhāvāt| nyāyavaiśeṣikābhyāṃ hi sukhiduḥkhodyanuvādato dehādimātravivekanātmā prathamabhūmikāyāmanumāpitaḥ, ekadā parasūkṣme praveśāsambhavāt, tadīyaṃ ca jñānaṃ dehādyātmatānirasanena vyāvahārikaṃ tattvajñānaṃ , yathā puruṣe sthāṇubhramanirasakatayā karacaraṇādimattvajñānaṃ vyavahāratastattvajñānam tadvat| ata eva -- 
prakṛterguṇasammūḍhāḥ sajjante guṇakarmasu|
tānakṛtsnavido mandān kṛtsnavinna vicālayet||
iti gītāyāṃ kartṛtvābhimāninastārkikasya akṛtsnavittvameva kṛtsnavitsāṃkhyāpekṣayoktaṃ, na tu sarvathaivājñajñatvamiti| tathā tadīyamapi jñānamaparavairāgyadvārā paramparayā mokṣasādhanaṃ bhavatyeveti tajjñānāpekṣayāpi ca sāṃkhyajñānameva pāramārthikaṃ, paravairāgyadvārā sākṣānmokṣasādhanaṃ ca bhavati| uktagītāvākyenātmākartṛtvavittvasyaiva
kṛtsnavittvasiddheḥ "tīrṇo hi tadā bhavati hṛdayasya śokān kāmādikaṃ mana eva manyamānḥ sannubholokāvanusañcarati dhyāyatīva lelāyatīva sa yadatra kañcit paśyatvananvāgatastena bhavati" ityādi tāttvikaśrutiśataiḥ,
prakṛteḥ kriyamāṇāni guṇaiḥ karmāṇi sarvaśaḥ|
ahaṅkāravimūḍhātmā karttāhamiti manyate ||
nirvāṇamaya evāyamātmā jñānamayo'malaḥ|
duḥkhājñānamayāṃ dharmāḥ prakṛteste tu nātmanaḥ||
ityāditāttvikasmṛtiśataiśca | nyāyavaiśeṣikoktajñānasya paramārthabhūmau bādhitatvācca | na caitāvatā nyāyādyaprāmāṇyam | vivakṣitārthe dehādyatirekāṃśe bādhābhāvād, "yatparaḥ śabdaḥ sa śabdārtha" iti nyāyāt| ātmanisapakhādimattvasya lokasiddhatayā tatra pramāṇāntarānapekṣaṇena tadaṃśasyānuvādatvānna śāstratātparyaviṣayatvamiti||
syādetat| nyāyavaiśeṣikābhyāmatrā'virodho bhavati | brahmamīmāṃsāyogābhyāṃ tu virādho'styeva, tābhyāṃ nityeśvarasādhanāt, atra ceśvarasya pratiṣiddhamānatvāt| na cā'trāpi vyāvahārakapāramārthikabhedena seśvaranirīśvaravādayoravirodho'stu , seśvaravādasyopāsanāparatvasambhavāditi vācyam| vinigamakābhāvāt | īśvaro hi durjñeya iti nirīśvaratvamapi lokavyavahārasiddhamaiśvaryavairāgyāyānuvaditaṃ śakyate, ātmanaḥ saguṇatvamiva , na tu kvā'pi śrutyādāvīśvaraḥ sphuṭaṃ pratiṣiddhyate, yena seśvaravādasyaiva vyāvahārikatvamavadhāryeteti|
atrocyate | atrāpi vyāvahārikapāramārthikabhāvo bhavati -- 
asatyamapratiṣṭhaṃ te jagadāhuranīśvaram||
ityādiśāstrairnirīśvaravādasya ninditatvātṣa asminneva śāstre vyāvahārikasyaiveśvarapratiṣedhasyaiśvaryavairāgyādyarthamanuvādatvaucityāt| yadi hi laukāyatikamatānusāreṇa nityaiśvaryaṃ na pratiṣiddhyet tadā paripūrṇanityanirdoṣaiśvaryadarśanena tatra cittāveśato vivekābhyāsapratibandhaḥ syāditi sāṃkhyācāryāṇāmāśayaḥ | seśvaravādasya na kvāpi nindādikamasti| yenopāsanādiparatayā tacchāstraṃ saṅkocyeta | yatta -- 
nāsti sāṅkhyasamaṃ jñānaṃ nāsti yogasamaṃ balam|
atra vaḥ saṃśayo mābhūjjñānaṃ sāṃkhyaṃ paraṃ matam||
ityādivākyaṃ, tad vivekāṃśa eva sāṃkhyajñānasya darśanāntarebhya utkarṣaṃ pratipādayati , na tvīśvarapratiṣedhāṃśe'pi | tathā parāśarādyakhilaśiṣṭasaṃvādādapi seśvaravādasyaiva pāramārthikatvamavadhāryate| api ca -- 
akṣapādapraṇīte ca kāṇāde sāṃkhyayogayoḥ|
tyājyaḥ śrutiviruddhoṃ'śaḥ śrutyekaśaraṇairnṛbhiḥ||
jaiminīye ca vaiyāse viruddhāṃśo na kaścan|
śrutyāṃ vedārthavijñāne śrutipāraṃ gatau hi tau||
iti parāśaropurāṇādibhyo'pi brahmamīmāṃsāyā īśvarāṃśe balavattam| tathā -- 
nyāyatantrāṇyanekāni taistairuktāni vādibhiḥ|
hetvāgamasadācārairyadyuktaṃ tadupāsyatām||
itimokṣadharmavākyadapi parāśarādyakhilaśiṣṭyavahāreṇa brahmamīmāṃsānyāyavaiśeṣikādyukta īśvarasādhakanyāya eva grāhyo balavattvāt| tathā -- 
yaṃ na paśyanti yogondrāḥ sāṃkhyā api maheśvaram|
anādinidhanaṃ brahma tameva śaraṇaṃ vraja ||
ityādikaurmādivākyaiḥ sāṅkhyanāmīśvarājñānasyaiva nārāyaṇādinā proktatvācca| kiñca brahmamīmāṃsāyā īśvara eva mukhyo viṣaya upakramādibhiravadhṛtaḥ | tatrāṃśe tasya bādhe śāstrasyaivāpramāṇyaṃ syād, 'yatparaḥ śabdaḥ sa śabdārtha' iti nyāyāt| sāṅkhyaśastrasyaca tu puruṣārthatatsādhanaprakṛtipuruṣavivekāveva mukhyo viṣaya iti īśvarapratiṣedhāṃśābādhe'pi nāprāmāṇyam "yatparaḥ śabdaḥ saśabdārthaḥ" iti nyāyāt| ataḥ sāvakāśatayā sāṅkhyameveśvarapratiṣedhāṃśe durbalamiti | na ca brahmamīmāṃsāyāmapīśvara eva mukhyo viṣayo, na tu nityaiśvaryamiti vaktuṃ śakyate | smṛtyanavakāśadoṣaprasaṅgapūrvapakṣasyānupapattyā
nityaiśvaryaviśiṣṭatvenaiva brahmamīmāṃsāviṣayatvā'vadhāraṇāt| brahmaśabdasya parabrahmaṇyeva mukhyatayā tu, 'athātaḥ parabrahmajijñāsā' iti na sūtritamiti | etena sāṅkhyavirodhād brahmayogadarśanayoḥ kāryeśvaraparatvamapi na śaṅkanīyam| prakṛtisvātantryāpattyā racanānupatteśca, 'nānumānam', ityādibrahmasūcatraparamparānupapatteśca| tathā "sa pūveṣāmapi gurūḥ kālenānavacchedāt iti yogasūtradīyabyāsabhāṣyābhyāṃ sphuṭamīśanityatā'vagamācceti| tasmādu abhyupagamavadāprauḍhavādādinaiva sāṃkhyasya vyāvahārikeśvarapratiṣedhaparatayā brahmamīmāṃsāyogābhyāṃ saha na virodhaḥ| abhyupagamavādaśca śāstre duṣṭaḥ| yathā viṣṇupurāṇe -- 
eto bhinnadṛśāṃ daitya ! vikalpitāḥ kathitā māyā|
kṛtvābhyupagamaṃ tatra saṅkepaḥ śrūyatāṃ mama || iti |
astu vā pāpināṃ jñānapratibandhārthamāstikadarśaneṣvapyaṃśataḥ śrutismṛtyaviruddheṣu tu mukhyaviṣayeṣu prāmāṇyamastyeva | ata eva padmapurāṇe brahmayogadarśanātiriktānāṃ darśanānāṃ nindāpyupapadyate | yathā tatra pārvatīṃ pratīśvaravākyam --
śruṇudevī ! pravakṣyāmi tāmasāni yathākramam|
yeṣāṃ śravaṇamātreṇa pātityaṃ jñānināmapi ||
prathamaṃ hi mayaivoktaṃ śaivaṃ pāśupatādikam|
macchaktyāveśitairvipraiḥ saṃproktāni tataḥparam||
kaṇādena tu samproktaṃ śāstraṃ vaiśeṣikaṃ mahat|
gotamena tathā vyāyaṃ sāṅkhyantu kapilena vai||
dvijanmanā jaimininā pūva vedamayārthataḥ|
nirīśvareṇa vādena kṛtaṃ śāstraṃ mahattaram||
dhiṣaṇena tathā proktaṃ cārvākamatigarhitam|
daityānāṃ nāśanārthāya viṣṇunā buddharūpiṇā|
bauddhaśāstramasat proktaṃ nagnanīlapaṭādikam||
māyāvādamasacchāstraṃ pracchannaṃ bauddhameva ca |
mayaiva kathitaṃ devi! kalau brāhmaṇarūpiṇā||
apārthaṃ śrutivākyānāṃ darśayam̐llokagarhitam
karmasvarūpatyājyatvamatra ca pratipādyate|
sarvakarmaparibhraṃśānnaiṣkarmyaṃ tatra cocyate|
parātmajīvayoraikyaṃ mayātra pratipādyate||
brahmaṇo'sya paraṃ rūpaṃ nirguṇaṃ darśitaṃ mayā|
sarvasya jagato'pyasya nāśanārthaṃ kalo yuge|
vedārthavanmahāśāstraṃ māyāvādamavaidikam|
mayaiva kathicataṃ devi ! jagatāṃ nāśakāraṇāt, || iti
adhikaṃ tu brahmamīmāṃsābhāṣye prapañcitamasmābhiriti tasmādāstikaśāstrasya na kasyāpyaprāmāṇyaṃ virodho vā, svasvaviṣayeṣu sarveṣām abādhāt, avirodhācceti||
nanvevaṃ puruṣabahutvāṃśe'pyasya śāstrasyābhyupagamavādatvaṃ syāt| na syāt avirodhāt | brahmamīmāṃsāyāmapi "aṃśo nānāvyapadeśāt" tyādisūtrajātaiḥ jīvātmabahutvasyaiva nirṇayāt| sāṅkhyasiddhapuruṣāṇāmātmatvaṃ tu brahmamīmāṃsayā bādhyata eva | 'ātmeti tūpagacchanti' ititatsūtreṇa paramātmana eva paramārthabhūmāvātmatvā'vadhāraṇāt | tathāpi ca sāṅkhyasya nāprāmāṇyam| vyāvahārikātmano jīvasyetaravivekajñānasya mokṣasādhanatve vivakṣatārthe bādhā bhāvāt| etena śrutismṛtiprasiddhayornānā'tmaikātmatvayorvyāvahārikapāramārthikabhedena avirodha iti brahmamīmāṃsāyāṃ prapañcitamasmābhiriti dik||
nanvevamapi tattvasamāsākhyasūtraiḥ sahasyāḥ ṣaḍadhyāyyāḥ paunaruktyamiti cenmaivam|
saṃkṣepavistararūpeṇobhayorapyapaunaruktyāt| ata evā'syāḥ ṣaḍadhyāyyā yogadarśanasyeva sāṅaakhyapravacanasaṃjñāyuktā| tattvasamāsākhyaṃ hi yat saṅkṣiptaṃ sāṅkhyadarśanaṃ tasyaiva prakarṣeṇāsyāṃ nirvacanamiti| viśeṣastvayaṃ yat ṣaḍadhyāyyāṃ tattvasamāsākhyoktārthavistaramātram| yogadarśane tvābhyāmabhyupagamavādapratiṣiddhasyaiveśvarasya nirūpaṇena nyūnatāparihāro'pīti| asya ca sāṅkhyasaṃjñā sānvayā -- 
saṅkhyāṃ prakurvate caiva prakṛtiṃ ca pracakṣate|
tattvāni ca caturviśat tena sāṅkhyā) prakīrttitāḥ||
ityādibhyo bhāratādivākyebhyaḥ | saṅkhyā samyagvivekenā'tmakathanamityarthaḥ| ataḥ sāṅaakhyaśabdasya yogarūḍhatayā "tatkāraṇaṃ sāṅkhyayogā'dhigamyam" ityādi śrutiṣu -- 
eṣā te'bhihitā sāṃkhye buddhiryoge tvimāṃ śrṛṇu|
ityādismṛtiṣu ca sāṃkhyaśabdena sāṃkhyaśāstrameva grāhyaṃ , na punararthāntaraṃ kalpanīyamiti |
tadidaṃ mokṣaśāstraṃ cikitsāśāstravaccaturvyūham| yathā hi -- roga, ārogyaṃ roganidānaṃ bhaiṣajyam, iti catvāro vyūhāḥ samūhāścitsāśāstrasya pratipādyāḥ, tathaiva heyaṃ hānaṃ heyahetu rhānopāyaśceti, catvāro vyūhā mokṣaśāstrasya pratipādyāṃ bhavanti mumukṣubhirjijñāsitatvāt| tatra trividhaṃ duḥkhaṃ heyam| tadatyantanivṛttirhānam| prakṛtipuruṣasaṃyogadvārā cā'viveko heyahetuḥ| vivekakhyātistu hānopāya iti | vyūhaśabdena caiṣāmupakaraṇasaṃgrahaḥ| tatra cādau phalatvenābhyarhitaṃ hānaṃ , tatpratiyogividhayaiva ca heyaṃ pratipādayiṣyan śāstrakāraḥ śiṣyā'vadhānāya śāstrā''rabhyaṃ pratijānīte -- 
atha trividhaduḥkhātyantanivṛttiratyantapuruṣārthaḥ|| 1 ||
athaśabdo'yamuccāraṇamātreṇa maṅgularūpaḥ | ata eva 'maṅgalācaraṇaṃ śiṣṭācārāt ' iti svayameva pañcamādhyāye vakṣyati | arthastvatrārthaśabdāsyādhikāra eva | praśnānantaryādīnāṃ puruṣārthena sahānvayāsambhavāt| jñānādyānantaryasya ca sūtraireva vakṣyamāṇatayā tatpratipādanavairyyāt| adhikārabhinārthatve śāstrārambhapratijñādyalābhaprasaṅgācca| tasmāt puruṣārthasyopākramopasaṃhāradarśanādadhikārārthatvamevocitam| 'taducchittiḥ puruṣārtha' ityupasaṃhāro bhaviṣyatīti | adhikāraścādhikyena prādhānyenāramṇam | āramśca yadyapi sākṣācchāstrasyaiva tathāpi taddvārā śāstrārthatadvicārayorapīti|
tathā ca sādhanādyupakaraṇasahito yathoktapuruṣārtho'dhikṛtaḥ prādhānyena nirūpayitumasmābhiḥ prārabdha iti sūtravākyārthaḥ| trividhamādhyātmikamādhibhautikamādhidaivikaṃ ca duḥkham| tatrātmānaṃ svasaṃghātam adhikṛtya pravṛttamityādhyātmikam -- śarīraṃ mānasaṃ ca | śārīraṃ vyādhyādyuttham [vātapittaśleṣmaṇāṃ vaiṣamyeṇa janitaṃ śārīram| kāmakrodhalobhamohamadamatsarādīnāṃ prakopeṇa bhavam mānasikañca] | tathā bhūtāni prāṇino'dhikṛtya pravṛttamityādhibautikam vyāghracorādyuttham| devānagnivāyvādīn adhikṛtya pravṛttamityādhidaivikam, dohaśītādyutthamiti bhāvaḥ| yadyapi sarvameva duḥkhaṃ mānasaṃ tathāpi manomātrajanyatvājanyatvābhyāṃ mānasatvāmānasatvāviśeṣaḥ| eṣāṃ trividhaduḥkhānāṃ yā'tyantanivṛttiḥ sthūlasūkṣmasādhāraṇyena niḥśeṣato nivṛttiḥ, so'tyantaṃḥ paramaḥ puruṣārthaḥ puruṣāṇāṃ buddheriṣṭa ityavāntaravākyārthaḥ| tatra sthūlaṃ duḥkhaṃ vartamānāvastham| tacca dvitīyakṣaṇādupari svayameva naṅkṣyati| ato na tatra jñānāpekṣāḥ atīte tu prāgeva naṣṭamiti na tatra sādhanāpekṣeti pariśeṣādanāgatāvasthasūkṣmaduḥkhanivṛttireva puruṣārthatayā prakṛte paryavasyati tathā ca yogasūtram -- "heyaṃ duḥkhamanāgatam" iti | nivṛttiśca na nāśo'pi tvatītāvasthā dhvaṃsaprāgabhāvayoratītānāgatāvasthāsvarūpatvāt satkāryavādibhirabāvanaṅgīkārāt| nanu kadācidapyavartamānamanāgatakaṃ duḥkhamapramāṇikam, ataḥ khapuṣpanivṛttivat tannivṛtterna puruṣārthatvaṃ yuktamiti| maivam| sarvacatra hi svasvakāryajananaśaktiryāvaddravyasthāyinīti pātañjale siddham| dāhādiśaktiśūnyasyāgnyādeḥ kvāpyadarśanāt| sā ca śaktiranāgatāvasthatattatkāryarūpā | iyameva copādānakāraṇasvarūpayogyatetyapi gīyate| ato yāvaccittasattā tāvadevānāgataduḥkhasattānumīyate | tannivṛttiśca puruṣārthaṃ iti | jīvanmuktidaśāyāṃ ca prārabdhakarmaphalātiriktānāṃ duḥkhānāmanāgatāvasthānāṃ bījākhyānaṃ dāho, videhakaivalye tu cittena saha vināśa ityavāntaraviśeṣaḥ| bījadāhaścāvidyāsahakāryucchedamātram| jñānasyāvidyāmātrocchedakatvasya loke siddhatvāt | ata eva cittena sahaiva duḥkhasya nāśaḥ| jñānasya sākṣādduḥkhādināśakatve pramāṇābhāvāditi|
nanu tathāpi duḥkhanivṛttirna puruṣārthaḥ sambhavati duḥkhasya cittadharmatvena puruṣe tannivṛttyasambhavāt | duḥkhanivṛttiśabdasya duḥkhānutpādārthakatve'pi puruṣe tasya nityasiddhatvāt | yattu kaṇṭhacāmīkaravat siddhe'pya'siddhatvabhramāt puruṣārthatā syāditi | tanna | evamapi pumānnirduḥkha iti śravaṇamananottaraṃ duḥkhahānārthaṃ nididhyāsanādau pravṛttyamupapatteḥ| bahvāyāsasādhye hyupāye phalaniścayādeva pravṛttirbhavati | prakṛte tu śravaṇamananābhyāṃ siddhatvajñānānnāprāmāṇyajñānā'nāskanditaḥ phalasyāsiddhatvaniścayo'stīti| kiñca bhavatu kadācidbhramādinā puruṣecchāviṣayatvaṃ duḥkhābhāvasya, śrutistu mohanāśinī kathaṃ siddhasya phalatvaṃ pratirādayet | "tarati śokamātmavit" , "vidvān harṣaśokau jahāti" ityādiriti|
atrocyate | " na nityaśuddhabuddhamuktasvabhāvasya tadyogastadyogādṛte" iti heyahetvavadhārakasūtreṇaivāyaṃ pūrvapakṣaḥ samādhasyate | tathāhi -- pratibimbarūpeṇa puruṣe'pi sukhaduḥkhe staḥ, anyathā tayorbhogyatvānupapatteḥ| sukhādigrahaṇaṃ hi bhogaḥ| grahaṇaṃ ca tadākāratā | sā ca kūṭambhacittau buddherarthākāravāt pariṇāmo na sambhavatītyagatyā pratibimbasvarūpatāyāmeva paryavasyati| ayameva buddhivṛttipratibimbo " vṛttisārūpyamitaratra " iti yogasūtreṇoktaḥ| 'satve'nutapyamāne tadākārānurodhāt puruṣo'pyanutapyata iva dṛśyate' iti yogabhāṣye ca tadākārānurodhaśabdena viśiṣyaiva tāpādiduḥkhasya pratibimba uktaḥ| ata eva ca puruṣasya buddhivṛttyuparāge sphaṭikaṃ dṛṣṭāntaṃ sūtrakāro vakṣyati -- 'kusumavacca maṇeḥ' iti | vedāntibhirapi cetane'dhyastatayaiva dṛśyamānamucyate| sa cādhyāsaḥ pratibimbaṃ vinā na ghaṭate| jñānamātrasyādhyāsattve ātmāśrayāt | adhyāsājjñānaṃ jñānameva cādhyāsa iti | tadetat smaryate 'pi -- 
tasmiṃściddarpaṇe sphāre samastā vastudṛṣṭayaḥ|
imāstāḥ pratibimbanti sarasīva taṭadrumāḥ|| iti |
atra hi dṛṣṭiśabdo buddhivṛttisāmānyaparo yuktisāmyāt| pratibimbaśca tattadupādhiṣu bimbākāraścittapariṇāma iti | tasmāt pratibimbarūpeṇa puruṣe duḥkhasambandho bhogākhyo'sti | atastenaiva rūpeṇa tannivṛtteḥ puruṣārthatvaṃ yuktam| ata eva puruṣārthatvamanyaśeṣatayā na sambhavatīti saiva svataḥ puruṣārthaḥ| duḥkhanivṛttistu kaṇṭhakādinivṛttivat tāvadarthyena na svataḥ puruṣārthaḥ| evaṃ sukhamapi na svataḥ puruṣārthaḥ| kintu tadbhoga eva svataḥ puruṣārthatvaṃ yātīti | tadidaṃ duḥkhaboganivṛtteḥ puruṣārthatvaṃ yogabhāṣye vyāsadevairuktam -- "tasmin nivṛtte punaridaṃ tāpatrayaṃ na bhuṅkta " iti | ataḥ śrutāvapi duḥkhanivṛtteḥ puruṣārthatvaṃ viṣayatāsambandhenaiva bodyam| tadetadyogavārtike prapañcitamasmābhiriti dik||
tadevamanena sūtreṇa vyūhadvayaṃ saṅkṣepeṇoddiṣṭam| vistarastvanayoḥ paścādbhaviteti|| 1||
ataḥ paraṃ vakṣyamāṇasya hānopāyavyūhasyākāṅkṣārthaṃ taditareṣāṃ hānopāyatvaṃ pratyācaṣṭe sūtrajātena -- 
nadṛṣṭāt tatsiddhirnivṛtte'pyanuvṛttidarśanāt || 2 ||
lokikādupāyāddhānāderatyantaduḥkhanivṛttisiddhirnāsti| kutaḥ? dhanādinā duḥkhe nivṛtte paścātte paścād dhanādikṣaye punarapi duḥkhānuvṛttidarśanādityarthaḥ| tathā ca śrutiḥ -- " amṛtatvasya tu nā''śā'sti vittena ityādi || 2||
nanvevaṃ dhanādyarjanasya kuñjaraśaucavad duḥkhānivarttakatve kathaṃ tatra pravṛttistatrāha -- 
prātyahikakṣutpratīkāravat tatpratīkāraceṣṭanāt
puruṣārthatvam|| 3||
dṛṣṭasādhanajanyāyāṃ duḥkhanivṛttāvatyantapuruṣārthatvameva nāsti| yathā kathañcit puruṣārthatvaṃ tvastyeva | kutaḥ ? prātyahikasya [ahani ahani bhavasye tyarthaḥ|] kṣudduḥkhasya nirākaraṇavadeva tena dhanādinā duḥkhanirākaraṇasya ceṣṭanādanveṣaṇādityarthaḥ| ato dhanādyarjjane pravṛttirūpapadyata iti bhāvaḥ| kuñcaraśaucādikamapyāpātaduḥkhanivarttakatayā mandapuruṣārtho bhavatyeveti || 3 ||
sa ca dṛṣṭasādhanajo mandapuruṣārtho vijñairheya ityāha -- 
sarvāsambhavāt sambhave'pi sattvāsambhavāddheyaḥ
pramāṇakuśalaiḥ|| 4 ||
sa ca dṛṣṭasādhanajo duḥkhapratīkāro duḥkhāduḥkhavivekaśāstrā'bhijñairheyo duḥkhapajñe nijñepaṇīyaḥ|
kutaḥ?sarvāsambhavāt -- sarvaduḥkheṣu dṛṣṭasādhanaiḥ pratīkārāsambhavāt| yatrāpi sambhavastatrāpi pratigrahapāpādyutthaduḥkhā''vaśyakatvamāha -- sambhave'pīti | sambhave'pi dṛṣṭopāyanāntarīyakādiduḥkhasamparkāvaśyambhāvādityarthaḥ| tathāca yogasūtram -- 'pāraṇāmatāpasaṃskāraduḥkhairguṇavṛttivirodhācca sarvameva duḥkhaṃ vivekina' iti || 4 ||
nanu dṛṣṭasādhanajanye sarvasminneva duḥkhapratīkāre duḥkhasambhedaniyamo 'prayojakaḥ, tathā ca smaryate -- 
yanna duḥkhena samminnaṃ na ca grastamanantaram|
abhilāṣopanītaṃ ca tat sukhaṃ svaḥpadāspadam|| iti , tatrāha -- 
utkarṣādapi mokṣasya sarvotkarṣaśruteḥ|| 5 ||
dṛṣṭasādhanāsādhyasya mokṣasya dṛṣṭasādhanasādhyarājyadibhyā utkarṣāta teṣu duḥkhasattā'vadhāryate | apiśabdāt triguṇatvāderapi | mokṣasyotkarṣe pramāṇaṃ 'sarvotkarṣaśrute' riti| 'na ha vai saśarīrasya sataḥ
priyāpriyayorapahatirasti | aśarīraṃ vā va santaṃ priyāpriye na spṛśata' ityādinā videhakaivalyasyotkarṣaśraterityarthaḥ|| 5 ||
nanu mā bhavatu dṛṣṭasādhanādatyantaduḥkhanivṛttiḥ| adṛṣṭasādhanāt tu vaidikakarmaṇaḥ syāt -- 'apāma' ityādiśrateriti tatrāha -- 
aviśeṣaścobhayoḥ|| 6 ||
ubhayoreva dṛṣṭādṛṣṭayoratyantaduḥkhanivṛttyasādhakatve yathoktataddhetutve cāviśeṣa eva mantavya ityarthaḥ| etadeva kārikāyāmuktam -- 
dṛṣṭavadānuśravikaḥ sa hyaviśuddhikṣayātiśayuktaḥ, iti|
guroranuśrūyata ityamuśravo vedaḥ| tadvihitayāgādirānuśravikaḥ| sa dṛṣṭopāyavadevāśuddhyā hiṃsādipāpena vināśisātiśayaphalakatvena ca yukta ityarthaḥ| nanu vaidhahiṃsāyāḥ pāpajanakatve balavadaniṣṭānanubandhīṣṭasādhanatvarūpasya vidhyarthasyānupapattiriti cenna| vaidhahiṃsājanyāniṣṭasyeṣṭotpattināntarīyakatveneśṭotpattināntarīyaka -- duḥkhādhikaduḥkhājanaratvarūpasya balavadaniṣāṭananubandhitvasya vidhyaṃśasyākṣateḥ| yattu vaidhahiṃsātiriktahiṃsāyā eva pāpajanakatvamiti | tadasat | saṅkoce pramāṇābhāvāt yudhiṣṭhirādīnāṃ svadharme.pi yuddhādau jñātavidhādipratyavāyaparihārāya prāyaścittaśravaṇācca|
tasmādyāsyāmyahaṃ tāta! dṛṣṭemaṃ duḥkhasannidhim|
trayīdharmamadharmāḍhyaṃ kimpākaphalasannibham||
iti mārkaṇḍeyavacanācca| "ahiṃsan sarvabhūtānyanyatra tīrthebhyaḥ" iti śrutistu vaidhātiriktahiṃsānivṛtteriṣṭasādhanatvameva vakti, na tu vaidhahiṃsāyā aniṣṭasādhanatvābhāvamapītyadikaṃ yogavārttike draṣṭavyamiti dik||
" na karmaṇā na prajayādhanena tyāgenaike amṛtatvamānaśuḥ" iti
'tameva viditvā'timṛtyumetinānyaḥ panthā vidyate'yanāya'
ityādiśrutivirodhena tu somapānādibhiramṛtatvaṃ gauṇameva mantavyam -- 
ābhūtasaṃplavaṃ sthānamamṛtatvaṃ hi bhāṣyate | iti viṣṇupurāṇāt|| 6 ||
tadevaṃ dṛṣṭādṛṣṭopāyayoḥ sākṣātparamapuruṣārthāsādhanatve sādhite tadupāyākāṅkṣāyāṃ vivekajñānapupāyo vaktavyaḥ| tatra vivekajñānamavivekākhyaduḥkhahetūcchedadvāraiva hānopāya ityāśayenādāvapi vivekamevetarapratiṣedhena heya.hetutayā pariśeṣayati praghaṭṭakena -- 
na svabhāvato baddhasya mokṣasādhanopadeśavidhiḥ|| 7 ||
duḥkhātyantanivṛttermokṣatvasyoktatayā bandho'tra duḥkhayoga eva| tasya bandhasya puruṣe na svābhāvikatvaṃ vakṣyamāṇalakṣaṇamasti | yato na svabhāvato baddhasya mokṣāya sādhanopadesya śrautasya vidhiranuṣṭhānaniyojyānāṃ
ghaṭate | na hyagneḥ svābhāvikādauṣṇyānmokṣaḥ sambhavati | svābhāvikasya yāvaddravyabhāvitvādityartaḥ| taduktamīśvaragītāyām -- 
yadyātmā malino'svaccho vikārī syāt svabhāvataḥ|
na hi tasya bhavenmuktirjanmāntaraśatairapi || iti ||
yasmin sati kāraṇavilambāt vilambo yasyotpattau na bhavati tasya tat svābhāvikamiti svābhāvikatvalakṣaṇam| nanu sarvadopalabhyāpatterduḥkhasya svābhāvikamiti svābhāvikatvalakṣaṇam| nanu sarvadopalabhbhāpatterduḥkhasya svābhāvikatvaśaṅkaiva nāstīti cenna. triguṇātmakatvena cittasya duḥkhasvabhāvatve'pi sattvādhikyenābhibhavāt sadā duḥkhānupalabdhivadātmano'pi tadamupalabdhisambhavāt | duḥśasvābhāvikatvavādibhibauddhaiścittasyaivātmatābhyupagamācva| athaivamātmanāśādeva mokṣo'stviti cenna| ahaṃ baddho vimuktaḥ syāmiti bandhasāmānādhikaraṇyenaiva mokṣasya puruṣārthatvāditi || 7 ||
bhavatvananuṣṭhānaṃ , tena kimityata āha -- 
svabhāvasyānapāyitvādananuṣṭhānalakṣaṇamaprāmāṇyam|| 8 ||
svabhāvasya yāvaddravyabhāvitvānmokṣā,mbhavena tatsādhanopadeśaśruteranuṣṭhānalakṣaṇamaprāmāṇyaṃ syādityarthaḥ|| 8 ||
nanu śrutibalādevānuṣṭhānaṃ syāt tatrāha -- 
nāśakyopadeśavidhirūpadiṣṭe'pyanupadeśaḥ|| 9 ||
nāśakyāya phalāyopadeśasyānuṣṭhānaṃ sambhavati | yata upadiṣṭe'pi vihite'pyaśakyasyopāye sa upadeśo na bhavati | kintūpadeśābhāsa eva | 'bādhitamarthaṃ vedo'pi na bodhayatīti' nyāyādityarthaḥ|| 9 ||
atra śaṅkate -- 
"śupaklapaṭavad bījavaccet"|| 10 ||
"nanu svābhāvikasyāpyapāyo dṛśyate | yathā śuklapaṭasya svābhāvikaṃ śauklyaṃ rāgeṇāpanīyate| yathā ca bījasya svābhāvikya'pyaṅkuraśaktiragnināpanīyate | ataḥ śuklapaṭavad bījavacca svābhāvikasya bandhasyāpya pāyaḥ puruṣesambhavatīti tadvadeva tatsādhanopadeśaḥ syāditi cediti yedityarthaḥ|" || 10 ||
samādhatte -- 
śaktyudṅavānudbhavābhyāṃ nā'śakyopadeśaḥ|| 11 ||
uktadṛṣṭāntayorapi nā'śakyāya svābhāvikāyāpāyopadeśo lokānāṃ bhavati | kutaḥ? śaktyudbhavābhyām| dṛṣṭāntadvaye hi śauklyāderāvirbhāvatirobhāvāveva bhavataḥ , na tu śauklyāṅkuraśaktyorabhāvo bhavati| rajakādivyāpārairyogisaṅkalpādibhiśca raktapaṭabhṛṣṭabījayoḥ punaḥ śauklyāṅkuraśaktyāvirbhāvādityarthaḥ| nanvevaṃ puruṣe'pi duḥkhaśaktirobhāva eva mokṣo'stviti cenna| duḥkhā'tyantanivṛttereva loke puruṣārthatvānubhavāt, śrutismṛtyā puruṣārthatvasiddheśca , na tu dṛṣṭāntayoriva tirobhāvamātrasyeti| kiñca duḥkhaśaktitirobhāvamātrasya mokṣatve kadācidyogīśvarasaṅkalpādinā śaktyudbhavasya bhṛṣṭabījeṣviva mukteṣvapi sambhavenānirmokṣāpattiriti|| 11||
svabhāvato bandhaṃ nirākṛtya nimittebhyo'pi bandhamapākaroti sūtrajātena | puruṣe duḥkhasya naimittikatve'pi 5ānādyupāyocchedyatvaṃ na ghaṭate, anāgatāvasthasūkṣmaduḥkhasya yāvaddravyabhāvitvādityāśayena naimittikatvaṃ nirākriyate -- 
na kālayogato vyāpino nityasya sarvasambandhāt|| 12 ||
nāpi kālasambandhanimittakaḥ puruṣasya bandhaḥ| kutaḥ ?| vyāpino nityasya kālasya sarvāvacchedena sarvadā muktāmuktasakalapuruṣasambandhāt sarvāvacchedena sadā sakalapuruṣāṇāṃ bandhāpatterityartaḥ| atra ca prakāraṇe kāladeśakarmādīnāṃ nimittatvasāmānyaṃ nāpalapyate śrutismṛtiyuktibhiḥ siddhatvāt, kintu yannaimittikatvaṃ
pākajarūpādivantimittajanyatvaṃ tadeva bandhe pratiṣidhyate -- puruṣe bandhasyaupādhikatvābhyupagamāt| nanu kālādinimittakatve'pi sahakāryantarasambhavāsambhavābhyāṃ vyavasthā syāditi cet| evaṃ sati yatsaṃyoge satyavaśyaṃ bandhastatraiva sahakāriṇi lāghavād bandho yuktaḥ| puruṣe bandhavyavahārasyaupādhikatvenāpyupapatteriti kṛtaṃ naimittikatveneti
|| 12 ||
na deśayogato'pyasmāt|| 13 ||
deśayogato'pi bandhaḥ| kutaḥ? asmāt pūrvasūtroktānmuktāmuktasarvapuruṣasambandhānmuktasyāpi bandhāpatterityartaḥ|| 13 ||
nāvasthāto dehadharmatvāt tasyāḥ|| 14 ||
saṅghātaviśeṣarūpatākhyā deharūpā yā'vasthā tannimittato'pi puruṣasya bandhaḥ| kutaḥ? tasyā avasthāyā dehadharmatvāt | acetanadharmatvādityarthaḥ. anyadarmasya sākṣādanyabandhakatve'tiprasaṅgānmuktasyāpi bandhāpatterityarthaḥ|| 14 ||
nanu puruṣasyāpyavasthāyāṃ kiṃ bādhakaṃ, tatrāha -- 
asaṅge'yaṃ puruṣa iti || 15 ||
iti -- śabdo hetvarthe| puruṣasyāsaṅaagatvādavasthāyā dehamātradharmatvamiti pūrvasūtreṇānvayaḥ| puruṣasyāvasthārūpavikārasvīkāre | vakārahetusaṃyogākhyaḥ saṅgaḥ prasajjyeteti bhāvaḥ| asaṅgatve ca śrutiḥ -- 'sa yadatra kiñcit paśyatyananvāgatastena bhavati' | asaṅgo hyayaṃ puruṣa iti saṅgaśca saṃyogamātraṃ na bhavati, kāladeśasambandhasya pūrvamuktatvāt| śrutismṛtiṣu padmapattrasthajalaneva padmapatrasyāṅgatāyāḥ puruṣāsaṅgatāyāṃ dṛṣṭāntatāśravaṇācca
|| 15 ||
na karmaṇānya dharmatvādatiprasakteśca|| 16 ||
na hi vihitaniṣiddhakarmaṇāpi puruṣasya bandhaḥ| karmaṇāmanātmadharmatvat| anyadharmeṇa sākṣādanyasya bandhe ca muktasyāpi bandhāpatteḥ| nanu svasvopādhikarmaṇā bandhāṅgīkāre nāyaṃ doṣa ityāśayena hetvantaramāha| atiprasakteśceti || pralayādāvapi duḥkhayogarūpabandhāpatteścetyarthaḥ| sahakāryyantaravilambato vilambakalpanaṃ ca prāgeva nirākṛtaṃ , na kālayoga ityādisūtra iti || 16 ||
nanvevaṃduḥkhayogarūpo'pi bandhaḥ karmasāmānādhikaraṇyānurodhena cittasyaivāstu, duḥkhasya cittadharmatāyāḥ siddhatvāt, kimarthaṃ puruṣasyāpi kalpate ityāśaṅkāyāmāha -- 
vicitrabhogānupapattiranyadharmatve|| 17 ||
duḥkhayogarūpabandhasya cittamātradharmatve vicitrabogānupapattiḥ| puruṣasya hi duḥkhayogaṃ vināpi duḥkhasākṣātkārākhyabhogasvīkāre sarvapuruṣaduḥkhādīnāṃ sarvapuruṣabhogyatā syānniyāmakābhāvāt | tataścāyaṃ duḥkhabhoktā'yāṃ ca sukhabhoktetyādirūpabhogavaicitryaṃ nopapadyatetyarthaḥ| ato bhogavaicitryopapattaye bhoganiyāmakatayā duḥkhādiyogarūpo bandhaḥ puruṣe'pi svīkāryaḥ| sa ca papuruṣe duḥkhayogaḥ pratibimbarūpa eveti prāgevoktam| pratibimbaśca svopādhivṛttereva na sarvapuṃsā sarvaduḥkhabhoga iti yogabhāṣyādayaṃ siddhāntaḥ siddhaḥ| citte ca puruṣasya svatvaṃ svabhuktavṛttivāsanāvattvamiti | ya.ttu cittasyaiva bandhamokṣo, na puruṣasyeti śrutismṛtiṣu gīyate, tad bimbarūpaduḥkhayogarūpaṃ pāramārthikaṃ bandhamādāya bodhyam||17||
sākṣāt prakṛtinimittakatvamapi bandhasyāpākaroti -- 
prakṛtinibandhanāccenna, tasyā api pāratantryam|| 18 ||
nanu pirakṛtinimittād bandho bhavatviti cenna | yatastasyā api bandhakatve saṃyogapāratantryamuttaratra vakṣyamāṇamasti | saṃyogaviśeṣaṃ vināpi bandhakatve pralayādāvapi duḥkhabandhaprasaṅaagādityarthaḥ| prakṛtinibadhanā cediti pāṭhe tu prakṛtinibandhanā ced bandhanetyarthaḥ|| 18 ||
ato yatparatantrā prakṛtirbandhakāraṇaṃ sambhavet tasmādeva saṃyogaviśeṣādaupādhiko bandho'gnisaṃyogājjalauṣṇyavaditi svasiddhāntamanenaiva prasaṅgenāntarāla evādhārayati -- 
na nityaśuddhabuddhamuktasvabhāvasya tadyogastadyogādṛte || 19 ||
tasmāt tadyogādṛte prakṛtisaṃyogaṃ vinā na puruṣasya tadyogo bandhasamparko'sti, api tu sa eva bandhaḥ| bandhasyaupādhikatvalābhāya nañdvayena vakroktiḥ| yadi hi bandhaḥ prakṛtisaṃyogajanyaḥ syāt pākajarūpavat, tadā tadvadeva tadviyoge'pyanuvartteta| na ca dvitīyakṣaṇāderduḥkhanāśakatvaṃ kalpyam| kāraṇanāśasya kāryanāśakatāyāḥ kluptatvena tenaivopapattāvasmābhistadakalpanāt| vṛttirhi duḥkhāderūpādānam| ato dīpaśikhāvat kṣaṇabhaṅgurāyā vṛtterāśuvināśaṣitvenaiva taddharmāṇāṃ duḥkhecchādīnāṃ vināśaḥ sambhavatīti | ataḥ prakṛtiviyoge bandhābhāvādaupādhika eva bandho, na tu svābhāviko naimittiko veti | tathā saṃyogānivṛttireva sākṣāddhānopāya ityapi vakroktiphalam| tathā ca smṛtiḥ -- 
yathā jvaladgṛhāśliṣṭagṛhaṃ vicchidya rakṣyate|
tathā sadoṣaprakṛtivicchinno'yaṃ na śocati || iti ||
vaiśeṣikāṇāmiva pāramārthiko duḥkhayoga iti bhramo mā bhūdityetadarthaṃ nityetyādi || yathā svabhāvaśuddhasya sphaṭikasya rāgayogo na japāyogaṃ vinā ghaṭate , tathaiva nityaśuddhādisvabhāvasya puruṣasyopādhisaṃyogaṃ vināduḥkhasaṃyogo na ghaṭate, svato duḥkhādyasambhavādityardha| taduktaṃ saure -- 
yathā hi kevalo raktaḥ sphaṭiko lakṣyate janaiḥ|
rañjakādyupadhānena tadvat paramapūruṣaḥ|| iti ||
nityatvaṃ kālānavacchinnatvam| śuddhādisvabhāvatvaṃ ca nityaśuddhatvādikam ṣa tatra nityaśuddhatvaṃ sadā pāpapuṇyaśūnyatvam| nityabuddhatvamaluptacidrūpatvam| nityamuktatvaṃ sadā pāramārthikaduḥkhā'yuktatvam| pratibimbarūpaduḥkhayogastvapāramārthiko bandha iti bhāvaḥ| ātmano nityaśuddhatvādau ca śrutiḥ -- 'ayamātmā sanmātro nityaḥ śuddho buddhaḥ satyo mukto nirañjano vibhuḥ' ityādi ḥ| nanvasya mananaśāstratvādatrarthe yuktirapi vaktavyeti cet, satyam| 'na tadyogastadyogādṛta' ityanena nityaśuddhatvādau yuktirapyuktaiva | tathāhi ātmani nityatvavibhutvādikaṃ tāvannyādidarśaneṣveva sādhitam| tatra nityasya vibhorātmano yadyogaṃ vinā duḥkhādyakhilavikārairyogo na bhavati, tasyaivāntaḥkaraṇasya sarvasammatakāraṇasya tadupādānakāraṇatvameva yuktam, lāghavāt| sarvavikāreṣvantaḥkaraṇasyaivānvayavyatirekābhyāṃ ca | na punarantarvikāreṣu manaso nimittatvamātmanaścopādānatvaṃ yuktam| kāraṇadvayakalpane gauravāt| nanvahaṃ sukhī duḥkhī karomātyādyanubhavādātmano vikāropādānatvasiddhiriti cenna| ahaṃ gaura ityādibhramaśatāntaḥpātitvenāprāmāṇyaśaṅkāskanditatayoktapratyakṣāṇāmuktatarkānugṛhītānumānāpekṣayā durbalatvāt| ātmanaścinmātratve tu yuktiragre vakṣyata iti dik||
tasmāt tatsaṃyogādayetanaṃ cetanāvadiva liṅgam|
guṇakartṛtve ca tathā kartteva bhavatyudāsīnaḥ|| iti || 20 ||
kartṛtvamatra duḥkhātvādisakalavikāropalakṣaṇam| tathā yogasūtre'pyasya sūtrasyaivārtha uktaḥ -- draṣṭṛdṛśyayoḥ saṃyogo heyaheturiti| gītāyāṃ ca -- 
puruṣaḥ prakṛtistho hi bhuṅkte prakṛtijān guṇān| iti||
prakṛtisthaḥ prakṛtau saṃyuktaḥ| tathā ca śrutāvapi -- 
ātmondriyamanoyuktaṃ bhoktetyāhurmanīṣiṇaḥ| iti ||
na ca kālādivadeva prakṛtisaṃyogo'pi muktāmuktapuruṣasādhāraṇatayā kathaṃ bandhaheturiti vācyam| janmā'paranāmnaḥsvasvabuddhibhāvāpannaprakṛtisaṃyogaviśeṣasyaivātra saṃyogaśabdārthatvāt | yogabhāṣye vyāsaistathā vyākhyātatvāt | buddhivṛttyupādhinaiva puruṣe duḥkhayogācca| vaiśeṣikādivadeva bhogajanakatāvacchedakatvenā'ntaḥkaraṇasaṃyoge vaijātyaṃ cāsmābhirapīṣṭam| ato na suṣuptyādau bandhaprasaṅgaḥ| svasvabhuktavṛttivāsanāvadyatkiñcidavṛttitatsaṃskārapravāho'pyanādirataḥ svasvāmibhāvavyavasthitiḥ||
kaścit tu prakṛtipuruṣayoḥ saṃyogāṅgīkāre puruṣasya pariṇāmasaṅgau prasajjyeyātām, ato'trā'viveka eva yogaśabdārthau, na tu saṃyoga iti | tanna. 'tadyogo'pyavivekā' diti sūtreṇāvivekasya yogahetutāyā eva sūtrakāreṇa vakṣyamāṇatvāt| 'svasvāmiśaktyoḥ svarūpavabdhihetu saṃyogaḥ', 'tasya heturavidyeti' sūtrābhyāṃ pātañjale'pi saṃyogahetutvasyaivāvidyāyā uktatvācca| kiñca vivekābhāvarūpasyāvivekasya saṃyogatve pralayādāvapi prakṛtipuruṣasaṃyogasattvena bhogādyāpattiḥ| mithyājñānarūpasyāvivekasya ca saṃyogatve ātmāśrayaḥ,pumprakṛtisaṃyogasyājñānādihetutvāditi| tasmādavivekātirikto yogo vaktavyaḥ| sa ca saṃyoga eva, anyasyāprāmāṇikatvāt| saṃyogaśca na pariṇāmaḥ, sāmānyaguṇātiriktadharmotpattyaiva pariṇāmitvavyavahārāt| anyathā kūṭasthasya sarvagatatvarūpavibhutvānupapatteḥ| nāpi saṃyogamātraṃ saṅgaḥ, pariṇāmahetusaṃyogasyaiva saṅgaśabdārthatāyā vaktavyatvāditi ||
nanu tathāpi kathaṃ nityayoḥ prakṛtipuruṣayormahadādiheturanityaḥ saṃyogo ghaṭata iti cenna| prakṛteḥ paricchinnāparicchinnatacrividhaguṇasamudāyarūpa tayā paricchinnaguṇāvacchedena puruṣasaṃyogotpatteḥ sambhavāt| śrutismṛtisiddhatvāt prakṛtisaṃyogakṣobhayoriti| etacca yogavārtike prapañcitamasmābhiḥ||
aparastu bhogyabhoktṛyogyataivānayoḥ saṃyoga ityāha | tadapi na| yogyatāyā nityatve jñānanivartyatvānupapteḥ| anityatve kimaparāddhaṃ saṃyogena, pariṇāmitvāpatteḥ samānatvāt| bhogyabhoktṛyogyatāyāḥ saṃyogarūpatvasya sūtradiṣvanuktatvena aprāmāṇikatvācceti | tasmātsaṃyogaviśeṣa evātra bandhākhyaheyahetutayā sūtrakārābhipreta iti svayaṃ bandhaheturavadhāritaḥ|| 19 ||
idānīṃ nāstikābhipretā api bandhahetavo nirākarttavyāḥ | tatra, 'ṣaḍabhijño daśabalo'dvayavādī vināyakaḥ' ityānuśāsanādisiddhāḥ kṣaṇikavijñānātmavādino bauddhaprabhedā evamāhuḥ -- nāsti prakarṛtyādi bāhyāṃ vastvanyad , yena tatsaṃyogādaupādhikastāttviko vā bandhaḥ syāt| kintu kṣaṇikavijñānasantānamātramadvitīyaṃ tattvam, anyat sarvaṃ sāṃvṛttikama| saṃvṛttiścāvidyā mithyājñānākhyā | tata eva bandha iti | tathā ca tairuktam -- 
abhinno'pi hi buddhātmā viparyasanidarśanaiḥ|
grāhyagrāhakasaṃvittivedavāniva lakṣyate || iti ||
tanmatamādau nirākriyate -- 
nāvidyāto'pyavastunā bandhāyogāt || 20 ||
apiśabdaḥ pīrvoktakālādyapekṣayā, avidyāto'pi na sākṣādbandhayogaḥ| udvaitavādināṃ teṣāmavidyāyā apyavastutvena tayā bandhānaucityāt| na hi svāpnarajjvā bandhanaṃ dṛṣṭamityarthaḥ| bandho'pyavāstava iti cenna| svayaṃ sūtrakāreṇa nirākariṣyamāṇatvāt | vijñānādvaitaśravaṇottaraṃ bandhanivṛttaye yogābhyāsabhyupagamavirodhācca| bandhamithyātvaśravaṇenabandhanivṛttyākhyaphalasiddhatvaniścayāt tadarthaṃ bahvāyāsasādhyayogāṅgānuṣṭhānāsambhavāditi || 20 ||
vastutve siddhāntahāniḥ|| 21 ||
yadi cāvidyāyā vastutvaṃ svīkriyate tadā svābhyuugatasyā'vidyānṛtatvasya hānirityarthaḥ|| 21 ||
vijātīyadvaitāpāttiśca || 22 ||
kiñcavidyāyā vastutve kṣaṇikavijñāsantānādvijātīyaṃ dvaitaṃ prasajyeta| tacca bhavatāmaniṣṭamityarthaḥ| santānāntaḥpātakivyaktīnāmānantyāt sajātīyadvaitamipyata evetyāśayena vijātīyeti viśeṣaṇam| nanvavidyāyā api jñānaviśeṣatvādavid‌yayāpi kathaṃ vijātīyadvaitamiti cenna| jñānarūpāvidyāyā bandhottarakālīnatayā vāsanārūpā'vidyāyā eva tairbandhahetutvābhyupagamāt| sāvanā tu jñānādvijātīyaiveti||
ebhiśca sūtrairbrahmamīmāṃsāsiddhānto nirākriyata iti bhramo nakarttavyaḥ| brahmamīmāṃsāyāṃ kenāpi sūtreṇāvidyāmātrato bandhasyā'nuktatvāt|'avibhāgo vacanādi'tyādi sūtrairbrahmamīmāṃsāyā abipretasyāvibhāgalakṣaṇādvaitasyāvidyādivāstavatve'pyavirodhacca | yattu vedāntibruvāṇāmādhunikasya māyāvādasyātra liṅgaṃ dṛśyate tat teṣāmapi vijñānavādyekadeśitayā yuktameva -- 
māyāvādamasacchāstraṃ pracchannaṃ bauddhameva ca |
mayaiva kathitaṃ devi kalau brahmaṇarūpiṇā||
ityādipadmapurāṇasthaśivavākyaparamparābhyaḥ| na tu tadvedāntamatam -- 
vedārthavanmahāśāstraṃ māyāvādamavaidikam|
iti tadvākyaśeṣāditi | māyāvādino'tra cana sākṣāt prativāditvaṃ, vijātīyetiviśeṣaṇavairthyāt | māyāvāde sajātīyādvaitasyāpyanabhyupagamāditi| tasmādatra prakaraṇe vijñānavādināṃ bandhahetuvyavasthaiva sākṣānnirākriyate||
anayaiva ca rītyā navīnānāmapi pracchannabauddhānāṃ māyāvādināmavidyāmātrasya tucchasya bandhahetutvaṃ nirākṛta veditavyam| asmanmate tvavidyāyāḥ kūṭasthanityatārūpapāramārthikasattvābhāvo'pi ghaṭādivadvāstatvena vakṣyamāṇasaṃyogadvārā bandhahetutve yathoktabādhā'navakāśaḥ| evaṃ yogamate brahmamīmāṃsāmate'pīti || 22 ||
punaḥ śaṅkate -- 
viruddhobhayarūpā cet || 23 ||
nanu viruddha yadubhayaṃ sadasidvilakṣamaṃ vā tadrūpaivāvidyāvaktavyā| ato na tayā pāramārthikādvaitabhaṅga iti cedityarthaḥ| svayaṃ tu sadasattvaṃ prapañcasya yad vakṣyati, tatra sattvāsattve vyaktā'vyaktatvarūpatvādviruddhe eva na bhavata iti sabacayituṃ viruddhapadopādānam|| 23 ||
pariharati -- 
na tādṛkpadārthāpratīteḥ || 24 ||
sugamam || api cāvidyāyāḥ sākṣādeva duḥkhayogākhyabandhahetutve jñānenāvidyākṣayānantaraṃ prārabdhabhogānupapattiḥ, bandhaparyāyasya duḥkhabhogasya kāraṇanāśādati | asmadādimate tu nāyaṃ doṣaḥ| saṃyogadvāraivāvidyākarmādīnāṃ bandhahetutvāt | janmākhyaśca saṃyogaḥ prārabdhasamāptiṃ vinā na naśyatīti || 24 ||
punaḥ śaṅkate -- 
na vayaṃ ṣaṭpadārthavādino vaiśeṣikādivat || 25 ||
nanu vaiśaṣeṣikādyāstikavanna vayaṃ ṣaṭṣoḍaśādiniyatapadārthavādinaḥ| ato'pratīto'pi sadasadātmakaḥ sadasadvilakṣaṇo vā padārtho'vidyetyabhyupeyamiti bhāvaḥ|| 25 ||
pariharati -- 
aniyatatve'pi nāyauktikasya saṅgraho'nyathā
bālonmattādisamatvam|| 26 ||
padārthaniyamo mā'stu, tathāpi bhāvābhāvavirodhena yuktiviruddhasya sadasadātmakapadārthasya saṅgraho bhavadvacanamātrācchiṣyāṇāṃ na sambhavati| anyathā bālakādyusyāpyayauktikasya saṅgrahaḥ syādityarthaḥ| śrutyādikaṃ cāsminnarthe
sphuṭaṃ nāsti | yuktivirodhena ca sandigdhaśruterarthāntarasiddhiriti bhāvaḥ||
nāsadrūpā na sadrūpā māyā naivobhayātmikā|
sadasadbhyāmanirvācyā mithyābhūtā sanātanī||
ityādisaurādivākyānāṃ tvayamarthaḥ -- 
vikārajananīṃ māyāmaṣṭarūpāmajāṃ dhruvām|
ityādiśrutisiddhā māyākhyā prakṛtiḥ paramārthasatī na bhavati , pūrvapūrvavikārarūpaiḥ pratikṣaṇamapāyāt| nāpi paramārthasatī bhavati | arthakriyākāritvena śaśaśrṛṅgavilakṣaṇatvāt| nā'pi tadubhayātmikā virodhācca| ataḥ sadasadbhyāmanirvācyā, satyevetyasatyeveti ca nirdhāryopadeṣṭumaśakyā| kintu mithyābhūtālayākhyavyāvahārikāsattvavatī, pariṇāminityatārūpavyavahārikasattvavatī ceti | etaccāgre prapañcayiṣyāma iti dik||
etatprakāraṇopanyastāni ca sarvāṇyeva dūṣaṇānyādhunike'pi māyāvāde yojanīyāni || 26 ||
apare nāstikā āhuḥ -- 'kṣaṇikā bāhyaviṣayāḥ santi teṣāṃ vāsanayā jīvasya bandha' iti | tadapi dūṣayati -- 
nānādiviṣayoparāganimittako'pyasya || 27 ||
asyātmanaḥ pravāharūpeṇānādiryā viṣayavāsanā, tannimittako'pi bandho na sambhavatītyarthaḥ| nimittato'pyasyeti pāṭhastu samīcīnaḥ || 27 ||
atra hetumāha -- 
na bāhyābhyantarayorūparajyoparañjakabhāvo'pi deśavyavadhānāt snādhnasthapāṭaliputrasthayoriva || 28 ||
tanmate paricchinno dehāntaḥstha evātmā | tasyā''bhyantarasya na bāhyaviśeṣayeṇa sahoparajyoparañjakabhāvo'pi sambhavati | kutaḥ? snudhnasthapāṭaliputrasthayoriva, deśavyavadhānādityarthaḥ| saṃyoge satyeva hi vāsanākhya uparāgo dṛṣṭaḥ| yathā mañjiṣṭhāvastrayoḥ, yathā vā puṣpasphaṭikayoriti| apiśabdena svamate'pi saṃgābhāvādiḥ samuccīyate| smudhnapāṭaliputrauviprakṛṣṭau deśaviśeṣau || 28 ||
nanu bhavatāmindriyāṇāmivāsmākamātmano viṣayadeśe gamanādviṣayasaṃyogena viṣayoparāge vaktavyastatrāha -- 
dvayorekadeśalabdhoparāgānna vyavasthā|| 29 ||
dvayoḥ baddhamuktātmanorekasmin viṣayadeśe labdhaviṣayoparāgānna bandhamokṣavyavasthā syāt| muktasyāpi bandhāpatterityarthaḥ|| 29 ||
atra śaṅkate -- 
adṛṣṭavaśāccet || 30 ||
nanvekadeśasambandhena viṣayasaṃyogasamye'pyadṛṣṭadevoparāgalābha iti cedityarthaḥ|| 30 ||
pariharati -- 
na dvayorekakālāyogādupakāryopakārakabhāvaḥ|| 31 ||
kṣamikatvābhyupagamād dvayoḥ kartṛbhoktrorekakālāsattvena nopakāryopakārakabhāvaḥ| na kartṛniṣṭhādṛṣṭena bhoktṛniṣṭho viṣayoparāgaḥ sambhavatītyarthaḥ|| 31 ||
śaṅkate -- 
putrakarmavaditi cet || 32 ||
nanu yathā pitṛniṣṭhena putrakarmaṇā putrasyopakāro bhavataci tadvadravyadhikaraṇenaivādṛṣṭena viṣayoparāgaḥ syādityarthaḥ|| 32 ||
dṛṣṭāntāsiddhyā pariharati -- 
nāsti hi sthira ekātmā yo garbhādhānādinā saṃskriyate || 33 ||
putreṣṭyāpi tanmate putrasyopakāro na ghaṭate | hi yasmāt tatra tanmate garbhādhānamārabhya janmaparyantaṃ sthāyī eka ātmā nāsti yo janmottarakālīnakarmādhikārārthaṃ putreṣṭyā saṃskriyeteti dṛṣṭāntasyāpyasiddhirityarthaḥ| putreṣṭyā janitena putropādhiniṣṭhā'dṛṣṭenaiva putropādhidvārā putrasyopakārādityasmanmate'pi na dṛṣṭāntāsiddhiriti bhāvaḥ|| 33 ||
nanu bandhasyāpi kṣaṇikatvādaniyatakāraṇako'bhāvakāraṇako vā bandho'stvityāśayenāparo nāstikaḥ pratyavatiṣṭhate -- 
sthirakāryāsiddheḥ kṣamikatvam|| 34 ||
bandhasyeti śeṣaḥ| bhāvastūkta eva| atrāyaṃ prayogaḥ| vivādāspadaṃ bandhādi kṣaṇikaṃ sattvāt dīpaśikhādivaditi | na ca ghaṭādau vyabhicārastasyāpi pakṣasamatvāt | etadevoktaṃ sthirakāryāsiddheriti || 34 ||
samādhatte -- 
na pratyabhijñābādhāt || 35 ||
na kasyāpi kṣaṇikatvamiti śeṣaḥ| yadevāhamadrākṣaṃ tadevāhaṃ spṛśāmītyādipratyabhijñayā sthairyasiddheḥ, kṣaṇikatvasya bādhāt, pratipakṣānumānenetyarthaḥ| tadyathā bandhādi sthira sattvāt ghaṭādivaditi asmanmata evānukūlatarkasattvena na satpratipakṣatā | pradīpādau ca sūkṣmānekakṣaṇānākalanena kṣaṇikatvabhrama eva pareṣāmiti|| 35 ||
śrutinyāyavirodhācca || 36 ||
'sadeva saumyedamagra āsīt' 'tama evedamagra āsī' dityādiśrutibhiḥ kathamasataḥ sajjāyetetyādiśrautādiyuktibhiśca kāryakāraṇātmakākhilaprapañce kṣaṇikatvānumānasya virodhānna kṣaṇikatvaṃ kasyāpītyarthaḥ
||36 ||
dṛṣṭāntāsiddheśca|| 37 ||
pradīpaśikhādidṛṣṭānte kṣamikatvāsiddheśca na kṣaṇikatvānumānamityarthaḥ|. 37 ||
kiñca kṣaṇikatāvādināṃ mṛdghaṭādisthale'pi kāryakāraṇabhāvaḥ pravṛttinivṛttyanyathānupapattisiddho nopapadyetetyāha -- 
yugapajjāyamānayorna kāryakāraṇabhāvaḥ|| 38 ||
kiṃyugapajjāyamānayoḥ kāryakāraṇabhāvaḥ kiṃ vā kramikayoḥ ? tatra nādyo, vinigamakābhāvādibhya iti bhāvaḥ|| 38 ||
nāntya ityāha -- 
pūrvāpāye uttarāyegāt || 39 ||
pūrvasya kāraṇasyāpāyakāle uttarasya kāryasyotpattyanaucityādapi nakṣaṇikavāde sambhavati
kāryakāraṇabhāvaḥ| upādānakāraṇānugatatayaiva kāryānubhavādityarthaḥ|| 39 ||
upādānakāraṇamadhikṛtyaiva dūṣaṇāntaramāha -- 
tadbhāve tadayogādubhayavyabhicārādapi || 40 ||
yataḥ pūrvasya bhāvakāle uttarasyāsambandho'ta ubhayavyabhicāradanvayavyatirekavyabhicārādapi na kāryakāraṇabhāva ityarthaḥ| tathāhi -- yadopādeyotpattistadopādānaṃ, yadā copādānābhāvastadopādeyotpattyabhāva ityanvayatirekeṇaivopādānopādeyayoḥ kāryakāraṇabhāvagraho bhavati | tatra kṣaṇikatvena kramikayostayorvirūddhakālatayā'nvayavyabhicārābhyāṃ na kāryakāraṇabhāvasiddhiriti|| 40 ||
nanu nimittakāraṇasyevopādākāraṇasyāpi pūrvabhāvamātreṇaiva kāraṇatāstu, tatrāha -- 
pūrvabhāvamātre na nibhamaḥ|| 41 ||
pūrvabhāvamātrābhyupagame cedamevopādānamiti niyamo na syānnimittakāraṇānāmapi pūrvabhāvāviśeṣāt | upādānanimittayorvibhāgaḥ sarvalokasiddha ityarthaḥ|| 41 ||
apare ta nāstikā āhuḥ 'vijñānātiriktavastvabhāvena bandho'pi vijñānamātraṃ svapnapadārthavat | ato'tyantamithyātvena na tatra kāraṇamastī'ti| tanmatamapākaroti -- 
na vājñānamātraṃ bāhyapratīteḥ|| 42 ||
na vijñānamātraṃ tattvam, bāhyārthānāmapi vijñānavat pratītisiddhatvādityarthaḥ|| 42 ||
nanu lāghavatarkeṇa svapnādidṛṣṭāntairdṛśyatvahetukamithyātvānumānena bāhyavastvanubhavo bādhanīyo,'trabhavatāṃ śrutismṛtī api staḥ -- 'ciddhādaṃ sarvaṃ, 'tasmādvijñānamevāsti na prapañco na saṃsṛtiḥ' ityādītyato dūṣaṇāntaramāha -- 
tadabhāve tadabhāvācchūnyaṃ tarhi || 43 ||
tarhi bāhyābhāve śūnyameva prasajjeya, na tu vijñānamapi | kutaḥ? tadabhāve tadabhāvāt bāhyābhāve vijñānasyāpyabhāvaprasaṅgādvijñānapratīterapi bāhyapratītivadavastuviṣayatvānumānasambhavāt| vijñānapramāṇyasya kvāpyasiddhatvācca| tathā vijñāne pramāṇānāmapi bāhyatayāpalāpaccetyarthaḥ| nanvanubhave kasyāpi vivādābhāvena nāsti tatra pramāṇāpekṣeti cenna| śūnyavādināmeva tatra vivādāt| athāsatāpi pramāṇena vastu siddhyati| viṣayābādhasyaiva pramāṇyaprayojakatvād, na tu pramāṇapāramārthikatvasyeti cenna| evaṃ satyasatpramāṇasya sarvatra sulabhatvena kvāpyarthe pramāṇānveṣaṇasyāyogāt | athāsanmadhye'pi vyāvahārikasattvarūpo viśeṣaḥ pramāṇādiṣveṣaṭavya iti cet| āyātaṃ mārgeṇa| kiṃ punaridaṃ vyāvahārikatvam| yadi pariṇāmitvaṃ tadāsmābhirapīdṛśameva prapañce'smābhiḥ pratiṣedhāt | yadi punaḥ pratīyamānatāmātraṃ tadāpi tādṛśaireva pramāṇairbāhyārthasyāpi siddhiprasaṅgāt| lāghavatakrānugṛhītena yathākathañcidanumānenaiva bādhastu vijñāne'pi samāna iti ||
etenādhunikānāṃ vedāntibruvāṇāmapi mataṃ vijñānavādatulyayogakṣematayā nirastam| vijñānamātrasatyatāpratipādakaśrutismṛtayastu kūṭasthatvarūpaṃ pāramārthikasattāmeva bāhyānāṃ pratiṣedhanti, na tu pariṇāmitvarūpāṃ vyāvahārikasattāmapi ||
yattu kālāntareṇāpi nānyasaṃjñāmupaiti vai|
pariṇāmādisambhūtāṃ tadvastu nṛpa tacca kim||
vastu rājeti yalloke yattu rājabhaṭādikam|
tathānyacca nṛpetthaṃ tu na sat saṅkalpanāmayam||
iti viṣṇupurāṇādibhyaḥ pariṇāmitvasyavāsattātvāvagamāditi| saṅkalpanāmayamīśvarādisaṅkalparacitam| etena -- 'vijñānamayamevaitadaśeṣamavagacchata' ityādinā viṣṇupurāṇe māyāmoharūpiṇā viṣṇunā asurebhyo'pi tattvamevopadiṣṭam, te tvanadhikārādidoṣairviparītārthagrahaṇena vijñāna vādino nāstikā
babhūvuratyavagantavyam| tadetat sarvaṃ brahmamīmāṃsābhāṣye māyāvādanirasanaprasaṅgato vistāritamasmābhiḥ|| 43 ||
nanvevaṃ bhavatu śūnyameva tattvaṃ , tadā sutarāmeva bandhakāraṇānveṣaṇāṃ na yuktam tucchatvāditi nāstikaśiromaṇiḥ pratyavatiṣṭhate -- 
śūnyaṃ tattvaṃ bhāvo vinaśyati vastudharmatvādvināśasya || 44 ||
śūnyameva tattvam| yataḥ sarvo'pi bhāvo vinaśyati | yaśca vināśī sa mithyā svapnavat| ataḥ sarvavastūnāmādyantayorabhāvamātratvānmadhye kṣaṇikasattvaṃ sāṃvṛttikaṃ, na pāramārthikaṃ bandhādi| tataḥ kiṃ kena badhyetetyāśayaḥ| bhāvānāṃ vināśitve heturvastudharmatvādvināśasyeti| vināśasya vastusvabhāvatvāt| svabhāvaṃ tu vihāya na padārthastiṣṭhatītyarthaḥ|| 44 ||
pariharati -- 
apavādamātramabuddhānām|| 45 ||
bhāvatvādvināśitvamiti mūḍhānāmapavādamātraṃ mithyāvāda eva, nāśakāraṇābhāvena niravayavadravyāṇāṃ nāśāsambhavāt, kāryāṇāmapi vināśāsiddheśca | ghaṭo jīrṇa iti pratyayavadeva ghaṭo'tīta ityādipratīyā ghaṭāderatītākhyāyā avasthāyā eva siddheḥ| avavyaktatāyāśca kāryatītatābhyupagame'smanmatapraveśa eva| kiñca vināśasya prapañcatattvatābhyupagame'pi vināśa eva bandhasya puruṣārthaḥ sambhavatyeveti ||
kaścittu vyācaṣṭe -- śūnyaṃ tattvamityajñānāṃ kutsitavādamātraṃ , na punaratra yuktirasti , pramāṇasattvāsattvavikalpāsahatvāt| śūnye pramāṇāṅgīkāre tenaiva śūnyatākṣatiḥ, anaṅgīkāre pramāṇābhāvānna śūnyasiddhiḥ| svataḥ siddhau ca cidrūpatādyāpattirityartha iti|| na ca -- 
na nirodho na cotpattirna baddho na ca sādhakaḥ|
na mumukṣurna va mukta ityeṣā paramārthatā|| 
sarvaśūnyaṃ nirālambaṃ svarūpaṃ yatra cintyate|
abhāvayogaḥ sa prokto yenātmānaṃ prapaśyati ||
iti śrutismṛtibhyāmapi śūnyaṃ tattvatayā pratipādyata iti vācyam| puruṣāṇāṃ nirodhādyabhāvasyaiva tādṛśīṣu śrutiṣu tattvatayoktatvāt| pūrvottaravākyābhyāṃ puruṣasyaiva prakaraṇāt| vilīnaviśvacidākāśasyaivaitādṛśasmṛtiṣu tattvatayā pratipādanācca||
trailokyaṃ gaganākāraṃ nabhastulyaṃ vapuḥsvakam|
viyadgāmi mano dhyāyan yogī brahmaiva gīyate||
ityādivākyāntarairekavākyatvādākāśaśūnyayoḥ paryāyatvāditi| manomahattvādyakhilāntaḥkaraṇaṃ viyadgāmicidākāśe līnam|| 45 ||
dūṣamāntaramāha -- 
ubhayapakṣasamānakṣematvādayamapi || 46 ||
kṣaṇikabāhyāvijñānobhayapakṣayoḥ samānakṣematvāt tulyanirasanahetukatvādayamapi pakṣo vinaśyatītya [anuṣaṃgo vākyasamāptiḥ sarveṣu tulyayogitvāt' iti jaimininā proktaṃ atra tū pūrvasya prakaraṇsya vicchedābhāvāt 'vyavāyānnānuṣajjyate' iti nyāyo na pravartate| pūrvasūtroktasya 'vinaśyati ' padasyātrānvayasaṃbhavāt, laukikakalpitādhyāhārāpekṣayā sūtroktapadasyaivādhyāhāro yukta ityabhiprāyeṇedamuktam|]nuṣaṅgaḥ| kṣaṇikapakṣanirāsaheturhi yatprabhijñānupapattyādiḥ śūnyavāde'pi samānaḥ| tathā vijñānapakṣanirāsaheturbāhyapratītyādirapyatra samāna ityarthaḥ||46 ||
yadapi dukhanivṛttirūpatayā tatsādhanatayā vā śūnyataivāstu puruṣārtha iti tairmanyate, tadapi durghaṭamityāha -- 
apuruṣārthatvamubhayathā|| 47 ||
abhayathā svataḥ parataśca śūnyatāyāḥ puruṣārthatvaṃ na sambhavati, svaniṣṭhatvenaiva sukhādīnāṃ puruṣārthatvāt| sthirasya ca puruṣasyānabhyupagamādityarthaḥ|| 47 ||
tadevaṃ bandhakāraṇaviṣaye nāstikamatāni dūṣitāni | idānīṃ pūrvanirastāvaśiṣṭānyāstikasambhāvyānyapyanyāni bandhakāraṇāni nirasyante -- 
na gativiśeṣāt|| 48 ||
prakaraṇād bandho labhyate | na gativiśeṣāt śarīrapraveśādirūpādapi puruṣasya na bandha ityarthaḥ|| 48 ||
atra hetumāha -- 
niṣkriyasya tadasambhavāt || 49 ||
niṣkriyasya vibhoḥ puruṣasya gatyasambhavādityarthaḥ|| 49 ||
nanu śrutismṛtyorihalokaparalokagamanāgamanaśravaṇāt puruṣasya paricchinnatvamevāstu, tathāca śrutirapi -- 'aṅguṣṭhamātraḥ puruṣo'ntarātme' tyādirityāśaṅkāmapākareti -- 
mūrtatvād ghaṭādivat samānadharmāpattāvapasiddhāntaḥ|| 50 ||
yadi ca ghaṭādivat pumān mūrttaḥ paricchinnaḥ svīkriyate, tadā sāvayavatvavināśitvādinā ghaṭādisamānadharmāpattāvapasiddhāntaḥ syādityarthaḥ|| 50 ||
gatiśrutimupapādayati -- 
gatiśrutirapyupādhiyogādākāśavat|| 51 ||
yā ca gatiśrūyatirapi puruṣe'sti sā vibhutvaśrutismṛtiyuktyanurodhenākāśasyevopādhiyogādeva mantavyetyarthaḥ| tatra ca pramāṇam -- 
ghaṭasaṃvṛtamākāśaṃ nīyamāne ghaṭe yathā|
ghaṭo nīyeta nākāśaṃ tadvajjīvo nabhopamaḥ||
'buddherguṇenātmaguṇena caiva ārāgramātro hyavaro'pi dṛṣṭaḥ' ityādiśrutiḥ| 'nityaḥ sarvagataḥ syāṇu' rityādikā ca smṛtiḥ| madhyamaparimāṇatve sāvayavatvāpattyā vināśitvam, [sāṃkhyamate paramāṇorapi kāryatvameva -- 'nāṇunityatā tatkāryatvaśruteḥ' ityādisūtreṇa paṃcame vakṣyamāṇatvāt|] amutve ca dehavyāpajñānādyanupapāttirityādiśca yuktiriti | ata eva -- 
prakṛtiḥ kurute karma śubhāśubhaphalātmakam|
prakṛtiśca tadaśnāti triṣu lokeṣu kāmagā||
ityādismṛtibhiḥ prakṛtereva viśiṣya kriyārūpā gatiḥ smaryata iti || 51 ||
na karmaṇāpyataddharmatvāt|| 52 ||
karmaṇā'dṛṣṭenāpi sākṣānna puruṣasya bandhaḥ| kutaḥ? puruṣadharmatvābhāvādityarthaḥ| pūrvaṃ vihitaniṣiddhavyāpārarūpeṇa karmaṇā bandho narākṛtaḥ| atra tu tajjanyādṛṣcenetyārthikavibhāgādapaunaruktyam|| 52 ||
nanvanyadharmeṇāpyanyasya bandhaḥ syāt, tatrāha -- 
atiprasaktiranyadharmatve || 53 ||
bandhatatkāraṇayorbhinnadharmatve'tiprasaktiḥ muktasyāpi bandhāpattirityarthaḥ|| 53 ||
kiṃ bahunā svābhāvādikarmāntairanyena vā kenāpi puruṣasya bandhotpattirna ghaṭate, śrutivirodhāditi sādhāraṇaṃ bādhakamāha -- 
nirguṇādiśrutivirodhaśceti|| 54 ||
puruṣabandhasyānaupādhikatve 'sākṣī cetā kelavo nirguṇaśye'tyādiśrutivirodhaścetyarthaḥ| itiśabdo bandhahetuparīkṣāsamāptau || 54 ||
tadevaṃ na svabhāvato baddhasyetyādinā praghaṭṭakenetarapratiṣedhataḥ prakṛtipuruṣasaṃyoga eva sākṣādbandhaheturavadhāritaḥ| tatreyamāśaṅkā| nanu prakṛtisaṃyogo'pi puruṣe svābhāvikatvādivikalpagrastaḥ kathaṃ na bhavati | saṃyogasya svābhāvikatvakālādinimittakatve hi muktasyāpi bandhāpatterityādidoṣā yathāyogyaṃ samānā eveti | tābhimāmāśaṅkāṃ pariharati -- 
tadyogo'pyavivekānna samānatvam|| 55 ||
pūrvoktatadyogo'pi puruṣasyāvivekādvakṣyamāṇādavivekādeva hi nimittāt saṃyogo bhavati | ato noktadoṣāṇāṃ samānatvamastītyarthaḥ| sa cāviveko mukteṣu nāstīti , na teṣāṃ punaḥ saṃyogo bhavatīti| nanvaviveko'tra prakṛtipuruṣābhedasākṣātkāraḥ, saṃyogāt prāgasattvāt , kintu vivekaprāgabhāvo vivekākhyajñānavāsanā vā | tadubhayamapi na puruṣadharmaḥ, kintu buddhidharma evetyanyadharmeṇānyatra saṃyogo'tiprasaṅgadoṣasāmyamastyeveti cenmaivam| viṣayatāsambandhenāvivekasya puruṣadharmatvāt, tathā ca prakṛtirbuddhirūpā satī yasmai svāmipuruṣāya tanuṃ saṃyujyata iti vyavasthayātiprasaṅgābhāvāt | taduktaṃ kārikayā -- 
puruṣasya darśanārthaṃ kaivalyārthaṃ tathā pradhānasya|
paṅgvandhavadubhayorapi saṃyogastatkṛtaḥ sargaḥ||
iti | svāmine puruṣāya pradhānena darśayituṃ tayoḥ kaivalyārthaṃ cetyarthaḥ| avivekasya vṛttirūpatvaṃ tu vāṅmātraṃ na tupa tattvaṃ 'cittisthite' rityāgāmisūtre vakṣyāmaḥ| avivekaśca saṃyogadvāraiva bandhakāraṇam, pralaye bandhā'darśanāt, avivekanāśe'pi jīvanmuktasya duḥkhabhogadarśanācca| ataḥ sākṣādevāviveko bandhakāraṇaṃ prāṅnoktaḥ| nanu bhogyabhoktṛbhāvaniyāmakatvena kluptasyānādisvasvāmibhāvasya karmādīnāṃ vā saṃyogahetutvamastu , kimityaviveko'pi saṃyogaheturiṣyata iti cenna|
puruṣaḥ prakṛtistho hi bhuṅkte prakṛtijāna guṇān|
kāraṇaṃ guṇasaṅgo'sya sadasadyonijanmasu ||
iti gītāyāṃ saṅgākhyābhimānasya saṃyogahetutvasmaraṇātsa vakṣyamāṇādivākyayukartiścānyathā jñānato mokṣasya śrutismṛtisiddhasyānipapatteśca | athaivamapi svopādhikarmādikamapi saṃyogakāraṇaṃ bhavati, tadvihāya kathamaviveka eva kevalaṃ tatra kāraṇamucyata iti | ucyate | avivekāpekṣayā karmādīnāmapi paramparayaiva puruṣasambandhaḥ| tathā'viveka eva puruṣeṇa sākṣātchettuṃ śakyate| karmādikaṃ tvavivekākhyahetucchedadvāraivetyāśayenāviveka eva mukhyataḥ saṃyogahetutayokta iti| ayaṃ cāviveko'gṛhītāsaṃsargakamubhayajñānamavidyāsthalābhiṣikta eva vivakṣitaḥ| 'bandho viparyayabhedāḥ pañce' tyāgāmisūtradvayāt, 'tasya heturavidye' ti yogasūtre'pyavidyāyā eva pañcaparvāyā buddhipuruṣasaṃyogahetutāvacanāccānyathākhyātyanabhyupagamamātra eva yogeto'tra viśeṣaucityāt| na punaraviveko'trābhāvamātraṃ vivekaprāgabhāvo vā, muktasyāpi bandhāpatteḥ, jīvanmuktasyāpi bhāvivekavyaktiprāgabhāvena dharmādharmotpattidvārā punarbandhaprasaṅgācca, tathā'ṣagāmisūtrasthadhvāntadṛṣṭāntānupapatteścābhāvasya dhvāntavadāvarakatvā'sambhavāt, tathā vṛddhihnāsāvapyavivekasya śrūyamāṇau nopapadyeyātāmiti | asmanmate ca vāsanārūpasyaivāvivekasya saṃyogākhyajanmahetutayā tamovadāvarakatvavṛddhihnāsādikamañjasaivopapadyate| 'tasya heturavidyeti'
pātañjalasūtre ca bhāṣyakārairavidyāśabdenāvidyābījaṃ vyākyātam, jñānasya saṃyogottarakālīnatvena saṃyogājanakatvāditi| api ca 'puruṣaḥ prakṛtistho hu bhuṅkta' ityādivākyeṣva'bhimānākhyasaṃyogasyaiva prakṛtisthatākhyasaṃyogahetutāvagasyate| ata eva cā'vidyā nābhāvo'pi tu vidyāvirodhi jñānāntaramiti yogabhāṣye vyāsadevaiḥ prayatnenāvadhṛtam| tasmādavivekāvidyayostulyayogakṣematayā vivekasyāpi jñānaviśeṣatvamiti siddham| ayaṃ cā'vivekastridhā saṃyogākhyajanmahetuḥ, sākṣāddharmādharmotpattidvārā gārādidṛṣṭadvārā ca bhavati | 'sati mūle tadvipāka' iti yogasūtrāt| 'kartāsmīti nibaddhyate' -- iti smṛteḥ, 'vītarāgajanmādarśanā'diti nyāyasūtrācca| taduktaṃ mokṣadharme'pi -- 
jñānendriyāṇīndriyārthā nopasarpantyatarṣulam|
hīnaśca karaṇairdehī na dehaṃ punararhati||
tasmāt tarṣātmakādrāgād bījājjāyanti jantavaḥ|| iti |
'rāgastvavivekakārya' iti| yogasūtrābhyāmapyetat pratyetavyam, samānatantranyāyāt | tacca sūtradvayaṃ -- 'kleśāmūlaḥ karmāśayaḥ sati mūle tadvipāko jātyāyurbhogā' iti | kleśāścāvidyādipañcakamiti | avivekasya bandhajanane dvārajātaṃ ca piṇḍīkṛtyeśvaragītāyāmuktam -- 
anātmanyātmavijñānaṃ tasmād duḥkhaṃ tathetarat|
rāgadveṣādayo doṣāḥ sarve bhrāntinibandhanāḥ||
kāryo hyasya bhaveddoṣaḥ puṇyāpuṇyamiti śrutiḥ|
taddoṣadeva sarveṣāṃ sarvavedasamudbhavaḥ|| iti |
etadeva nyāye sūtritam| 'duḥkhajanmapravṛttidoṣamithyājñānamuttarottarāpāye tadanantarāpāyādapavarga iti | tadevaṃ saṃyogākhyajanmadvārā bandhākhyaheyasya mūlakāraṇamaviveka iti heyahetuḥ pratipādita ḥ|| 55 ||
itaḥ paraṃ kramaprāptaṃ hānopāyavyūhamativistareṇaśāstrasamāpti pratipādayati | ntarāntarā coktavyūhānapi vistarayiṣyati|
niyatakāraṇāttaducchittirdhvāntavat || 56 ||
śuktirajatādisthale lokasiddhaṃ yanniyatakāraṇaṃ vivekasākṣātkārastasmāt tasyāvivekasyocchittirbhavati, dhvāntavat| yathā dhvāntamālokādeva niyatakāraṇānnaśyati nopāyāntareṇa, tathaivā'viveko'pi vivekādeva naśyati, na tu karmādibhyaḥ sākṣādityarthaḥ| tadetaduktaṃ yogasūtreṇa -- 'vivekakhyātiraviplavā hānopāya' iti | karmādīni tu jñānasyaiva sādhanāni | 'yogāṅaagānuṣṭhānādaśuddhikṣaye jñānidīptirāvivekakhyāte' riti yogasūtreṇa sattvaśuddhidvārā jñāna eva yogāṅgāntargatasarvakarmaṇāṃ sādhanatvāvadhāraṇāditi||
prācīnāstu vedāntino mokṣe'pi karmaṇo jñānāṅgutvamāhuḥ|
vidyāṃ cāvidyāṃ ca yastadvedobhayaṃ saha|
avidyayā mṛtyuṃ tīrvā vidyayā'mṛtamaśnute||
iti śruto, 'sahakārikatvena ce'ti vedāntasūtre cāṅgāṅgibhāvena jñānakarmaṇoḥ sahakāritvāvadhāraṇāt,
jñāninā'jñāninā vāpi yāvaddehasya dhāraṇam|
tāvadvarṇāśramaproktaṃ kartavyaṃ karma muktaye||
ityādasmṛteśca | 'upamardaṃ ce'ti vedāntasūtreṇa tu karmatyāgo yogārūḍhasya nyāyaprāpto'nūdyata eva jñānasya mukhyato mokṣahetutvaṃ vyavasthāpayitum| yadi hi vikṣepakatvāt karma jñānābhyāsasya virodhi bhavet tadā 'guṇalope na guṇina' iti nyāyena pradhānarakṣārthamaṅgabhūtaṃ karmaiva tyājyaṃ jaḍabharatādivadityāśayāditi | teṣāṃ mate'pi vivekadvāratāṃ vinā vivekanāśakatvaṃ karmaṇo naiva sidhyatīti na tadvirodhaḥ| atra sūtre dhvāntasyālokanāśyatvavacanāt tamo'pi dravyameva, na tvālokā'bhāvaḥ, asati bādhake nīlaṃ tama ityādipratyayānāṃ bhramatvānaucityāt| naca kluptenaivopapattāvatiriktakalpanāgauravameva bādhakamiti vācyam| evañcasati vijñānamātreṇaiva svapnavat
sarvavyavahāropapattāvatiriktakalpanāgoraveṇa bāhyārthapratīterapi bādhāpatteḥ | tasmādatra prāmāṇikatvād gauravaṃ na doṣayeti| nanu vivekajñānaṃ vināpyavivekākhyajñānavyaktīnāṃ svasvatṛtīyakṣaṇe'vaśyaṃ vināśajjñānasya tannāśakatvaṃ kimarthamiṣyata iti cet| avivekaśabdena tadvāsanāyā eva pūrvasūtre vyākhyātatvāt |anāgatāvasthasyāvivekasyāsmanmate nāśasambhavācceti || 56 ||
nanu prakṛtipuruṣāviveka eva cetthaṃ saṃyogadvārā bandhahetustayorviveka eva ca mokṣahetupastarhi dehādyabhimānasattve'pi mokṣaḥ syāt| tacca śrutismṛtinyāyaviruddhamiti tatrāha -- 
pradhānavivekādanyāvivekasya taddhāne hānam|| 57 ||
puruṣe pradhānavivekāt kāraṇādyo'navyāviveko buddhyādyaviveko jāyate kāryātayānādikāraṇāvivekamūlakatvāt, tasya pradhānāvivekahāne satyavaśyaṃ hānamityarthaḥ| yathā śarīrādātmani vivikte śarīrakāryeṣu rūpādiṣvaviveko na sambhavati, tathā kūṭasthatvādidharmaiḥ pradhānāt puruṣe vivikte tatkāryeṣu pariṇāmādidharmakeṣubuddhyādiṣvabhimāno notpattumutsahate tulyanyāyāt, kāraṇanāśācceti bhāvaḥ| tedata smaryate -- 
citrādhārapaṭatyāge tyaktaṃ tasya hi citrakam|
prakṛtervirame cetthaṃ dhyāyināṃ ke smarādayaḥ || iti |
viramo virāmastyāgaḥ| ādiśabdena dravyarūpā api vikārā grāhyā iti | yacca buddhipuruṣavivekādeva mokṣa ityapi kvaciducyate, tatra sthūlasūkṣmabuddhigrahaṇāt prakṛterapi grahaṇam | anyathā buddhiviveke'pi prakṛtyabhimānasambhavāditi| nanu buddhyādyabhimānātirikte prakṛtya'bhimānekiṃ pramāṇamahamajña ityādyakhilābhimānānāṃ buddhyādiviṣayatvenaivopapatteriti cenna|
mṛtvāmṛtvā punaḥ mṛṣṭau svarga sthāṃ mā ca nārakī||
ityādyabhimānānāṃ pradhānaviṣayatvaṃ vinānupapatteḥ, atītānāṃ buddhyādyakhilakāryāṇāṃ punaḥ sṛṣṭayabhāvāt | pradhānasya tvidameva pralayānantaraṃ janma yadbuddhyādikarūpaikapariṇāmatyāgenāparabuddhyādirūpatayā pariṇāmanamiti| na cātmani janmādijñānamabhimāna eva na bhavati, puruṣasyāpi liṅgaśarīrasaṃyogaviyogarūpayorjanmamaraṇayoḥ paramārthikatvāditi vācyam|
na jāyate mniyate vā kadācinnāyaṃ bhūtvā bhavitā vā na bhūyaḥ|
ityādivākyairjanmādipratiṣedhenotpattivināśābhimānarūpasyātmani janmādijñānasya siddheraprasaktasya pratiṣedhāyogāt | kiñca buddhyādiṣu puruṣāṇāmabhimāno'nādirvaktuṃ na śakyate , buddhyādīnāṃ kāryatvāt| ataḥ kāryeṣvabhimānavyavasthārthaṃ niyāmakākāṅkṣāyāṃ kāraṇābhimāna eva niyāmakatayā siddhyati, loke dṛṣṭatvāt; kalpanāyāśca dṛṣṭānusāritvāt | yathālokedṛṣṭaḥkṣetrābhimānāt kṣetra janyadhānyādiṣvabhimānaḥ suvarṇābhimānācca tajjanyakaṭakādiṣvabhimānaḥ, tayornivṛttyā ca tayornivṛttiriti pradhānābhimānatadvāsanayoścabījāṅkuravadanāditvānna tadabhimāne niyāmakānta rāpe7eti || 57 ||
evaṃ pratipādite caturvyūhe punariyamāśaṅkā| nanu puruṣe ced bandhamokṣau vivekā vivekau svakṛtau, tarhi nityaśuddhabuddhamuktasyeti svoktivirodhaḥ| tathā ca, -- 
na virodho na cotpattirna baddho na ca sādhakaḥ|
na mumukṣurna vai mukta ityeṣā paramārthatā||
ityādiśrativirodhaśceti | tāṃ pariharati -- 
vāṅmātraṃ na tu tattvaṃ cittastheteḥ|| 58 ||
bandhādīnāṃ sarveṣāṃ citta evāvasthānāt tat puruṣe vāṅmātraṃ sarvaṃ sphaṭikalauhityavat pratibimbamātratvanna tu tattvaṃ tasya bhāvaḥ| anāropitaṃ japālauhityavadityarthaḥ| ato noktāvirodha iti bhāvaḥ| 'sa samānaḥ
sannubhau lokāvanusañcarati dhyāyatīva lelaiyatīve' tyādiśrutayastvatra pramāṇam| puruṣaḥ samāno lokayorekarūpaḥ| ivaśabdābhyāṃ nānārūpatvasyaupādhikatvamuktam| yathā cektam -- 
bandha mokṣau duḥkhaṃ mohāpattiśca māyayā|
svapne yathātmanaḥ khyātiḥ saṃsṛtirna tu vāstavī || iti ||
māyayā māyākhyaprakṛtyaupādhikītyarthaḥ| nannevaṃ tucchasya bandhasya hānaṃ kathaṃ puruṣārthaḥ, kathaṃ vānyadharmābhyāmanyasya bandhamokṣasvīkāre karmādibhiriva nāvyavastheti cedatroktaprāyamapi punaḥ prapañcyate| yadyapi duḥkhayogarūpo bandho vṛttirūpau ca vivekāvivekau cittasyaiva, tathāpi puruṣe duḥkhapratibimba eva bhoga ityavastutvepi taddhānaṃ puruṣārthaḥ, duḥkhaṃ mā bhuñjīyeti prārthanāt | evaṃ yasmai puruṣāya prakṛtiravivekenātmānaṃ darśitavatī tadvāsanāvaśāt tameva saṃyogadvārā badhnāti nānyam| tathā yasmai vivekenātmānaṃ darśitavatī tameva svaviyogadvārā mocayati, vāsanocchedāditi vyavasthāpi ghaṭata iti | karmādibhirbandhābhyupagametvevaṃ vyavasthā na ghaṭate | karmādīnāṃ sākṣibhāsyatvābhāvena sākṣāt puruṣeṣvapratibimbanāditi || 58 ||
nanu bandhādikaṃ cet puruṣe vāṅmātraṃ tarhi śravaṇena yuktyā vā tasya bādho bhavatu, śrutismṛtyoḥ sākṣātkāraparyantaṃ vivekajñānamupadiśyate mokṣahetutayeti, tatrāha -- 
yuktito'pi bādhyate diṅmūḍhavadaparokṣādṛte|| 59 ||
yuktirmananam| apiśabdaḥ śravaṇasamuccayārthaḥ| vāṅmātramapi puruṣasya bandhādikaṃ śravaṇamananamātreṇa na bādhyate sākṣātkāraṃ vinā| yathā diṅmūḍhastha janasya vāṅmātramapi digvaiparītyaṃ śravaṇayuktibhyāṃ na bādhyate sākṣātkāraṃ vinetyarthaḥ| prakṛte cedameva bādhyatvaṃ yat puruṣe bandhādibuddhinivṛttirna tvabhāvasākṣātkāra, śravaṇādinā tadutpattisambhāvanāyā apyabhāvāditi ||
ayavetthaṃ vyākhyeyam | nanu niyatakāraṇāt taducchittirityanena vivekajñānamavivekocchedakamuktam, tajjñānaṃ kiṃ śravaṇādisādhāraṇamutāsti kaścidviśeṣa ityākāṅkṣāyāmāha -- yuktito'pītyādisūtram| aviveko yuktitaḥ śravaṇataśca na bādhyate nocchidyate | vivekāparokṣaṃ vinā virodhitvāditi || 59 ||
tadevaṃ vivekasākṣātkārānmokṣaṃ pratipādya itaḥ paraṃ vivekaḥ pratipādanīyaḥ| tatrādau prakṛtipuruṣādīnāṃ vivekataḥ siddhau pramāṇānyupanyasyante -- 
acākṣuṣāṇāmanumānena bodho dhūmādibhiriva vahneḥ|| 60 ||
acākṣuṣāṇāmapratyakṣāṇām| kecit tāvat padārthāḥ sthīlabhūtatatkāryadehādayaḥ pratyakṣasiddhā eva, pratyakṣeṇāsiddhānāṃ prakṛtipuruṣādīnāmanumānena pramāṇena bodhaḥ puruṣaniṣṭhaphalasiddhirbhavati | yathā dhūmādibhirjanitenānumānena vahneḥ siddhirityarthaḥ| anumānāsiddhamapyāgamāt siddhyatītyapi bodhyam| asya śāstrasyānumānaprādhānyāt tu kevalānumānasya mukhyatayaivopanyāso na tvāgamasyānapekṣeti | tathā ca kārikā -- 
sāmānyatastu dṛṣṭādatīndriyāṇāṃ pratītiranumānāt |
tasmādapi cāsiddhaṃ parokṣamāptāgamāt siddham||
iti || anena ca sūtreṇedaṃ mananaśāstramityavagamyate || 60 ||
uktapramāṇaiḥ sādhyasya vivekasya pratiyogyanuyogipadārthānāṃ saṅgrahasūtraṃ vakṣyamāṇānumānopayogikāryakāraṇabhāvamapi pradarśayati -- 
sattvarajastamasāṃ sāmyāvasthā prakṛtiḥ prakṛtermahān mahato
'haṅkāro'haṅkārātpañcatanmātrāṇyubhayamindriyaṃ tanmātre
bhyaḥ sthūlabhūtāni puruṣa iti pañcaviṃśatirgaṇaḥ||61||
sattvādīni dravyāṇi na vaiśeṣikā guṇāḥ saṃyogavibhāgavattvāt| laghutvacalatvagurutvādidharma
[satva laghuprakāśakamiṣṭamupaṣṭaṃbhakaṃ calaṃ ja rajaḥ|
guruvaraṇakameva tamaḥ pradīpavaccārthato vṛttiḥ||
iti īśvarakṛṣṇenāpyuktam|] katvācca| teṣvatra śāstre śrutyādau ca guṇaśabdaḥ puruṣopakaraṇatvāt puruṣapaśubandhakatriguṇātmakamahadādirajjunirmātṛtvācca prayujyate| teṣāṃ sattvādidravyāṇāṃ ya sāmyāvasthā'nyūnānatiriktāvasthā nyūnādhikabhāvenāsaṃhatāvastheti yāvat| akāryāvastheti niṣkarṣaḥ| akāryā'vasthopalakṣitaṃ guṇasāmānyaṃ prakṛtirityarthaḥ| yathāśrute vaiṣamyāvasthāyāṃ prakṛtināśaprasaṅgāt|
sattvaṃ rajastama iti eṣaiva prakṛtiḥ sadā|
eṣaiva saṃsṛtirjantorasyāḥ pāre paraṃ padam||
ityādismṛtibhirguṇamātrasyaiva prakṛtitvavacanācca| sattvādīnāmanugamāya sāmānyeti| puruṣavyāvarttanāya guṇeti | mahadādivyāvartanāya copalakṣitāntamiti | mahadādayo'pi kāryasattvādirūpāḥ puruṣopakaraṇatayā guṇāśca bhavantīti| tadatra prakṛteḥsvarūpamevoktam| asyā viśeṣastu paścādvakṣyate | prakṛteḥ kāryo mahān mahattattvam| mahadādīnāṃ svarūpaṃ viśeṣaśca vakṣyate | mahataśca kāryo'haṅkāraḥ | ahaṅkārasya kāryadvayaṃ, tanmātrāṇyubhayamindriyaṃ ca | tatrobhayamindriyaṃ bāhyābhyantarabhedenaikādaśavidham| tanmātrāṇāṃ kāryāṇi pañca sthūlabhūtāni | sthūlaśabdāt tanmātrāṇāṃ sūkṣmabhūtatvamabhyupagatam| puruṣastu kāryyakāraṇavilakṣaṇa iti| ityevaṃ pañcaviṃśatirgaṇaḥ padārthavyūhaḥ | etadatiriktaḥ padārtho nāstītyarthaḥ| thavā sattvādīnāṃ pratyekavyaktyānantyaṃ gaṇaśabdo vakti| ayaṃva pañcaviṃśatikogaṇo dravyarūpaeva| dharmadharmyabhedāttu guṇakarmasāmānyādīnāmatraivāntarbhāvaḥ| etadatiriktapadārthasattve hi tato'pi puruṣasya vivektavyatayā tadasadgrahanyūnatāpadyeta | etena sāṅkhyānāmaniyatapadārthābhyupagama iti mūḍhapralāpa upekṣaṇīyaḥ| dikkālau [ata eva "diśaḥ śrotramiti " śruterna virodhaḥ|] cākāśameva dikkālāvākāśādibhya ityāgāmisūtrāt| eta eva padārthāḥ paraspakapraveśāpraveśābhyāṃ kvacit tantre ekameva,[vaiśeṣikamate|] kvacittu ṣaṭ, kvacicca[nyāyanaye] ṣoḍaśa, kvacicca saṅkhyāntarairapyupadiśyante | viśeṣastu sādharmyavaidharmyamātra iti mantavyam| tathā coktaṃ bhāgavate -- 
ekasminnapi dṛśyante praviṣṭānītarāṇi ca |
pūrvasmin vā parasmin vā tattve tattvāni sarvaśaḥ||
iti nānāprasaṃṅkhyānaṃ tattvānām ṛṣibhiḥ kṛtam|
sarvaṃ nyāyyaṃ yuktimattvādviduṣāṃ kimaśobhanam|| iti ||
ete ca padārthāḥ śrutiṣvapi gaṇitāḥ| yathā garbhopaniṣadi -- 'aṣṭau prakṛtayaḥ ṣoḍaśavikārā' iti| praśnopaniṣadi ca -- 'pṛthivī ca pṛthivīmātrāce'tyādinā | evaṃ maitreyopaniṣadādiṣvapi| aṣṭau ca prakṛtayaḥ kārikayā vyākhyātāḥ -- 
mūlaprakṛtiravikṛtirmahadādyāḥ prakṛtivikṛtayaḥ sapta|
ṣoḍaśakastu vikāro na prakṛtirna vikṛtiḥ puruṣaḥ|| iti |.
ekamevādvitīyaṃ tattvamiti śrutismṛtipravādastu sarvatattvānāṃ puruṣe vilāpanena śaktiśaktimadabhedenetyavirodhaḥ| layastu sūkṣmībhāvenāvasthānaṃ na tu nāśa iti | taduktam -- 
āsījjñānamayo'pyartha ekamevāvikalpitam||
avikalpitamavibhaktam| etacca brahmamīmāṃsābhāṣye'dvaitaprasaṅgato vistareṇopapāditam| viśeṣastvayaṃ yat, seśvaravāde'nyatattvānāṃ tatraivāvibhāgād īśvaracaitanyamevaikaṃ tattvam| nirīśvaravāde tu triveṇivadanyonyāvibhaktatayaikasminkūṭasthe tojomaṇḍavavadādityamaṇḍale prakṛtyākhyasūkṣmāvasthayā mahadāderavibhāgātmaivaikaṃ tattvamiti | tathāca vakṣyati -- 'nādvaitaśrutivirodho jātiparatvāditi ' || 61 ||
eteṣu padārtheṣvacākṣuṣāṇāmanumānena bodhaṃ pratipādayati sūtrajātena -- 
sthūlāt pañcatanmātrasya || 62 ||
bodha ityanuvartate| sthūlaṃ tāvaccakṣuṣameva | tacca tanmātrakāryatayoktam| tataḥ sthūlabhūtāt kāryāt tatkāraṇatayā tanmātrānumānena sthūlavivekato bodha ityarthaḥ| ākāśasādhāraṇyāya sthūlatvamatra bāhyendriyagrāhyaguṇakatvaṃ,
śāntādiviśeṣavattvaṃ vā| tanmātrāṇi ca yajjātīyeṣu śāntādiviśeṣatrayaṃ na tiṣṭhati tajjātīyānāṃ śabdasparśarūparasagandhānāmādhārabhūtāni sūkṣmadravyāṇi sthūlānāmaviśeṣāḥ, -- 
tasmiṃstasmiṃstu tanmātrāstena tanmātratā smṛtā|
na śāntā nāpi ghorāste na mūḍhāścāviśeṣiṇaḥ||
iti viṣṇupurāṇādibhyaḥ| asyāmarthaḥ| teṣu teṣu bhūteṣu tanmātrāstiṣṭhantīti kṛtvā dharmadharmyabhedād dravyāṇāmapi tanmātratā smṛtā| te ca padārthāḥ śāntaghoramūḍhākhyaiḥ stūlagataśabdādiviśeṣaiḥ śūnyāḥ, ekarūpatvāt| tathā ca śāntādiviśeṣaśūnyaśabdādimattvameva bhūtānāṃ śabdāditanmātratvamityāśayaḥ| ato'viśeṣiṇo'viśeṣasaṃjñitā iti | śāntaṃ sukhā tmakaṃ , ghoraṃ dukhātmakaṃ , mūḍhaṃ mohātmakam| tanmātrāṇi ca devādimātrabhogyatvena kevalaṃ sukhātmakānyeva, sukhādhikyāditi||
atredamanumānam -- apakarṣakāṣṭhāpannāni sthūlabhūtāni svaviśeṣaguṇavaddravyopādākāni sthūlatvād, ghaṭapaṭādivaditi| atrānavasthāpattyā sūkṣmamādāyaiva sādhyaṃ paryavasyati | anukūlatarkaścātra kāraṇaguṇakrameṇa kāryaguṇotpatterbādhakavyatirekeṇāparihāryatvam, śrutismṛtayaṣceti| prakṛteḥ sabdāsparśādimāttve tu bādhakamasti -- 
śabdasparśavihīnaṃ tadrūpādibhirasaṃyutam|
triguṇaṃ tajjagadyoniranādiprabhavāpyayam||
iti viṣṇupurāṇādivākyajātam| buddhyahaṅkārayośca śabdasparśādimattve bhūtakāraṇatvaśrutismṛtaya eva bādhikāḥ santi| bāhyendriyagrāhyajātīyaviśeṣaguṇavattvasyaiva bhūtalakṣaṇatvena tayorapi bhūtatvāpattyā svasya svakāraṇatvānupapatteriti| nanvevaṃ kāraṇadravyeṣu rūpādyabhāve tanmātrarūpādeḥ kiṃ kāraṇamiti cet svakāraṇadravyāṇāṃ nyūnādhikabhāvenānyo'nyaṃ saṃyogaviśeṣa eva | haridrādīnāṃ saṃyogasya tadubhayārabdhadravye raktarūpādihetutvadarśanāt | dṛṣṭānusāreṇa svāśrayahetusaṃyogānāmeva rūpādihetutvasambhave tārkikāṇāṃ paramāṇuṣu rūpakalpanaṃ tu heyam| sajātīyakāraṇaguṇasyaiva kāryaguṇārambhakateti tu teṣāmapi na niyamaḥ| ,jātīyakāraṇaguṇasyaiva kāryaguṇārambhakateti tu teṣāmapi na niyamaḥ| tryasareṇumahattvādāvavayavabahutvādereva tairapi hetutvābhyupamāditi dik||
indriyānumānaṃ cākāśānumānaddarśanavacanādibhiḥ pratyakṣābirvṛttibhireveti tadatra noktam| tattvāntareṇa tattvāntarānumānānāmeva prakṛtatvāditi na nyūnatā |tanmātrāṇāṃ cotpattau yogabhāṣyoktaprakriyaiva grāhyā| yathāhaṅkārācchabdatanmātram| tataścā'hahkārasahakṛtācchabdatanmātrācchabdasparśaguṇakaṃ sparśatanmātram| evaṃ krameṇaikaikaguṇavṛddhyā tanmātrāṇyutpadyanta iti | yā tu -- 
ākāśastu vikurvāṇaḥ sparśamātraṃ sasarja ha|
balavānabhavadvāyustataḥ sparśo guṇo mataḥ||
ityādinā viṣṇupurāṇe sparśāditanmātrasṛṣṭirākāśādisthūlabhūtacatuṣṭayāduktā| sā bhūtarūpeṇa pariṇāmanarūpaiva mantavyā | ākāśādīni jalāntāni hi sthūlabhūtāni svasvottarabhūtarūpeṇa svānugatatanmātrāḥ svopaṣṭambhataḥ pariṇamayantīti || 62 ||
bāhyābhyantarābhyāṃ taiścāhaṅkārasya|| 63 ||
bāhyābhyantarābhyāmindriyābhyāṃ taiḥ pañcatanmātraiśca kāryaistatkāraṇatayā'haṅkārasyānumānena bodha ityarthaḥ| ahaṅkāraścābhimānavṛttikamantaḥkaraṇadravyaṃ , na tvabhimānamātram dravyasyaiva loke dravyopādānatvadarśanāt, suṣuptyādāvahaṅkāravṛttināśena bhūtanāśaprasaṅgādvāsanāśrayatvenaivāhaṅkārākhyadravyasiddheśceti|| atretthamanumānam -- tanmātrendriyāṇyabhimānavaddravyopādānakāni abhimānakāryadravyatvāt, yannaivaṃ tannaivam yathā puruṣādiriti| nanvabhimānakāryadravyamevāsiddhamiti ced, ahaṃ gora ityādivṛttyupādanatayā cakṣurādivat tatsiddheḥ| anena cānumānena mana ādyātirekamātrasya tatkāraṇatayā prasādhyatvāt | atra cāyamanukūlastarkaḥ| 'bahusyāṃ prajāyeye'tyādiśrutismṛtibhyastāvadbhūtādisṛṣṭerabhimānapūrvakatvād buddhivṛttipūrvakasṛṣṭau kāraṇatayābhimānaḥ siddhaḥ| tatra caikārthasamavāyapratyāsattyaivābhimānasya sṛṣṭihetutvaṃ lāghavāt kalpyata iti | nanvevaṃ kulālāhaṅkārasyāpi ghaṭopādānatvāpattyā kulālamuktau tadantaḥkaraṇanāśe tannirmitaghaṭanāśaḥ syāt, na caitadyaktam puruṣāntareṇa sa evāyaṃ ghaṭa iti
pratyabhijñāyamānatvāditi| maivam| muktapuruṣabhogahetupariṇāmasyaiva tadantaḥkaraṇamokṣottaramucchedāt, na tu pariṇāmasāmānyasyāntaḥkaraṇasvarūpasyaivocchedaḥ | 'kṛtārthaṃ' pratinaṣṭamapyanaṣṭaṃ tadanyasādhāraṇatvāditiyogasūtre muktapuruṣopakaraṇasyāpyanyapuruṣārthasādhakatvasiddheriti | athavā ghaṭādiṣvapi hiraṇyagarbhāhaṅkāra eva kāraṇamastu, na kulāladyahaṅkārastathāpi sāmānyavyāptau na vyabhicāraḥ| samaṣṭibuddhyādyupādānikaiva hi sṛṣṭiḥ purāṇādiṣu sāṅkhyayogayośca pratipādyate, na tu tadaṃśavyaṣṭhibuddhyādyupādānikā | yathā mahāpṛthivyā eva sthāvarajaṅgamādyupādānatvaṃ na tu pṛthivyaṃśaloṣṭhāderiti|| 63 ||
tenāntaḥ karaṇasya || 64 ||
tenāhaṅkāreṇa kāryeṇa tatkāraraṇatayā mukhyasyāntaḥkaraṇasya mahadākhyabuddheranumānena bodha ityarthaḥ| atrāpyayaṃ prayogaḥ -- ahaṅkāradravyaṃ niścayavṛttimaddravyopādānakam niścayakāryadravyatvāt, yannaivaṃ tannaivam yathā puruṣādiriti | atrāpyayaṃ tarkaḥ | sarvo'pi lokaḥ padārthamādau svarūpato niścitya paścādabhimanyate ayamahaṃ mayedaṃ karttavyamityādirūpeṇeti tāvat siddhameva | tatrāhaṅkāradravyakāraṇākāṅkṣāyāṃ vṛttyoḥ kāryakāraṇabhāvena tadāśrayayoreva kāryakāraṇabhāvo lāghavāt kalpyate | kāraṇasya vṛttilābhena kāryavṛttilābhasyautsargikatvāditi | śrutāvapi 'sa īkṣāñcakre tadaikṣate'tyādau sargādyutpannabuddhita eva taditarākhilasṛṣṭiravagamyate iti | yadyapyekamevāntaḥ karaṇaṃ vṛttibhedena trividhaṃ lāghavāt|
guṇakṣobhe jāyamāne mahān prādurbabhūva ha|
mano mahāṃśca vijñeya ekaṃ tadvṛttibhedataḥ||
iti laiṅgāt| ' pañcavṛttirmanovad vyapadiśyata' iti vedāntasūtreṇaprāṇadṛṣṭāntavidhayā manaso'pi vṛttimātrabhedena bahutvasiddheśca | abhyathā niścayādivṛttibhiriva bhramasaṃśayanidrākrodhānivṛttibhirapi svasamasaṅkhyānantāntaḥkaraṇāpatteḥ| buddhyādiṣvavyavasthayā mana ādiprayogasya pātañjalādisarvaśāstreṣvanupapatteśca| tathāpi vaṃśaparvasvivāvāntarabhedamāśrityāntaḥkaraṇatraye kramaḥkāryakāraṇabhāvaścoktaḥ, yogopayogiśrutiparibhāṣānusārāditi mantavyam, taduktaṃ vaśiṣṭhe -- 
ahamarthodayo yo'yaṃ cittātmā vedanātmakaḥ|
etaccittadrumasthāsya bījaṃ viddhi mahāmate!||
etasmāt prathamodbhinnādaṅkuro'bhimavākṛtiḥ|
niścayātmā nirākāro buddhirityabhidhīyate||
asya buddhyabhidhānasya yāṅkurasya prapīnatā |
saṅkalparūpiṇī tasyāścittacetomano'bhidhā || iti ||
ahamartho'ntaḥkaraṇasāmānyam| atra vākye bījāṅkuranyāyena ekasyaivāntaḥkaraṇavṛkṣasya vṛttimātrarūpeṇa cittādyākhyāvasthābhedāḥ kramikāstrividhāḥ pariṇāmā uktā iti | sāṅkhyaśāstre ca cintāvṛttikasya cittasya buddhāvevāntarbhāvaḥ| ahaṅkārasya cātra vākye buddhāvantarbhāvaḥ|| 64 ||
tataḥ prakṛteḥ|| 65 ||
tato mahattattvāt kāryāt kāraṇatayā prakṛteranumānena bodha ityarthaḥ| antaḥkaraṇasāmānyasyāpi kāryatvaṃ tāvadekadā pañcendriyajñānānutpattyā madhyamaparimāṇatayā dehādivadeva siddham, śrutismṛtiprāṇāṇyācca| tasya ca prakṛtikāryatve'yaṃ prayogaḥ -- sukhaduḥkhaṃmohadharmiṇī buddhiḥ sukhaduḥkhamohadharmakadravyajanyā kāryatve sati sukhaduḥkhamohātmakatvāt, kāntādivaditi | kāraṇaguṇānusāreṇaiva kāryaguṇaucityaṃ cātrānukūlastarkaḥ,śrutismṛtayo'pīti mantavyam| nanu viṣayeṣu sukhādimattve pramāṇaṃ nāsti, ahaṃsukhītyādyevānubhavāt, tatkathaṃ kāntādiviṣayo dṛṣṭānta iti cenna| sukhādyātmakabuddhikāryatayā srakasukhaṃ candanasukhamityādyanubhavena ca viṣayāṇāmapi sukhādidharmakatvasiddheḥ, śrutismṛtiprāṇāṇyacca | kiñca yasyānvayavyatirekau sukhādinā saha dṛśyete tasyaiva sukhādyupādānatvaṃ kalpyate| tasya nimittattvaṃ parikalpyānyasyopādānatvakalpane kāraṇadvayakalpanāgoravāt | api cānyo'nyasaṃvādena pratyabhijñayā ca viṣayeṣu
sarvapuruṣasādhāraṇasthirasukhasiddhiḥ| tatsukhagrahaṇāyāsmannaye vṛttiniyamādikalpanāgoravaṃ ca phalalamukhatvānna doṣāvaham| anyathā pratyabhijñayā'vayavyasiddhiprasaṅgāt tatkāraṇādikalpanāgauravāditi | viṣaye'pi sukhādikaṃ ca mārkaṇḍeye proktam -- 
tat santu cetasyathavāpi dehe
sukhāni duḥkhāni ca kiṃ mamātra || iti ||
ahaṃ sukhītyādipratyayastu , ahaṃ dhanītyādipratyayavat svasvāmibhāvākhyasambandhaviṣayakaḥ| teṣāṃ pratyayānāṃ samavāyasambandhaviṣayakatvabhramanirāsārthaṃ tu sukhiduḥkhimūḍhebhya puruṣo vivacyate śāstreṣviti| śabdādiṣu ca sukhādyātmatāvyavahāra ekārthasamavāyāt | astu vā śabdādiṣu sākṣādeva sukhamuktaprameṇebhyaḥ, viṣayagatasukhādeśca buddhimātragrāhyatvaṃ phalabalāt| yattu viṣayāsamprayogakāle śāntisukhaṃ sāttvikaṃ suṣuptyādau vyajyate, tadeva buddhidharma ātmasukhamucyate iti | yadyapi vaiśeṣikādyā api tārkikāḥ prapañce'nyathāpi kāryakāraṇavyavasthāmanumimate, tathāpi bahulaśrutismṛtyupodvalanenāsmābhiranumitaiva vyavasthā mumukṣubhirūpādeyā | mūlaśaithilyadoṣeṇa parānumānānāṃ durbalatvāt | ata eva 'tarkāpratiṣṭhānā' diti vedāntasūtreṇāpratiṣṭhādoṣataḥ kevalatarko'pāstaḥ| tathā manunāpi -- 
ārṣaṃ dharmopadeśaṃ ca vedaśāstrāvirodhinā|
yastarkeṇā'nusandhatte sa dharmaṃ veda netaraḥ||
iti vedāviruddhatarkasyaivārthaniścāyakatvamuktam| tasmāt 'śrotavyaḥ śrutivākyebhyo mantavyaścopapattibhiḥ ' ityādivākyebhyaḥ śraṇasamānārthakameva mananaṃ balavat | anyākāraṃ mananaṃ tu pareṣāṃ durbalam| evaṃ puruṣe'pi sukhaduḥkhādisattvena teṣāmanumānaṃ bahulaśrutyādivirodhād durbalamiti dik| prakṛtigataviśeṣaṃ ca paścādvakṣyāmaḥ
|| 65 ||
nanvakhilajaḍebhyaḥ puruṣaviveka eva muktau hetustat kimarthaṃ jaḍānāmanyo'nyaviveko'tra darśita iti cet, prakṛtyāditattvopāsanayā sattvaśuddhyarthaṃ vivekasyāpyapekṣitatvāditi| kāryakāraṇamudrayā prakṛtiparyantasyāmumānena vivekataḥ siddhimuktvā yathoktakāryakāraṇabhāvaśūnyasya puruṣasya prakārāntareṇā'numānatastathā siddhimāha -- 
saṃhataparārthatvāt puruṣasya || 66 ||
saṃhananamārambhakasaṃyogaḥ| sa cāvayavāvayavyabhedāt prakṛtikāryasādhāraṇaḥ| tathā ca saṃhatānāṃ prakṛtitatkāryāṇāṃ parārthatvānumānena puruṣasya bodha ityarthaḥ|tadyathā vivādāspadaṃ prakṛtimahadādikaṃ parārtham -- svetarasya bhogā'pavargaphalakam saṃhatatvāt, śayyāsanādivadityanumānena prakṛteḥ paro'saṃhataeva puruṣaḥ siddhyati| tasyāpi saṃhatatve'navasthāpatteḥ| pātañjale ca -- 'parārthaṃ saṃhatya kāritvā' diti sūtrakāreṇānumānaṃ kṛtam| tattu yathāśrutamevāntyā'vayavasādhāraṇam, itarasāhityenārthakriyākāritvasyaiva saṃhatyakāritāśabdārthatvāt| puruṣastu viṣayaprakāśarūpāyāṃ svārthakriyāyāṃ nānyadapekṣate, nityaprakāśarūpatvāt puruṣasyārthasambandhamātre buddhivṛttyapekṣaṇāt| sambandhastu nāsādhāraṇyarthakriyeti| atra ca 'na vā are sarakvasya kāmāya sarvaṃ priyaṃ bhavatyātmanastu kāmāya sarvaṃ priyaṃ bhavatī' tyādiśrutismṛtayo'nukūlatarkāḥ| anyacca sukhādimat pradhānādikaṃ yadi svasya sukhādibhogārthaṃ syāttadā tasya sākṣāt svajñeyatve karmakartṛvirodhaḥ| na hi dharmibhānaṃ vinā sukhasya bhānaṃ sambhavati, ahaṃ sukhītyevaṃ sukhānubhavāditi| api ca saṃhanyamānānāṃ bahūnāṃ gaduṇānāṃ tatkāryāṇāṃ cānekavikārāṇāmanekacaitanyaguṇakalpanāyāṃ gauraveṇa lāghavādeka eva citprakāśarūpaḥ puruṣaḥ sarvasaṃhatebhyaḥ paraḥ kalpayituṃ yujyata iti, sūtreṇa nimittakāraṇatayā puruṣānumānamuktam, puruṣārthasyā'khilavastusaṃhanananimittatvavacanāt| ata eva sargādyutpannapuruṣaṃ prakṛtya viṣṇupurāṇādau smaryate -- 
nimittamātramevāsau sṛjyānāṃ sargakarmaṇi|
pradhānakāraṇībhūtā yato vai sṛjyaśaktyaḥ||
guṇasāmyāttatastasmāt kṣetrajñādhiṣṭhitānmune!|
guṇavyañjana,mbhūtiḥ sargakāle dvijottama !||
ityādikṣetrajñā'dhiṣṭhānaṃ cāsamāptapuruṣārthasya saṃyogamātraṃ guṇavyañjanaṃ mahattatvaṃ kāraṇatayā triguṇātmapradhānavyañjakatvāditi | tadevamacākṣuṣāṇāmanumānena siddhiruktā|| 66 ||
idānīṃ sarvakāraṇatvopapattaye prakṛtinityatvamupapādyate puruṣakauṭasthyasiddhyartham -- 
mūle mūlābhāvādamūlaṃ mūlam || 67 ||
trayoviṃśatitattvānāṃ mūlamupādānaṃ pradhānaṃ mūlaśūnyam| anavasthāpattyā tatra mūlāntarāsambhavādityarthaḥ|| 67 ||
nanu -- 
tasmādavyaktamutpannaṃ triguṇaṃ dvijasattama !|
ityādinā pradhānasyāpi puruṣādutpattiśravaṇāt puruṣa eva prakṛtermūlaṃ bhavatu, puruṣasya nityatayā ca nānavasthā, avidyādvārakatayā ca na puruṣa kauṭastyahāni , tathāca smaryate -- 
tasmādajñānamūlo'yaṃ saṃsāraḥ puruṣabhya hi | iti ||
ityāśaṅkyāha -- 
pāramparye'pyekatra pariniṣṭheti saṃjñāmātram|| 68 ||
avidyādidvāreṇa paramparayā puruṣasya jaganmūlakāraṇatve'pyekasminnavidyādau yatra kutracinnitye dvāre paramparāyāḥ paryavasānaṃ bhaviṣyati, puruṣasyāpariṇāmitvāt| ato yatra paryavasānaṃ saiva nityā prakṛtiḥ | prakṛtirihamūlakāraṇasya saṃjñāmātramityarthaḥ|| 68 ||
nanvevaṃ pañcaviṃśatitattvānīti nopapadyatesa mahattattvakāraṇāvyaktāpekṣayāpi jaḍatattvāntarāpatterityāśayena mūlasamādhānamāha -- 
samānaḥprakṛterdvayoḥ|| 69 ||
vastutastuprakṛtermūlakāraṇavicāre dvayorvādiprativādinorāvayoḥ samānaḥ pakṣaḥ| etaduktaṃ bhavati | yathā prakṛterutpattiḥ śrūyate, evamavidyāyā api |
avidyā pañcaparvaiṣā prādurbhūtā mahātmanaḥ|
ityādivākyaiḥ| ata ekasyā śravaśyaṃ gauṇyutpattirvaktavyā| tatra ca prakṛtereva puruṣasaṃyogādibhirabhivyaktirūpā goṇyutpattiryuktā, -- 
saṃyogalakṣaṇotpattiḥ kathyate karmajñānayoḥ|
iti kaurmavākye prakṛtipuruṣayorgauṇotpattismaraṇāt| avidyāyāśca kvāpi goṇotpattyaśravaṇāt tasyā anāditā| vākyāni tu mithyājñānarūpā buddhidharma iti yoge sūtritamato na tattvādhikyam| athavā dvayoḥ prakṛtipuruṣayoḥ samāna eva nyāya ityarthaḥ|
yataḥ pradhānapuruṣau yataścaitaccarācaram|
kāraṇaṃ sakalasyā'sya sa no viṣṇuḥ prasīdatu||
ityādivākyaiḥ puruṣasyāpyutpattiśravaṇāditi bhāvaḥ| tathāca puruṣasyeva prakṛterapi gauṇyevotpattiḥ| nityatvaśravaṇādityapi samānamiti| tasmāt prakṛtirevopādānaṃ jagataḥ| prakṛtidharmaścā'vidyā jagannimittakāraṇam| tathā puruṣo'pīti siddham| yattu, -- 
avidyāmāhuravyaktaṃ sargapralayadharmiṇam|
sargapralayanirmuktaṃ vidyāṃ vai pañcaviṃśakam||
iti mokṣadharme prakṛtipuruṣayoravidyāvidyeti vacanam| tattu tadubhayaviṣayatayopacaritameva | pariṇāmitvena hi puruṣāpekṣayā prakṛtirasatīti tasyā avidyāviṣayatvamuktam| evameva tasmin prakaraṇe svasvakāraṇā'pekṣayā bhūtānāṃ kāryajātamavidyetyuktam. svasvapekṣayā ca svasvakāraṇaṃ vidyeti | puruṣasya pariṇāmarūpaṃ jagadupādānatvaṃ tu prakṛtyupādhikameva kartṛtvādivat śrutismṛtyorūpāsārthamevānūdyate| anyathā 'asthūlamanaṇvahnasvami'tyādiśrutivirodhāpatteriti mantavyam| māyāśabdena ca prakṛtirevocyate| 'māyāṃ tu prakṛtiṃ vidyāditi ' śrutau 'asmānmāyīsṛjate viśvametate, tasmiṃścānyo māyayā sanniruddhaḥ' iti pūrvaprakāntamāyāyāḥ prakṛtisvarūpatāvacanāt,
sattvaṃ rajastama iti prākṛtaṃ tu guṇatrayam|
etanmayī ca prakṛtirmāyā yā vaiṣṇavī śrutā||
lohitaśvetakṛṣṇeti tasyāstādṛgbahuprajāḥ||
ityādismṛtibhyaśca| na tu jñānanāśyā avidyā māyāśabdārthau , nityatvānupapatteḥ| kiñcāvidyāyā dravyatve śabdamātrabhedo, guṇatve ca tadā'dhāratayā prakṛtisiddhiḥ, puruṣasya nirguṇatvādibhyaḥ| atha dravyaguṇakarmavilakṣaṇaivā'smābhiravidyā vaktavyeti cenna| tādṛkapadārthāpratīteruktatvāditi |. 69 ||
nannevañcet prakṛtipuruṣādyanumānaprakāro'sti tarhi sarveṣāmeva kathaṃ vivekamananaṃ na jāyate, tatrāha -- 
adhikāritraividhyānna niyamaḥ|| 70 ||
śravaṇādāviva manane'pyadhikāriṇastrividhā mandamadhyamottamā ityo na sarveṣāmeva manananiyamaḥ| kutarkādibhirmandamadhyamayorbādhasatpratipakṣatāsambhavādityarthaḥ| mandairhi bauddhādyuktakutarkajātenoktā'nubhānāni vādhyante| madhyamaiśca buddhādyukteraiva viruddhāsalliṅgaiḥ satpratipakṣitāni kriyante| ata uttamādhikāriṇāmevaitādṛśamananaṃ bhavatīti bhāvaḥ| prakṛteḥ svarūpaṃ guṇasāmyaṃ prāgevoktam| sūkṣmabhūtādikaṃ ca prasiddhamevāstīti|| 70 ||
avaśiṣṭayormahadahaṅkārayoḥ svarūpamāha sūtrābhyām -- 
mahadākhyamādyaṃ kāryaṃ tanmanaḥ|| 71 ||
mahadākhyamādyaṃ kāryaṃ tanmano mananavṛttikam| mananamatra niścayaḥ| tadvṛttikā buddhirityarthaḥ|
yadetadvistṛtaṃ bījaṃ pradhānapuruṣātmakam|
mahattattvamiti proktaṃ buddhitattvaṃ taducyate||
ityādivākyebhyo buddherevāgyakāryatvāvagamāt|| 71 ||
caramo'haṅkāraḥ|| 72 ||
tasyānantaro yaḥ so'haṅkārotītyahaṅkāro'bhimānavṛttika ityarthaḥ|| 72 ||
yato'bhimānavṛttiko'haṅkāro'tastatkāryatvamuttareṣāmupapannamityāha -- 
tatkāryatvamuttareṣām|| 73 ||
sugamam| evaṃ trisūtrīṃ vyākhyāya ponaruktyāśaṅkāpāstā || 73 ||
nanvevaṃ prakṛtiḥ sargakāraṇamiti śrutismṛtivirodha ityaśaṅkāyāmāha -- 
adyahetutā taddvārā pāramparye'pyaṇuvat|| 74 ||
pāramparye'pi sākṣādahetutve'pyādyāyāḥ prakṛterhetutāhaṅkārādiṣu mahadādidvārāsti | yathā vaiśeṣikamate'ṇūnāṃ ghaṭādihetutā dvyaṇukādidvāraivetyarthaḥ|| 74 ||
nanu prakṛtipuruṣayorubhayoreva nityatvāt prakṛtereva kāraṇatve kiṃ niyāmakaṃ ? tatrāha -- 
pūrvabhāvitve dvayorekatarasya hāne'nyatarayogaḥ|| 75 ||
