dvayoreva pumprakṛtyorakhilakāryapūrvabhāvitve'pyekatarasya puruṣasyāpariṇāmitvena kāraṇatāhānyā'nyatarasyāḥ kāraṇatvaucityamityarthaḥ| puruṣasyāpariṇāmitve cedaṃ bījam | puruṣasya saṃhatya kāritve parārthatvāpattyā'navasthā | asaṃhatya kāratve sarvadā mahadādikāryaprasaṅgaḥ| prakṛtidvārā pariṇāmakalpane ca lāghavāt tasyā eva pariṇāmo'stu | puruṣe tu svāmitvena sraṣṭṭatvopacāraḥ| yathā yodheṣu vartamānau jayaparājayau rādanyupacaryete, tatphalasukhaduḥkhabhoktṛtvena tatsvāmitvāditi| kiñca dharmigrāhakamānena kāraṇatayaiva prakṛteḥ siddhau nānyakāraṇākāṅkṣāsti| yathā dharmigrāhakapramāṇena draṣṭṭatayā puruṣasiddhau nānyadraṣṭrākāṅkṣeti | api ca puruṣasya pariṇāmitve kadāciccakṣurmanādivad bandhyatvamapi syāt| tathā ca vidyamānamapi sukhaduḥkhādikaṃ na jñāyeta| tataścāhaṃ sukhī na vetyādisaṃśayāpattiḥ| ataḥ sadā prakāśasvarūpatvānapāyena puruṣasyāpariṇāmitvaṃ siddhyati | taduktaṃ yogasūtreṇa -- 'sadā jñātāścittavṛttayayastatprabhoḥ puruṣasyāpariṇāmitvā'diti | tadbhaiṣyeṇa ca 'sadā jñānaviṣayatvaṃ tu puruṣasyā'pariṇāmitvaṃ paridīpayatī'ti| sadā prakāśasvarūpatve'pi yathā naikadā viśvaprakāśatvaṃ tathā vakṣyāmaḥ|| 75 ||
prakṛteryugapatkāraṇatvopapattaye vibhutvamapi pratipādayati -- 
paricchinnaṃ na sarvopādānam|| 76 ||
sarvopādānaṃ pradhānaṃ na paricchinnaṃ vyāpakamityarthaḥ| sarvopādānatvamatra hetugarbhaviśeṣaṇam, pariccinne tadasambhavāditi|
nanu prakṛteraparicchinnatvaṃ nopapadyate, prakṛtirhi sattvādiguṇatrayādatiriktā na bhavati 'sattvādīnāmataddharmatvaṃ tadrūpatvādi'tyāgāmisūtrāt, yogasūtrabhāṣyābhyāṃ spaṣṭamavadhṛtatvācca| teṣāṃ sattvādīnāṃ laghutvacalatvagurutvādayo dharmā vakṣyamāṇā vibhutve sati viruddhyante, sṛṣṭyādihetavaḥ saṃyogavibhāgādayaśca nopapadyanta iti||
atrocyate paricchinnatvamatra daiśikābhāvapratiyogitāvacchedakā'vacchinnatvam| tadabhāvaśca vyāpakatvam| tathāca jagatkāraṇatvasya daiśikābhāvapratiyogitānavacchedakatvameveti prakṛtervyāpakatvamiti paryavasitam| yathā prāṇasya sthāvarajaṅgamādyakhilaśarīravyāpakatvaṃ prāṇatvasāmānyenocyate, prāṇavyaktīnāṃ sarvadehasambandhāt, tadvat prakṛtervyāpakatvamiti| prakṛterakriyaikatvādikaṃ ca sādharmyavaidharmyasūtre pratipādayiṣyāmaḥ || 76 ||
na kevalaṃ sarvopādānatvāt, api tu -- 
tadutpattiśruteśaśca || 77 ||
teṣāṃ paricchinnānāmutpattiśravaṇācca| 'atha yadalpaṃ tanmarttya' mityādiśrutiṣu maraṇadharmakatvena paricchinnasyotpattyavagamāt, śrutyantarebhyaścetyarthaḥ|| 77 ||
idānīṃ prakṛtikāraṇatopapattaye'bhāvādikāraṇatāṃ nirasyati -- 
nāvastuno vastusiddhiḥ|| 78 ||
avastuno'bhāvanna vastusiddhirbhāvotpattiḥ| śaśaśṛṅgājjagadutpattyā mokṣādyanupapattestadadarśanāccetyarthaḥ|| 78 ||
nanu jagadapyavastvevāstu svapnādivaditi, tatrāha -- 
abādhādaduṣṭakāraṇajanyatvācca nāvastutvam|| 79 ||
svapnapadārthasyeva prapañcasya bādhaḥ śrutyādipramāṇairnāsti| tathā śaṅkhapītimāderiva duṣṭendriyādijanyatvamapi nāsti | doṣakalpane pramāṇābhāvādityato na kāryasyāvastutvamityarthaḥ| nanu 'vācārambhaṇaṃ vikāro nāmadheyaṃ mṛttiketyeva satya' mityādaśrutibhireva prapañcasya bādho, bādhāccāvidyākhyadoṣo'pi svakāraṇe'stīti cenna| mṛddṛṣṭāntasiddhyanyathānupapattyā svakāraṇāpekṣakāsthairyarūpāsattvaparatvāt tādṛgvākyānām, anyathā sṛṣṭyādivakyavirodhācca| kiñca śrutyā prapañcabādhe ātmāśrayaḥ| svasyāpi prapañcāntargatatayā bādhena tadbodhitārthe punaḥ saṃśayāpattiśceti| ata eva bādhābādhādivaidharmyādupalambhācca jāgratprarañcasya svapnakhapuṣpāditulyatvamatinirbandhena pratyācaṣṭe vedāntasūtradvayam -- 'vaidharmyācca na svapnādivaditi' 'upalabdheśceti' ca | 'neti netī'tyevaṃvidhavākyāni ca vivekaparāṇyeva, na tu svarūpataḥ prapañcaniṣedhaparāṇi -- 'prakṛtaitāvattvaṃ hi pratiṣedhatīti' vedāntasūtrāt| evamanyānyapi vākyāni brahmamīmāṃsābhāṣye'smābhirvyākhyātāni|| 79 ||
nāvastuno vastusiddhiriti yaduktaṃ, tatra hetumāha -- 
bhāve tadyogena tatsiddhirabhāve tadabhāvāt
kutastarāṃ tatsiddhiḥ|| 80 ||
bhāve kāraṇasya sadrūpatve tadyogena sattāyogena kāryasiddhirghaṭeta| kāraṇasyābhāve'sadrūpatve tu tadabhāvāt kāryasyāpyasattvāt kathaṃ vastubhūtakāryasiddhiḥ| kāraṇasvarabapasyaiva kāryasyaucityādityarthaḥ|| 80 ||
nanu tathāpi karmaivāvaśyakatvājjagatkāraṇamastu, kiṃ pradhānakalpanayeti tatrāpyāha -- 
nakarmaṇa upādānatvāyogāt|| 81 ||
karmaṇo'pi vastusiddhirnimittakāraṇasya karmaṇo na mūlakāraṇatvam,guṇānāṃ dravyopādīnatvāyogāt | kalpanā hi dṛṣṭā'nusāreṇaiva bhavati| vaiśeṣikoktaguṇānāṃ copādānatvaṃ na kvāpi dṛṣṭipnityarthaḥ| atra karmaśabdo'vidyādīnāmapyupalakṣakaḥ, guṇatvā'viśeṣeṇa teṣāmapyupādānatvāyogāt| cakṣuṣaḥ paṭalādivadavidyāyāśyetanagatadravyatve tu pradhānasya saṃjñāmātrabheda iti || 81 ||
tadevaṃ pariṇāmitvāpariṇāmitvaparārthatvāparārthatvābhyāṃ pusprakṛtyorviveko darśitaḥ| idānīṃ vivekajñānasyaivāvivekanāśadvārā paramapuruṣārthahetutvaṃ, na tu tatra vaidikakarmaṇāṃ sākṣāddhetutāstīti yat prāguktam 'aviśeṣaścobhayo'riti sūtreṇa tadeva prapañcayati pañcabhiḥ sūtreḥ -- 
nānuśravikādapi tatsiddhiḥ sādhyatvenāvṛttiyogādapuruṣārthatvam|| 82 ||
apiśabdena na dṛṣṭāt tatsiddhiriti prāguktadṛṣṭasamuccayaḥ| guroranuśrūyata ityanuśravo vedaḥ| tadvihito yāgādirānuśravika' karma| tasmādapi na pūrvoktapuruṣārthasiddhiḥ| yataḥ sarmasādhyatvena punarāvṛttisambandhādatyantapuruṣārthatvābhāva ityarthaḥ| karmasādhyasya cānityatve śrutiḥ -- 'tadyatheha karmacito lokaḥ kṣīyata evamevāmutra puṇyacito lākaḥ kṣīyata' iti | ' na karmaṇāmanyadharmatvā' diti sūtacreṇa pūrvaṃ karmaṇā bandho nirākṛtaḥ| idānīṃ ca mokṣo nirākriyata ityapaunaruktyam| anyadharmatvena pūrvoktahetunā bandha iva mokṣe'pi karmaṇo hetutvaṃ nirākṛtaprāyamiti punarāśaṅkaiva nodetīti cenna | bandhahetutvenāviveke siddhe tatpuruṣīyāvivekajatvena karmaṇā tadīyatvavyavasthopapatteriti
|| 82 ||
nanvevaṃ pañcāgnividyārūpeṇopāsanākhyakarmaṇā tīrthamaraṇādikarmaṇā ca brahmalokaṃ gatasyānāvṛttiśrutiḥ kathamupapadyate, tatrāha -- 
tatra prāptavivekasyānāvṛttiśrutiḥ|| 83 ||
tatrānuśravikakarmaṇi brahmalokagatānāṃ yāṃ'nāvṛttiśrutiḥ sā tatraiva prāptavivekasya mantavyā |
anyathā hi brahmalokādapyāvṛttiṃ pratipādayatāṃ vākyāntarāṇāṃ virodha ityarthaḥ| tathāpi sāpyanāvṛttirvivekajñānasyaiva phalaṃ, na tu sākṣādeva karmaṇa iti | etacca ṣaṣṭādhyāye ppañcayiṣyati| brahmamīmāṃsābhāṣye ca tayorvākyānyudāhṛtyāsmābhirvyākhyātāni || 83 ||
karmaṇastu phalaṃ tadāha -- 
duḥkhād duḥkhaṃ jalābhiṣevanna jāḍyavimokaḥ|| 84 ||
ānuśravikāttu hiṃsādidoṣeṇa duḥkhātmakabhogena ca duḥkhād duḥkhaṃ duḥkhadhāraiva bhavati, na tu jāḍyavimoko'vivekanivṛttiḥ| duḥkhavimokastvatidara eva tiṣṭhati | yathā jāḍyārttasya jalābhiṣekād duḥkhānivṛttireva bhavati, na tu jāḍyavimokṣa ityarthaḥ| taduktam -- 
yathā paṅkena paṅkāmbhaḥ surayā cā surākṛtam|
bhūtahatyāṃ tathaivaikāṃ na yajñairmārṣṭumarhati || iti ||
śrūyate ca brahmalokasthānāṃ viṣṇupārṣadānāmapi jayavijayādīnāṃ punaḥ rākṣasayonau duḥkhadhāreti | kārikayā cedamuktaṃ -- 
dṛṣṭavadānuśravikaḥ sa hyaviśuddhikṣayātiśayayuktaḥ| iti|| 84 ||
nanu niṣkāmādantaryāgajapādirūpakarmaṇo na duḥyakhaṃ, pratyuta mokṣaḥ phalaṃ śrūyata iti, tatrāha -- 
kāmye'kāmye'pi sādhyatvāviśeṣāt ||85||
kāmye'kāmye ca karmaṇi duḥkhād duḥkhaṃ bhavati | kutaḥ? sādhyatvāviśeṣāt | karmasādhyasya sattvaśuddhidvārakajñānasyāpi triguṇātmakatayā duḥkhātmakatvādityarthaḥ| ' na karmaṇā na prajayā dhanena tyāgenaike amṛtatvamānaśu'rityādiśrutibhyaśca karmaṇo na sākṣānmokṣaḥ phalamiti bhāvaḥ| tyāgenābhimānatyāgena eke kecidevāmṛtatvamānaśuḥ prāptavanto, na sarve| abhaimānatyāgasya tattvajñānajanyatayā durlabhatvādityarthaḥ|| 85 ||
nanu bhavanmate'pi kathaṃ jñānasādhyasya na duḥkhatvaṃ sādhyatvāviśeṣāditi tatrāha -- 
nijamuktasya bandhadhvaṃsamātraṃ paraṃ na samānatvam|| 86 ||
nijamuktasya svabhāvamuktasyāvidyākhyakāraṇanāśena yathokkabandhanivṛttimātraṃ paramātyantikaṃ vivekajñānasya phalam| dhvaṃsaścāvināśī, na tu karṇaṇa iva sukhādikaṃ bhāvarūpaṃ kāryaṃ , yena nāśitayā duḥkhadaṃ tat syāt | karmaṇaśca dṛṣṭakāraṇaṃ vinā na sākṣādevāvidyānāśakatvaṃ ghaṭata iti | ato jñānasyā'kṣayatvānna samānatvaṃ jñānakarmaṇorityarthaḥ| jñānānna punarāvṛttiḥ sambhavati, avivekā''khyakāraṇanāśāditi siddham| tadevaṃ vivekajñānameva sākṣājjñānopāya ityuktam|| 86 ||
idānīṃ vivekajñānasyāpi sākṣādupāyāḥ pramāṇāni parākṣyante | 'ātmā vā are draṣṭavyaḥ śrotavyo mantavyaṃ' ityādiścatibhirhi pramāṇatrayeṇātmajñānamityavagamyate| karmādikaṃ tvanyanmanādipramāṇānāṃ śudhyādikarameveti -- 
dvayorekatarasya vāpyasannikṛṣṭārthaparicchittiḥ pramā
tatsādhakatamaṃ yattat trividhaṃ pramāṇam|| 87 ||
asannikṛṣṭaḥ pramātaryanārūḍho'nadhigata iti yāvat| evambhūtasyārthasya vastunaḥ paricchittiravadhāraṇaṃ pramā | sā ca dvayorbuddhipuruṣayorubhayoreva dharmo bhavatu, kiṃ vaikataramātrasyobhayathaiva tasyāḥ pramāyā yat sādhakatamaṃ phalāyogavyavacchinnaṃ kāraṇam, tacca trividhaṃ vakṣyamāṇarūpeṇetyarthaḥ| smṛtivyāvartanāyānadhigateti | bhramavyāvartanāya vastviti | saṃśayavyāvartanāya tvavadhāraṇamiti | atra yadi pramārūpaṃ phalaṃ puruṣaniṣṭhamātramucyate, tadā buddhivṛttireva pramāṇam| yadi ca buddhiniṣṭhamātramucyate, tadā tuktendriyasannikarṣādireva pramāṇam| puruṣastu pramāsākṣyeva, na pramāteti| yadi ca pauruṣeyabodho buddhi
vṛttiścobhayamapi pramocyate, tadātūktamubhayameva pramābhedena pramāṇaṃ bhavati | cakṣurādiṣu tu pramāṇavyavahāraḥ paramparayaiva sarvatheti bhāvaḥ| pātañjalabhāṣye tu vyāsadevaiḥ puruṣaniṣṭhabodhaḥ prametyuktaḥ| puruṣārthameva karaṇānāṃ pravṛttyā phalasya puruṣaniṣṭhatāyā evaucityāt| ato'trāpi sa eva mukhyaḥ siddhāntaḥ | na ca puruṣadoṣasvarūpasya nityatayā kathaṃ phalatvamiti vācyam | kevalasya nityatve'pyarthoparaktasya kāryatvāt| puruṣārthoparāgasyaiva vā phalatvāditi||
atreyaṃ prakriyā| indriyapraṇālikayārthasannikarṣeṇa liṅgajñānādinā vā''dau buddherarthākārā vṛttirjāyate| tatra cendriyasannikarṣajā pratyakṣāvṛttirindriyaviśiṣṭabuddhyāśritā nayanādigatapittādidoṣaiḥ pittādyākāravṛttyadayāditi viśeṣaḥ sā ca vṛttirarthoparaktā pratibimbarūpeṇa puruṣārūḍhā satī bhāsate | puruṣasyāpariṇāmitayā buddhivat svato'rthākāratvāsambhavāt, arthākāratayā eva cārthagrahaṇatvāt, anyasya durvacatvāditi | tadetade vakṣyati 'japāsphaṭikayoriva noparāgaḥ kintvabhimāna' iti | yogasūtraṃ ca 'vṛttisārūpyamitaratreti'| smṛtirapi -- 
tasmiṃściddarpaṇe sphāre samastā vastudṛṣṭayaḥ|
imāstāḥ pratibimbanti sarasīva taṭadrumāḥ|| iti |
yogabhāṣyācca -- 'buddheḥ pratisaṃvedī puruṣa' iti | pratidhvanivat pratisaṃvedaḥ saṃvedanapratibimbastasyāśraya ityarthaḥ| etena puruṣāṇāṃ kūṭasthavibhucidrūpatve'pi na sarvadā sarvābhāsanaprasaṅgaḥ, asaṅgatayā svato'rthākāratvābhāvāt| arthākāratāṃ vinā ca saṃyogamātreṇārthagrahaṇasyātīndriyādisthale buddhāvadṛṣṭatvāditi| puruṣe ca svasvabuddhivṛttīnāmeva pratibimbārpaṇasāmarthyamiti phalabalāt kalpyate| yathā rūpavatāmeva jalādiṣu pratibimbanasāmarthyaṃ netarasyeti | rūpavattvaṃ ca na sāmānyataḥ pratibimbaprayojakam śabdasyāpi pratidhvanirūpapratibimbadarśanāt| na ca śabdajanyaṃ śabdāntarameva pratidhvaniriti vācyam| sphaṭikalauhityāderapi japāsannikarṣajanyatāpattyā pratibimbamithyātvasiddhāntakṣateriti| pratibimbaśca buddhereva pariṇāmaviśeṣo bimbākāro jalādigata iti mantavyam||
kecittu vṛttau pratibimbitaṃ sadeva cātanyaṃ vṛttiṃ prakāśayati, tathā vṛttigatapratibimba eva vṛttau caitanyaviṣayatā, na tu cetanye vṛtti pratibimbo'stītyāhuḥ|tadasat | upadarśitaśāstravirodhena kevalatarkasyāprayojakatvāt, vinigamanāviraheṇa vṛtticaitanyayoranyonyaviṣayatākhyasambandharūpatayānyonyapratibimbasiddheśca| bāhyasthale'rthākāratayā eva viṣayatārūpatvasiddhyāntare'pi tattarthākāratāyā eva viṣayatātvaucityācceti |
ye tu tārkikā jñānasya viṣayatāṃ necchanti, tanmate jñānavyaktīnāmanugamakadharmābhāvena ghaṭaviṣayakaṃ paṭaviṣayakaṃ jñānamityādyanugatavyavahārānupapattiḥ||
kecitta tārkikā anayaivānupapattyā viṣayatāmatiriktapadārthamāhuḥ| tadapyasat | anubhūyamānārthākāratāṃ vihāya viṣayatāntarakalpane gauravāditi ||
nanu tathāpi svasvopādhivṛttirūpaiva vṛtticaitanyayauranyoniyaviṣayatāstu, svopādhivṛttitvenaivānugamād alamākārākhyapratibimbadvayeneti cenna | pratibimbaṃ vinā svattvasyāpi durvacatvāt| svattvaṃ hi svabhuktavṛttivāsanāvattvam| bhogaśca jñānam| tathāca viṣayatālakṣaṇasya viṣayasāmagrīghaṭitvenātmāśvayaḥ| tasmādacaitanyacaitanyayoranyonyaviṣayatārūpo'nyonyasminnanyonyapratibimbaḥ siddhaḥ| adhikantu yogavārtike draṣṭavyamiti dik | atrāyaṃ pramātrādivibhāgaḥ -- 
pramātā cetaneḥ śuddhaḥ pramāṇaṃ vṛttireva naḥ|
pramārthākāravṛttīnāṃ cetane pratibimbanam||
pratibimbitavṛttīnāṃ viṣayo meya ucyate|
sākṣāddarśanarūpaṃ ca sākṣitvaṃ vakṣyati svayam||
ataḥ syāt kāraṇābāvād vṛtteḥ sākṣyeva cetanaḥ|
viṣṇavādeḥ sarvasākṣitvaṃ gauṇaṃ liṅgodyabhāvataḥ|| iti || 87 ||
nanu -- 
yathā prakāśayatyekaḥkṛtsnaṃ lokamimaṃ raviḥ|
kṣetraṃ kṣetrī tathākṛtsnaṃ prakāśyati bhārata ! ||
ityādivākyeṣūpamānādi prakṛtipuruṣaviveke pramāṇamupanyastam, tat kathamucyate trividhamiti, tatrāha -- 
tatsiddhau sarvasiddhernādhikyāsiddhiḥ|| 88 ||
trividhapramāṇasiddhau ca sarvasyārthasya siddherna pramāṇādhikyaṃ siddhyati, goravādityarthaḥ| ata eva manunāpi pramāṇatrayamevopanyastam pratyakṣamanumānaṃ ca śāstraṃ ca vividhāgamam|
trayaṃ suviditaṃ kāryaṃ dharmaśuddhimabhīpsatā|| iti |
upamānaitibyadīnāṃ cānumānaśabdayoḥ praveśaḥ| anupalabdhyādīnāṃ ca pratyakṣe praveśa iti | uktavākye cedamanumānamabhipretam| āpādatavamastakaṃ kṛtsnaṃ svavyatiriktenaikena prakāśyaṃ svayamaprakāśatvāt| trailokyavaditi| tejaścaitanyasādhāraṇaṃ ca prakāśatvamakhaṇḍopādhiḥ prakāśavyavahāṃraniyāmakatayā siddha iti || 88 ||
puruṣaniṣṭhā prameti mukhyasiddhāntamāśritya pramāṇānāṃ viśeṣalakṣaṇāni vaktumupakramate -- 
yat sambaddhaṃ sat tadākārollekhi vijñānaṃ
tat pratyakṣam|| 89 ||
sambaddhaṃ bhavat sambaddhavastvākāradhāri bhavati yadvijñānaṃ buddhivṛttistat pratyakṣaṃ pramāṇamityarthaḥ| atra sadityantaṃ hetugarbhaviśeṣaṇam| tathā ca svārthasannikarṣajanyākārasyāśrayaṃ vṛttiḥ pratyakṣaṃ pramāṇamiti niṣkarṣaḥ| 'vṛttiḥ sambandhārthaṃ sarpatī'tyāgāmisūtrānna vṛtteḥ sannikarṣajanyatvamityākārāśrayagrahaṇam| cakṣurādidvārakabṛddhivṛttiśca pradīpasya śikhātulyā bāhyārthasannikarṣānantarameva tadākārollekhanī bhavatīti nāsambhavaḥ|| 89 ||
nanu yogināmatītānāgatavyavastupratyakṣe'vyāptiḥ, sambaddhavastvākārābhāvādityāśaṅkya tasyālakṣyatvena samādhatte -- 
yogināmabāhyapratyakṣatvānna doṣaḥ|| 90 ||
aindriyakapratyakṣamevātra lakṣyam| yoginaścābāhyapratyakṣakāḥ| ato na doṣo, na tatpratyakṣe'vyāptirityarathaḥ|| 90 ||
vāstavaṃ samādhānamāha -- 
līnavastulabdhātiśayasambandhādvā'doṣaḥ|| 91 ||
athavā tadapi lakṣyameva tathāpi na doṣo navyaptiḥ. yato līnavastuṣu labdhayogajadharmajanyātiśayasya yogicittasya sambandho ghaṭata ityarthaḥ| atra līnaśabdaḥ parābhipretāsannikṛṣṭavācī | satkāryavādinā hyatītādikamapi svarūpato'stīti tatsambhavediti viyavahitaviprakṛṣṭeṣu sambandhahetuvidhayā labdhātiśayeti viśeṣaṇam| atiśayaśca vyāpakatvaṃ , vṛttipracibandhakatamonivṛttyādiśceti |
idaṃ cātrā'vadheyam| yatsambaddhaṃ saditi pūrvasūtre buddherarthasannikarṣasyaiva pratyakṣahetutālābhāt pratyakṣasāmānyo bāhyārthasādhāraṇe buddhyarthasannikarṣa eva kāraṇam| indriyasannikarṣāstu cākṣuṣādipratyakṣeṣu viśiṣyaiva kāraṇāni |
nanvevamindriyasannikarṣayogajadharmādyabhāve'pi buddhyā bāhyārthapratyakṣāpattiḥ| maivam| tamaḥpratibandhena tadānīṃ buddhisattvasya vṛttyasambhavāt| tacca tamaḥkadācidarthendriyayoḥ sannikarṣeṇa kadācicca yogajadharmeṇāpasāryate| añjanasaṃyogena nayanamālinyavat | na caivaṃ taddhetoreva tadastviti nyāyenendriyasannikarṣādereva bāhyārthapratyakṣasāmānye hetutāstviti vācyam| suṣuptyādau tamaso buddhivṛttipratibandhakatvasiddheḥ||
sattvājjāgaraṇaṃ vidyādrajasā svapnamādiśet|
prasvāpanaṃ tu tamasā turīyaṃ triṣu santatam||
ityādismṛtibhyaḥsuṣuptyādau vṛttipratibandhakāntarāsambhavācca| cākṣuṣavṛttāvapi tamasaḥ pratibandhadarśanācca|
yatta śuṣkatārkikāḥ suṣuptau vṛttyanutpādārthaṃ jñānasāmānye tvaṅmānoyogaṃ kāraṇaṃ kalpayanti| tadasat| tvagindriyotpatteḥ prāgapi kevalabuddhyā svayambhuvaḥ sarvapratyakṣaśravaṇāt| tvaṅmanoyogānutpāde'pi tamasa eva nimittatāyā vaktavyatvācca| kevalatarkasyāpratiṣṭhādoṣagrastatvācceti dik|| 91 ||
nanu tathāpīśvarapratyakṣe'vyāptiḥ, | tasya nityatvena sannikarṣā'janyatvāditi,tatrāha -- 
īśvarāsiddheḥ|| 92 ||
īśvare pramāṇābhāvānna doṣa ityanuvartate| ayaṃ ceśvarapratiṣedha ekadeśināṃ praudhivādenaiveti prāgeva pratipāditam| anyathā hīśvarā'bhāvādityevocyeta| īśvarābhyupagame tu sannikarṣajanyajātīyatvameva pratyakṣalakṣaṇaṃ vivakṣitam| sājātyaṃ ca jñānatvasākṣādvyāpyajātyeti bhāvaḥ|| 92 ||
śrṛtismṛtibhyāṃ kathamīśo na siddhyatītyākāṅkṣāyāṃ tarkavirodhaṃ laukikameva bādhakamāha -- 
muktabaddhayoranyatarābhāvānna tatsiddhiḥ|| 93 ||
sa īśvaro'bhimataḥ kiṃ kleśādimukto vā tairbaddho vā| anyatarasyāpyasambhavānneśvarasiddhirityarthaḥ|| 93 ||
ubhayathāpyasatkaratvam|| 94 ||
muktatve sati sraṣṭṭatvādyakṣamatvaṃ tatprayojakābhimānarāgādyabhāvāt | baddhatve'pi mūḍhatvānna sṛṣṭyādikṣamatvamityarthaḥ|| 94 ||
nanvevamīśvarapratipādakaśrutīnāṃ kā gatistatrāha -- 
muktātmanaḥ praśaṃsā, upāsā siddhasya vā || 95 ||
yathāyogaṃ kāciccharutirmuktātmanaḥ kevalātmasāmānyasya jñeyatā'bhidhānāya sannidhimātraiśvaryeṇa stutrūpā prarocanārthā | kācicca saṅkalpapūrvakasraṣṭṭatvādipratipādikā śrutiḥ siddhasya brahmaviṣṇuharāderevānityeśvarasyābhimānādimato'pi[goṇanityatvādimatvānnityatvādyupāsā | ityapi pāṭhāntaram] gauṇanityatvādimattvādyupāsāparetyarthaḥ|| 95 ||
nanu tathāpi prakṛtyādyakhilādhiṣṭhātṛtvaṃ śrūyamāṇaṃ nopamāṇaṃ nopapadyate, lokesaṅkalpādinā pariṇāmanasyaivādhiṣṭhātṛtvavyavahārāditi| tatrāha -- 
tatsannidhānādadhiṣṭātṛtvaṃ maṇivat|| 96 ||
yadi saṅkalpena sraṣṭṭatvamadhiṣṭhātṛtvamucyate, tadāyaṃ doṣaḥ syāt| asmābhistu puruṣasya sannidhānādevādhiṣṭhātṛtvaṃ sraṣṭṭatvādirūpamiṣyate, maṇivat| yathā'yaskāntamaṇeḥ sānnidhyamātreṇa śalyaniṣkarṣakatvaṃ, na saṅkalpādinā, tathaivādipuruṣasya saṃyogamātreṇa prakṛtermahattvarūpeṇa pariṇamanam | idameva ca svopādhisraṣṭṭatvamityarthaḥ| tathā coktam -- 
niricche saṃsthite ratne yathā lohaḥ pravartate|
sattāmātreṇa devena tathā cāyaṃ jagajjanaḥ||
ata ātmani kartṛtvamakartṛtvaṃ ca saṃsthitam|
niricchatvādakartā'sau kartā sannidhimātrataḥ|| iti ||
' tadaikṣata bahu syām' ityādiśrutistu, kūlaṃ pipatiṣatītivad gauṇī, prakṛterāsannabahutaraguṇasaṃyogāt|
athavā buddhipūrvasṛṣṭiviṣayametīdṛśavākyajātaṃ, na tvādisargaparam, tasyā'buddhipūrvakatvasmaraṇāditi bhāvaḥ| yathā kaurme -- 
ityeṣa prākṛtaḥ sargaḥ saṃkṣepāt kathito mayā |
abuddhipūrvakasteṣa brāhmī sṛṣṭiṃ nibodhata || iti ||
asya ca vākyasyādipuruṣabuddhyā janyatvena saṅkoce gauravamiti || 96 ||
na kevalaṃ sargādāveva puruṣasya saṃyogamātreṇa sraṣṭṭatvādikamapitvanyeṣvapi saṅkalpādipūrvakeṣu bhūtādiṣvakhileṣu viśeṣakāryeṣvapi sarvapuruṣāṇāmityāha -- 
viśeṣakāryeṣvapi jāvīnām|| 97 ||
adhiṣṭātṛtvaṃ sannidhānādityanuṣajjyate| antaḥkaraṇopalakṣitasyaiva jīvaśabdārthatvaṃ ṣaṣṭādhyāye vakṣyati | tathā ca viśeṣakārye visargākhye vyaṣṭisṛṣṭāvapi jāvīnāmantaḥkaraṇapratibimbitacetanānāṃ sannidhānādevādhiṣṭhātṛtvaṃ, na tu kenāpi vyāpāreṇa kūṭasthacinmātrarūpatvādityarthaḥ|| 97 ||
nanu cet sadā sarvajña īśvaro nāsti, tarhi vedāntamahāvākyārthasya vivekasyopadeśe'ndhaparamparāśaṅkayā'prāmāṇyaṃ prasajjyeta, tatrāha -- 
siddharūpabodadhṛtvādvākyārthopadeśaḥ || 98 ||
hiraṇyagarbhādīnāṃ siddharūpasya yathārthasya boddhatvāttadvaktṛkā''yurvedādiprāmāṇyenāvadhṛtāccaiṣāṃ vākyārthopadeśaḥ pramāṇamiti śeṣaḥ|| 98 ||
nanu puruṣasya cet sannidhimātreṇa gauṇamadhiṣṭhātṛtvaṃ tarhi mukhyamadhiṣṭātṛtvaṃ kasyetyākāṅkṣāyāmāha -- 
antaḥkaraṇasya tadujjvalitatvāllohavadadhiṣṭhātṛtvam|| 99 ||
antaḥ karaṇasyānupacaritamadhiṣṭhātṛtvaṃ saṅkalpādidvārakaṃ pratyetavyam| nanvadhiṣṭhātṛtvaṃ ghaṭādivadaṭetanasya na yuktam, tatrāha lohavat tadujjvalitvāditi| antaḥkaraṇaṃ hi taptalohavaccetanojjvalitaṃ bhavati| atastasya cetanāyamānatayā'dhiṣṭhātṛtvaṃ ghaṭādivyāvṛttamupapadyata ityarthaḥ| nanvevaṃ caitanyenāntaḥkaraṇasyojjvalaṃ citeḥ saṅgitvamagnivadeva syāditi cenna| nityojjvalacaitanyasaṃyogaviśeṣamātrasya saṃyogaviśeṣajanyacaitanyapratibimbasyaivāntaḥkaraṇājjvalanarūpatvāt | na tu caitanyam antaḥ karaṇesaṃkrāmati, yena saṅgitaḥ syāt|| agnerapi hi prakāśādikaṃ na lohe saṃkrāmati, kinvagniyogaviśeṣa eva lohasyojjvalanamiti | nanvevamapi saṃyogena pariṇāmitvamiti cenna| sāmānyaguṇātiriktadharmotpattāveva pariṇāmavyavahārāditi | ayaṃ ca saṃyogaviśeṣo'ntaḥkaraṇasyaiva sattvodrekarūpāt pariṇāmādbhavatīti phalabalāt kalpyate | puruṣasyā'pariṇāmitvena saṃyoge tannimittakaviśeṣāsambhavāditi | ayameva ca saṃyogaviśeṣeṇa buddhyātmanoranyo'nyapratibimbane hetuḥ | nanu pratibimbahetutayā saṃyogaviśeṣāvaśyakatve pratibimbakalpanā vyarthā, pratibimbakāryasyārthajñānādeḥsaṃyogaviśeṣādeva sambhavāditi cet | maivam| buddhau caitanyapratibimbaścaitanyadarśanārthaṃ kalpyate, darpaṇe mukhapratibimbavat| anyathā karmakartṛvirodhena svasya sākṣāt svadarśanānupapatteḥ| ayameva ca citpratibimbo buddhau cicchāyāpattiriti caitanyādhyāsa iti cidāveśa iti cocyate| yaśca caitanye buddheḥ pratibimbaḥ sa cārūḍhaviṣayaiḥ saha buddherbhānārthamiṣyate| arthākāratayaivārthagrahaṇasya puruṣe'pyanaucityāt| arthākārasyaivārthagrahaṇaśabdārthatvācceti | sa cārthākāraḥ puruṣe pariṇāmo na sambhavatītyarthāt pratibimbarūpa eva paryavasyatīti dik |
sa cāyamanyo'nyapratibimbo yogabhāṣye vyāsadevaiḥ siddhāntitaḥ|
"citiśaktirapariṇāminyapratisaṃkramā ca pariṇāminyarthe pratisaṃkrānteva tad vṛttimanupatati| tasyāśca prāptacaitanyopagraharūpāyā buddhivṛttenukārimātratayā buddhivṛttyaviśiṣṭā hi jñānavṛttirityākhyāyata" ityādinā | yogavārtike caitadvistarato'samābhiḥ pratipāditam||
kaścita tu buddhigatayā cicchāyayā buddhereva sarvārthajñātṛtvamicchādibhirjñānasya sāmānādhikaraṇyānubhavādanyasya jñānenānyasya pravṛttyanaucityāccetyāha| tat ātmajñānamūlakatvādupekṣaṇīyam| evaṃ hi buddhereva
jñātṛtve 'cidavasāno bhoga' ityāgāmisūtradvayavirodhaḥ| puruṣe pramāṇābhāvāśca, puruṣaliṅgasya bhogasya buddhāveva svīkārāt | na ca pratibimbānyathānupapattyā bimbabhūtaḥ puruṣaḥ setsyatīti vācyam| anyonyāśrayāt| pṛthagbimbasiddhau buddhisthacaitanyasya pratibimbatāsiddhiḥ, pratibimbatāsiddhau ca tatpratiyogitayā bimbasiddhiriti| asmanmate ca jñātṛtayā puruṣasiddhyanantaraṃ tasya jñeyatvānyayānupapattyā pratibimbasiddhau nānyonyāśrayaḥ| atha vṛttisākṣitayā bimbarūpaścetanaḥ siddhyatīti cet, tarhi sākṣiṇa eva pramātṛtvamapyucitam| ubhayorjñātṛtvakalpane gauravāt| vṛttijñānaghaṭajñānayoḥ sāmānādhikaraṇyānubhavācca| kiñcaivaṃ sati buddhereva bhoktṛtve 'bhoktṛbhāvā' dityāgāmisūtreṇa bhoktṛtayā puruṣasādhanaṃ viruddhayet | atha buddhigatavicchāyārūpeṇa sambandhena bimbasyaiva jñānaṃ , na tu citau buddhipratibimbaḥ kalpyata ityetāvanmātratayā cet tasyāśayovarṇyeta| tadapyasat| sūryyādeḥ svapratibimbarūpasambandhena jalāditatsthavastubhāsakatvādarśanāt, kiraṇaireva tadubhayabhāsanāt | marumarīcikādau tu svādhyastajalādibhāsakatvaṃ dṛṣṭameveti dṛṣṭānusāreṇāsmābhiścitau buddhipratibimba eva sarvārthabhānahetutayā sambandhaḥ kalpita iti | yaccoktamanyasya jñānenānyasya pravṛttyanupapattiriti| tadapi na | 'akarturapi phalopabhogo'nnādyavat' ityāgāmisūtreṇa jñānapravṛttyorvaiyadhikaraṇyasya dṛṣṭāntenopapādayipyamāṇatvāt| buddheḥ saṅkalpena dehakriyāyābhivātrāpi saṃyogaviśeṣādereva niyāmakatvāditi|| 99 ||
pratyakṣapramāṇaṃ lakṣayitvānumānaṃ lakṣayati -- 
pratibandhadṛśaḥ pratibaddhajñānamanumānam|| 100 ||
pratibandho vyāptiḥ| vyāptidarśanād vyāpakajñānaṃ vṛttirūpamanumānaṃ pramāṇamityarthaḥ| anumitistu pauruṣeyo bodha iti || 100 ||
śabdapramāṇaṃ lakṣayati -- 
āptaupadeśaḥ śabdaḥ|| 101 ||
āptiratra yogyatā | vedasyāpauruṣeyatāyāḥ pañcamādhyāyo vakṣyamāṇatvāt| tathāca yogyaḥ śabdastajjanyaṃ jñānaṃ śabdākhyaṃ pramāṇamityarthaḥ| phalaṃ ca pauruṣeyaḥ śabdo bodha iti || 101 ||
pramāṇapratipādanasya svayameva phalamāha -- 
ubhayasiddhiḥ pramāṇāt tadupadeśaḥ|| 102 ||
abhayorātmānātmanorvivekena siddhiḥ pramāṇādeva bhavati | atastasya pramāṇasyopadeśaḥ kṛta ityarthaḥ|| 102 ||
tatra yonānumānaviśeṣeṇa pramāṇena mukhyato'tra vivicya sādhanīyau tadvarṇayati -- 
sāmānyato dṛṣṭādubhayasiddhiḥ|| 103 ||
anumānaṃ tāvat trividhaṃ bhavati | pūrvavat, śeṣavat, sāmānyato dṛṣṭaṃ ceti| tatra pratyakṣīkṛtajātīyaviṣayakaṃ pūrvavat| yathā dhūmena vahnyanumānam [ parvato vahnimān dhūmāt ] | vāhna jātīyo hi mahānasādau pūrvaṃ pratyakṣīkṛtaḥ| vyatirekānumānaṃ śeṣavat | śeṣo'pūrvo'rtho'sya viṣayatvenāstīti śeṣavat | aprasiddhasādhyakamiti yāvat | yathā pṛthavītvenetarabhedānumānam [ pṛthavī itarebhyo bhidyate gaṃdhavatvāt | yannaivaṃ tannaivam|] | pṛthivītarabhedo hi prāgasiddhaḥ| sāmānyato dṛṣṭaṃ ca tadubhayabhinnamanumānam| yatra sāmānyataḥ pratyakṣādijātīyamādāya vyāptigrahāt pakṣadharmatābalena tadvijātīyo'pratyakṣādyarthaḥ siddhyati | yathā rūpādijñāne kriyātvena karaṇavattvānumānam| atra hi pṛthivītvādijātīyaṃ kuṭhārādikaraṇamādāya vyāptiṃ gṛhītvā tadvijātīyamatīndriyaṃ jñānakaraṇamindriyaṃ sādhyata iti | tatra sāmānyato dṛṣṭādanumānāt dvayoḥ prakṛtipuruṣayoḥ siddhirityarthaḥ| tatra sāmānyato dṛṣṭādanumānāt dvayoḥ prakṛtipuruṣayoḥ siddhirityarthaḥ| tatra prakṛteḥ sāmānyatā dṛṣṭamanumānam| yathā mahattattvaṃ sukhaduḥkhamohadharmakadravyopādānakam kāryatve sati sukhaduḥkhamohadharmakatvāt,
suvarṇādijakuṇḍalādivadityādi | puruṣe tu yadyapyanumānāpekṣā nāsti sarvasammatatvāt, tathāpi prakṛtyādiviveke sāmānyato dṛṣṭamevāpekṣyate| tadyathā pradhānaṃ parārthaṃ saṃhatya kāritvād, gṛhādivaditi | atra hi pratyakṣasiddhaṃ dehādyarthakatvaṃ gṛhādiṣu gṛhītvā tadvijātīyaḥ puruṣaḥ pradhānādiparatvenānumīyate| dehādīnāṃ ca bhokkṛtvamavivekena prāggṛhītamityubhayasiddhiriti
|| 103 ||
yā pramāṇasya phalabhūtā pramākhyasiddhiruktā tayā puruṣasya pariṇāmāpattirityāśaṅkāyāṃ tasyāṃ svarūpamāha -- 
cidavasāno bhogaḥ|| 104 ||
puruṣarūpe cantaye paryavasānaṃ yasyaitādṛśā bhogaḥ siddhirityarthaḥ| buddherbhogasya vyāvartanāya cidavasāna iti | citaḥ pariṇāmitvasadharmatvādiśaṅgānirāsāyāvasānapadam| cittau bhogasya sivarūpe paryavasitatvānna kauṭasthyādihānirityāśayaḥ| tathāhi pramāṇākhyavṛttyā rūḍhaṃ prakṛtipuruṣādikaṃ prameyaṃ vṛttyā saha puruṣe pratibimbitaṃ sadbhāsate | ato'rthoparaktavṛttipratibimbāvacchinnaṃ svarūpacaitanyameva bhānaṃ , puruṣasya bhogaḥ,pramāṇasya ca phalamiti| tataśca pratibimbarūpeṇārthasambandhe dvāratayā vṛttīnāṃ karaṇatvamiti | taduktaṃ viṣṇupurāṇe -- 
gṛhītānindriyairarthātmane yaḥ prayacchati|
antaḥkaraṇarūpāya tasyai viśvātmane namaḥ|| iti ||
rājño hi karaṇavargaḥ svāmine bhogyajātaṃ samaparyatīti dṛṣṭamiti| bhogaśabdārthaścābhyavaharaṇam| ātmasātkaraṇamiti yāvat| sa ca dehādicetanānteṣu sādhāraṇaḥ | viśeṣastvayam| apariṇāmitvāt puruṣasya viṣayabhogaḥ pratibimbādānamātram| anyeṣāṃ tu pariṇaāmitvāt puṣṭyādi rapīti| ayameva ca pariṇāmarūpaḥ pāramārthiko bhogaḥ puruṣe pratiṣiddhyate, buddherbhoga ivātmanītyādibhirati mantavyam| asmin sūtre puruṣasyāpi phalavyāpyatā siddhā | cidavasānatāyā evobhayasiddhitvavacanāditi|| 104 ||
nanukartureva loke kriyāphalabhogo dṛṣṭaḥyathā sañcarata eva sañcārotthaduḥkhabhoga iti, tat kathaṃ buddhikṛtadharmādiphalasya sukhādyātmikāyā arthoparaktabuddhivṛtterbhogaḥ puruṣe ghaṭetetyāśaṅkāyāmāha -- 
akarturapi phalopabhogo'nnādyavat|| 105 ||
buddhikarmaphalasyāpi vṛtterupabhogastadakarturapi puruṣasya yuktaḥ| annādyavat| yathānyakṛtasyānnāderūpabhogo rājño bhavati tadvadityarthaḥ| avivekasya svasvāmibhāvasya vā bhoganiyāmakatvāt tu nātiprasaṅgaḥ| sukhaduḥkhādeḥ karmaphalatvamabhyupetya buddhigataṃ karmaphalaṃ puruṣo bhuṅkta ityuktam|| 105 ||
idānīṃ puruṣāgatabhogasyaiva karmaphalatvaṃ svīkṛtya buddhikarmaṇā puruṣa eva phalamutpadyata iti mukhyasiddhāntamāha -- 
avivekādvā tatsiddheḥ kartuḥ phalāvagamaḥ|| 106 ||
athavā kartari phalameva na bhavati | sukhaṃ bhuñjīyetyādikāmanābhirbhogasyaiva phalatvāt ato bhoktṛniṣṭhameva phalaṃ bhavati, śāstravihitaṃ phalamanuṣṭhātarīti| śāstreṣu kartuḥ phalāvagamastu tatsiddherakartṛniṣṭhāyā bhogākhyasiddheḥ kartṛbuddhāvavivekādityarthaḥ| yo'haṃ karomi sa evāhaṃ bhuñje iti hi laukikānubhava iti | yā ca sukhaṃ me bhūyādityādikāmanā , sā putro me bhūyāditivat phalasādhanatvenaivopapadyate| bhogastu nānyasya sādhanam| ataḥ sa eva phalamiti mukhyaḥ siddhāntaḥ| bhogasya puruṣasvarūpatve'pi vaiśeṣikāṇāṃ mate śrotravat kāryatā bodhyā | sukhādyavacchinnacitireva bhogatvāt | asmiścaṃ bhogasya phalatvapekṣe duḥkhabhogābhāvaevāpavargo bodhyaḥ| athavā bhogyatārūpasvattvasambandhena sukhaduḥkhābhāvayoreva phalatvamastu| tena sambandhena dhanāderiva sukhāderapi puruṣaniṣṭhatvāditi
|| 106 || 
tadevaṃ pramāṇāni pramāṇaphalabhūtāṃ prameyasiddhiṃ ca pratipādya prameyasiddherapi phalamāha -- 
nobhayaṃ ca tattvākhyāne || 107 ||
pramāṇena prakṛtipuruṣayostattvākhyāne tattvasākṣātkare sati ubhayamapi sukhaduḥkhe na bhavataḥ| 'vidvān harṣaśokau jahāti' iti śruternyāyāccetyarthaḥ|| 107 ||
saṃkṣepato vivekenānumāpitau prakṛtipuruṣau | tayoḥ prakṛtipuruṣayoranumāne'vāntaraviśeṣā itaḥ paramadhyāyasamāptiṃ yāvad vicāryāḥ| tatra cādau prakṛtyādyanumāneṣvanupalambhabādhakamapākaroti -- 
viṣayo'viṣayo'pyatidūrāderhānopādānābhyāmindriyasya || 108 ||
indriyānupalabhyatāmātrato ghaṭādyabhāvavat pratyakṣeṇa cārvākaiḥ prakṛtyādyābhāvaḥ sādhayituṃ na śakyate | yato vidyamāno'pyartha imdriyaghātendriyagrahābhyāṃ cetyarthaḥ| sāmagrīsamavadhāne sati anupalambhasyaivābhāvapratyakṣahetutā | prakṛtyādyupalabhyate tu vakṣyamāṇapratibandhānna sāmagrīsamavadhānamiti bhāvaḥ| atidūrāyaśca doṣā viśiṣya kārikayā parigaṇitāḥ -- 
atidūrāt sāmīpyādindriyaghātānmano'vasthānāt |
saukṣmyād vyavadhānādabhibhavāt sapnābhihārācca|| iti ||
samānābhihāraḥ sajātīyasaṃvalanam| yathā māhiṣe gavyamiśraṇānmāhiṣatvāgrahaṇamiti || 108||
nanvatidūratvādiṣu madhye prakṛtyādyupalambhe kiṃ pratibandhakamiti, tatrāha -- 
saukṣmyāttadanupalabdhiḥ|| 109 ||
tayoḥ pūrvoktayoḥ prakṛtipuruṣayoranupalabdhistu sokṣmyādityarthaḥ| sūkṣmatvaṃ ca nāṇutvam, viśvavyāpanāt | nāpi durūhatvādikam, durvacatvāt | kintu pratyakṣapramāpratibandhikā jātiḥ| yogajadharmasya cottejakatayā prakṛtipuruṣādīnāṃ pratyakṣapramā bhavati | jātisāṅkaryaṃ ca na doṣāvaham| athavā niravayavadravyatvamevā'tra sūkṣmatvaṃ, yogajadharmaścottejaka eveti|| 109 ||
nanvabhāṣādevānupalabdhisambhave kimarthaṃ saukṣmyaṃ kalpyate, anyathā ca śaśaśrṛṅgādorapi saukṣmyādanupalabdhiḥ kiṃ na syāditi, tatrāha -- 
kāryadarśanāttadupalabdheḥ|| 110 ||
kāryānyathānupapattyā prakṛtyādisiddhī satyāṃ teṣāṃ sūkṣmatvaṃ kalpyate | anumānāt pūva sūkṣmatvādisaṃśa,yenābhāvānirṇayādanumānamipapadyata ityarthaḥ|| 110 |.
atra śaṅkate -- 
vādivipratipattestadasiddhiriti cet || 111 ||
nanu kāryaṃ cedutpatteḥ prāk siddhaṃ syāt , tadā tadādhāratayā nityā prakṛtiḥ setsyati | kāryasāhityenaiva kāraṇānumānasya vakṣyamāṇatvāt| vādivipratipattestu satkāryasyaivāsiddhiriti yadītyarthaḥ|| 111 ||
abhyupetya pariharati -- 
tathāpyekataradṛṣṭyā ekatarasiddhernāpalāpaḥ|| 112 ||
māstu sat kāryaṃ , tathāpyekatarasya kāryasya dṛṣṭyā'nyatarasya kāraṇasya siddherapalāpo nāstyeveti kāraṇaṃ siddhameva | tata eva ca pariṇāminaḥ sakāśādapariṇaāmitayā puruṣasya vivekena mokṣopapattirityarthaḥ| anenaivābhyupagamavādena vaiśeṣikādyāstikaśāstraṃ pravarttate | ato na satkāryavādiśrutismṛtivirodhe'pi
teṣāmaṃśāntareṣvaprāmāṇyamiti mantavyam|| 112 ||
paramārthataḥ parihāramāha -- 
trividhavirodhāpatteśca || 113 ||
atha sarvaṃ kāryaṃ trividhaṃ sarvavādisiddhamatītamanāgataṃ varttamānamiti | tatra yadi kāryaṃ sadā sanneṣyate, tadā trividhatvānupapattiḥ| atītādikāle ghaṭādyabhāvena ghaṭāderatītādidharmakatvānupapatteḥ| sadasatoḥ sambandhānupapatteḥ| kiñca papratiyogitvasya pratiyogirūpatve taddoṣatādavasthyāt| abhāvamātrasvarūpatve paṭādyabhāvo ghaṭādyabhāvaḥ syādyabhāvaḥ syādabhāvatvāviśeṣāt| abhāveṣvapi svarūpo viśeṣā'ṅgīkāre cābhāvatvasya paribhāṣāmātratvaprasaṅgāt | atha pratiyogyevābhāvaviśeṣaka iti cenna| asataḥ pratiyoginaḥ prāgabhāvādiṣu viśeṣakatvāsambhavāditi | tasmānnityasyaiva kāryasyātītānāgatavarttamānāvasthābhedā eva vaktavyāḥ| ghaṭo'tīto ghaṭo varttamāno ghaṭo bhaviṣyanniti pratyayānāṃ tulyarūpataucityāt | na tvekasya bhāvaviṣayatvamanyayoścābhāvaviṣayatvamiti | te evātītānāgatatve avasthe dhvaṃsaprāgabhāvavyavahāraṃ janayataḥ | tadatiriktābhāvadvaye pramāṇābhāvāditi dik||
adhikaṃ tu pātañjale draṣṭavyam| evamatyantābhāvā'nyonyābhāvāvapyadhikaraṇasvarūpāveva | na caivaṃ pratiyogisattākāle'pyadhikaraṇasvarūpānapāyādatyantābhāva tyayaprasaṅga iti vācyam| parairapi pratiyogimati deśe tadatyantābhāvāṅgīkārāt , pratiyogisambandhasyātītānāgatāvasthayoreva sāmayikātyantābhāvatvasambhavācca| tasmānnāsmatsiddhānte'bhālo'tiriktaḥ| kiñca ghaṭo dhvasto ghaṭo bhāvī ghaṭo'tra nāstītyādipratyayaniyāmakatayā kiñcidvastvākāṅkṣāyāṃ tadbhāvarūpameva kalpyate lāghavāt | abhāvasyādṛṣṭasya kalpane gauravāditi mantavyam|| 113 ||
itaśca satkāryasiddhirityāha -- 
nāsadutpādo nṛśrṛṅgavāt || 114 ||
naraśrṛṅgatulyasyāsata utpādo'pi na sambhavatītyarthaḥ|| 114 ||
atra hetumāha -- 
upādānaniyamāt|| 115 ||
mṛdyeva ghaṭa utpadyate tantuṣveva paṭa ityevaṃ kāryāṇāmupādānakāraṇaṃ prati niyamo'sti sa na sambhavati | utpatteḥ prāk kāraṇe kāryāsattāyāṃ hi na ko'pi viśeṣo'sti, yena kañcidevāsantaṃ janayet netaramiti | viśeṣāṅgīkāre ca bhāvatvāpattergatamasattayā | sa eva ca viśeṣo'smābhiḥ kāryasyānāgatāvasthetyucyata iti |
etenayadvaiśeṣikāḥ prāgabhāvameva kāryotpattiniyāmakaṃ kalpayanti tadapyapāstam| abhāvakalpanāpekṣayā bhāvakalpane lāghavāt, bhāvānāṃ dṛṣṭatvādanyānapekṣatvācca | kiñcābhāveṣu svato viśeṣe bhāvatvāpattiḥ| pratiyogirūpaviśeṣaśca pratiyogyasattākāle nāsti| ato'bhāvānāmaviśiṣṭatayā na kāryotpattau niyāmakatvaṃ yuktamiti ||115 ||
upādānaniyame pramāṇamāha -- 
sarvatra sarvadā sarvāsambhavāt || 116 ||
sugamam| upādānāniyame ca sarvatra sarvadā sarvaṃ sambhavedityāśayaḥ|| 116 ||
itaśca nāsadutpāda ityāha -- 
śaktasya śakyakāraṇāt || 117 ||
kāryaśaktimattvamevopādānakāraṇatvam| anyasya durvacatvāt, lāghavācca| sā śaktiḥ kāryasyānāgatāvasthaivetyataḥ śaktasya śakyakāryakaraṇānnāsata utpāda ityarthaḥ|| 117 ||
itaśca -- 
kāraṇabhāvācca|| 118 ||
utpatteḥ prāgapi kāryasya kāraṇābhedaḥ śrūyate| tasmācca satkāryasiddhyā nāsadutpāda ityaparthaḥ| kāryasyāsattve hi sadasatorabhedānupapattiriti| utpatteḥ prāk kāryāṇāṃ kāraṇābhede ca śrutiḥ| "taddhīdaṃ tarhyavyākṛtamāsīt | sadeva somyedamagra āsīt | āthmaivedamagra āsīt | āpa evedamagra āsurityādyāḥ" [ante "tapa evamemagra āsa | ayamevemagra āsa" ityapyatra pāṭhāntaraṃ kecitpaṭhanti|] || 118 ||
śaṅkate -- 
na bhāve bhāvayogaścet || 119 ||
nanvevaṃ kāryasya nityatve sati bhāvarūpe kārye bhāvayoga utpattiyogo na sambhavati | asataḥ sattva evotpattivyavahārāditi cedityarthaḥ|| 119 ||
pariharati -- 
nābhivyaktinibandhanau vyavahārāvyavahārau || 110 ||
kāryotpattervyavahārāvyavahārau kāryābhivyaktinimittakau | abhivyaktita utpattivyavahāro'bhivyaktabhāvāccotpattivyavahārābhāvaḥ | na tvasataḥ sattayetyarthaḥ| abhivyaktiśca na jñānaṃ, kintu vartamānāvasthā| kāraṇavyāpāro'pi kāryasya vartamānalakṣaṇapariṇāmeva janayati | sataśca kāryasya kāraṇavyāpārādabhivyaktimātraṃ loke'pi dṛṣṭam| yathā śilāmadhyasthapratimāyā laiṅgikavyāpāreṇā'bhivyaktimātraṃ, tilasthatailasya ca niṣpīḍanena, dhānasthataṇḍulasya cāvaghāteneti | taduktaṃ vāśiṣṭhe -- 
suṣuptāvasthayā cakrapadmarekhāḥ śilodere|
yathā sthitā citerantastatheyaṃ jagadāvalī || iti ||
prakṛtidvāreṇetyarthaḥ|| 120 ||
nanu bhavatūtpatteḥ prāk sato yathākathañcidutpattiḥ nāśastvanādibhāvasya kathaṃ syādityākāṅkṣāyāmāha -- 
nāśaḥ kāraṇalayaḥ|| 121 ||
līṅ śleṣeṇa ityanuṣāsanāllayaḥ sūkṣmatayā kāreṇeṣvavibhāgaḥ| sa evātītāśyo nāśa ityucyata ityarthaḥ| anāgatākhyastu layaḥ prāgabhāva ityucyata iti śeṣaḥ| līnakāryavyaktestu punarabhivyaktirnāsti, pratyabhijñādyāpattyā pātañjale nirākṛtatvāt | pareṇāmivāsmākamapyanāgatāvasthāyāḥ prāgabhāvākhyā abhivyaktihetutvācceti | nanvatītamapyastītyatra kiṃ pramāṇam ? na hyānāgatasattāyāmiva śrutyādayo'tītasattāyāmapi sphuṭamupalabhyanta iti| maivam| yogipratyakṣatvānyathānupapattyānāgatātītayorubhayoreva sattvasiddheḥ, pratyakṣasāmānye viṣayasya hetutvāt | anyathā varttamānasyāpi pratyakṣeṇāsiddhyāpatteḥ| tasmāddhiyāmaitsargikaprāmāṇyenā'sati bādhake yogipratyakṣeṇātītamapyastīti siddhyati| yogināmatītānāgatapratyakṣe ca śrutismṛtītihāsādikaṃ pramāṇaṃ yogavārtike prapañcitamiti dik|| tadevamabhivyaktilayābhyām kāryāṇāmutpattināśavyavahārāvuktau|
nanvabhivyaktirapi pūrvaṃ satī vā'satī vā? ādye kāraṇavyāpārāt viphalaḥ| antye cābhivyaktāveva satkāryasiddhāntakṣatiḥ, asatyāṃ evābhiṣyakterabhivyaktyaṅgīkārāditi||
atrocyate| kāraṇavyāpārāt prāk sarvakāryāṇāṃ sadā sattvābhyupagamenoktavikalpānavakāśād ghaṭavat tadabhivyakterapi vartamānā'vasthayā prāgasattvena tadasattānivṛttyarthaṃ kāraṇavyāpārāpekṣaṇāt | anāgatāvasthayā ca
satkāryasiddhāntasyā'kṣateḥ | nanvekadā sadasattvayorvirodha iti cet| prakārabhedasyoktatvāt | nanvevamapi prāgabhāvānaṅgīkāreṇa prāgasattvameva kāryāṇāṃ durvacamiti | maivam| avasthānāmeva parasparābhāvarūpatvāditi || 121 ||
nanu satkāryasiddhāntarakṣārthamabhivyakterapyabhivyaktireṣṭavyā, tathā cānavasthetyāśaṅkyāha -- 
pāramparyato'nveṣaṇā bījāṅkuravat || 122 ||
pāramparyataḥ paramparārūpeṇaivābhivyakteranudhāvanaṃ kartavyam| bījāṅkuravat, prāmāṇikatvena cāsyā adoṣatvādityarthaḥ| bījāṅkurābhyāṃ cātrayameva viśeṣo, yad bījāṅkurasthale kramikaparamparayānavasthā, abhivyaktau caikakālīnaparamparayeti | prāmāṇikatvantu tulyameveti | sarvakāryāṇāṃ svarūpato nityatvamavasthābhirvināśitvaṃ ceti pātañjalabhāṣye vadadbhirvyāsadevairapīyamanavasthā prāmāṇikatvena svīkṛteti | atra ca bījāṅkuradṛṣṭānto lokadṛṣṭyopanyastaḥ| vastutastu janmakarmādividatyatraiva tātparyam| tena bīdāṅkurapravāhasyādisargā'vadhikatvenānavadhikatvenānavasthāvirahe'pi na kṣatiḥ| ādisarge hi vṛkṣaṃ vinaiva bījamutpadyate hiraṇyagarbhasaṅkalpena taccharīrādibhya iti śrutismṛtyoḥ prasiddham -- 
yathā hi pādapo mūlaskandhaśākhādisaṃyutaḥ|
ādibījāt prabhavati bījānyanyāni vai tataḥ||
iti viṣṇupurāṇādivākyairiti || 122 ||
vastutastvanavarathāpi nāstītyāha -- 
utpattidvā'doṣaḥ|| 123 ||
yathā ghaṭotpatterutpattiḥ svarūpameva vaiśeṣikādibhirasadutpādavādibhiriṣyate lāghavāt , tathaivāsmābhirghaṭābhivyakterapyabhivyaktiḥ svarūpameveṣṭhavyā, lāghavāt| ata utpattāvivābhivyaktavapi nānavasthādoṣa ityarthaḥ| athaivamabhivyakterabhivyaktyanaṅgīkāre kāraṇavyāpārāt prāk tasyāḥ sattvānupapattyā satkāryavādakṣatiriti cenna. asmin pakṣe sata evābhivyaktirityeva satkāryasiddhānta ityāśayāt | abhivyakteścābhivyaktyabhāvena tasyāḥ prāgasattve'pi nāsatkāryavādatvāpattiḥ| nanvevaṃ mahadādīnāmeva prāga'sattvamiṣyatāṃ kimabhivyaktyākhyāvasthākalpaneneti cenna | "tdadhīdaṃ tarhyavyākṛtamāsīt" ityādiśrutibhiravyaktāvasthayā satāmeva kāryāṇāmabhivyaktisiddheḥ| tathāpyabhivyakteḥ prāgabhāvādisvīkārāpattiriti cenna| tisṛṇāmanāgatādyavasthāmāmanyo'nyāsyābhāvarūpatayoktatvāt, tādṛśābhāvanivṛttyaiva ca kāraṇavyāpārasāphalyādisambhavāt | ayameva hi satkāryavādināmasatkāryavādibhyāṃ viśeṣo yat tairucyamānau prāgabhāvapradhvaṃsau satkāryavādibhiḥ kāryasyā'nāgatātītāvasthe bhāvarūpe procyete | vartamānatākhyā cābhivyaktyavasthā ghaṭād vyatirikteṣyate| ghaṭāderavasthātrayavattvānubhavāditi| anyat tu sarvaṃ samānam| ato nāstyasmāsvadhikaśaṅkāvakāśa iti dik || 123 ||
" kāryadarśanamāt tadupalabdheḥ" iti sūtreṇa kāryeṇa mūlakāraṇamanumeyamityuktam, tatra kiyatparyantaṃ kāryamityavadhārayituṃ sarvakāryāṇāṃ sādharmyamāha -- 
hetumadanityamavyāpi sakriyamanekamāśritaṃ liṅgam|| 124 ||
kāraṇānumāpakatvāllayagamanādvā'tra liṅgaṃ kāryajātam| na tu mahattattvamātramatra vivakṣitam| hetumattvādīnāmakhilakāryasādhāraṇyāt||
hetumadanityamavyāpi sakriyamanekamāśritaṃ liṅgam|
sāvayavaṃ paratantraṃ vyaktaṃ viparītamavyaktam||
iti kārikāyāmapyata eva vyaktākhyaṃ sarvaṃ kāryameva liṅgamityuktam| tathā ca talliṅgaṃ hetumattvādidharmakamiti vākyarthaḥ | tatra hetumattvaṃ kāraṇavattvam| anityatvaṃ vināśitā| pradhānasya yā vyāpitā pūrvoktā tadvaiparītyamavyāpitvam| sakriyatvam -- adhyavasāyādirūpaniyatakāritvam| pradhānasya tu sarvakriyāsādhāraṇyena kāraṇatvānna kāryaikadeśamātrakāritvam| na ca kriyā karmaiva vaktuṃ śakyate | prakṛtikṣobhāt sṛṣṭiśravaṇena prakṛterapi karmavattayātra sakriyatvāpatteriti | anekatvaṃ sargabhedena bhinnatvam| sargadvayāsādhāraṇyamiti yāvat | na punaḥ sajātīyānekavyaktikatvam,
prakṛtāvativyāpteḥ, prakṛterapi sattvādyanekarūpatvāt| " sattvādīnāmataddharmatvaṃ tadrūpatvāt" ityāgāmisūtrāditi | āśritatvaṃ cāvayaveṣviti || 124 ||
kāryakāraṇayorbhede hetumattvādi siddhyatītyataḥ kāraṇātiriktakāryasiddhau pramāṇānyāha -- 
āñjasyādabhedato vā guṇasāmānyādestatsiddhiḥ
pradhānavyapadeśādvā|| 125 ||
tatsiddhirliṅgākhyakāryasya kāraṇātirekataḥ siddhiḥ kvacidāñjasyāt pratyakṣata evānāyāsena bhavati yathā stholyādinā dharmeṇa tantvādibhyaḥ paṭādīnām | kvacicca guṇasāmānyoderabhedato guṇasāmānyādyātmakatvena liṅgenānumānena bhavati, yathā ca mahāpṛthivītvādiguṇātmakatvarūpeṇa kāraṇavaidharmyeṇa mahadādīnām| yathā ca mahāpṛthivītvādisāmānyātmakatārūpeṇa tanmātravaidharmyeṇa pṛthivyādīnām| kvacittu ādiśabdagṛhātena karmādyātmakatāvaidharmyeṇa| yathā sthirāvayavebhyo'tiriktasya cañcalāvayavinaḥ | tathā pradhānavyapadeśāt pradhānaśruterapi kāraṇātiriktakāryasiddhirbhavati| pradhīyate'smin hi kāryajātamiti pradhānamucyate| tacca kāryakāraṇayorbhedābhedau vinā na ghaṭate| atyantābhede svasyādhāratvāsaṃbhavādityarthaḥ|| 125 ||
kāryāṇāṃ sādharmyarūpaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ kāraṇātiriktakāryeṇa pramāṇaṃ ca sūtrābhyāṃ darśitam idānīṃ kāryasadharmakatayā kāraṇānumānāya kāryakāraṇayorapi sādharmyaṃ pradarśayati -- 
triguṇācetanatvādi dvayoḥ || 126 ||
dvayoḥ kāryakāraṇayoreva triguṇatvādisādharmyamityarthaḥ| ādiśabdagrāhyāśca kārikāyāmuktāḥ -- 
triguṇamavivekaviṣayaḥ sāmānyamacetanaṃ pravavadharmi|
vyaktaṃ tathā pradhānaṃ tadviparītastathā ca pumān || iti ||
trayaḥ sattvādidravyarūpā guṇā atra santīti triguṇam| tatra mahadādiṣu kāraṇarūpeṇa sattvādīnāmavasthānaṃ[guṇatrayasamūharūpeṇa tu pradhāne satvādīnāmavasthānam| atre damadhikaṃ pāṭhāntaraṃ dṛśyate|] vane vṛkṣavadevāvagantavyam| athavāsattvādiśabdena sukhaduḥkhamohānāmapi vacanāt kāryakāraṇayostriguṇatva samañjasamiti avivekiviṣayo'jñaireva dṛṣyam| bhogyamiti yāvat| aviveki ca viṣayaśceti tacchede tvavivekatvaṃ sambhūyakāritvaṃ viṣayitvaṃ tu bhogyatvameva | sāmānyaṃ sarvapuruṣasādhāraṇam| puruṣabhede'pyabhinnamiti yāvat| prasavadharmi -- pariṇāmi| vyaktaṃ kāryam| pradhānaṃ kāraṇamityarthaḥ| kāryakaraṇayoranyonyavaidharmyamapi kārikayā darśitam -- 
hetumadanityamavyāpi sakriyamanekamāśritaṃ liṅgam|
sāvayavaṃ paratantraṃ vyaktaṃ viparītamavyaktam|| iti ||
atraikatvaṃ sargabhede'pyabhinnatvam| ataḥ prakṛteranekavyaktikatve'pi naikatvakṣatiḥ||
mahāntaṃ ca samāvṛtya pradhānaṃ samavasthitam|
anantasya na tasyāntaḥ saṅkhyānaṃ cāpi vidyate||
iti viṣṇupurāṇenāsaṃkhyeyatāvacanāt tu pradhānasya vyaktibahutvasiddhiriti|| 126 ||
pradhānākhyānāṃ jagatkāraṇaguṇānāmanyo'nyavivekāya teṣāmavāntaramapi vaidharmyaṃ siddhāntayati, vividhajñagatkāraṇatvopapattaye ca, na hyekarūpāt kāṇādvicitrakāryāṇi sambhavantīti -- 
prītyaprītiviṣādādyairguṇānāmanyo'nyaṃ vaidharmyam|| 127 ||
guṇānāṃ sattvādidravyatrayāṇāmanyo'nyaṃ sukhaduḥkhādyairvaidharmyaṃ, kāryeṣu taddarśanādityarthaḥ| sukhādikaṃ ca ghaṭāderapi rūpādivadeva dharmo'ntaḥkaraṇopādānatvād 'anyakāryāṇām' ityuktam| atrādiśabdagrāhyāḥ pañcaśikhācāryairuktāḥ| yathā sattvaṃ nāma prasādalāghavabhiṣvaṅgaprītititikṣāsantoṣādirūpānantabhedaṃ samāsataḥ sukhātmakam| evaṃ rajo'pi śokadinānābhedaṃ samāsato duḥkhātmakam| evaṃ tamo'pi nidrādinānābhedaṃ samāsato mohātmakamiti | atra prītyādīnāṃ guṇadharmatvavacanādāgāmisūtre ca laghutvādervakṣyamāṇatvāt sattvādīnāṃ dravyatvaṃ siddam| sukhādyātmakatā tu guṇānāṃ manasaḥ
saṅkalpātmakatāvaddharmyabhedādevopapadyate| na vaiśeṣikoktāḥ sukhādaya eva sattvādiguṇā iti | sattvāditrayamapi vyaktibhedādanantam| anyathā hi vibhumātratve guṇavimardavaicitryāt kāryavaicitryāmiti siddhānto nopapadyeta | vimarde'vāntarabhedāsambhavāt|| 127 ||
guṇānāṃ sattvādīnāmekaikavyaktimātratve vṛddhihnāsādikaṃ nopapadyate| tathā paricchannatve ca tatsamūharūpasya pradhānasya paricchinnatvāpattyā śrutismṛtisiddhamekadā'saṅkhyabrahmāṇḍādikaṃ nopapadyeta | ato'saṅkhyatve guṇānāṃ tritvasaṅkhyopapādanāya vivekādyarthaṃ ca teṣāṃ sādharmya pratipādayati -- 
ladhvādidharmaiḥ sādharmyaṃ vaidharmyaṃ ca guṇānām|| 128 ||
ayamarthaḥ ladhvādīti bhāvapradhāno nirdeśaḥ| laghutvādidharmeṇa sarvāsāṃ sattvavyaktīnāṃ sādharmyaṃ vaidharmyaṃ ca rajastamobhyām| tathā ca pṛthavāvyaktīnāṃ pṛtivātveneva sattvavyaktīnāmekajātīyatayaikatā sajātīyovaṣṭambhādinā vṛddhihnāsādikaṃ ca yuktamityāśayaḥ| evaṃ cañcalatvādidharmeṇa sattvādirajāvyaktīnāṃ sādharmyaṃ sattvatamobhyāṃ ca vaidharmyam| śeṣaṃ pūrvavat | evaṃ gurutvādidharmeṇa sarvāsāṃ tamovyaktīnāṃ sādharmyaṃ sattvarajobhyāṃ vaidharmyam| śeṣaṃ pūrvavaditi| vaidharmyasya prāgevoktatayātra punarvaidharmyakathanaṃ sampātāyātam| atra vaidharmyaṃ ceti pāṭhaḥ prāmādika eveti | athra sūtre sattvādīnāṃ kāraṇadravyāṇāṃ pratyekamanekavyaktikatvaṃ siddham, anyathā laghutvādīnāṃ sādharmyatvānupapatteḥ, samānānāṃ dharmasyaiva sādharmyatvāt| na ca kāryasattvādīnāmanekatayā laghutvādikaṃ sādharmyaṃ syāditi vācyam| triguṇātmakatvena ghaṭādīnāmapi kāryasatvādirūpatayā laghutvādīnāṃ sattvādisādharmyatvānupapatteḥ|tasmāt kāraṇaguṇānāmevātra sādharmyādikamucyata iti | sattvādīnāṃ laghutvādikaṃ coktaṃ kārikayā -- 
sattvaṃ laghu prakāśakamiṣṭamupaṣṭambhakaṃ calaṃ ca rajaḥ|
guru varaṇakameva tamaḥ pradīpavaccārthato vṛttiḥ|| iti ||
arthataḥ -- puruṣārthanimittāt | nanvevaṃ mūlakāraṇasya paricchinnāparicchinnāsaṅkhyavyaktikatve vaiśeṣikamatādatra ko viśeṣa iti cet| kāraṇadravyasya śabdasparśādirāhityameva ||
śabdasparśavihīnaṃ tu rūpādibhirasaṃyutam|
triguṇaṃ tajjagadyoniranādiprabhavāpyayam||
iti viṣṇupurāṇādibhyaḥ|| etacca pātañjale'smābhiḥprapaḍhcitam|| 128 ||
nanu mahadādīnāṃ svarūpataḥ siddhāvapi teṣāṃ pratyakṣeṇotpattyadarśanāt kāryatve nāsti pramāṇaṃ yena teṣāṃ hetumattvaṃ sādharmyaṃ syāt, tatrāha -- 
ubhayānyatvāt kāryatvaṃ mahadāderghaṭādivat || 129 ||
mahadādi pañcabhūtāntaṃ vivādāspadaṃ tāvanna puruṣo, bhogyatvāt | nāpi pirakṛtiḥ; mokṣānyathānupapatt‌yā vināśitvāt| ataḥ prakṛtipuruṣabhinnaṃ tadbhinnatvācca kāryaṃ ghaṭādivadityarthaḥ|| 129 ||
nanu vikāraśaktidāhādinaiva mokṣādyupapattervināśitvamapi teṣāmasiddhamityāśaṅkāyāṃ kāryatve hetvantarāṇyāha -- 
parimāṇāt || 130 ||
paricchinnatvād daiśikābhāvapratiyogitāvacchedakajātimattvādityarthaḥ| tena guṇavyaktīnāṃ paricchinnatve'pi tatra vyabhicāraḥ|| 130 ||
kiñca -- 
samanvayāt|| 121 ||
upavāsādinā kṣīṇaṃ hi buddhyāditattvamannādibhiḥ samanvayena samanugatena punarucīyate | ataḥ
samanvayāt kāryatvamunnīyata ityarthaḥ| nityasya hi niravayavatayā'vayavānupraveśarūpaḥ samanvayo na ghaṭata iti | samanvaye ca śrutiḥ pramāṇam manaḥ prakṛtya -- 'evaṃ te saumya ṣoḍaśānāṃ kalāvāmekākalā'tiśiṣṭābhūt | sānnenopasamāhitā prājvālī'diti | yogasūtraṃ ca -- 'jātyantarapariṇāmaḥ prakṛtyāpūrāt' iti || 131 ||
kiñca -- 
śaktitaśceti || 132 ||
karaṇataścetyarthaḥ| puruṣasya yat tat kāryaṃ cakṣurādivaditi bhāvaḥ | puruṣe sākṣādviṣayārpakatvaṃ prakṛternāstīti prakṛtiraṃna karaṇamiti | ato mahattvasya karaṇatayā kāryatve siddhe sutarāmanyeṣāmapi kāryatvam| itiśabdaśca hetuvargasamāptisūcanārtha ḥ|| 132 ||
yadi ca mahadādimadhye kiṃṭidakāryaṃ svīkriyate tadāpi tadeva prakṛtiḥ puruṣo veti siddhaṃ naḥ samīhitam| prakṛtipuruṣau prasādhya pariṇāmitvāpariṇāmitvābhyāṃ vivektavyāvityatraivāsmākaṃ tātparyādityāha -- 
taddhāne prakṛtiḥ puruṣo vā || 133 ||
taddhāne kāryatvahāne yadi pariṇāmī tadā prakṛtiḥ| yadi vā apariṇāmī bhoktā tadā puruṣa ityarthaḥ|| 133 ||
nanu nityamapyubhayabhinnaṃ syāt, tatrāha -- 
tayoranyatve tucchatvam|| 134 ||
akāryasya prakṛtipuruṣabhinnatve tucchatvaṃ śaśaśrṛṅgādivat pramāṇābhāvāt | akāryaṃ hi kāraṇatayā vā bhoktṛtayā vā siddhyati, nānyathetyarthaḥ|| 134 ||
tadevaṃ mahadādiṣu kāryatvaṃ prasādhya sāmprataṃ taiḥ prakṛtyanumāne'nuktaṃ viśeṣamāha -- 
kuryāt kāraṇānumānaṃ tatsāhityāt|| 135 ||
kāryānmahattattvāderliṅgāt sāmānyato dṛṣṭaṃ kāraṇānumānaṃ yaduktaṃ tat tāṭasthyanivṛttaye tatsāhityāt kāryasāhityenaiva karttavyam| "sadeva saumyedamagra āsīt, tama evedamagna āsīt" ityādi śrutyanusārāt | tadyathā -- mahadādikaṃ svopahitatriguṇātmakavastūpādānakam kāryatvāt śilāmadhyasthapratimāvat, tailā divaccetyarthaḥ| atrānukūlatarkaḥ prāgeva darśitaḥ|| 135 ||
tasyāḥ prakṛteḥ kāryādvaidharmyaṃ vivekārthamāha -- 
avyaktaṃ triguṇālliṅgāt || 136 ||
abhivyaktāṃt triguṇānmahattattvādapi mūlakāraṇamavyaktaṃ sūkṣmam | mahattvasya hi sukhādirguṇaḥ sākṣāt kriyate, prakṛteśca guṇo'pi na sākṣātkriyata iti pradhānam paramāvyaktam| mahattattvaṃ tu tadapekṣayā vyaktamityarthaḥ
|| 136 ||
nanupa paramasūkṣmaṃ cet tarhi tasyāpalāpa evocita ityākāṅkāyāṃ pūrvoktaṃ smārayati -- 
tatkāryatastatsiddhernāpalāpaḥ|| 137 ||
sugamam|| 137 ||
prakṛtyanumānagata viśeṣā vistarato vicāritāḥ| itaḥ paramadhyāyasamāptiparyantaṃ puruṣānumānagatā vicāryāḥ| tatra kañcanādau viśeṣamāha -- 
sāmānyena vivādābhāvāddharmavanna sādhanam || 138 ||
yatra vastuni sāmānyato vivādo nāsti na tasya svarūpataḥ sādhanamapekṣyate, dharmasyevetyarthaḥ| ayaṃ bhāvaḥ| yathā prakṛteḥ sāmānyenāpi sādhanamapekṣitaṃ, dharmiṇyapi vivādāt | naivaṃ puruṣasya sādhanamapekṣitam cetanāpalāpe jagadāndhyaprasaṅgato bhoktaryahampadārthe sāmānyato bauddhānāmapyavivādāt | dharma iva| dharmo hi sāmānyato bauddhairapi svīkriyate taptaśilārohaṇādiṣu dharmatvābhyupagamāt | ataḥ puruṣe vivekanityatvādisādhanamātramanumānaṃ kāryamiti || 138 ||
" saṃhataparārthatvāta puruṣasya" ityuktasūtreṇāpi vivekānumānamevā'bhipretam, na tu papuruṣasya sasarvathaivāpratyakṣatvamabhipretamiti | tatra cādau vivekapratijñānasūtram -- 
śarīrādivyatiriktaḥ pumān || 139 ||
śarīrādiprakṛtyantaṃ yaccaturviśatitattvātmakaṃ vastu tato'tiriktaḥ pumān bhoktetyarthaḥ| bhoktṛtvaṃ ca draṣṭṛtmamiti || 139 ||
atra hetanāha sūtraiḥ -- 
saṃhataparārthatvāt || 240 ||
yataḥ sarvaṃ saṃhataṃ prakṛtyādikaṃ parārthaṃ bhavati śayyādivat ato'saṃhatadehādibhyaḥ paraḥ puruṣaḥ siddhyatītyarthaḥ| ayaṃ ca hetuḥ saṃhataparārthatvāt puruṣasyetyatra vyākhyātaḥ| uktasyāpi hetoḥ punarupanyāso hetuvargasaṅkalanārthaḥ|| 140 ||
kiñca -- 
triguṇādiviparyayāt || 141 ||
sukhaduḥśamohātmakatvādivaiparītyādityarthaḥ| śarīrādīnāṃ hi yaḥ sukhadyātmakatvaṃ dharmaḥ sa sukhādibhoktari na sambhavati, svayaṃ sukhādigrahaṇe karmakartṛvirodhāt, dharmipuruskāreṇaiva sukhādyanubhavāditi|| nanu buddhivṛttipratibimbataṃ svasukhādikaṃ puruṣeṇa gṛhyatāṃ svavaditi cenna| evaṃ sati buddhereva sukhādikalpanaucityāt | puruṣagatasukhāderbuddhau pratibimbakalpane goravāt| ahaṃ sukhī duḥkhī mūḍhaḥ ityādipratyayāstu na puruṣe sukhādisādhakāḥ| tatsvāmittavenāpyupapatteśca| buddheḥ sukhādimattvenāpyupapatteḥ| laukikyāṃ hyahaṃbuddhāvavaśyaṃ buddhirapi viṣayo mithyājñānavāsanādirūpadoṣānuvṛttestatpratibimbakalpanāyāṃ ca goravāditi | ādiśabdena cātra triguṇamaviveki viṣaya iti kārikoktā'vivekitvādayo grāhyāḥ| tathā rūpadayaḥ śarīrādidharmāṃ grāhyāḥ|| 142 ||
kiñca -- 
adhiṣṭhānācceti || 142 ||
bhokturadhiṣṭhātṛtvāccādhiṣṭheyebhyaḥ prakṛtyantebhyo'tiriktatetyarthaḥ| adhiṣṭhānaṃ hi bhoktuḥ saṃyogaḥ| sa ca prakṛtyādīnāṃ bhogahetupariṇāmeṣu kāraṇam| bhokturadhiṣṭhānād bhogāyatananirmāṇamiti vakṣyamāṇasūtrāt | saṃyogaśca bhede satyeva bhavatīti bhāvaḥ| iti śabdo hetusamāptau || 142 ||
uktānumāne'nukūlatarkaṃ pradarśayati sūtrābhyām -- 
bhoktṛbhāvāt || 143 ||
yadi hi śarīrādisvarūpa eva bhoktā syāt tadā bhoktṛtvameva vyāhanyeta, karmakartṛvirodhāt | svasya sākṣāt svabhoktṛtvānupapatterityarthaḥ| anupapattiśca pūrvameva vyākhyātā| atra sūtre puruṣasya bhogaḥ svīkṛta iti smartavyam| apariṇāminaśca purapuṣasya bhogaḥ cidavasāno bhoga ityatra vyākhyātaḥ || 143 ||
kiñca -- 
kaivalyārthaṃ pravṛtteśca || 144 ||
śarīrādikameva cedbhoktṛ syāt bhoktuḥ kaivalyārthaṃ duḥkhātyantocchedārthaṃ kasyāpi pravṛttirnopapadyeta| śarīrādīnāṃ vināśitvāt prakṛteśca dharmigrāhakamānena duḥkhasvābhāvyasiddhyā kaivalyā'sambhavāt| na hi svabhāvasyātyantocchedo ghaṭata ityarthaḥ| atra kaivalyārthaṃ prakṛteriti sūtrapāṭhaḥ prāmādikatvādupekṣaṇīyaḥ||
saṅghātaparārthatvāt triguṇādiviparyayādadhiṣṭhānāt |
puruṣo'sti bhoktṛbhāvāt kaivalyārthaṃ pravṛtteśca||
iti kārikātaḥ kaivalyārthaṃ pravṛtteśceti pāṭhāt, arthāsaṅgateśceti|| 144 ||
caturviṃśatitattvātiriktatayā puruṣaḥ sādhitaḥ idānīṃ puruṣagato viśeṣo vivekasphuṭīkaraṇāyānumīyate -- 
jaḍaprakāṣāyogāt prakāśaḥ || 145 ||
vaiśeṣikā āhuḥ -- prāgaprakāśarūpasya jaḍasyātmano manaḥsaṃyogāḍjñānākhyaḥ prakāśo jāyata -- iti | tanna loke jaḍasyāprakāśasya loṣṭādeḥ prakāśotpattyadarśanena tadayogāt | ata sūryādivat prakāśasvarūpa eva puruṣa ityarthaḥ| tathā ca smṛtiḥ -- 
yathā prakāśatamasoḥ sambandho nopapadyate|
tadvadaikyaṃ na sambaddhaṃ prapañcaparamātmanoḥ|| iti ||
yathā dīpaḥ prakāśātmā hnasvo vā yadi vā mahān|
jñānātmānaṃ tathā vidyāt puruṣaṃ sarvajantuṣu || iti ca ||
prakāśatvaṃ ca tejaḥsattvacaitanyeṣvanugatamakhaṇḍopādhiranugatavyavahārāditi|| 145 ||
nanu prakāśa,svarūpatve'pi tejovaddharmadharmibhāvo'sti, na vā ?tatrāha -- 
nirguṇatvānna ciddharmā || 146 ||
sugamam|| puruṣasya prakāśarūpatve siddhe tatsambandhamātreṇānyavyavahāropapattau prakāśātmakadharmakalpanāgauravīmityapi boddhavyam| tejasaśca prakāśākhyarūpaviśeṣāgrahe'pi sparśapuraskāreṇa grahāt prakāśatejasorbhedaḥ siddhyati| ātmanastu jñānākhyaprakāśāgrahakāle grahaṇaṃ nāstītyato lāghavāddharmadharmibhāvaśūnyaṃ prakāśarūpamevā''tmadravyaṃ kalpyate | tasya ca na guṇatvam saṃyogādimattvāt, anāśritatvācceti | athāca smaryyate -- 
jñānaṃ naivātmano dharmo guṇo vā kathañcana|
jñānasvarūpa evātmā nityaḥ pūrṇaḥ sadā śivaḥ|| iti ||
nanu nirguṇatve eva kā yuktiriti cet| ucyate| puruṣasyecchādyāstāvinnityā na sambhavanti, janyatāpratyakṣāt| janyaguṇāṅgīkāre pariṇāmitvāpattiḥ| tathā cobhayoreva prakṛtipuruṣayoḥ pariṇāmahetutvakalpane goravam| āndhyapariṇāmena kadācidajñatvasyāpattyā jñānecchādigocarasaṃśayāpattiśca | tathā jaḍaprakāśāyogasyoktatvādapi na nityasyānityajñāna sambhava iti | icchādikamanvayavyatirekābhyāṃ manasyeva lāghavāt siddhyati|manaḥsaṃyogasyātmanaścobhayostaddhetutvaṃ goravāt| guṇaśabdaśca viśeṣaguṇavācītyuktameva | ata ātmā nirguṇaḥ| api ca ye tārkikā ātmanaḥ kartṛtvamicchanti teṣāṃ mokṣānupapattiḥ, ahaṃ karteti buddhereva gītādiṣvadṛṣṭotpattihetutayoktatvāt | tasyāśca tanmate mitthājñānatvābhāvena tattvajñānanivartyatvāsambhavāt ataḥ śrutyuktamokṣānupapattyātmano'katṛtvamasmābhiriṣyate| akartṛtvāccādṛṣṭasukhādyabhāvaḥ| tataśca manasaḥ kṛtyādihetutve kalpanīye lāghavādantarduśya guṇatvāvacchedenaitat kalpyate| ata ātmā nirguṇa iti | yathoktasya ca paramasūkṣmasyātmanaḥ svarūpaṃ vāśiṣṭhe karāmalakavat proktaṃ vivicya pratipāditam| yathā -- 
asambhavati sarvatra digbhūmyākāśarūpiṇi|
prakāśye yādṛśaṃ rūpaṃ prakāśasyāmalaṃ bhavet||
trijagat tvamahaṃ ceti dṛśye sattāmupāgate |
draṣṭuḥ syāt kevalābhāvastādṛśo vimalātmanaḥ|| iti || 146 ||
nanvahaṃ jānāmiti dharmadharmibhāvānubhavāt puruṣasya ciddharmakatvaṃ siddhyati; goravasya prāmāṇikatvenā'doṣatvāditi | tatrāha -- 
śrutyā siddhasya nāpalāpastatpratyakṣabādhāt|| 147 ||
bhavadevaṃ yadi kevalatarkeṇāsmābhirnirguṇatvācciddharmatvādikaṃ prasādhyate, kintu śrutyāpi| ataḥ śrutyā siddhasya nirguṇatvādernāpalāpaḥ sambhavati, tatpratyakṣasya guṇādipratyakṣasya śrutyaiva bādhāt | ahaṃ gaura ityādipratyakṣavadityarthaḥ| anyathā hi gore'hamiti pratyakṣabalena dehātiriktātmasādhikā api yuktayo bādhitāḥ syuriti jitaṃ nāstikaiḥ | nirguṇatve ca śrutayaḥ -- "sākṣī cetā kevalo nirguṇaśca" ityādyā| cinmātratve tu śrutayaḥ -- "akartā caitanyaṃ cinmātraṃ saccidekaraso hyayamātmā" ityādyā iti | sarvajñatvādiśrutayastu rāhoḥ śira itivallaukikavikalpānuvādamātrāḥ. vidhiniṣedhaśratimadhye niṣedhajhaśratareva balavattvāt -- "athāta ā deśo neti neti na hyetasmāditi netyanyat paramasti" iti śruteḥ| kiñcājñānāmahaṃ jānāmīti pratyaye pramātvasyaivautsargikatvāt| ato bhramaśatāntaḥpātitvenāprāmāṇyaśaṅkāskanditatvāccaitatpratyakṣabādhane lāghavatarkādyanugṛhītamanumānamapi samarthamiti ||
nanvātmano nityajñānasvarūpatve kīdṛśaṃ lāghavamiti cet | ucyate| naiyāyikādibhirantaḥkaraṇaṃ vyavasāyānuvyavasāyau tadāśrayaśceti catvāraḥ padārthāḥ kalpyante| asmābhistvantaḥkaraṇaṃ vyavasāyasthānīyā ca tadvṛttiranantānuvyasāyasthānīyaśca nityaikajñānarūpa ātmeti trayaḥ padārthāḥ kalpyanta iti || 147 ||
nanu yadi yadi prakāśarūpa evātmā tadā suṣuptyādyavasthābedo nopapadyate, sadā prakāśānapāyāditi | tatrāha -- 
suṣuptyādyasākṣitvam|| 148 ||
suṣuptyādyasyāvasthātrayasya buddhiniṣṭhasya sākṣitvameva puṃsītyarthaḥ|
taduktam -- 
jāgrata svapnaḥ suṣuptaṃ ca guṇato buddhivṛttayaḥ|
tāsāṃ vilakṣaṇo jīvaḥ sākṣitvena vyavasthitaḥ || iti ||
tāsāṃ buddivṛttīnāṃ sākṣitvena tadvilakṣaṇo jāgradādyavasthārahito nirṇīta ityarthaḥ| tatra jāgrannāmāvasthendriyadvārā buddherviṣayākāraḥ pariṇāmaḥ| svapnāvasthā ca saṃskāramātrajanyastādṛṣaḥ pariṇāmaḥ| suṣuptyavasthā ca dvividhārdadhasamagralayabhedena | tatrārddhalaye viṣayākārā vṛttirna bhavati -- kintu svagarasukhaduḥkhamohākāraiva buddhivṛttirbhavati | anyathotthitasya sukhamahasvāpsamityādirūpasuṣuptikālūnasukhādismaraṇānupapatteḥ| taduktaṃ vyāsasūtreṇa -- 'mugdhe'rdadhasampattiḥ pariśeṣāt" iti | samagralaye tu buddhervṛttisāmānyābhāvo maraṇādāviva bhavati | anyathā samādhisuṣuptimokṣeṣu brahmarūpatetyāgāmisūtrānupapatteriti | sā ca samagrasuṣuptivṛttyabhāvarūpeti puruṣastasākṣī na bhavati, puruṣasya vṛttimātrasākṣitvāt| anyathā saṃskārāderapi buddhidharmasya sākṣibhāsyatāpatteḥ| suṣuptyādisākṣitvaṃ tu tādṛśabuddhivṛttīnāṃ svapratibimbitānāṃ prakāśanamiti vakṣyāmaḥ | ato jñānārthama puruṣasya na pariṇāmāpekṣeti |
syādetat | suṣupte yadi sukhaduḥkhādigocarā buddhivṛttiriṣyate, tarhi jāgradādāvapyakhilavṛttīnāṃ vṛttigrāhyatvasvīkāra eva yukta iti vyarthā tatsākṣipuruṣakalpanā | svagocaravṛttitvenaiva svavyavahārahetutāyāḥ sāmānyataḥ suvacatvāditi|
maiṣam| niyamena svagocaravṛttikalpate'navasthāpattirgauravaṃ ca syāt| kiñcāhaṃ sukhītyādivṛttiṣu sukhādīnāṃ viśeṣaṇatayānirvikalpakaṃ tajjñānamādāvapekṣyate | tatra cānantanirvikalpavṛttyapekṣayā lāghavena nityamekamevātmasvarūpaṃ jñānaṃ kalpate|ahaṃ sukhītyādiviśiṣṭajñānārthaṃ buddhivṛttereva tādṛśākāratvaṃ puruṣe vṛttisārūpyamātrasvīkāreṇa vṛttyākārātiriktākārānabhyupagamāt svatantrākāreṇa pariṇāmīpatteriti || 148 ||
athaivaṃ puruṣasya suṣuptyādisākṣimātratvena puruṣaikyasyāpyupapattau sa kimeko'neko veti saṃśayaḥ| tatrāyāṃ pūrvapakṣaḥ| lāghavatarkasahakāreṇa bavalatībhyo'bhedaśrutibhya eka evātmā siddhyati | jāgradādyavasthārūpāṇāṃ vaidharmyāṇāṃ buddhidharmatvāt | yadyapyekasyātmanaḥ sarvabuddhisākṣitvaṃ , tathā'pi yasyā buddheryā vṛttiḥ saiva buddhistadvṛttiviśiṣṭatayā sākṣiṇaṃ gṛhṇāti, ghaṭaṃ jānāmītyādirūpaiḥ| ata ekasyā buddherayaṃ ghaṭa iti vṛttai satyāmanyabuddhivṛttidvārā nānubhavo, ghaṭamahaṃ jānāmīti||
tatra siddhāntamāha -- 
janmādivyavasthātaḥ puruṣabahutvam|| 149 ||
"puṇyavān svarge jāyate, pāpī narake, ajño badhyate jñānī mucyate" ityādeḥ śrutismṛtivyavasthāyā vibhāgasyānyathānupapattyā puruṣā bahava ityarthaḥ| janmamaraṇe cātra notpattivināśau, puruṣaniṣṭhatvābhāvāt | kintvapūrvadehendriyādisaṅghātaviśeṣeṇa saṃyogaśca viyogaśca bhogatadabhāvaniyāmakāviti| janmādivyavasthāyāṃ ca śrutiḥ -- 
ajāmekāṃ lohitaśuklakṛṣṇāṃ bahvīḥ prajāḥ sṛjamānāṃ sarūpāḥ|
ajo hyeko juṣamāṇo'nuśete | jahātyenāṃ bhuktabhogāmajo'nyaḥ||
ye tadviduramṛtāste bhavantyathetare duḥkhamevāpiyanti, ityādiriti || 149 ||
nanu puruṣaikye'pyupādhirūpāvacchedakabhedena janmādivyavasthā bhavet, tatrāha -- 
upādhibhede'pyekasya nānāyoga ākāśasyeva
ghaṭādibhiḥ|| 150 ||
upādhibhede'pyekasyaiva puruṣasya nānopādhiyogo'styeva | yathaikasyaivākāśasya ghaṭakuḍyādinānāyogaḥ| ato'vacchedakabhedenaikasyātmana eva vividhajanmamaraṇādyāpattiḥ kāyavyūhādāviveti na sambhavati vyavasthā, ekaḥ puruṣe jāyate nāpara ityādirityarthaḥ| na hyavacchedakabhedena kapisaṃyogatadabhāvavatyekasminneva vṛkṣe vyavasthā ghaṭate | eko vṛkṣaḥ kapisaṃyogī, anyaśca neti | kiṃmacaikopādhito muktasyāpyātmapradeśasyopādhyantaraiḥ punarbandhāpattyā bandhamokṣā'vyavasthā tadavasthaiva | yathaikaghaṭamuktasyākāśapradeśasyānyaghaṭayogād ghaṭākāśavyavasthā tadviditi| na ca bandhamokṣa vyavasthāśrutirapi laukikabhramānuvādamātramiti vācyam| mokṣasyā'laukikatvāt| mithyāpuruṣārthapratipādanena śruteḥ pratāraktavādyāpatteśca|| 150 ||
nanu caitanyaikye'pi tattadupādhiviśiṣṭasyātiriktatāmabhyupagamya vyavasthopapādanīyā, tatrāha -- 
upādhirbhidyate na tu tadvān|| 151 ||
upādhireva nānā, na tu tadvānupādhiviśiṣṭo'pi nānābhyupeyo viśiṣṭasyātiriktatve nānātmatāyā eva śāstrāntare'pyabhyupagamāpatterityarthaḥ| bandhabhāgino viśiṣṭatve viśeṣaṇaviyogena viśiṣṭanāśānna mokṣopapattirityādīnyapi dūṣaṇāni | nanu viśiṣṭasyā jīvatvamanvayavyatirekāditi ṣaṣṭādhyāye svayamevāhaṅkāraviśiṣṭasyaiva jīvatvaṃ vakṣyatīti cenna| prāṇadhārakatvarūpajīvatvasyaiva viśiṣṭādheyatvavacanāt | na tu bandhamokṣavyavasthāyā viśiṣṭāśritatvaṃ , mokṣakāle viśiṣṭāsattvāditi||
yadapi kecinnavīnā vedāntibruvā āhuḥ| ekasyaivātmanaḥ kāryakāraṇopādhiṣupratibimbāni jīveśvarāḥ, pratibimbānāṃ cānyo'nyaṃ bhedājjanmādyakhilavyavasthopapattiriti|
tadapyasat | bhedābhedavikalpāsahatvāt| bimbapratibimbayorbhede pratibimbasyā'cetanatayā bhoktṛtvabandhamokṣādyanupapattiḥ| jīvabrahmābhedarūpatatsiddhāntakṣatiśca | jīveśvarabhinnasyātmano'prāmāṇikatvaṃ ca | abhede tu sāṅkaryāparihāraḥ| bhedābhedābhyupagame tu tatsiddāntahāniḥ, bhedābhedavirodhaśca | asmanmate tvabhedo'vibhāgalakṣaṇo bhedaścānyonyābhāva ityavirodha iti | avacchedapratibimbādidṛṣṭāntavākyāni tvagre vyākhyāsyāmaḥ||
syādetat binbapratibimbādibhedaṃ parikalpya śrutyā bandhamokṣavyavasthā kalpitetyevāsmābhirūcyate, na tu paramārthato bimbapratibimbabhāvastayorbhedo bandhamokṣādikaṃ ceṣyata iti |
maivam| evaṃ sati bandhamokṣādiśrutigaṇasya bhedaśrutigaṇasya cobhayorbādhāpekṣayā
kevalābhedaśrutigaṇasyaivāvibhāgaparataiva saṅkoco lāghavādyuktaḥ| śruti smṛtyantarairavibhāgasya siddhatvācceti|| 151 ||
ātmekyavādiṣūktaṃ dūṣaṇamupasaṃharati -- 
evamekatvena parivarttamānasya na viruddhadharmādhyāsaḥ|| 152 ||
evaṃ rītyaikatvena sarvato vartamānasyā'tmano janmamaraṇādirūpaviruddhadharmaprasaṅgo na yukta ityarthaḥ| yadvaikatva iti chedaḥ| ekatve'bhyupagamyamāne paritaḥ sarvato vartamānasya sarvopādhiṣvanugatasya viruddhadharmādhyāso neti, na kintu sarvathā viruddhadharmasaṅkaro'parihāya ityarthaḥ| nanu puruṣo nirdharmakaḥ, tatra kathaṃ janmamaraṇabandhamokṣādivirūddhadharmasāṅkaryamāpadyate, bhavadbhirapi sarveṣāṃ dharmāṇāmupādhiniṣṭhatvā'bhyupagamāditi cenna| uktadharmāṇāṃ saṃyogaviyogābhogarūpatayā puruṣe svīkārāt | pariṇāmarūpadharmāṇāmeva puruṣe pratiṣaidhasyoktatvāditi| yathā sphaṭike|u lāhityanīlimādidharmāṇāmāropitānāmapi vyavasthāsti tathā puruṣeṣvapi buddhidharmāṇāṃ sukhaduḥkhādīnāṃ śarīrādidharmāṇāṃ ca brahyaṇyakṣatrtriyatvādīnāmāropitānāmapi vyavasthā'sti śāstreṣu| yathā viṣṇupurāṇe -- 
yathaikasmin ghaṭākāśe rajodhūmādibhirvṛte|
naca sarve prayujyanta evaṃ jīvāḥ sukhādibhiḥ || iti || 152 ||
sā'pi vyavasthaikātmye sati janmādivyavasthāvadeva nopapadyata ityāha -- 
anyadharmatve'pi nāropāt tātsiddhirekatvāt || 153 ||
anyadharmatve'pi dharmāṇāṃ sukhādīnāmāropāt puruṣe vyavasthā na siddhyati | āropādhiṣṭhānapuruṣasyaikatvādityarthaḥ| ākāśasyaikatve'pi ghaṭāvacchinnākāśānāṃ ghaṭabhedena bhinnatayopādhikadharmavyavasthā ghaṭate | ātmatvajīvatvādikantu nopādhyavacchinnasya, upādhiviyoge ghaṭākāśanāśavat tannāśena jīvo na mriyata ityādiśrutivirodhaprasaṅgāt | kintu kaivalacaitanyasyeti prāgevoktam| imāṃ bandhamokṣādivyavasthā'nupapattiṃ sūkṣmāmabuddhvaivādhunikā vedāntibruvā upādhibhedena bandhamokṣavyavasthāmaivātmye'pyāhuḥ|te'pyetena nirastāḥ| ye'pi tadekadeśina imāmevānupapattiṃ paśyanta upādhigatacitpratibimbānāmeva bandhādīnyāhuḥ te tvatīva bhrāntāḥ, uktādbhedādivikalpāsahatvādidoṣāt, antaḥkaraṇasya tadujjvalitatvādityatroktadoṣācca| kiñca vedāntasūtre kvā'pi sarvātmanāmatyantaikyaṃ noktamasti| pratyuta "bhedavyapadeśāccānyaḥ adhikastu bhedanirdeśāt, aṃśo nānāvyapadeśā"disūtrairbheda uktaḥ| ata ādhunikānāmavaddhedapratibimbadivādā apasiddhāntā eva| svasaśāstrā'nuktasandigdhārtheṣu yamānatantrasiddhāntasyaitra siddhāntatvāccetyādikaṃ brahmamīmāṃsābhāṣyepratipāditamasmābhiḥ
|| 153 ||
nanvevaṃ puruṣanānātve sati -- 
eka eva hi bhūtātmā bhūte bhūte vyavasthitaḥ|
ekadhā bahudhā caiva dṛśyate jalacandravat||
nityaḥ sarvagato hyātpnā kūṭastho doṣavarjitaḥ|
ekaḥ sa bhidyate śaktyā māyayā na svabhāvataḥ||
ityādyāḥ śrutismṛtaya ātmaikatvapratipādikā nopapadyanta iti , tatrāha -- 
nādvaitaśrapativirodho jātiparatvāt|| 154 ||
ātmaikyaśrutīnāṃ virodhastu nāsti, tāsāṃ jātiparatvāt| jātiḥ sāmānyamekarūpatvaṃ , tatraivādvaitaśrutīnāṃ tātparyāt, na tvakhaṇḍatve | prayojanābhāvādityarthaḥ| jātiśabdāsya caikarūpatārthakatvamuttarasūtrāllabhyate| yathāśrutajātiśabdasyādare -- "ātmā vā idameva evāgra āsīt, sadeva saumyedamagra āsīt, ekamevādvitīyam, ityādyadvaitaśrutyapapādakatayaiva sūtraṃ vyākhyeyam| jātiparatvāt -- vijātīyadvaitaniṣedhaparatvādityarthaḥ| tatrādyavyākhyāyām ayaṃ
bhāvaḥmaikyaśrutiṣvekādiśabdāścidekarūpatāmātraparāḥ bhedādiśabdāśca vaidharmyalakṣaṇabhedaparāḥ||
eka evātmā mantavyo jāgratsvapnasuṣuptiṣu |
sthānatrayavyatītasya punarjanma na vidyate||
ityādivākyeṣvekarūpārthatvāvaśyakatvāt| anyathāvasthātraya'pyātmana ekatāmātrajñānena sthānatrayavyatītaśabdoktāyā avasthātrayābhimānanivṛtterambhavāt | tathaikarūpatāpratipādanenaiva nikhilopādhivivekena sarvātmanāṃ brahmaṇāpi śabdena sākṣātpratipādayituṃ śakyate , śabdānāṃ sāmānyamātragocāratvāt| ābrahmastambarpanteṣvātmana ekarūpatve tu pratipādite tadupapattyarthaṃ śiṣyaḥ svayameva tāvadvivecayati yāvannirviśeṣe śabdagocaresvarūpe paryavasyatīti | tataśca niḥśeṣā'bimānanivṛttyā kṛtakṛtyo bhavati| yadi punaradvaitavākyānyakhaṇḍatāmātraparāṇi syastarhi tebhyo nābhimānanivṛttiḥ sambhavati . ākāśe vividhaśabdavadakhaṇḍe'pyātmani sukhaduḥkhatadabhāvādīnāmavacchedakabhedairupapatteḥ| ekasyaiva vākyasyākhaṇḍatvāvaidharmyobhayaparatve ca vākyabhedo'khaṇḍatāparakalpanāyāṃ phalābhāvaśca, avaidharmyajñānādeva sarvābhijñānadeva sarvābhimānanivṛtteḥ|ato'dvaitavākyāni nākhaṇḍatāparāṇi, nyāyānugraheṇa balavatībhirbhedagrāhakaśratismṛtibhirvirodhācca| kintvavaidharmyalakṣaṇābhedaparāṇyeva | sāmyabodhakaśratibhirekavākyatvāt|
" sāmānyāttu " iti brahmasūtrapācceti | tatra sāmye śrutayaḥ -- "yathodakaṃ śuddhe śuddhamāsiktaṃ tādṛgeva bhavati | e munervijānata ātmā bhavati gotama !, nirañjanaḥ paramaṃ sāmyamupaitī" tyādyāḥ|
samṛtayaśca -- 
jyātirātmani nānyatra sarvabhūteṣu tat samam|
svayaṃ ca śakyate draṣṭuṃ susamāhitacetasā||
yāvanātmani bodhātmā tāvānātmā parātmani||
ya evaṃ veda veda janastho'pi muhyati|| ityādyāḥ||
uktaśrutau mokṣadaśāyāmapi bhedaghaṭitasāmyavacanāt svarūpabhedā'pyātmanāstīti siddham| avaidharmyābhedaparatvaṃ cāsmanmate "viṣṇurahaṃ śivo'haṃ mityādivākyānāṃ mantavyam, na tu "tattvamasyahaṃ brahmāsmī" tyādivākyānāmapi | tatra sāṅkhyamate pralayakālīnasya pūrṇātmana eva tadādipadārthatayā nityaśuddhamukatastvamasītyādiyathāśrutasya tādṛśavākyārthatvāt| yadi tu sargādyutpannapuruṣo nārāyaṇā''kya eva tatpadārthastadātattvamasītyādivākyānāmapyavaidharmyārthakataivāstu | nanu prayojanābhāvānna bhedaparatvaṃ śrutīnāṃ sambhavatīti cenna| mokṣopapādanaśyaiva prayojanatvāt| sṛṣṭisaṃhārayoḥ pravāharūpeṇānucchedāt tasyaikye mokṣānupapatteḥ. athavamātmabhedasya lokasiddhitayā na tatparatvaṃ śrutīnāṃ ghaṭata iti | maivam| lāghavatarkeṇākāśavadātmanyekatvasyānumānataḥ prasaktasya śrutyādibhirniṣedhāt| svaparacaitanyayorbhedasya cāpratyakṣatvāt, dehādiṣvevānubhavāt| "ya etasminnudaramantaraṃ kurute'tha tasya bhayaṃ bhavatī" tyādibhedanindā tu vaidharmyavibhāgānyataralakṣaṇabedapareti | nanvevaṃ muktānāṃ pratibimbāvacchedaśrutīnāṃ kā gātariti ceducyate| anekatejomayādityamaṇḍalavat anekātmamayamapi cidādityamaṇḍalamekarasamavibhaktamekapiṇḍīkṛtya tasya kiraṇavat svāṃśabhūtairasaṅkhyapuruṣairasaṅkhyopādhiṣvasaṃkhyavibhāga eva pratibimbādidṛṣṭāntaiḥ pratipādyate vibhāgalakṣaṇānyatvasya vācārambhaṇāmātratvaṃ bodhayituṃ , na punarakhaṇḍatvam|
vāyuryathaiko bhuvanaṃ praviṣṭo rūpaṃ rūpaṃ pratirūpo vabhūva|
ityādisāṃśadṛṣcāntaśrutīnāṃ nyāyānugraheṇa balavatvāditi| yatā ca smaryate -- 
yasya sarvātmakatve'pi khaṇḍyate naikapiṇḍatā|| iti ||
brahmamīmāṃsāyāṃ tu nityābhivyakte parameśvaracaitanye'nyeṣāṃ layarūpāvibhāgenāpyadvaitamuktam, avibhāgo vacanāditi sūtreṇeti | adhikaṃ tu brahmamīmāṃsābhāṣye proktamasmābhiriti dik||
sūtrasya dvitīyavyākhyāyāṃ tvayaṃ bhāvaḥ| pralayakāle puruṣavijātīyaṃ sarvamevāsat , arthākiyākāritvābhāvāt | puruṣāṇāṃ kūṭasthatvenārthakriyaivāprasiddheti | ataḥ sargakāla iva pralaye'pi sattvam| atastadātmanāṃ vijātīyadvaitarāhityam| tathā sargakāle'pi kūṭasthatvarūpapāramārthikasattvenānyanneti vijātīyadvaitarāhityāt sargākālīnādvaitaśrutayo'pyupapannā iti || 154 ||
nanvātmana ekatvavadekarūpatvamapi nānārūpatāpratyakṣeṇa viruddham, tat kathamuktaṃ jātiparatvāditi, tatrāha -- 
viditabandhakāraṇasya dṛṣṭyā'tadrūpam||
viditaṃ spaṣṭaṃ bandhakāraṇamaviveko yatra dṛṣṭyaiva puruṣeṣvadrapaṃ rūpabheda ityarthaḥ| ato bhrāntadṛṣ‌ṭyā na rūpabhedasiddhiriti|| 155 ||
nanu tathāpyanupalambhādekarūpatvābhāvaḥ setsyati -- tatrāha -- 
nāndhādṛṣṭyā cakṣuṣmatāmanupalambhaḥ|| 156 ||
anupalambha evāsiddhaḥ| ajñairadarśane'pi jñānabhirekarūpatvasya darśanādityarthaḥ|| 156 ||
advaitaśrutyanupapattiṃ samādhāyākhaṇḍādvaite bādhakāntaramāha -- 
vāmadevādirmukto nādvaitam|| 157 ||
vāmadevādirmukto'sti tathāpīdānīṃ bandhaḥ svasminnanubhavasiddhaḥ| ato nākhaṇḍādvaitamityarthaḥ|
sa cāpi jātismaraṇāptabodhastatraiva janmanyapavargamāpa |
ityādivākyaśatavirodhaśceti śeṣaḥ| na caivaṃ bandhamokṣāvupādherevetyavagantavyam, śrutismṛtisiddhāntavirodhāt | duḥkhaṃ mā bhuñjīyeti kāmanādarśanena puruṣamokṣākhyaparamapuruṣārthatvācca| upādhairduḥkhahānasya ca tādarthyaiva puruṣārthatvāt putrādivaditi||
yadapyādhunikairmāyāvādibhirucyate -- advaitaśrutivirodhād bandhamokṣasṛṣṭisaṃhārādiśrutayo bādhyanta iti |
tadapyasat| mokṣākhyaphalasyāpi śravaṇakāla evābhāvaniścaye śravaṇottaraṃ mananādi vidherananuṣṭhānalakṣaṇāprāmāṇyaprasaṅgāt| prapañcāntargatasya vedāntasyāpyadvaitaśrutyā bādhe vedāntāvagate'pyadvaite punaḥ saṃśayāpatteśca| svāpnavākyasya jāgrati bādhe tadvākyārthe punaḥ saṃśayāvat| kiñca " mithyābuddhirnāstikate" tyanuśāsanāddharmādiṣu svāpavanmithyādṛṣṭayo bauddhaprabhodā eva sāṃvṛttikaśabdena prapañcasyāvidyakataśca tairabhyupagamāditi dik|| 157 ||
nanu vāmadevāderapi paramamokṣo na jāta ityabhyupeyam, tatrāha -- 
anādāvadyayāvadabhāvādbhaviṣyadapyevam|| 158 ||
anādau kāle'dyayāvaccenmokṣo na jātaḥ kasyāpi, tarhi bhaviṣyatkālo'pyevaṃ mokṣaśūnya eva syāt | samyaksādhanānuṣṭhānasyāviśeṣādityarthaḥ|| 158 ||
tatra prayogamāha -- 
idānīmiva sarvatra nātyantocchedaḥ|| 159 ||
sarvatra kāle bandhasyātyantocchedaḥ kasyāpi puṃso nāsti varttamānakālavadityanumānaṃ sambhavedityarthaḥ|| 159 ||
puruṣāṇāṃ yadekarūpatvamekatvapratipādakaśrutyarthāvadhāritaṃ , tat kiṃ mokṣakāle, kiṃ sarvadaivetyākāṅkṣāyāmāha -- 
vyāvṛttobhayarūpaḥ|| 160 ||
sa ca puruṣo vyāvṛttobhayarūpo, vyāvṛtto rupabhedo yasmāt tathetyarthaḥ| śrutismṛtinyāyebhyaḥ sadakarūpāsiddheriti śeṣaḥ| taduktam -- 
baharūpa ivābhāti māyayā bahurūpayā |
ramamāṇo guṇeṣvasyā mamāhamiti baddhyate|| iti ||
jagadākhyamahāsvapne svapnāt svapnāṃntaraṃ vrajat|
rūpaṃ tyajati no śāntaṃ brahmaṃ śāntatvabṛṃhitam|| iti ca || 160 ||
nanu sākṣitvasyānityatvāt puruṣārthānāṃ kathaṃ sadaikarūpatvaṃ, tatrāha -- 
sākṣātsambandhāt sākṣitvam|| 161||
puruṣasya yat sākṣitvamuktaṃ tat sākṣātsambandhamātrāt, na tu pariṇāmata ityarthaḥ| sākṣātsambandhena buddhimātrasākṣitāvagamyate, "sākṣāddraṣṭari saṃjñāyām" iti sākṣiśabdavyutpādanāt | sākṣāddraṣṭṛtvaṃ cāvyavadhānena draṣṭṛtvam| puruṣe ca sākṣātsambandhaḥ svabuddhivṛttereva bhavati ato buddhereva sākṣā puruṣo'nyeṣāṃ tu draṣṭṛmātramiti śāstrīyo vibhāgaḥ jñānaniyāmakaścārthākāratāsthānīyaḥ pratibimbarūpa eva sambandho, na tu saṃyogamātramatiprasaṅgādityasakṛdāveditam| viṣṇvādeḥ sarvasākṣitvaṃ tvindriyādivyavadhānābhāvamātreṇa gauṇam| akṣasambandhāt sākṣitvamiti pāṭhe tvakṣamatra buddhiḥ karaṇatvasāmānyāt | tasyā yathoktāt pratibimbarīpāt sambandhādityarthaḥ|| 161 ||
ubhayarūpatvābhāvasiddhyartha puruṣasyāparau viśeṣāvāha sūtrābhyām -- 
nityamuktatvam || 162 ||
sadaiva puruṣasya duḥkhākhyabandhaśūnyatvam| duḥkhāderbuddhipariṇāmatvādityarthaḥ| puruṣārthastu duḥkhābhoganivṛttirvetyuktameva || 162 ||
audāsīnyaṃ ceti || 163 ||
audāsīnyamakartṛtvam| tena cānye'pi niṣkāmatvādaya upalakṣaṇīyāḥ|"kāmaḥ saṅkalpo vicikitsā śraddhā'śraddhā dhṛtiradhṛtirdhīrhnīrbhirityetat sarvaṃ eveti" śruteḥ| itiśabdaḥ puruṣadharmaprātapādamasamāptau || 163 ||
nanvevaṃ prakṛtipuruṣayoranyonyaṃ vaidharmyeṇa viveke siddhe puruṣasya kartṛtvaṃ buddherapi ca jñātṛtvaṃ śrutismṛtyocyamānaṃ kathamupapadyeyātāṃ, tatrāha --
uparāgāt kartṛtvaṃ citsānnidhyāccitsānnidhyāt|| 164 ||
atra yathāyogyamanvayaḥ| puruṣasya yat kartṛtvaṃ tad buddhayuparāgāt | buddheśca yā cittā sā puruṣasānnidadhyāt | etadubhayaṃ na vāstavamityarthaḥ| yathā'gnyayasoḥparasparaṃ saṃyogaviśeṣāt parasparadharmavyavadāra aupadhiko, yathā vā jalasūryayoḥ saṃyogāt parasparadharmāropastathaiva buddhipuruṣayoriti bhāvaḥ| etacca kārikayā'pyuktam -- 
tasmāt tatsaṃyogādayetanaṃ cetanāvadiva liṅgam|
guṇakartṛtve ca tathā karteva bhāvatyudāsīnaḥ|| iti ||
citsānnidhyāditi dviḥpāṭho'dhyāyasamāptisūcanārthaḥ|| 164 ||
heyahāne tayorhetū iti vyūhā yathākramam||
catvāraḥ śāstramukhyārthā adhyāye'smin prapañcitāḥ|| 1 ||
saṅkṣiptasāṅkhyasūtrāṇāmarthasyātra prapañcanāt||
śāstraṃ yogevadevedaṃ sāṅkhyapravacanābhidham|| 2 ||
iti śrīvijñānācāryanirmite kāpilasāṅkhyapravacanasya
bhāṣye viṣayādhyāyaḥ prathamaḥ|| 1 ||
athadvitīyodhyāyaḥ|| 2 ||
śāstrasya viṣayo nirūpitaḥ | sāmprataṃ puruṣasyāpariṇāmitvopapādanāya prakṛtitaḥ sṛṣṭiprakriyāmativistareṇa dvitīyādhyāye vakṣyati | tatraiva pradhānakāryāṇāṃ svarūpaṃ vistarato vaktavyam, tebhyo'pi puruṣasyātisphuṭavivekāya | ata eva -- 
vikāraṃ prakṛtiṃ caiva puruṣaṃ ca sanātanam|
yo yathāvadvijānāti sa vitṛṣṇo vimucyate||
iti mokṣadharmādiṣu trayāṇāmeva jñeyatvavacanam| tatrādayacetanāyāḥ prakṛterniṣprayojanasraṣṭṭatve muktasyāpi bandhaprasaṅga ityāśayena jagatsarjane prayojanamāha -- 
vimuktamokṣārthaṃ svārthaṃ vā pradhānasya || 1 ||
kartṛtvamiti pūrvādhyāyaśeṣasūtrādanuṣajjyate svabhāvato duḥkhabandhādvimuktasya puruṣasya pratibimbarūpaduḥkhamokṣārthaṃ pratibimbasambandhena duḥkhamokṣārthaṃ vā pradhānasya jagatkartṛtvam| athavā svā'rtham| svasya pāramārthikaduḥkhamokṣārthamityarthaḥ| yadyapi mokṣavadbhogo'pi sṛṣṭeḥprayojanaṃ, tathāpi mukhyatvānmokṣa evoktaḥ|| 1 ||
nanu mokṣārthaṃ cet sṛṣṭistarhi sakṛt sṛṣṭyaiva mokṣasambhave punaḥ punaḥ sṛṣṭirna syāditi, tatrāha -- 
viraktasya tatsiddheḥ|| 2 ||
naikadā sṛṣṭermokṣaḥsa kintu bahuśo janmamaraṇavyādhyādivividhaduḥkhena bhṛśaṃ taptaśca prakṛtipuruṣayorvivekakhyātyotpanna paravairāgyasyaiva mokṣātrattisiddherityarthaḥ|| 2 ||
sakṛtsṛṣṭyā vairāgyāsiddhau hetumāha -- 
na śravaṇamātrāt tatsiddhiranādivāsanāyā
balavattvāt || 3 ||
śravaṇamapi bahujanmakṛtapuṇyena bhavati| tatrāpi śravaṇamātrānna vairāgyasiddhiḥ, kintu sākṣātkāraśca jhaṭiti na bhavati -- anādimithyāvāsanāyā balavattvāt, kintu yoganiṣṭhayā | yoge ca pratibandhabāhulyamityato bahujanmabhireva vairāgyaṃ mokṣaśca kadācit kasyacideva siddhyatītyarthaḥ|| 3 || sṛṣṭipravāhe hetvantaramāha -- 
bahubhṛtyavadvā pratyekam|| 4 ||
yathā gṛhasthānāṃ pratyekaṃ bahavo bhartavyā bhavanti strīputrādibhedena | evaṃ sattvādiguṇānāmapi pratyekamasaṅkhyapuruṣā vimocanīyā bhavanti| ataḥ kiyatpuruṣamokṣe'pi puruṣāntaramocanārthaṃ sṛṣṭipravāho ghaṭate, puruṣāṇāmānantyādityarthaḥ| tathā ca yogasūtram -- "kṛtārthaṃ naṣṭamapyanaṣṭhaṃ tadanyasādhāraṇatvāka" iti|| 4 ||
nanu prakṛtereva sraṣṭṛtvaṃ kathamucyate "etasmādātmana ākāśaḥ sambhata" iti śrutyā puruṣasyāpi sraṣṭṛtvasiddheriti , tatrāha -- 
prakṛtivāstave ca puruṣasyadhyāsasiddhiḥ|| 5 ||
prakṛtau sraṣṭṛtvasya vastutve ca siddhe puruṣasya sraṣṭṛtvādhyāsa eva śratiṣu siddhyati, upāsanāyāmeva śrutestātparyāt | " ajomekā"mityādiśrutyantareṇa prakṛteḥ sraṣṭṛtvasiddheḥ puṃsāṃ kūṭasthacinprātratābodhakaśrutyantaravirodhāccetyarthaḥ| ayaṃ cādhyāsa upacārarūpo loke siddha evāsti| yathā svaśaktiṣu yodheṣu vartamānau jayaparājayau rājanyupacaryete tathā svaśaktau prakṛtau vartamānaṃ sraṣṭṛtvādikaṃ śaktimatsu puruṣeṣūpacaryate , śaktiśaktimadabhedāt | taduktaṃ kaurme -- 
śaktiśaktimatorbhedaṃ paśyanti paramārthataḥ
abedaṃ cānupaśyanti yogimastattvacintakāḥ || iti ||
bhedamanyobhāvabhedaṃ cāvibhāgarūpaṃ prakṛtyāditattvopāsakāḥ paśyantītyarthaḥ| tayoścodāharaṇam -- " athāta ādeśo neti netī " tyādiśrutiḥ, "ātmaivedaṃ sarvami"tyādiśrutiśceti bhāvaḥ|| 5 ||
nanvevaṃ prakṛtāvapi sraṣṭṛtvaṃ vāstasamiti kuto'vadhuttam, sṛṣṭeḥ svapnāditulyatāyā api śravaṇāditi, tatrāha -- 
kāryatastatsiddheḥ|| 6 ||
kāryāṇāmarthakriyākāritayā vāstavatvena kāryata eva dharmigrāhakapramāṇena prakṛtervāstavasraṣṭṛtvasiddherityarthaḥ| svapnāditulyatāśrutayastvanityatārūpāsattvāṃśamātre puruṣādhyastatvāṃśe vā bodhyāḥ, anyathā sṛṣṭipratipādakaśrutivirodhāt | svapnapadārthānāmapi manaḥpariṇāmatvenātyantāsattāvirahācceti || 6 ||
nanu prakṛte svārthatvapakṣe muktapuruṣaṃ pratyapi sā pravartate, tatrāha -- 
cetanoddeśānniyamaḥ kaṇṭakamokṣavat || 7 ||
cetī saṃjñāna iti vyutpattyā cetano'trābhijñaḥ| yathaikameva kaṇṭakaṃ prati tu bhavatyeva | tathā prakṛtirapi cetanādabhijñāt kṛtārthādeva mucyate| taṃ pratyeva duḥkhātmikā na bhavati | anyānanabhijñāna prati tu duḥkhātmikā bhavatyeveti niyamo vyavasthetyarthaḥ| etena svabhāvato baddhāyā api prakṛteḥ svamokṣo ghaṭata ityato na muktapuruṣaṃ prati pravartate|| 7 ||
nanu puruṣe sraṣṭṛtvamadhyastamātramiti yaduktaṃ, tanna yuktam| prakṛtisaṃyogena puruṣasyāpi mahadādipariṇāmaucityāt| dṛṣṭo hi pṛthivyādiyogena kāṣṭhādeḥ pṛthivyādisadṛśaḥ pariṇāma iti | tatrāha -- 
anyayoge'pi tatsiddhirnāñjasyenāyodāhavat || 8 ||
prakṛtiyoge'pi puruṣasya na sraṣṭṛtvasiddhirāñjasyena sākṣāt| tatra dṛṣṭānto'yodāhavat | yathā'yaso na dagdhṛtvaṃ sākṣādasti, kintu svasaṃyuktāgnidvārakamadhyastamevetyarthaḥ| uktadṛṣṭānte tūbhayoḥ pariṇāmaḥ pratyakṣasiddhatvādiṣyate | sandigdhasthale tvaikasyaiva pariṇaāmenopapattāvubhayoḥ pariṇāmakalpane gauravam| anyathā japāsaṃyogāt sphaṭikasya rāgapariṇāmāpatteriti|| 8 ||
sṛṣṭeḥ phalaṃ mokṣa iti prāguktam| idānīṃ sṛṣṭermukhyaṃ nimittakāraṇamāha -- 
rāgavirāgayoryogaḥ sṛṣṭiḥ|| 9 ||
rāge sṛṣṭirvairāgye ca yogaḥ svarūpe'vasthānam| muktiriti yāvat| athavā cittavṛttinirodha ityarthaḥ| tathā cānvayavyatirekābhyāṃ rāgaḥ sṛṣṭikāraṇamityāsayaḥ tathā ca srutirapi brahmādirūpāṃ vividhakarmagatimuktatvāha -- "iti tu kāmayamāno yo'kāmo na tasya prāṇā utkrāmanti"iti | rāgavairāgye api prakṛtidharmāveva || 9 ||
itaḥ paraṃ sṛṣṭiprakriyāṃ vaktumārabhate -- 
mahadādikrameṇa pañcabhūtānām|| 10 ||
sṛṣṭiriti pūrvasūtrādanuvarttate | yadyapyetasmādātmana ākāśaḥ sambhūta ityādiśrutāvādāveva pañcabhūtānāṃ sṛṣṭiḥ śrūyate, tathāpi mahadādikrameṇaiva pañcabhūtānāṃ sṛṣṭiriṣṭetyarthaḥ| tejādisṛṣṭiśrutau gaganavāyusṛṣṭerāpūraṇavaduktaśratāvapyādau mahadādisṛṣṭiḥ pūraṇīyeti bhāvaḥ| atra ca pramāṇaṃ ghaṭasṛṣṭivadantaḥkaraṇātiriktākhilasṛṣṭerantaḥkaraṇavṛttipūrvakatvānumānam|
kiñca -- 
etasmājjāyate prāṇo manaḥ sarvendriyāṇi ca |
khaṃ vāyurjyotirāpaśca pṛthvī viśvasya dhāriṇī||
iti śrutyantarasthapāṭhakamānurodhena "sa prāṇasṛjat prāṇacchraddhāṃ khaṃ vāyu" mityādi śrutyāntareṇa ca pañcabhūtasṛṣṭeḥ prāṅmahadādisṛṣṭiravadhāryata iti | prāṇaścāntaḥkaraṇasya vṛttibheda iti vakṣyati | ato'syāṃ śrutau prāṇa eva mahattattvamiti | tathā vedāntasūtramapi mahadādikrameṇaiva sṛṣṭiṃ vakti| 'antarā vijñānamanaso krameṇa talliṅgāt ' iti | sadākāśayormadhye buddhimanasī utpādye iti krameṇetyarthaḥ| manasi cā'haṅkārasya praveśa iti
|| 10 ||
prakṛtereva sraṣṭṛtvaṃ svamokṣārthaṃ , tasyā nityatvāt, mahadādīnāṃ tu svasvavikārasraṣṭṛtvaṃ na svamokṣārthamanityatvāditi, viśeṣamāha -- 
ātmārthatvāt sṛṣṭernaiṣāmātmārtha ārambhaḥ|| 11 ||
eṣāṃ mahadādīnāṃ sraṣṭṭatvasyātmārthatvāt puruṣamokṣārthatvānna svārtha ārambhaḥ| sraṣṭṭatvam vināśitvena mokṣāyogādityarthaḥ| paramokṣārthakatve cāvaśyake puruṣamokṣārthakatvameva yuktaṃ, na prakṛtimokṣārthakatvam| tasyāḥ puruṣaguṇatvāditi || 11 ||
khaṇḍadikkālayoḥ sṛṣṭimāha -- 
diktālāvākāśādibhyaḥ|| 12 ||
nityai yau dikkālau tāvakāśaprakṛtibhūtau prakṛterguṇaviśeṣāveva| ato diktālayorvibhutvopapattiḥ| "ākāvat sarvagataśca nitya" ityādiśrutyuktaṃ vibhutvaṃ cākāśasyopapannam| yau tu khaṇḍadikkālau tau tu tattadupādhisaṃyogādākāśādutpadyate ityarthaḥ| ādiśabdenopādhigrahaṇāditi| yadyapi tattadupādhiviśiṣṭākāśameva khaṇḍadikkālau. tathāpi viśiṣṭasyātiriktatābhyupagamavādena vaiśeṣikanaye śrotrasya kāryatāvat tatkāryatvamatroktam|| 12 ||
idānīṃ mahadādikrameṇetyuktān svarūpato dharmataśca krameṇa darśayati -- 
adhyavasāyo buddhiḥ|| 13 ||
mahattattvasya paryāyo buddhiriti | adhyavasāyaśca niścayākhyastasyā'sādhāraṇī vṛttirityarthaḥ| abhedanirdeśastu dharmadharmyabhedāt | astāśca buddhermahattvaṃ svetarasakalakāryavyāpakatvānmahaiśvarācca
mantavyam||
savikārāt pradhānāt mahattattvamajāyata|
mahāniti yataḥ khyātirlokānāṃ jāyate sadā,||
