iti smṛteḥ|| "asya mahato bhūtasya niḥśvasitametadyadṛgveda" ityādiśratismṛtiṣu ca hiraṇyagarbhe cetane'pi mahāniti śabdo buddhyabhimānitvenaiva | yathā pṛthivyabhimānicetane pṛthivīśabdastadvat| evameva rudrādiṣvahaṅkārādiśabdo'pi bodhyaḥ| prakṛtyabhimānidevatāmārabhya sarveṣāmeva bhūtābhimāniparyantānāṃ svasvabuddhirūpāśca pratiniyatopādhayo mahattattvasyaivāṃśā iti || 13 ||
mahattattvasyāparānapi dharmānāha -- 
tatkāryaṃ darmādi|| 14 ||
dharmajñānavairāgyaiśvaryāṇyapi buddhyupādānakāni nāhaṅkāradyupādānakāni | buddhereva niratiśayasattvakāryatvādityarthaḥ|| 14 ||
nanvevaṃ kathaṃ narapaśvādigatānāṃ buddhyaṃśānāmadharmaprābalyamupapadyatāṃ tatrāha -- 
mahaduparāgādviparītam|| 15 ||
tadeva mahanmahattattvaṃ rajastamobhyāmuparāgādviparītamadharmā'jñānāvairāgyānaiśvaryadharmakamapi bhavatītyarthaḥ| etena sarva eva puruṣā īśvarā iti śrutismṛtipravādo'pyupapāditaḥ sarvopādhīnāṃ svābhāvikaiśvaryasya rajastamobhyamevāvaraṇāditi| nanvevaṃ dharmādyavasthānā'rthaṃ buddherapi nityatvāt kathaṃ kāryateti cenna| prakṛtyaṃśarūpe bījāvasthamahattattve sattvaviśeṣe karmavāsanādīnāmavasthānāt tasyaiva jñānakāraṇāvasthāyāmaṅkuravadupapattyaṅgīkārāt| tathā cā''kāśavadeva nityānityobhayarūpā buddhiḥ| yathākāraṇaṃ svākāraḥ prakṛtiprabhāvāditi|| 15 ||
mahattattvaṃ lakṣayitvā tatkāryamahaṅkāraṃ lakṣayati -- 
abhimāno'haṅkāraḥ|| 16 ||
ahaṅkarotītyahaṅkāraḥ kumbhakāravat[karmaṇyam iti sūtreṇa cāṇapratyayaḥ| aṇṇantatvādviddhiḥ|]| antaḥkaraṇadravyam| sa ca dharmadharmyabhedādabhimāna ityukto'sādhāraṇavṛttitāsūcanāya | buddhyāniścita evārthe'haṅkāramamakārau jāyete| ato vṛttyoḥ kāryakāraṇabhāvānusāreṇa vṛttimatorapi kāryakāraṇabhāva unnīyate iti progevoktam| antaḥkaraṇamāpadyata iti ca prāgevoktam| ata eva vāyumātsyayoḥ -- 
mano mahān matirbrahmā pūrbuddhiḥkhyātirīśvaraḥ||
iti manobuddhyorekaparyāyatvamuktamiti|| 16 ||
kramāgatamahaṅkārasya kāryamāha -- 
ekādaśapañcatanmātraṃ yatkāryam|| 17 ||
ekādaśendriyāṇi śabdādipañcatanmātraṃ cāhaṅkārasya kāyamityarthaḥ| mayā'nena indriyeṇa idaṃ rūpādikaṃ bhoktavyabhidameva sukhasādhanamityādyabhimānadevādisargeṣvindriyatadviṣayotpattyā'haṅkāra indriyādihetuḥ| loke bhogābhimāninaiva rāgadvārā bhogopakaraṇakaraṇadarśanāt | 'rūparāgādabhūccakṣu'rityādinā mokṣadharme hiraṇyarbhasya rāgādeva samaṣṭhicakṣurādyotpattismaraṇācceti bhāvaḥ | ataśca bhūtendriyayormadhye rāgadharmakaṃ mana evādāvahaṅkārādutpadyata iti viśeṣastanmātrādīnāṃ rāgakāryatvāditi || 17 ||
tatrāpi viśeṣamāha -- 
sāttvikamekādaśakaṃ pravartate vaikṛtādahaṅkārāt || 18 ||
ekadaśānāṃ pūraṇamekādaśakaṃ manaḥ ṣoḍaśātmagaṇamadhye sāttavikam| atastadvaikṛtāt sāttvikāhaṅkārājjāyata ityarthaḥ| ataśca rājasāhaṅkārāddeśendriyāṇi, tāmasāhaṅkārācca tanmātrāṇītyadhigantavyam|
vaikārikastaijasaśca tāmasaścecyahaṃ tridhā|
ahaṃtattvādikurvāṇānmano vaikārikādabhūta||
vaikārikāśca ye devā arthābhivyañjanaṃ yataḥ|
taijasādindriyāṇyeva jñānakarmamayāni ca ||
tāmasāṃ bhūtasūkṣmādiryataḥ khaṃ liṅgamātmanaḥ||
ityādismṛtibhya eva nirṇayāt | ata eva purāṇādyanusāreṇa kārikāyāmapyetaduktam -- 
sāttvika ekādaśakaḥ pravartate vaikṛtādahaṅkārāt|
bhūtādestanmātraḥ sa tāmasastaijasādubhayam|| iti ||
taijaso -- rājasaḥ| ubhayaṃ jñānakarmendriye | nanu 'devatālayaśruti'rityāgāmisūtre karaṇānāṃ devān vakṣyati | tat kathaṃ kārikayāpi devānāṃ sāttvikāhaṅkārakāryatvaṃ noktamiti | ucyate | samaṣṭicakṣurādiśarīriṇaḥ sūryādicetanā eva cakṣurādidevatāḥ śrūyante| ataśca vyaṣṭisamaṣṭyorekatā''śayenātra śāstre devāḥ karaṇebhyo na pṛthaṅnirdiśyante | ataḥ samaṣṭīndriyāṇi mano'pekṣayālpasatttratvena rājasāhaṅkārakāryatvenaiva nirdiṣṭāni | smṛtiṣu ca vyaṣṭīndriyāpekṣayādhikasattvatvena sāttvikāhaṅkārakāryatayoktanītyavirodha ityavagantavyam | tadevamahaṅkārasya traividhyānmahato'pi tatkāraṇasya traividhyaṃ mantavyam||
sāttviko rājasaścaiva tāmasaśca tridhā mahān||
iti smaraṇāt | traividhyaṃ cānayorvyaktibhedādvetyanyadetat|| 18 ||
ekādaśendriyāṇi darśayati -- 
karmendriyabuddhīndriyairāntaramekādaśakam|| 19 ||
karmendriyāṇi -- vākpāṇipādapāyūpasthāni pañca, jñānendriyāṇi cacakṣuḥśrotratvagrasanāghrāmākhyāni pañca | etairdaśabhiḥ sahā''ntaraṃ mana ekā daśakamekādaśendriyamityarthaḥ| indrasya saṅghāteśvarasya karaṇamindriyam| tathā cā'haṅkārakāryatve sati karaṇatvamindriyatvamiti || 19 ||
indriyāṇāṃ bhātikatvamataṃ nirākaroti -- 
ahaṅkārikatvaśruterna bhotikāni|| 20 ||
indriyāṇīti śeṣaḥ| āhaṅkārikatve pramāṇabhūtā śrutiḥ rakālaluptā'pyācāryavākyānmanvādyakhilasmṛtibhyaścānumīyate[ācārāttu smṛtiṃ jñātvā , smṛteśca śrutikalpanam| kalpanamanumānaṃ iti pūrvamīmāṃsāyāṃ prathamādhyaye dvitīyapāde spaṣṭam|] | pratyakṣā śrutiḥ "ahaṃ bahu syā" mityādiḥ| nanu 'annamayaṃ hi saumya manaḥ' ityādirbhautikatve'pi śrutirastīti cenna| prakāśakatvasāmyenāntaḥkaraṇopādanatvasyaivocitatayā ''haṅkārikatvaśrutereva mukhyatvāt| bhūtānāmapi hiraṇyagarbha,ṅkalpajanyatayā'nnasya manojanyatvācca| vyaṣṭimanādīnāṃ bhūtasaṃsṛṣṭatayaiva ciṣṭatāṃ bhūtebhyo'bhivyaktimātreṇa tu bhautikaśrutirgauṇīti || 20 ||
nanu tathāpyāhaṅkārikatvanirṇayo na ghaṭate, 'asya puruṣasyā'gniṃ vāgapyeti vātaṃ prāṇaścakṣurādityam' ityādiśrutau devatāsvindriyāṇāṃ layakathanena devatopādānakatvasyāpyavagamāt kāraṇa eva hi kāryasya laya ityāśaṅkayāha -- 
devatālayaśrutirnārambhakasya || 21 ||
devatāsu yā layaśrutiḥ sā nārambhakasya, nārambhakaviṣayiṇītyarthaḥ| yathānārambhake'pi bhūtale jalabindorlayadarśanāt| anārambhakeṣvapi bhūteṣvātmano layaśravaṇācca| 'vijñānadhana evaitebhyo bhūtebhyaḥ samutthāya tānyevānuvinaśyatī'tyādiśrutāviti bhāvaḥ || 21 ||
indriyāntargataṃ mano nityamiti kecit, tat pariharati -- 
tadutpattiśrutervināśadarśanācca || 22 ||
teṣāṃ sarveṣāmevendriyāṇāmutpattirasti | 'etasmājjāyate prāṇo manaḥ sarvendriyāṇi ca' | ityādiśruteḥ vṛddhādyavasthāsu cakṣurādīnāmiva manaso'pyapacayādinā vināśanirṇayoccetyarthaḥ| tathā coktam, ' daśakena nivartante manaḥ sarvendriyāṇi ca "iti|| manaso nityatvavacanāni ca prakṛtyākhyabījaparāṇīti || 22 ||
golakajātamevendriyamiti nāstikamatamapākaroti -- 
atīndriyamindriyaṃ bhrāntānāmadhiṣṭhāne || 23 ||
indriyaṃ sarvamatīndriyaṃ, na tu pratyakṣam. bhrāntānāmeva tvadhiṣṭhāne golake tādātmyenendriyamityarthaḥ| adhiṣṭhānamityeva pāṭhaḥ|| 23 ||
ekamevendriyaṃ śaktibhedādvilakṣaṇakāryakārīti matamapākaroti -- 
śaktibhede'pi bhedasiddhau naikatvam|| 24 ||
ekasyaivendriyasya śaktibhedasvīkāre'pīndriyabhedaḥ siddhyati | śaktīnāmapīndriyatvāt| ato naikatvamindriyatvāt| ato naikatvamindriyasyetyarthaḥ|| 24 ||
nanvekasmādahaṅkārānnānāvidhendriyotpattikalpanāyāṃ nyāyavirodhastatrāha -- 
na kalpanāvirodhaḥpramāṇadṛṣṭasya|| 25 ||
sugamam|| 25 ||
ekasyaiva mukhyendriyasya manaso'nye daśa śaktibhedā ityāha -- 
ubhayātmakaṃ manaḥ|| 26 ||
jñānakarmendriyātmakaṃ mana ityartaḥ|| 26 ||
ubhayātmakamityasyārthaṃ svayaṃ vivṛṇoti -- 
guṇapariṇāmabhedānnānātvamavasthāvat|| 27 ||
yathaika eva naraḥ saṅgavaśānnānātvaṃ bhajate, kāminīsaṅgāt kāmuko viraktasaṅgādvirakto'nyasaṅgāccānyaḥ, evaṃ mano'pi cakṣurādisaṅgāccakṣurādyekībhāvena darśanādivṛttiviśiṣṭatayā nānā bhavati | tatra hetuḥ -- guṇotyādi | guṇānāṃ sattvādīnāṃ pariṇāmabhedeṣu sāmarthyādityarthaḥ| etacca 'anyatramanā abhūvaṃ, nāśrauṣa' mityādiśrutisiddhāccakṣurādīnāṃ manaḥsaṃyogaṃ vinā vyāpārākṣamatvādanumīyate || 27 ||
jñānakarmendriyayorviṣayamāha -- 
rūpādirasamalānta ubhayoḥ|| 28 ||
annarasānāṃ malaḥpurīṣādiḥ tathā rūparasagandhasparśaśabdā vaktavyādātavyagantavyānandayitavyotsraṣṭavyāścobhayorjñānakarmendriyayordaśa viṣayā ityarthaḥ| ānandayitavyaṃ copasthasyopasthāntaraṃ viṣaya iti || 28 ||
yasyendriyasya yenopakāreṇaitānīndriyāṇītyucyate, tadubhayamāha -- 
draṣṭṭacvādirātmanaḥ karaṇatmamindriyāṇām|| 29 ||
draṣṭṭatvādipañcakaṃ vaktṛtvādipañcakaṃ ,ṅkalpayitṛtvaṃ cātmanaḥpuruṣasya darśanādivṛttau karaṇatvaṃ tvindriyāṇāmityarthaḥ| nanu draṣṭṭatvaścotṛtvādikaṃ kadācidanubhave paryavasānāt puruṣasyā'vikāriṇo'pi ghaṭatām, vaktṛtvādikaṃ
kriyāmātram, tat kathaṃ kūṭasthasya ghaṭatāmiti cenna| ayaskāntavat sānnidhyamātreṇa darśanādivṛttikartṛtvasyaivātra draṣṭṭatvādiśabdārthatvāt | yathā hi mahārājaḥ svayamavyāpriyamāṇo'pi sainyena karaṇena yoddhā bhavatyajñāmātreṇa prerakatvāt, tathā kūṭastho'pi puruṣaścakṣurādyakhilakaraṇairdraṣṭā vaktā saṅkalpitā cetyevamādirbhavati | saṃyogākhyasānnidhyamātreṇaiva teṣāṃ prerakatvādayaskāntamaṇivaditi | kartṛtvaṃ cātra kārakuṭhārādivat | yattu śāstreṣu puruṣe darśanādikartṛtvaṃ niṣiddhyate tadanukūlakṛtimattvaṃ tattatkriāvattvaṃ vā| tathā coktam -- 
ata ātmani kartṛtvamakartṛtvaṃ ca saṃsthitam||
niricchatvādakartā'sau kartā sannidhimātrataḥ|| iti ||
ata eva kārakacakraprayoktṛtāśakterātmasvararūpatayā draṣṭṭatvavaktṛtvādikamātmano nityamiti śrūyate| 'na draṣṭṭardṛṣṭerviparilopo vidyate, na vakturvakterviparilopo vidyata' ityādineti | nanu pramāṇavibhāge pratyakṣādivṛttīnāmeva karaṇatvamuktamatra kathamindriyasyocyata iti cenna| atra darśanādirūpāsu cakṣurādidvārakabuddhivṛttiṣvevendriyāṇāṃ karaṇatvavacanāt| tatra puruṣaniṣṭe bodhākhyaphale vṛttīnāṃ karaṇatvasyoktatvāditi
|| 29 ||
idānīmantadhakaraṇatrayasyāsādhāraṇavṛttīrāha -- 
trayāṇāṃ svālakṣaṇyam|| 30 ||
trayāṇāṃ mahadahaṅkāramanasāṃ svālakṣaṇyaṃ svaṃ svaṃ lakṣaṇamasādhāraṇī vṛttiryeṣāmiti madhyamapadalopī vigrahaḥ| tasya bhāvastattvamityarthaḥ| loke ca mahato lakṣaṇamadhyavasāyādiprakṛṣṭaguṇavattacvam| ahaṅkṛtasya cātmanyavidyamānaguṇāropaḥ| manasaścedamastvityaṅgīkaraṇamiti| tathā ca buddhervṛttiradhyavasāyo'bhimāno'haṅkārasya saṅkalyavikalpau manasa ityāyātam| saṅkalpaścikīrṣā| 'saṅkalpaḥkarmamānasa'mityanuśāsanāt| vikalpaśca saṃśayoḥ yogoktabhramaviśeṣo vā, na tu viśiṣṭajñānam, tasya buddhivṛttityāditi || 30 ||
trayāṇāṃ sādhāraṇīṃ vṛttimapyāha -- 
sāmānyakaraṇavṛttiḥ prāṇādyā vāyavaḥ pañca || 31 ||
prāṇādirūpāḥ pañca vāyuvat sañcārāt vāyavo ye prasiddhāste sāmānyā sādhāraṇī karaṇatrayasya vṛttiḥ pariṇāmabhedā ityartaḥ| tadetat kārikayoktam -- 
svāsakṣaṇyaṃ vṛttistrayasya saiṣā bhavatyasāmānyā|
sāmānyakaraṇavṛttiḥ prahāṇādyā vāyavaḥ pañca|| iti ||
atra kaścit, prāṇādyā vāyuviśeṣā eva | te cāntaḥkaraṇavṛtyā jīvanayoniprayatnapayā vyāpriyanta iti kṛtvā prāṇādyāḥ karaṇavṛttirityabhedanirdeṣa ityāha| tanna | 'na vāyukriye pṛthagupadeśā'diti vedāntasūtreṇa prāṇasya vāyutvavāyupariṇāmatvayoḥ sphuṭaṃ pratiṣedhādatrāpi tadekavākyataucityāt |manodharmasya kāmādeḥ prāṇakṣobhakayā sāmānādhikaraṇyenaivaucityācca||
vāyuprāṇayoḥ pṛthagupadeśaśrutayastu -- 
etasmājjāyate prāṇo manaḥ sarvendriyāṇi ca |
khaṃ vāyurjyotirāpaśca pṛthvī viśvasya dhāriṇī||
ityādyā iti|| ata eva liṅgaśarīramadhye prāṇanāmagaṇane'pi na nyūnatā| buddhereva kriyāśaktyā sūtrātmaprāṇādināmakatvāditi | antaḥkaraṇapariṇāme'pi vāyutulyasañcāraviśeṣād vāyudevatā'dhiṣṭhitatvācca vāyuvyavahāropapattiriti|| 31 ||
vaiśeṣikāṇāmivāsmākaṃ nāyaṃ niyamo yadindriyavṛttiḥ kramaṇaiva bhavati, naikadetyāha -- 
kramaśo'kramaśaścendriyavṛttiḥ|| 32 ||
sugamam. jātisāṅkarasyāsmākamadoṣatvāt sāmagrīsamavadhāne sati anekairapīndriyairekadaikavṛttyutpādane bādhakaṃ nāstīti bhāvaḥ| indriyavṛttīnāṃ vibhāgaśca kārikayā vyākhyātaḥ -- 
śabdādiṣu pañcānāmālocanamātramiṣyate vṛttiḥ|
vacanādānaviharaṇotsargānandāśca pañcānām||
ālocanaṃ ca pūrvācāryairvyākhyātam
asti hyālocanaṃ jñānaṃ prathamaṃ nirvikalpakam|
paraṃ punastathā vastudharmairjātyādibhistathā|| iti ||
paramuttarakālīnaṃ ca punarvastudharmairdravyarūpadharmaistathā jātyādibhirjñānaṃ savikalpakaṃ tathā'locanākhyaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ| tathā ca nirvikalpakasavikalpakarūpaṃ dvividhamapyaindriyakaṃ jñānamālocanasaṃjñamiti labdham| kaścittu nirvikalpakaṃ jñānamevālocanamindriyajanyaṃ ca bhavati savikalpakaṃ tu manomātrajamyamiti ślokārthamāha| tanna | yogabhāṣye vyāsadevairviśiṣṭajñānasyāpyaindriyaktavasya vyavasthāpitatvāt, indriyairviśiṣṭajñāne bādhakābhāvācca| sa eva sūtrārthamapyevaṃ vyācaṣṭe -- "bāhyendriyamārabhyabuddhiparyantasya vṛttirutsargataḥ krameṇa bhavati | kadācit tu vyāghrādidarśanakāle bhayaviśeṣādvidyullateva sarvakaraṇeṣvekadaiva vṛttirbhavatītyartha iti "| tadapyasat | sūtra indriyavṛttīnāmeva kramikākramikatvavacanāt, na buddhyahaṅkāravṛttyoḥ prasaṅgo'pyasti| kiñcaikadā'nekendriyavṛttāveva vādivipratipattyā tannirṇayaparatvameva sūtrasyocitaṃ mano'ṇutvapratiṣedhāya, na tu kākadantānveṣaṇaparatvamiti|| 32 ||
piṇḍīkṛtya buddhīvṛttīḥ saṃsāranidānatāpratipādanārthamādau darśayati -- 
vṛttayaḥ pañcatayyaḥ kliṣṭākliṣṭāḥ || 33 ||
kliṣṭā akliṣṭā vā bhavantu vṛttayaḥ, pañcatayyaḥpañcaprakārā eva, nādhikā ityarthaḥ| kliṣṭā duḥkhadāḥ sāṃsārikavṛttayaḥ | vṛttīnāṃ pañcaprakāratvaṃ pātañjalasūtreṇoktam[ samādhipāde 'prāmāṇaviparyayavilpanidrāsmṛtayaḥ paṃcavṛttayaḥ iti sūtrādārabhya 'pratyakṣānumānāgamāḥ pramāṇāni 1| 7 | viparyayo mithyājñānamatadrūpapatiṣṭham| 8 |9 |10 | 11 ityādiyogasūtreṣu| ] " pramāṇaviparyayavikalpanidrāsmṛtayaḥ" iti | tatra pramāṇavṛttiratrāpyuktā| viparyayastvasmākaṃ vivekāgraha eva, anyathākhyāternirāsyatvāt| vikalpastu viśeṣadarśanakāle'pi -- rāhoḥ śiraḥpuruṣasya caitanyamityādijñānam| nidrā ca suṣuptikālīnā buddhivṛttiḥ. smṛtiśca saṃskārajanyaṃ jñānamiti| etat sarvaṃ pātañjale sūtritam|| 33 ||
yā etā buddhivṛttaya uktā etadaupādhikyeva puruṣasyānyarūpatā na svataḥ, etannivṛttau ca puruṣaḥ svarūpe'vasthito bhavatītyanayāpi diśā puruṣasya svarūpaṃ paricāyayati -- 
tannivṛttāvupaśāntoparāgaḥ svasthaḥ || 34 ||
tāsāṃ vṛttīnāṃ virāmadaśāyāṃ śāntatatpratibimbakaḥ svastho bhavati kaivalya ivānyadāpītyarthaḥ| tathā ca yogasūtratrayam "yogaścittavṛttinirodhaḥ"| " tadādraṣṭuḥ svarūpe'vasthānam" | "vṛttisārūpyamitaratra " iti | idameva ca puruṣasya svasthatvaṃ yadupādhivṛtteḥ pratibimbasya nivṛttiriti| etādṛśo cāvasthā puruṣasya vāsiṣṭhe dṛṣṭāntena pradarśitā| yathā -- 
anāptākhilaśailādipratibimbe hi yādṛśī|
syāddarpaṇe darpaṇatā kevalātmasvarūpiṇī ||
ahaṃ tvaṃ jagadityādau praśānte dṛśyasambhrame|
syāt tādṛśī kevalatā sthite draṣṭaryavīkṣaṇe || iti || 34 ||
etadeva dṛṣṭāntena vivṛṇoti -- 
kusumavacca maṇiḥ|| 35 ||
cakāro hetau | kusumeneva maṇirityarthaḥ| yathā japākusumena sphaṭikamaṇī rakto'[avastha iti
pāṭhe svaniṣṭhasvābhāvika dhamavān na bhavatītyarthaḥ|] ccho'svastho bhavati, tannivṛttau ca rāgaśūnyaḥ svastho bhavati tadvaditi| tadetaduktaṃ kaurme -- 
yathā saṃlakṣyate raktaḥkevalaḥsphaṭiko janaiḥ|
rañjādyupadhānena tadvat paramapūruṇaḥ|| iti || 35 ||
nanu kasya prayatnena karaṇajātaṃ pravarttatām, puruṣasya kūṭasthatvādīśvarasya ca pratiṣiddhatvāditi, tatrāha -- 
puruṣārthaṃ karaṇodbhavo'pyadṛṣṭollāsāt|| 36 ||
pradhānapravṛttivat puruṣārthaṃ karaṇodbhavaḥ| karaṇānāṃ pravṛttirapi puruṣasyādṛṣṭābhivyaktereva bhavatītyarthaḥ| adṛṣṭaṃ copādhereva || 36 ||
parārthaṃ svataḥ pravṛttau dṛṣṭāntamāha -- 
dhanevadvatsāya || 37 ||
yathā vatsārthaṃ dhenuḥ svayameva kṣīraṃ sravati, nānyaṃ yatnamapekṣate, tathaiva svāminaḥ puruṣasya kṛte svayameva karaṇāni pravarttanta ityarthaḥ| dṛśyate ca suṣuptāt svayameva buddherutthānamiti | etadeva kārikayāpyuktam
svāṃsvāṃ pratipadyate parasparākūtahetukāṃ vṛttim|
puruśārthaṃ eva hetarna kenacit kāryate karaṇam || iti || 37 ||
bāhyābhyantarairmilitvā kiyanti karaṇānītyākāṅkṣāyāmāha -- 
karaṇaṃ trayodaśavidhamavāntarabhedāt || 38 ||
antaḥkaraṇatrayaṃ daśa bāhyakaraṇāni militvā trayodaśa | teṣvapi vyamityāśayenoktam -- 
'avāntarabhedāditi, ekasyaiva buddhyākhyakaraṇasya karaṇaānāmanekatvadityarthaḥ|| 38 ||
nanu buddhireva puruṣe'rthasamarpakatvānmukhyaṃ karaṇam, anyeṣāṃ ca karaṇatvaṃ gauṇam | tatra ko guṇa ityākāṅkṣāyāmāha -- 
indriyeṣu sādhakatamatvaguṇayogāt kuṭhāravat || 39 ||
indriyeṣu puruṣārthasādhakatamatvarūpaḥ karaṇasya buddherguṇaḥ paramparayā'styatastrayodaśavidhaṃ karaṇamupapadyata iti pūrvasūtreṇānvayaḥ| kuṭharavaditi| yathā phalāyogavyavacchinnatayā prahārasyaiva chidāyāṃ mukhyakaraṇatve'pi prakṛṣṭasādhanatvaguṇayogāt kuṭharasyāpi karaṇatvaṃ tathetyarthaḥ| antaḥkaraṇasyaikatvamabhipretyāhaṅkārasya gauṇakaraṇatvamatra noktam|| 39 ||
gauṇamukhyabhāve vyavasthāṃ viśiṣyāha -- 
dvayoḥpradhānaṃ mano lokavad bhṛtyavargeṣu|| 40 ||
dvayorbāhyāntarayormadhye mano buddhireva pradhānaṃ mukhyam| sākṣātkaraṇamiti yāvat, puruṣe'rthasamarpakatvāt| yathā bhṛtyavargeṣu madhye kaścideva loko rājñaḥ pradhāno bhavati, anye ca tadupasarjanībhūtā grāmādhyakṣādayastadvadityarthaḥ| atra manaḥśabdo na tṛtīyāntaḥkaraṇavācī| vakṣyamāṇasyākhilasaṃskārādhratvasya buddhyatirikteṣvasambhavāt| sambhave vā buddhikalpanavaiyarthyāditi|| 40 ||
buddheḥpradhānatve hetūnāha tribhiḥ sūtraiḥ -- 
avyabhicārāt|| 41 ||
sarvakaraṇavyāpakatvāt phalāvyabhicārādvetyarthaḥ|| 41 ||
tathā śeṣasaṃskārādhāratvāt || 42 ||
buddherevākhilasaṃskārādhāratā, na tu cakṣurāderahaṅkāramanasorvā pūrvadṛṣṭaśrutādyarthānāmandhavadhirādibhiḥ smaraṇānupapatteḥ| tattvajñānenā'haṅkāramanasorlaye'pi smaraṇadarśanācca| ato'śeṣasaṃskārādhāratayāpi buddhereva sarvebhyaḥ pradhānatvamityarthaḥ|| 42 ||
smṛtyānumānācca|| 43 ||
smṛtyā cintanarūpayā vṛttyā prādhānyā'numānāccetyarthaḥ| cintāvṛttirhi dhyānākhyā sarvavṛttibhyaḥ śreṣṭa'nyavṛttikaraṇebhya ityarthaḥ|| 43 ||
nanu cintāvṛttiḥ puruṣasyaivāstu, tatrāha -- 
sambhavenna svataḥ|| 44 ||
svataḥ puruṣasya smṛtirna sambhavet| kūṭasthatvādityarthaḥ| itthaṃ vāvyākhyeyam| nanvevaṃ buddhireva karaṇamastu kṛtamavāntaraṇairityāśaṅkāyāmāha --  sambhavenna svata iti| cakṣurādidvāratāṃ vinā'khilavyāpāreṣu buddheḥ svataḥ karaṇatvaṃ na sambhavedandhāderapi rūpādidarśanāpatterityarthaḥ|| 44 ||
nanvevaṃ buddhereva prādhānye kathaṃ manasa ubhayātmakatvaṃ prāguktaṃ , tatrāha -- 
āpekṣiko guṇapradhānabhāvaḥ kriyāviśeṣāt|| 45 ||
kriyāviśeṣaṃ prati karaṇānamāpekṣiko guṇapradhānabhāvaḥ | cakṣurādivyāpāreṣu manaḥ pradhānaṃ, manovyāpāre cāhaṅkāro'haṅkāravyāpāre ca buddhi pradhānam|| 45 ||
nanvasya puruṣasyeyaṃ buddhireva karaṇaṃ, na buddhyantaramityevaṃ vyavasthā kinnimittiketyākāṅkṣāyāmāha -- 
tatkarmārjjitatvāt tadarthamabhiceṣṭā lokavat || 46 ||
tatpurūṣīyakarmajatvāt karaṇasya tatpuruṣārthamabhiceṣṭā sarvavyāpāro bhavati lokavat | yathā loke yena puruṣeṇa kriyādikarmaṇā'rjjito yaḥ kuṭhārādistatpuṣārthameva tasya chidādivyāpāra ityarthaḥ| ataḥ karaṇavyavastheti bhāvaḥ| yadyapi kūṭasthatayā puruṣe karma nāsti, tathāpi bhogasādhanatayā puruṣasvāmikatvena rājño jayādivadeva puruṣasya karmocyate| nanu karmaṇa eva tatpuruṣīyatve kiṃ niyāmakamiti| cet tathāvidhaṃ karmāntarameva | anāditvāttu nānavasthā doṣāyeti | yattu kaścidavivekīvadati, -- buddhipratibimbitapuruṣasya karmeti| tanna| yogabhāṣye'smaduktaprakārasyaivoktatvenānyaprakārasyāprāmāṇikatvāt, pratibimbasyāvastutvena karmādyasambhavācca | anyathā pratibimbasya karmatadbhodādyaṅgīkāre bimbatvābhimatapuruṣakalpanāvaiyyarthyasya pūrvaṃ pratipāditatvāditi || 46 ||
buddheḥ prādhānyaṃ prakaṭīkartumupasaṃharati -- 
samānakarmayoge buddheḥ prādhānyaṃ lokavallokavat || 47 ||
yadyapi puruṣārthatvena samāna eva sarveṣāṃ karaṇānāṃ vyāpārastathāpi buddhereva prādhānyaṃ lokavat | loke hi rājārthakatvāviśeṣe'pi grāmādhyakṣādiṣu madhye mantriṇa eva prādhānyaṃ , tadvadityarthaḥ| ata eva buddhireva mahāniti sarvaśāstreṣu gīyata iti | vīpsādhyāyasamāptau || 47 ||
liṅgahedasya ghaṭakaṃ yat saptadaśasaṅkhayakam|
pradhānakāryaṃ tat sūkṣmamatrādhyāye'nuvarṇitam|| 1 ||
iti śrīvijñāvanācāryanirmite kāpilasāṃkhyapravacanasya
bhāṣye pradhānakāryādhyāyo dvitīyaḥ || 2 ||
atha tṛtīyo'dhyāyaḥ || 3||
itaḥ paraṃ pradhānasya sthūlakāryaṃ mahābhūtāni śarīradvayaṃ ca vaktavyam| tataśca vividhayonigatyādayo jñānasādhanānuṣṭhānahetutvaparavairāgyārtham| tataśca paravairāgyāya jñānasādhanānyakhilāni vaktavyānīti tṛtīyārambhaḥ||
aviśeṣādviśeṣārambhaḥ|| 1 ||
nāsti viśeṣaḥ śāntaghoramūḍhatvādirūpo yatretyaviśeṣo bhūtasūkṣmaṃ pañcatanmātrākhyam| tasmācchāntādirūpaviśeṣavattvena viśeṣāṇāṃ sthūlānāṃ mahābhūtānārambha ityarthaḥ| sukhādyātmakatā hi śāntādirūpā sthūlabhūteṣveva tāratamyādibhirabhivyajyate, na sūkṣmeṣu | teṣāṃ śāntaikarūpatayaiva yogiṣvabhivyakteriti || 1||
tadevaṃ pūrvādhyāyamārabhya yoviṃśatitattvānāmutpattimuktvā tasmāccharīradvayotpattimāha -- 
tasmāccharīrasya || 2 ||
tasmāt trayoviṃśatitattvāt sthūlasūkṣmaśarīradvayasyārambha ityarthaḥ|| 2 ||
samprati trayoviṃśatitattve saṃsārānyathānupapattiṃ pramāṇayati -- 
tadvījāt saṃsṛtiḥ|| 3 ||
tasya śarīrasya bījāt trayoviṃśatitattvarūpāt sūkṣmāddhetoḥ puruṣasya saṃsṛtirgatāgate bhavataḥ | kūṭasthasya vibhutayā svato gatyādyasambhavādityarthaḥ| trayoviṃśatitattve'vasthito hi puruṣasyenaivopādhinā pūrvakṛtakarmabhogārthaṃ dehāddehaṃ saṃsarati,
mānasaṃ manasaivāyamupabhuṅkte śubhāśubham|
vācā vācā kṛtaṃ karma kāyenaiva tu kāyikam||
ityādismṛtibhiḥ pūrvasargīyakaṇairevotsargataḥ sargāntareṣūpabhogasiddheḥ| ata eva brahmasūtramupasaṃharati 'sampariṣvaka ' iti || 3||
saṃsṛteravadhimapyāha -- 
āvivekācca pravarttanamaviśeṣāṇām|| 4 ||
īśvarānīśvaratvādiviśeṣarahitānāṃ sarveṣāmeva puṃsāṃ vivekaparyantameva pravarttanaṃ saṃsṛtirāvaśyakī | vivekottaraṃ ca na setyarthaḥ|| 4 ||
tatra hetumāha -- 
upabhogāditarasya || 5 ||
itarasyā vivekina eva svīyakarmaphasabhogāvaśyambhāvādityarthaḥ|| 5 ||
dehasattve'pi saṃsṛtikāle bhogo nāstītyāha -- 
samprati parimukto dvābhyām || 6 ||
samprati saṃsṛtikāle puruṣo dvābhyāṃ śītoṣṇasukhādidvandvaiḥ parimukto bhavatītyarthaḥ| tadetate kārikayoktam -- 
saṃsarati nirupabhogaṃ bhāvairadhivāsitaṃ liṅgam|| iti ||
bhāvāḥdharmādharmavāsanādayaḥ|| 6 ||
ataḥparaṃ śarīradvajaṃ viśiṣya vaktumupakramate -- 
mātāpitṛjaṃ sthūlaṃ prāyaśa itaratra tathā|| 7 ||
sthūlaṃ mātāpitṛjaṃ prāyaśo bāhulyenāyonijasyāpi sthūlaśarīrasya smaraṇāditaracca sūkṣmaśarīraṃ na tathā, na mātāpitṛjam| sargādyutpannatvādityarthaḥ| taduktaṃ kārikayā -- 
pūrvotpannamasaktaṃ niyataṃ mahadādisūkṣmaparyantam|
saṃsarati nirupabhogaṃ bhāvairadhivāsitaṃ liṅgam|| iti ||
niyataṃ nityaṃ dviparārdhasthāpi gauṇanityaṃ pratiśarīraṃ liṅgotpattikalpane goravāt | pralaye tu tannāśaḥ śrutismṛtiprāmāṇyādiṣyate | gatikāle bhogābhāvavacanamutsargābhiprāyeṇa | kadācittu vāyavīyaśarīrapraveśato gamanakāle'pi bhogo bhavati | ato yamamārge duḥkhabhogavākyānyupapadyante|| 7 ||
sthūlasūkṣmaśarīrayormadhye kimupādhikaḥ puruṣasya dvandvayogaḥ? tadava dhārayati -- 
pūrvotpattestatkāryatvaṃ bhogādekasya netarasya || 8 ||
pūrvaṃ largādāvatpattiryasya liṅgaśarīrasya tasyaiva tatkāryatvaṃ sukhaduḥkhakāryakatvam | kutaḥ? ekasya liṅgadehasyaiva sukhaduḥkhākhyabhogād, na tvirasya sthūlaśarīrasya | mṛtaśarīre sukhaduḥkhādyabhāvasya sarvasammatatvādityarthaḥ
|| 8 ||
uktasyasūkṣmaśarīrasya svarūpamāha -- 
saptadaśaikaṃ liṅgam|| 9 ||
sūkṣmaśarīramapyādhārādheyabhāvena dvividhaṃ bhavati | tatra saptadaśa militvā liṅgaśarīram | tacca sargādau samaṣṭirūpamekameva bhavatītyarthaḥ| ekādaśendriyāṇi pañca tanmātrāṇi buddhiśceti saptadaśa| ahaṅkārasya buddhāvevāntarbhāvaḥ| caturthasūtravakṣyamāṇapramāṇādetānyeva saptadaśa liṅgaṃ mantavyaṃ, na tu saptadaśamekaṃ cetyaṣṭādaśatayā vyākhyeyam| uttarasūtreṇa vyaktibhedasyopapādyātayātra liṅgaikatvaṃ ekaśabdasya tātparyāvadhāraṇācca||
karmātmā puruṣo yo'sau bandhāmokṣaiḥ prayujyate|
sa saptadaśakenā'pi rāśinā yujyate ca saḥ||
iti mokṣadharmādau liṅgaśarīrasya saptadaśatvasiddheśca | saptadaśāvayavā atra santīti saptadaśako rāśirityarthaḥ| rāśiśabdena sthūladehavalliṅgadehasyāvayavitvaṃ nirākṛtam| avayavarūpeṇa dravyāntarakalpanāyāṃ goravāt | sthūladehasya cāvayavitvamekatādipratyakṣānurodhena kalpyata iti | atra ca liṅgadehe buddhireva pradhānetyāśayena liṅgadehasya bhogaḥ prāguktaḥ| prāṇaścāntaḥkaraṇasyaiva vṛttibhedaḥ ; ato liṅgadehe prāṇapañcakasyāpyantarbhāva ityasyasaptadaśāvayavakasya śarīratvaṃ svayaṃ vakṣyati -- " liṅgaśarīranimittaka iti sanandanācārya" iti sūtreṇa | ato bhogāyatanatvameva mukhyaṃ śarīralakṣaṇam| tadāśrayatayā tvan.tra śarīratvamiti paścād vyaktībhaviṣyati | "ceṣṭendriyarthāśrayaḥ śarīra" miti tu nyāye'pi lakṣamaṃ kṛtamiti|| 9 ||
nanu liṅgaṃ cedeaikaṃ, tarhi kathaṃ puruṣabhedena vilakṣaṇā bhogāḥ syustatrāha -- 
vyaktibhedaḥ karmaviśeṣāt|| 10 ||
yadyapi sargādau hiraṇyagarbhopādhirūpamekameva liṅgaṃ, tathāpi tasya paścād vyaktibhodo vyaktirūpeṇāṃśato nānātvamapi bhavati | yathedānīmekasya pitṛliṅgadehasya nānātvamaṃśato bhavati putrakanyādiliṅgadeharūpeṇa | tatra kāraṇamāha -- karmāviśeṣāditi| jīvāntarāṇāṃ bhogāhetukarmāderityarthaḥ| atra viśeṣavacanāt samaṣṭisṛṣṭirjīvānāṃ sādhāraṇaiḥ karmabhirbhavatītyāyātam| ayaṃ ca vyaktibhedo manvādiṣvapyuktaḥ| yathā manau samaṣṭipuruṣasya ṣaḍindriyotpattyanantaram,
teṣāṃ tvavayavān sūkṣmān ṣaṇmāpyamitaujasām|
sanniveśayatmamātrāsu sarvabhūtīni nirmame || iti ||
ṣaṇmāṇāmiti samastaliṅgaśarīropalakṣaṇam. ātmamātrāsu ciṃdaśeṣu saṃyojyetyarthaḥ| tathā ca tatraiva vākyāntaram -- 
taccharīrasamutpannaiḥ kāryaistaiḥ karaṇaiḥ saha|
kṣetrajñāḥ samajāyanta gātrebhyastasya dhīmataḥ || iti || 10 ||
nanvevaṃ bhogāyatanatayā liṅgasyaiva śarīratve sthūle kathaṃ śarīravyavahārastatrāha -- 
tadadhiṣṭhānāśraye dehe tadvādāt tadvāda ḥ || 11 ||
yasya liṅgasya yadadhiṣṭhānamāśrayo vakṣyamāṇabhūtapañcakaṃ tasyāśraye ṣāṭkauśikadehe tadvādo dehavādastadvādāt tasyādhiṣṭhānaśabdoktasya dehavādādityarthaḥ| liṅgasambandhādadhiṣṭānasya dehatvamadhiṣṭānāśrayatvācca sthūlasya dehatvamiti paryavasito'rthaḥ| adhiṣṭhānaṃ na sūkṣmaṃ pañcabhūtātmakaṃ vakṣyate| tathāca śarīratrayaṃ siddham| yattu -- 
ātivāhika eko'sti deho'nyastvādhibhautikaḥ|
sarvāsāṃ bhūtajātīnāṃ brahmaṇastveka eva kim||
ityādiśāstreṣu śarīradvayameva śrūyate talliṅgaśarīrādhiṣṭhānaśarīrayoranyonyaniyatatvena sūkṣmatvena caikatābhiprāyāditi|| 11 ||
nanu ṣāṭkauśikātirikte liṅgaśarīrādhiṣṭhānabhūte śarītāntare kiṃ pramāṇamityākāṅkṣāyāmāha -- 
na svātantryāt tadṛte chāyāvaccitravacca|| 12 ||
talliṅgaśarīraṃ tadṛte'dhiṣṭhānaṃ vinā svātantryānna tiṣṭhati | yathā chāyā nirādhārā na tiṣṭhati , yathā vā citramityardhaḥ| tathā ca sthūladehaṃ tyaktvā lokāntaragamanāya liṅgahedasyādhārabhūtaṃ śarīrāntaraṃ siddhyatīti bhāvaḥ| tasya ca svarūpaṃ kārikāyāmuktam -- 
sūkṣmāṃ mātāpitṛjāḥ sahaprabhūtaistridhā viśeṣāḥ syuḥ|
sūkṣmāsteṣāṃ niyatā mātāpitṛjā nivartante || iti || 
atra tanmātrakāryaṃ mātāpitṛjaśarīrāpekṣayā sūkṣmaṃ yad bhūtapañcakaṃ yāvalliṅgasyāpi proktaṃ tadeva liṅgādhiṣṭhānaṃ śarīramiti labdhaṃ kārikāntareṇa -- 
citraṃ yathāśrayamṛte sthāṇvādibhyo vinā yathā chāyā|
tadvadvinā viśeṣairna sūkṣmākhyaiḥ| sthūlāvāntarabhedairiti | asyāṃ kārikāyāṃ sūkṣmākhyānāṃ sthūlabhūtānāṃ liṅgaśarīrādbhedāvagamena, pūrvotpannamasaktaṃ niyataṃ mahadādisūkṣmaparyantam' ityādipūrvodāhṛtakārikāyāṃ sūkṣmabhūtaparyantasya liṅgatvaṃ nā'rthaḥ, kintu mahadādirūpaṃ yalliṅgaṃliṅgaghaṭakapadārthāḥ kiyanta iti kathamavadhāryamiti cet vāsanābhūtasūkṣmaṃ ca karmavidye tathaiva ca |
daśendriyaṃ mano buddhiretalliṅgaṃ vidurbudhāḥ||
iti vāśiṣṭādivākyebhyaḥ atra liṅgaśarīrapratipādanenaiva puryaṣṭakamapi vyākhyeyamityāśayena buddhidharmāṇāmapi vāsanākarmavidyānāṃ pṛthagupanyāsaḥ bhūtasūkṣmaṃ cātra tanmātrā daśendriyāṇi ca jñānakarmendriyabhedena
puradvayamityāśayaḥ| yattu māyāvādino liṅgaśarīrasya tanmātrasthāne prāṇādipañcakaṃ prakṣipanti, puryaṣṭakaṃ cānyathā kalpayanti, tadaprāmāṇikamiti|| 12 ||
nanu mūrtadravyatayā vāyvāderiva liṅgasyākāśamevāsaṅgenādhāro'stuvyarthamanyatra saṅgakalpanamiti | tatrāha -- 
mūtratve'pi saṅghātayogāt taraṇivat|| 13 ||
mūtratve'pi svātantryādasaṅgatayā'vasthānaṃ prakāśarūpatvena sūryasyeva saṅghātasaṅgānumānādityarthaḥ| sūryādīni sarvāṇi tejāṃsi pārthivadravyasahgenaivaivasthitāni dṛśyante| liṅgaṃ ca sattvaprakāśamayayo bhūtasaṅgatamiti|| 13 ||
liṅgasya parimāṇamavadhārayati -- 
aṇuparimāṇaṃ tat kṛtiśruteḥ|| 14 ||
talliṅgamaṇuparimāṇaṃ paricchinnaṃ, na tvatyantamevāṇu | sāvayavasyoktatvāt| kutaḥ? kṛtiśruteḥ kriyāśruteḥ| "vijñānaṃ ya yajñaṃ tanute karmāṇi tamute'pi ca" ityādiśrutervijñānakhyabuddhipradhānatayā vijñānasya liṅgasyākhilakarmaśravaṇādityarthaḥ| vibhutve sati kriyā na sambhavati| śruteriti pāṭhastu samācīnaḥ| liṅgaśarīrasya ca gatiśrutiḥ " tamutkrāmantaṃ prāṇo'nukrāmati prāṇamanukrāmantaṃ sarve prāṇā anukrāmanti savijñāno bhavati savijñānamevānupakrāmiti" iti | savijñāno buddhisahita eva jāyate | savujñānaṃ yathā syāt tathā saṃsarati cetyarthaḥ
|| 14 ||
paricchinnatve yuktyantaramāha -- 
tadannamayatvaśruteścata || 15 ||
tasya liṅgasyaikadeśato'nnamayatvaśrucerna vibhutvaṃ sambhavatīti | vibhutve sati nityatāpatterityarthaḥ| sā ca śrutirhi "annamayaṃ hi saumya mana āyadyapi prāṇastejomayī vāg" ityādi | yadyapi manādīni na bhautikāni tathāpyannasaṃsṛṣṭasajātīyāṃśapūparaṇādannamayatvādivyavahero bodhyaḥ || 15 ||
acetanānāṃ liṅgānāṃ kimarthaṃ saṃsṛtirdehahāntarasañcāra ityāśaṅkāyāmāha -- 
puruṣārthaṃ saṃsṛtirliṅgānāṃ sūpakāravadrājñaḥ|| 16 ||
yathā rājñaḥ sūpakārāṇāṃ pākaśālāsu sañcāro rājārthaṃ , tathā liṅgaśarīrāṇāṃ saṃsṛtiḥ puruṣārthamityarthaḥ|| 16 ||
liṅgaśarīramaśeṣaviśeṣato vicāritam, idānīṃ sthūvaśarīramapi tathā vicārayati -- 
pāñcabhautiko hedaḥ|| 17 ||
pañcānāṃ bhūtānāṃ militānāṃ pariṇāmo deha ityarthaḥ|| 17 ||
matāntaramāha -- 
cāturbhautikamityeke || 18 ||
ākāśasyānārambhakatvalamabhipretyedam|| 28 ||
aikabhautikamityapare || 19 ||
pārthivameva śarīramanyāni ca bhūtānyupaṣcambhakamātrāṇīti bhāvaḥ| athavaikabhautikamekaikabhautikamityarthaḥ| manuṣyādiśarīre pārthivāṃśādhikyena pārthivatā sūryādilokeṣu ca tojādyādhikyena taijasāditā śarīrāṇāṃ suvarṇādīnāmivetīmameva pakṣaṃ pañcamādhyaye'pi siddhāntayiṣyatayi|| 19 ||
dehasya bhautikatvena yat siddhyati tadāha -- 
na sāṃsiddhikaṃ caitanyaṃ pratyekādṛṣṭeḥ|| 20 ||
bhūteṣu pṛthak kṛteṣu caitanyādarśaṣanādbhautikasya dehasya svābhāvikaṃ caitanyaṃ, kintvaupādhikamityarthaḥ|| 20 ||
bādhakāntaramāha -- 
prapañcamaraṇādyabhāvaśca || 21 ||
prapañcasya sarvasyaiva maraṇasuṣuptyādyabhāvaśca dehasya svābhāvikacaitanye sati syādityartaḥ| maraṇasuṣuptyādikaṃ hi dehasyā'cetanatā | sā ca svābhāvikacaitanye sati nopapadyate| svabhāvasya yāvaddravyabhāvitvāditi
|| 21 ||
pratyekādṛṣṭeriti yaduktaṃ, tatrāśaṅkya pariharati -- 
madaśaktivaccet pratyekaparidṛṣṭe sāṃhatye tadudbhavaḥ|| 22 ||
nanu yathā mādakatayā śaktiḥ pratyekadravyāvṛttirapi militadravye varttate, evaṃ caitanyamāpa syāditi cet? na pratyekaparidṛṣṭe sati sāṃhatye tadudbhavaḥ sambhavet | prakṛte tu pratyekaparidṛṣṭatvaṃ nāsti | ato dṛṣṭānte pratyekaṃ śāstrādibhiḥ sūkṣmatayā mādakatve siddhe saṃhatabhāvakāle mādakatvāvirbhāvamātraṃ siddyati | dārṣṭāntike tu pratyekabhūteṣu sūkṣmatayā na kenāpi pramāṇena caitanyaṃ siddhamityarthaḥ| nanu samuccite caitanyadarśanena pratyekabhūte sūkṣmacaitanyaśaktiramanumeyeti ce | anekabhūteṣvanekacaitanyaśaktikalpanāyāṃ goraveṇa lāghavādekasyaiva nityacitsvarūpasya kalpanaucityāt| nanu yathāvayave'varttamānamapi parimāṇajalāharaṇādikārya ghaṭādau dṛśyate, evameva śarīraṃ caitanyaṃ syāditi| maivam| bhūtagataviśeṣaguṇānāṃ sajātīyakāraṇaguṇajanyatayā kāraṇe caitanyaṃ vinā dehe caitanyāsambhavāditi || 2||
puruṣārthaṃ saṃsṛtirliṅgānāmityuktam| tatra liṅgānāṃ sthūladehasañcārākhyajanmano yoyaḥ puruṣārtho yena yena vyāpāreṇa siddhyati, tadāha sūtrābhyām -- 
jñānanmuktiḥ|| 23 ||
liṅgasaṃsṛtito janmadvārā vivekasākṣātkārastasmānmuktirūpaḥ puruṣārtho bhavatītyarthaḥ| jñānādikaṃ ca pratyayasargatayā kārikāyāṃ paribhāṣitam -- 
eṣa pratyayasargo viparyayāśaktitapaṣṭisiddhyākhāyaḥ|| iti ||
viparyayādayo vyākhyāsyante | atra ca sa eva buddhisargaḥ prayojanayo gena sūtrerucyata iti viśeṣaḥ || 23 ||
bandho viparyayāt || 24 ||
viparyayāt sukhaduḥkhātmako bandharūpaḥ puruṣārtho liṅgasaṃsṛtito bhavatītyarthaḥ|| 24 ||
jñānaviparyayābhyāṃ muktibandhāvuktau | tatrādau jñānānmuktiṃ vicārayati -- 
niyatakāraṇatvānna samuccayavikalpau || 25 ||
yadyapi, "vidyāṃ cāvidyāṃ ca yastadvedobhayaṃ saha" ityādi śrūyate| tathāpyavivekanivṛttau lokasiddhatayā jñānasya niyatakāraṇatvād avidyākhyakarmaṇā saha jñānasya mokṣajanane samuccayo vikalpo vā nāstītyarthaḥ| " tameva viditvātimṛtyumeti nānyaḥ panthā vidyate 'yanāya, na karmaṇā na prajayā dhanena tyāgenaike'mṛtatvamānaśuḥ" ityādiśrutibhyo'pi karmaṇo na sākṣānmokṣahetutvam. samuccayānuṣṭhānaṃ śrutiṣvaṅgāṅgibhāvādibhirabhyupapadyata iti || 25 ||
samuccayavikalpayorabhāve dṛṣṭāntamāha -- 
svapnajāgārābhyāmiva māyikāmāyikābhyāṃ nobhayormuktiḥ puruṣasya || 26 ||
yathā māyikāmāyikābhyāṃ svapnajāgarapadārthāmyāmanyonyasahakāribhāvenaikaḥ puruṣārtho na sambhavati| evamubhayormāyikā'māyikayoranuṣṭhitayoḥ karmajñānayoḥ puruṣasya muktirapi na yuktetyarthaḥ| māyikatvaṃ cāsatyatvam| asthiratvamiti yāvat | tacca svāpne'rthe'sti| jāgratpadārthastu svāpnāpekṣayā satya eva | kūṭasthapuruṣāpekṣayaivāsthiratvenāsatyatvādataḥ svapnavilakṣaṇasnānādikāryakaraḥ evaṃ karmāpyasthiratvāt prakṛtikāryatvācca māyikam| ātmā tu sthiratvādakāryatvāccāmāyikaḥ| atakṣaṇameva kāryaṃ yuktam|| 26 ||
nanvevamapyātmopāsanākhyajñānena saha tattvajñānasya samuccayanikalpau syātāmupāsyasyāmāyikatvāditi, tatrāha -- 
itarasyāpi nātyantikam||
itarasyāpyupāsyasya nātyantikamamāyikatvam| upāsyātmanyadhyastapadārthānāmapi praveśādityarthaḥ
|| 27 ||
upāsanasya māyikatvaṃ yasminnaṃśe tadāha -- 
saṅkalpite'pyevam|| 28 ||
manaḥsaṅkalpite -- dhyeyāṃśe, evamapi -- māyakatvamapītyarthaḥ. 'sarvaṃ khalvidaṃ brahma' ityādiśrutyukte hyupāsye prapañcāṃśasya māyikatvameveti || 28 ||
tarhyupāsanasya kiṃ phalamityākāṅkṣāyāmāha -- 
bhāvanopacayācchuddhasya sarvaṃ prakṛtivat || 29 ||
bhāvanākhyopāsanāniṣpattyā śuddhasya niṣpāpasya puruṣasya prakṛteriva sarvamaiśvaryaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ| prakṛtiryathā sṛṣṭisthitisaṃhāraṃ kareti, evamupāsakasya buddhisattvamapi prakṛtipreraṇena sṛṣṭyādikartṛ bhavatīti || 29 ||
jñānameva mokṣasādhanamiti sthāpitam| idānīṃ jñānasādhanānyāha -- 
rāgopahatirdhyānam|| 30 ||
jñānapratibandhako yo viṣayoparāgaścittasya tadupaghātaheturdhyānamityarthaḥ| upacāreṇa kāryakāraṇayorabhedanirdeśaḥ, rāgakṣayasya dhyānatvāsambhavāt | atra dhyānaśabdena dhāraṇādhyānasamādhayo yogoktāstraya eva grāhyāḥ pātañjale yogāṅgānāmaṣṭhānāmeva vivekasākṣātkārahetutvaśravaṇāditi | eteṣāṃ cāvāntaraviśeṣāstatraiva draṣṭavyāḥ| itarāṇi ca pañcāṅgāni svayaṃ vakṣyati || 30 ||
dhyānaniṣpattyaiva jñānotpattirnārambhamātreṇetyāśayena dhyānaniṣpatterlakṣaṇamāha -- 
vṛttinirodhāt tatsiddhiḥ|| 31 ||
dhyeyātiriktavṛttinirodharūpeṇa samprajñātayogena tatsiddhardhyānasya niṣpattirjñānākhya phalopadhānarūpā bhavatītyarthaḥ| atastāvatparyantameva dhyānaṃ karttavyamityāśayaḥ| itaravṛttinirodhe satyeva viṣayāntarasañcārākhyapratibandhāpagamāddhyeyasākṣātkāro bhavatīti kṛtvā yogo'pi jñāne kāraṇaṃ, yogāṅgadhyānādivadityapi mantavyam|"adhyātmayogādhigamena devaṃ matvādhīro harṣaśokau jahāti " ityādiśrutismṛtyostadavagamāditi || 31 ||
dhyānasyāpi sādhanānyāha -- 
dhāraṇāsanasvakarmaṇā tatsiddhiḥ|| 32 ||
vakṣyamāṇena dhāraṇāditrayeṇa dhyānaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ| 32 ||
dhāraṇāditrayaṃ kramāt sūtratrayeṇa lakṣayati -- 
nirodhaśchardividhāraṇābhyām|| 33 ||
prāṇasyeti prasiddhyā labhyate| " pracchardanavidhāraṇābhyāṃ vā prāṇasya" iti yogasūtre bhāṣyakāreṇa prāṇāyāmasya vyākhyātatvāt | chardiśca vamanam| vidhāraṇaṃ tyāga iti yāvat | tena pūraṇarecanayorlābhaḥ| vidhāraṇaṃ ca kumbhakam| tathāca prāṇasya pūrakarecakakumbhakairyo nirodho vaśīkaraṇaṃ, sādhāraṇetyarthaḥ| āsanakarmaṇoḥ svaśabdena paścāllakṣaṇīyatayā sūtre pariśe,ta eva dhāraṇāyā lakṣyatvalābhāddhāraṇāpadaṃ nopāttam| cittasya dhāraṇā tu samādhivad dhyānaśabdenaiva gṛhītetyuktam|| 33 ||
kramaprāptamāsanaṃ lakṣayati -- 
sthirasukhamāsanam|| 34 ||
yat sthiraṃ sat sukhasādhanaṃ bhavati svastikādi, tadāsanamityarthaḥ| 34 ||
svakarma lakṣayati -- 
svakarma svāśramavihitakarmānuṣṭhānam|| 35 ||
sugamam| tatra sarmaśabdena yamaniyamayorgrahaṇam| jitendriyatvarūpaḥ pratyāhāro'pi sarvāśramasādhāraṇatayā karmamadhye praveśanīyaḥ| tathā ca pātañjalasūtre jñānasādhatayā proktānyaṣṭau yogāṅgānyatrāpi labdhāni|
yathā tatsūtram| yamaniyamāsanaprāṇāyāmapratyāhāradhāraṇādhyānasamādhayo'ṣṭāvaṅgānīti | teṣāṃ ca svarūpaṃ tatraiva draṣṭavyam
|| 35 ||
mukhyādhikāriṇo nāsti bahiraṅgasya yamādipañcakasyāpekṣā, kevalāddhāraṇādhyānāditrayarūpāt saṃyamādeva jñānaṃ yogaśca bhavatīti pātañjalasiddhantaḥ jaḍabharatādiṣu ca tathā dṛśyate'pi | atastadanusreṇācāryo'pyāha -- 
vairāgyādabhyāsācca|| 36 ||
kevalābhyāsād dhyānarūpādeva vairāgyasahitājjñānaṃ tatsādhanayogaśca bhavatyuttamādhikāriṇāmityarthaḥtha taduktaṃ gāruḍe'pi -- 
āsanasthānavidhayo na yogasya prasādhakāḥ|
vilambajananāḥ sarve vistarāḥ parikīrtitāḥ||
śiśupālaḥ siddhimāpa smaraṇābhyāsagauravāt || iti ||
athavā vairāgyadhyanābhyāsāvatra dhyānasyaiva hetutayoktau, cakāraśca dhāraṇāsamuccayāyeti | tadevaṃ jñānānmokṣo vyākhyātaḥ|| 36 ||
ataḥ paraṃ bandho viparyayādityukto bandhakāraṇaṃ viparyayo vyākhyāsyate | tatrādau viparyayasya svarūpamāha -- 
viparyayabhedāḥ pañca || 37 ||
avidyāsmitārāgadveṣābhiniveśāḥ pañca yogoktā bandhahetuviparyayasyāvāntarabhedā ityarthaḥ| tena śuktyādijñānarūpāṇāṃ viparyayāṇāmasaṅgrahe'pi na kṣatiḥ| tatrāvidyā, anityāśuciduḥkhānātmasu nityaśucisukhātmakhyātiriti yoge proktā | evamasmitā'pi ātmānātmanorekatāpratyayaḥ, śarīrādyatirikta ātmā nāstītyevaṃrūpaḥ| avidyā tu naivaṃrūpā| ātmanaḥ śarīrāśarīrobhayarūpatve'pi śarīre'hambuddhyapapatteḥ| rāgadveṣau prasiddhāveva| abhiniveśaśca maraṇāditrāsa iti |
rāgādīnāṃ viparyayakāryatayā viparyayatvam|| 37 ||
viparyayasya svarūpamuktvā tatkāraṇasyāśakterapi svarūpamāha -- 
aśaktiraṣṭāviṃśatidhā tu ||38 ||
sugamam| etadapi kārikayā vyākyātam -- 
ekādaśendriyavadhāḥ saha buddhivadhairaśaktiruddiṣṭā|
saptadaśa vadhā buddherviparyayāt tuṣṭisiddhīnām|| iti ||
bādhiryaṃ kuṣṭitāndhatvaṃ jaḍatā'jighnatā tathā|
mūkatā kauṇyapaṅgutve klaibyodāvartamugdhatāḥ||
ityekādaśendriyāṇāmekādaśā'śaktayaḥ svataścabuddheḥsaptadaśā| śaktayaḥ| yathā vakṣyamāṇānāṃ navatuṣṭhīnāṃ vighātā nava, tathā vakṣyamāṇānāmaṣṭaviṃśatirbuddheraśaktya ityarthaḥ| tu -- śabda eṣāṃ viśeṣaprasiddhikhyāpanārthaḥ
|| 38||
yayorvighāte buddheraśaktī te tuṣṭisiddhī sūtradvayenāha -- 
tuṣṭarnavadhā || 39 ||
svayameva navadhātvaṃ vakṣyati|| 39 ||
siddhiraṣṭadhā|| 40 ||
etadapi svayaṃ vakṣyati || 40 ||
uktānāṃ viparyayāśaktituṣṭisiddhīnāṃ viśeṣajijñāsāyāṃ krameṇa sūtracatuṣṭayaṃ pravartate -- 
avāntara bhedāḥ pūrvavat|| 41 ||
viparyayasyāvāntarabhedā ye sāmānyataḥ pañcoktāste pūrvavat pūrvacāryairyathoktāstathaivā viśiṣya avadhāryāḥ| vistarabhayānnehocyanta ityarthaḥ| te cāvidyādayo mayāpi sāmānyata eva vyākhyātāḥ pañceti | viśeṣatastu dvāṣaṣṭibhedāḥ| taduktaṃ kārikāyām -- 
bhedastaso'ṣṭavidho mohasya daśavidho mahāmohaḥ|
tāmistro'ṣṭādaśadhā tathā bhavatyandhatamisraḥ|| iti ||
asyāyamarthaḥ| aṣṭasvavyaktamahadahaṅkārapañcatanmātreṣu prakṛtiṣvanātmasvātmabuddhiravidyātamo'ṣṭadhā bhavati| kāryakāraṇābhedena kevalavikṛtiṣvātmabuddherapyatrāntarbhāvaḥ| evamavidyāyā viṣayabhedenāṣṭavidhatvāt tatsamānaviṣayakasyāsmitākhyasya mohalyāṣṭavidhatvam| divyā'divyabhedena sabdādīnāṃ viṣayāṇāṃ daśatvāt tadviṣayako rāgākhyo mahāmoho daśavadhaḥ| avidyāsmitayoraṣṭau ye viṣayā ye rāgasya daśa viṣayāstadvighātakeṣvaṣṭādaśasvaṣṭādaśadhā tāmisrakhyo dveṣaḥ| evaṃ teṣāmaṣṭādaśānāṃ vināśādidarśanāgaṣṭādaśadhāndhamisrākhyo'bhiniveśo bhayamiti| eteṣāṃ ca tamādisaṃjñā taddhetutvāditi|| 41 ||
evamitarasyāḥ|| 42 ||
evaṃ sūtraṃ kārikayā vyākhyātam -- 
adhyātmikyāścatasraḥ prakṛtyupādānakālabhāgyākhyāḥ|
bāhyā viṣayoparamāt pañca nava tuṣṭayo'bhihitāḥ|| iti ||
asyāyamarthaḥ | ātmānaṃ tuṣṭimataḥ saṅghātamadhikṛtya vartanta ityādhyātmikyastuṣṭayaścatasraḥ| tatra prakṛtākhyā tuṣṭiryathā | sākṣātkāraparyantaḥ pariṇāmaḥ sarvo'pi prakṛtereva | taṃ ca prakṛtireva karotireva karotyahaṃ tu kūṭasthaḥ pūrṇa
ityātmabhāvanāt paritoṣaḥ iyaṃ tuṣṭirambha ityucyate| tataśca pravrajyopādānena yā tuṣṭiḥ sopādānā''khyā salilamityucyate | tataśca pravrajyoyāṃ bahukālaṃ samādhya'nuṣṭānena yā tuṣṭiḥ sā kālākhyā tuṣṭirogha ityucyate| tataśca prajñānaparamakāṣṭhārūpe dharmameghasamādhau sati yā tuṣṭiḥ sā bhāgyākhyāvṛṣṭirityucyata iti catasra ādhyātmikyaḥ| bāhyāḥ pañca tuṣṭayo bāhyaviṣayeṣu pañcasu śabdādiṣvarjanarakṣaṇakṣayabhogahiṃsādidoṣanimittoparamājjāyante| tāśca tuṣṭayo yathākramaṃ pāraṃ supāraṃ pārapāramanuttamāmbha uttamāmbha iti paribhāṣitā iti | kaścittvimāṃ kārikāmanyathā vyākhyātavān | tadyathā | vivekasākṣātkāro'pi prakṛtipariṇāma evetyalaṃ dhyānābhyāsenetyevaṃ dṛṣṭyā yā dhyānādinivṛttau tuṣṭiḥ sā prakṛtyākhyā | pravajyopādānenaiva mokṣo bhaviṣyati, kiṃ dhyānādineti yā tuṣṭiḥ sopādānākhyā| kṛtasaṃnyāsasyāpi kālenaiva mokṣo bhaviṣyatyalamudvegeneti yā tuṣṭiḥ sā kālākhyā bhāgyādeva mokṣo bhaviṣyati. na mokṣaśāstroktasādhanaiḥ evaṃ kutarke yā tuṣṭiḥ sā bhāgyākhyetyādirartha iti | tanna | tadvyākhyātatuṣṭīnāmabhāvasya jñānādyanukūlatvenāśaktiparibhāśānaucityāditi|| 43 ||
ūhādibhiḥ siddhiḥ || 44 ||
ūhādibhidaiḥ siddhiraṣṭadhā bhavatītyarthaḥ| idamapi sūtraṃ kārikayā vyākhyātam -- 
ūhaḥ śabdo'dhyayanaṃ duḥkhavighātāstrayaḥ suhṛtprāptiḥ| 
dānaṃ ca siddhayo'ṣṭau siddheḥ pūrvo'ṅkuśastrividhaḥ|| iti ||
asyāyamarthaḥ | atrādhyātmikādiduḥkhatrayapratiyogikatvāt trayo duḥkhavighātā mukhyasiddhayaḥ| itarāstu tatsādhanatvād gauṇyaḥ siddhayaḥ | tatroho yathā | upadeśādikaṃ vinaiva prāgbhavīyābhyāsavaśāt tattvasya svayamūhanamiti ṣa śabdastu yathā| anyadīyapāṭhamākarṇya svayaṃ vā śāstramākalayya yajjñānaṃ jāyate taditi| adhyayanaṃ ca yathā| śiṣyācāryabhāvena śāstrādhyayanājjñānamiti | suhṛtprāptiryatā | svayamupadeśārthaṃ gṛhāgatāt paramakāruṇikājjñānalābha iti | dānaṃ ca yathā | dhanādidānena paritoṣitājjñānalābha iti | eṣu ca pūrvastrividha ūhaśabdādhyayanarūpo sukhyasiddheraṅkaśa ākarṣakaḥ | suhṛtprāptidānayorūhāditrayāpekṣayā mandasādhanatvapratipādanāyemaduktam| kaścittvetāsāmaṣṭasiddhīnāmaṅkuśo nivārakaḥ pūrvastrividho viparyayāśaktituṣṭirūpo bhavati bandhakatvāditi vyācaṣṭe, tanna. tuṣṭyabhāvasyāśaktitayā bādhiryādivat siddhivirodhitālābhena tuṣṭyatuṣṭyorubhayoḥ siddhivirodhitvāsambhavāt || 44 ||
nanūhādibhireva kathaṃ siddhirucyate| mantratapaḥsamādhyādibhirapyaṇimādyaṣṭasiddheḥ sarvaśāstrasiddhatvāditi , tatrāha -- 
netarāditahānena vinā || 45 ||
itarādūhanādipañcakabhinnāt tapādestāttikī siddhiḥ| kutaḥ? itarahānena vinā | yataḥ sā siddhiritarasya viparyayasya hānaṃ vinaiva bhavatyataḥ saṃsārāparipanthitvāt sā siddhyābhyāsa eva na tu tāttvikī siddhirityarthaḥ| tathācoktaṃ yogasūtreṇa "te samādhāvupasargā vyutthāne siddhayaḥ" iti | tadevaṃ jñānānmuktirityārabhya vistarato buddhiguṇarūpaḥ pratyayasargaḥ sakāryabandho mokṣarūpapuruṣārthena sahoktaḥ| etau ca buddhitadguṇarūpau sargau pravāharūpeṇāmyonyahetu bījāṅkuravat | tathā ca kārikā -- 
na vinā bhāvairliṅgaṃ na vinā liṅgena bhāvavirvṛttiḥ|
liṅgākhyo bhāvākhyastasmāddvividhaḥ pravartate sargaḥ || iti ||
bhāvo vāsanārūpā buddhirjñānādiguṇā liṅgaṃ mahattattvaṃ buddhiriti | samaṣṭisargaḥ pratyayasargaśca samāptaḥ|| 45 ||
samprata vyaktibhedaḥ karmaviśeṣāditi saṃkṣepāduktā vyaṣṭisṛṣṭirvistarataḥ pratipādyate -- 
daivādiprabhedā || 46 ||
daivādiḥ prabhedo'vāntarabhedo yasyāḥ sā tathā sṛṣṭiriti śeṣaḥ| tadetat kārikayā vyākhyātam -- 
aṣṭavikalpo daivastairyagyonaśca pañcadhā bhavati|
mānuṣyaścaikavidhaḥ samāsato bhautikaḥ sargaḥ|| iti ||
brāhmaprājāpatyaindrapaitragāndharvayākṣarākṣasapaiśācā ityaṣṭavidho daivaḥ sargaḥ| paśumṛgapakṣisarīsṛpasthāvarā iti tairyagyonaḥ pañcavidhaḥ | mānuṣyasargaścaikaprakāra iti | bhautiko bhūtānāṃ vyaṣṭiprāṇināṃ virājaḥ sakāśāt sarga ityarthaḥ|| 46 ||
avāntarasṛṣṭerapyuktāyāḥ puruṣārthatvamāha -- 
ābrahmastambaparyantaṃ tatkṛte sṛṣṭirāvivekāt || 47 ||
caturmukhamārabhya sthāvarāntā vyaṣṭisṛṣṭirapi virāṭsṛṣṭivadeva puruṣārthā bhavati | tattatpuruṣāṇāṃ vivekakhyātiparyantamityartaḥ| 47 ||
vyaṣṭisṛṣṭāvapi vibhāgamāha sūtratrayeṇa -- 
urdhvaṃ sattvaviśālā || 48 ||
ūrdhvaṃ bhūrlokādupari sṛṣṭiḥ sattvādhikā bhavatītyarthaḥ|| 48 ||
tamoviśālā mūlataḥ || 49 ||
mūlato bhūrlokādadhā ityarthaḥ|| 49 ||
madhye rajoviśālā || 50 ||
madhye bhūrloke ityartaḥ|| 50 ||
nanvekasyā eva prakṛteḥ kena nimittena sattvādiviśālatayā vicitrāḥ sṛṣṭaya ityākāṅkṣāyāmāha -- 
karmavaicitryāt pradhānaceṣṭā garbhadāsavat || 51 ||
vicitrakarmanimittādeva yathoktā pradhānasya ceṣṭā kāryavaicitryarūpā bhavati vaicitrye dṛṣṭānto ragbhadāsavaditi | yathā garbhāvasthāmārabhya yo dāsastasya bhṛtyavāsanāpāṭavena nānāprakārā ceṣṭā paricaryyā svāmyarthe bhavati, tadvadityarthaḥ|| 51 ||
nanu cedīrdhvaṃ sattvaviśālā sṛṣṭirasti tarhi tata eva kṛtārthatvāt puruṣasya kiṃ mokṣeṇeti , tatrāha -- 
āvṛttistatrāpyuttarottarayoniyogāddheyaḥ|| 52 ||
tatrāpyūrdhvagatāvapi satyāmāvṛttirastyata uttarottarayoniyogādadho'dhoyoniḍanmanaḥ so'pi loko heya ityarthaḥ|| 52 ||
samānaṃ jarāmaraṇādijaṃ duḥkham|| 53 ||
kiñca ūrdhvādhogatānāṃ brahmādisthāvarāntānāṃ sarveṣāmeva jarāmaraṇādijaṃ duḥkhaṃ sādhāraṇamato'pi heya ityartaḥ| 53 ||
kiṃ bahunā kāraṇe layādapi na kṛtakṛtyatetyāha -- 
na kāraṇalayāt kṛtakṛtyatā magnavadutthānāt|| 54 ||
vivekajñānābhāve yadā mahadādiṣu vairāgyaṃ prakṛtyupāsanayā bhavati, tadā prakṛtau layo bhavati, 'vairāgyāt prakṛtilaya' iti vacanāt | tasmāt kāraṇalayādapi na kṛtakṛtyatāsti, magnavadutthānāt | yathā jale magnaḥ puruṣaḥ punarucciṣṭhati, evameva prakṛtilīnāḥ puruṣā īśvarabhāvena punarāvirbhavanti | saṃskārāderakṣayeṇa punarāgāmivyaktervivekakhyātiṃ vinā doṣadāhānupapatterityarthaḥ|| 54 ||
nanu kāraṇaṃ kenā'pi na kāryate'taḥ svatantrā kathaṃ svāpāsakasya duḥkhanidānamutthānaṃ punaḥ karoti, tatrāha -- 
akāryatve'pi tadyogaḥ pāravaśyāt|| 55 ||
prakṛterakāryatve'pyapreryatve'pyanyecchānadhīnatve'pi, tadyogaḥ -- punarutthānaucityaṃ tallīnasya | kutaḥ? pāravaśyāt -- puruṣārthatantratvāt | vivekakhyātirūpapuruṣārthavaśena prakṛtyā punarutthāpyate svalīna ityarthaḥ| puruṣārthādayaśca prakṛtena prerakāḥ kintu pravṛttisvabhāvāyāḥ pravṛttau nimittānīti na svātantryakṣatiḥ| tathā ca yogasūtram"nimittamaprayojakaṃ prakṛtīnāṃ varaṇabhedastu tataḥ kṣetrikavat" iti | varaṇabhedaḥ -- pratibandhanivṛttiḥ|| 55 ||
prakṛtilayāt puruṣasyotthāne pramāṇamapyāha -- 
sa hi sarvavit sarvakartā || 56 ||
sa hi pūrvasarge kāraṇalīnaḥ sargāntare sarvavit sarvakartteśvara ādipuruṣo bhavati | prakṛtilaye tasyaiva prakṛtipadaprāptyaucityāt | tadeva "saktaḥ saha karmaṇā" iti, "liṅgaṃ mano yatra niṣiktamasya" ityādi śruterityarthaḥ|| 56 ||
nanvevamīśvarapratiṣedhānupapattistatrāha -- 
īdṛśeṣvarasiddhiḥ siddhā|| 57 ||
prakṛtilīnasya janyeśvarasya siddhiḥ, "ya sarvajñaḥ sarvalidyasya jñānamayaṃ tapaḥ" ityādi śrutibhyaḥ sarvasammataiva| nityeṣvarasyaiva vivādāspadatvādityarthaḥ| sūtradvayamidaṃ vyākhyāya pāravaśyamapi pratipādayati 'sahīti ' sūtreṇa | sa hi paraḥ puruṣasāmānyaṃ sarvajñānaśaktimat sarvakartṛtāśaktimacca| ayaskāntavat sannidhimātreṇa prerakatvādityarthaḥ|tadā cāsamāptārtha puruṣasānnidhyāt tadarthamanyecchā'nadhīnāyā api prakṛteḥ pravṛttirāvaśyakīti ||
nanvevamīśvarapratiṣedhavirodhastatrāha -- īdṛśeśvarasiddhiḥ siddhā|| sānnidhyamātreṇeśvarasya siddhistu śrutismṛtiṣu sarvasammatetyarthaḥ||
aṅguṣṭamātraḥ puruṣo madhya ātmani tiṣṭati |
īśāno bhūtabhavyasya na tato vibhuraśnute||
sṛjate ca guṇān sarvān kṣetrajñastvanupaśyati|
guṇāt vikriyate sarvānudāsīnavadīśvaraḥ||
ityādiśrutismṛtayaścaitādṛśeśvare pramāṇamiti|| 57 ||
dvitīyādhyāyādimārabhyaitāvatparyantaṃ sūtravyūhaiḥ pradhānasṛṣṭiḥ samāpitā | itaḥ paraṃ mokṣopapattyarthaṃ pradhānalṛṣṭerjñānipuruṣāṃ pratyatyantanivṛttiratyantalayākhyā vaktavyā| tadupapattyarthamādau pradhānasṛṣṭeḥ prayojanaṃ dvitīyādhyāyasyādisūtre diṅmātreṇoktaṃ vistarataḥ pratipādayati -- 
pradhānasṛṣṭiḥ parārthaṃ svato'pyabhoktṛtvāduṣṭrakuṅkumavahanavat || 58 ||
pradhānasya svata eva sṛṣṭiryadyapi tathāpi parārthamanyasya bhogā'pavargārtham| yathoṣṭrasya kuṅkumavahanaṃ svāmyartham| kutaḥ? abhoktṛtvādacetanatvena bhogāpavargāsambhavādityarthaḥ| nanu vimuktamokṣārthaṃ svārthaṃ vetyanena svārthāpi sṛṣṭiraktena cet | satyam| tathāpi puruṣārthatāṃ vinā svārthatā'pi na siddhyati | svārtho hi pradhānasya kṛtabhogāpavargāt puruṣādātmavimokṣaṇamiti| nanu bhṛtyatulyā cet prakṛtistarhi kathaṃ svāmino duḥkhārthamapi pravartata iti cenna | sukhārthapravṛttyaiva nāntarīyakaduḥkhasambhavād duṣṭabhṛtyatulyatvādveti || 58 ||
nanu pradānasyācetanasya svataḥ sraṣṭatvameva nopapadyate rathādḥ paraprayatnenaiva pravṛttidarśanāditi , tatrāha -- 
acetanatve'pi kṣīravacteṣṭitaṃ pradhānasya || 59 ||
yatā kṣīraṃ puruṣaprayatnanairapekṣyeṇa svayameva dadhirūpeṇapariṇamate| evamacetanatve'pi paraprayatnaṃ vināpi mahadādirūpapariṇāmaḥ pradhānasya bhavatītyarthaḥ|" dhenuvadvatsāye'tyanena sūtreṇāsya na paunaruktyam| tatra karaṇa pravṛttereva vicāritatvāt dhenīnāṃ cetanatvācceti || 59 ||
dṛṣṭāntāntarapradarśanapūrvakamuktārthahetumāha -- 
karmavad dṛṣṭervā kālādeḥ|| 60 ||
kālādeḥ karmavadvā svataḥ pradhānasya ceṣṭitaṃ siddhyati, dṛṣṭatvāt| athaiko gacchati[atrāyamabhisaṃdhiḥ -- yathaikasya sūryasya gativiśeṣamanuruddhyā vasaṃtagrīṣmādiṣaṅṛtavaḥ śrutiṣu pratipāditāḥ, na kiṃtu āditya eva sarvaṃ kāraṇam| etadeva śatapathabrāhmaṇe spaṣṭaṃ pratipāditam, sā ceyam śrutiḥ -- "ādityo vā sarva ṛtavaḥ yadodeti tadā vasaṃtaḥ, yadā saṃgavo'tha grīṣmaḥ -- yadā madhyaṃdino'tha varṣā, yadāparāhṇo'tha hemantaśiśirau' iti ||] ṛturitaraśca pravartata ityādirūpaṃ kālādikarma svata eva bhavatyevaṃ pradhānasyāpi syāt| kalpanāyā dṛṣṭānusāritvādityarthaḥ
|| 60 ||
nanu tathāpi mamedaṃ bhogādisādhanamiti pratisandhānābhāvānmūḍhāyāḥ prakṛteḥ kadācit pravṛttirapi na syād viparītā ca pravṛttiḥ syāt | tatrāha -- 
svabhāvācceṣṭitamanabhisandhānād bhṛtyavat|| 61 ||
yathā prakṛṣṭabhṛtyasya svabhāvāt saṃskārādeva pratiniyatā''vaśyakī ca svāmisevā pravartate, na tu svabhogābhiprāyeṇa, tathaiva prakṛteśceṣṭitaṃ saṃskārādevetyarthaḥ|| 61 ||
karmākṛṣṭervāditaḥ || 62 ||
vāśabdā'tra samuccaye | yataḥ karmānādyataḥ karmabhirākarṣaṇādapi pradhānasyāvaśyakī vyavasthitā ca pravṛttirityarthaḥ|| 62 ||
tadevaṃ pradhānasya parārthataḥ sraṣṭṭatve siddhe paraprayojanasamāptau svata eva pradhānanivṛttyā mokṣaḥ siddhyatītyāha praghaṭṭakena -- 
viviktabodhāt sṛṣṭinivṛttiḥpradhānasya
sṛdavat pāke || 63 ||
viviktapuruṣajñānāt paravairāgyeṇa puruṣārthasamāptau pradhānasya sṛṣṭirnivartate| yathā pāke niṣpanne pāvakasya vyāpāro nivartata ityarthaḥ| ivamevātyantikapralaya ityucyate| tathā ca śrutiḥ -- tasyābhidhyānādyojanāt tattvabhāvād bhūyaścānteviśvamāyānivṛtti'riti || 63 ||
nanvevamekapuruṣasyopādhau vivekajñānotpattyai prakṛteḥ sṛṣṭinivṛttau sarvamuktiprasaṅga iti, tatrāha -- 
itara itaravat taddoṣāt|| 64 ||
itarastu viviktabodharahita itaravad baddhavadeva prakṛtyā tiṣṭhati| kutaḥ? taddoṣāt | tasya pradhānasyaiva tatpuruṣārthasamāpānākhyadoṣādityarthaḥ| taduktaṃ yogasūtre -- " kṛtārtha prati naṣṭamapyanaṣṭaṃ tadanyasādhāraṇatvāt" iti
tathā ca pūrvasūtre yā pradhānanivṛttiruktā sā viviktabāddhṛpuruṣaṃ pratyeveti bhāvaḥ| viśvamāyāśrutirapi jñāninaṃ pratyeva mantavyā| 'ajā' miti śrutyaikavākyatvāditi || 64 ||
sṛṣṭinivṛtteḥ phalamāha -- 
dvayorekatarasya vaudāsīnyamapavargaḥ || 65 ||
dvayoḥ pradhānapuruṣayorevaudāsīnyamekākitā | parasparaviyoga iti yāvat | so'pavargaḥ| athavā puruṣasyaiva kaivalyam| ahaṃ muktaḥ syāmityeva puruṣārthatādarśanādatyarthaḥ|| 65 ||
nanvekapuruṣamuktāveva vivekākāravṛttyā viraktā prakṛtiḥ kathamanyapuruṣārthaṃ punaḥ sṛṣṭau pravartataim | na ca prakṛteraṃśabhedānnaiva doṣa iti vācyam| muktapuruṣokaraṇairapi pṛthivyādibhiranyasya bhogyasṛṣcidarśanāditi| tatrāha -- 
anyasṛṣṭyuparāge'pi na virajyate, prabuddharajjutattvasyevoragaḥ|| 66 ||
ekasmin puruṣe viviktabodhādviraktamapi pradhānaṃ nānyasmin puruṣe sṛṣṭyuparāgāya viraktaṃ bhavati, kintu taṃ prati sṛjatyeva | yathā prabuddharajjutattvasyaivorago bhayādikaṃ na janayati mūḍhaṃ prati tu janayatyevetyarthaḥ| urāgatulyatvaṃ ca pradhānasya rajjutulye puruṣe samāropaṇāditi| evaṃvidhaṃ rajjusarpādidṛṣṭāntānāmāśayamabuddhvaivā'budhāḥ kecidvedāntibruvāḥ prakṛteratyantatucchatvaṃ manomātratvaṃ vā tulayanti| etena prakṛtisatyatāvādisāṅkhyoktadṛṣṭāntena śrutismṛtyarthā bodhanīyāḥ, na kevalaṃ dṛṣṭāntavattvenāyamarthaḥ siddhyati || 66 ||
karmanimittayogācca || 67 ||
sṛṣṭau nimittaṃ yat karma tasya sambandhādapyanyapuruṣārthaṃ sṛjatītyarthaḥ|| 67 ||
nanu sarveṣāṃ puruṣāṇāmaprārthakatayā nairapekṣyāviśeṣe'pi kañcit pratyeva pradhānaṃ pravartate, kañcit prati nivartata ityatra kiṃ niyāmakam| na ca karma niyāmakam | kasya puruṣasya kiṃ karmetyatra niyāmakābhāvāditi| tatrāhā -- 
nairapekṣye prakṛtyupakāre'viveko nimittam|| 68 ||
puruṣāṇāṃnairapekṣye'ryayaṃ me svāmyayamevāhamityavivekaideva prakṛtiḥ sṛṣṭyādibhiḥ puruṣānupakarotītyarthaḥ| tathā ca yasmai puruṣāyātmānamavivicya darśayituṃ vāsanā vartate taṃ pradhānaṃ pravartata ityeva niyāmakamati bhāvaḥ|| 68 ||
pravṛttisvabhāvatvāt kathaṃ viveke'pi nivṛttirupapdyatāṃ, tatrāha -- 
nartakīvat pravṛttasyāpi nivṛttiścāritārthyāt || 69 ||
puruṣārthameva pradhānasya pravṛttisvabhāvo, na tu sāmānyena | ataḥ pravṛttamyāpi pradhānasya puruṣārthasamāptiruparicāritārthatve sati nivṛttiryuktā| yathā pariṣadbhayo nṛtyadarśanārthaṃ pravṛtttāyā nartakyāstatsiddhaunivṛttirityarthaḥ|| 69 ||
nivṛttau hetvantaramāha -- 
doṣabodhe'pi nopasarpaṇaṃ pradhānasya kulavadhūvat || 70 ||
puruṣeṇa pariṇāmitvaduḥkhātmakatvādidoṣadarśanādapi lajjitāyāḥ prakṛteḥ punarna pratyupasarpaṇaṃ kulavadhūvat | yathā svāminā me doṣo dṛṣṭa ityavadhāraṇena lajjitā kulavadhīrna svāminamupasarpati tadvadityarthaḥ| taduktaṃ nāradīye -- 
savikārāpi mauḍhyena ciraṃ bhuktvā guṇātmanā|
prakṛtirjñātadoṣeyaṃ lajjayeva nivartate || iti ||
etadevoktaṃ kārikayāpi -- 
prakṛteḥ sukumārataraṃ na kiñcidastīti me matirbhavati|
yā dṛṣṭāsmīti punarna darśanamupaiti puruṣasya || iti || 70 
nanu puruṣārthaṃ cet pradhānapravṛttistarhi bandhamokṣābhyāṃ puruṣasya pariṇāmāpattiriti tatrāha -- 
naikāntato bandhamokṣo puruṣasyāvivekādṛte || 71 ||
duḥkhayogaviyogarūpau bandhamokṣau puruṣasya naikāntatastattvataḥ, kintu caturthasūtravakṣyamāṇaprakāreṇāvivekādevetyarthaḥ|| 71 ||
paramārthatastu yathoktau bandhamokṣau prakṛterevetyāha -- 
prakṛterāñjasyāt sasaṅgatvākat paśuvat || 72 ||
prakṛtereva tattvato duḥkhena bandhamokṣo sasaṅgatvād duḥkhasādhanairdharmādibhirliptatvāt | yathā paśuḥ rajjvā liptatayā bandhamokṣabhāgī tadvadityarthaḥ| etaduktaṃ kārikayā -- 
tasmānna badhyate'ddhā na mucyate nāpi saṃsarati puruṣaḥ|
saṃsarati badhyate mucyate ca nānāśrayā prakṛtiḥ|| iti || 
' dvayorekatarasya vaudāsīnyamapavarga' iti sūtre ca yaḥ puruṣasyāpavarga uktaḥ sa pratibimbarūpasya mithyāduḥkasya viyoga eveti || 62 ||
tatra kaiḥ sādhanairbandhaḥ kairvā mokṣa ityākāṅkṣāyāmāha -- 
rūpaiḥ saptabhirātmānaṃ badhnāti pradhānaṃ kośakāravadvimocayatyekarūpeṇa || 73 ||
dharmavairāgyaiśvaryadharmājñānāvairāgyānaiśvaryaiḥ saptabhī rūpadharmairduḥkhahetubhiḥ prakṛtirātmānaṃ duḥkhena badhnāti, kośakāravat | kośakārakṛtiryathā svanirmitenāvāsenātmānaṃ badhnāti tadvat | saiva ca prakṛtirekarūpeṇa jñānanaivātmānaṃ duḥkhānmocayatītyarthaḥ|| 73 ||
nanubandhamuktī avivekāditi yaduktaṃ, tadayuktam| avivekasyāheyānupādeyatvāt| loke duḥkhasya tadabhāvasukhādereva ca svato heyopādeyatvāt | anyathā dṛṣṭahānirityāśaṅkya caturthasūtroktaṃ svayaṃ vivṛṇoti -- 
nimittatvamavivekasya na dṛṣṭahāni ḥ || 74 ||
avivekasya puruṣeṣu bandhamokṣanimittatvameva puroktaṃ, na tvaviveka eva tāviti nāto dṛṣṭahānirityarthaḥ| etacca prathamādhyāyasūtreṣu spaṣṭam|
avivekanimittāt prakṛtipuruṣayoḥ saṃyogaḥ| tasmācca saṃyogādutpadyamānasya prākṛtaduḥkhasya puruṣe yaḥ pratibimbaḥ sa e duḥkhabhogo duḥkhasambandhastannivṛttireva ca mokṣākhyaḥ puruṣārtha iti || 74 ||
tadevamādisargamārabhyātyantikalayaparyanto'khilapariṇāmaḥ pradhānatadvikārāṇāmeva| puruṣastu kabaṭasthapūrṇacinmātra evetyadhyāyadvayena vistarato vivecitam| tasya vivekasya niṣpattyupāyeṣu sārabhūtamabhyāsamāha -- 
tattvābhyāsānneti netīti tyāgādvivekasiddhiḥ|| 75 ||
prakṛtiparyanteṣu jaḍeṣu neti netītyabhimānatyāgarūpāt tatvā'bhyāsādvivekaniṣpattirbhavati| itarata sarvamabhyāsasyāṅgamātramityartaḥ| tathāca śrutiḥ -- "athāta ādeśo neti neti | na hyetasmāditi netyanyat paramasti sa eṣa ātmā neti neti" ityādiriti ||
avyaktādyaviśeṣānte vikāre'smiṃśca varṇite |
cetanācetanānyatvajñānena jñānamucyate |. iti ||
yathā -- 
asmisthūṇaṃ snāyuyutaṃ māṃsaśoṇitalepanam|
carmāvanaddhaṃ durgāndhipūrṇaṃ rogāyatanamāturamā|
rajasvalamasanniṣṭhaṃ bhūtāvāsamimaṃ tyajet|
nadīkīlaṃ yathā vṛkṣo vṛkṣaṃ vā śakuniryathā|
tathā tyajannimaṃ dehaṃ kṛcchrād grāhādvimucyate|| iti ||
etadeva kārikayā'pyuktam -- 
evaṃ tattvābhyāsānnāsmi me nāhamityapariśeṣam|
aviparyayādviśuddhaṃ kevalamutpadyate jñānam|| iti || 
nāsmātyātmanaḥ kartṛtvaniṣedhaḥ | na me iti saṅganiṣedhaḥ | nāhamiti tadātmyaniṣedha| kevalamityasya vivaraṇam, aviparyayādviśuddhamiti | ato'ntarā viparyayeṇa viplutamityarthaḥ| idameva kevalatvaṃ siddhiśabdena sūtre proktam| "vivekaśyātiraviplavā hānopāyaḥ" iti yogasūtreṇaitādṛśajñānasyaiva mokṣahetutvasiddhiriti|| 75 ||
vivekasiddhau viśeṣamāha -- 
adhikāriprabhedānna niyamaḥ|| 76 ||
mandādyadhikāribhedasattvād abhyāse kriyamāṇe'pyasminneva janmani vivekaniṣpattirbhavatīti niyamo nāstītyarthaḥ| ata uttamādhikāramabhyāsapāṭavenātmanaḥ sapmādayediti bhāvaḥ|| 76 ||
vivekaniṣpattyaiva nistāro, nānyathetyāha -- 
bādhitānuvṛttyā madhyavivekato'pyupabhogaḥ || 77 ||
sakṛtsamprajñātayogenātmasākṣātkārottaraṃ madhyavivekāvastho madhyamaviveke'pi sati puruṣe bādhitānāmapi duḥkhādīnāṃ prārabdhavaśāt pratibimbarūpeṇa puruṣe'nuvṛttyā bogo bhavatītyarthaḥ| vivekaniṣpattiścāpunarutthānād asamprajñānādeva bhavatītyatastasyāṃ satyāṃ na bhogo'stīti pratipādayituṃ madyavivekata ityuktam| mandavivekastu sākṣātkārāt pūrvaṃ śravaṇamananadhyānamātrarūpa iti vibhāgaḥ|| 77 ||
jīvanmuktaśca || 78 ||
jīvanmukto'pi madhyavivekāvastha eva bhavatītyarthaḥ|| 78 ||
jīvanmukte pramāṇamāha -- 
upadeśyopadeṣṭṭatvāt tatsiddhiḥ || 79 ||
śāstreṣu vivekaviṣaye guruśiṣyabhāvaśravaṇajjīvanmuktasiddhirityarthaḥ|
jīvanmuktasyaivopadeṣṭṭatvasambhavāditi || 79 ||
śrutiśca|| 80 ||
śrutiśca jīvanmukte'sti -- 
dīkṣayaiva naro mucyet tiṣṭhenmukto'pi vigrahe|
kulālacakramadhyastho vicchinno'pi bhramed ghaṭaḥ||
'brahmaiva san brahmāpyetī'tyādiriti || nāradīyasmṛtirapi -- 
pūrvabhyāsabalāt kārye na loko na ca vaidikaḥ|
apuṇyapāpaḥ sarvātmā jīvanmuktaḥ sa ucyate|| iti || 80 ||
nanu śravaṇamātreṇāpyupadeṣṭatvaṃ syāt, tatrāha -- 
itarathāndhaparamparā || 81 ||
itarathā mandavivekasyāpyupadeṣṭatve'ndhaparamparāpattirityarthaḥ| sāmagvyeṇātmatattvamajñātvā cedupadiśet kasmiṃścidaṃśe svabhrameṇa śiṣyamapi bhrāntīkuryāt| so'pyanyaṃ so'pyanyaṃmityevamandhaparampareti|| 81 ||
nanu jñānena karmakṣaye sati kathaṃ jīvanaṃ syāt, tatrāha -- 
cakrabhramaṇavad dhṛtaśarīraḥ || 82 ||
kulālakarmanivṛttāvapi pūrvakarmavegāt svayameva kiyatkālaṃ cakraṃ bhramati, evaṃ jñānottaraṃ karmānutpattāvapi prārabdhakarmavegena ceṣṭamānaṃ śarīraṃ dhṛtvājīvanmuktastiṣṭhatītyarthaḥ|| 82 ||
nanu jñānahetusamprajñātayogena bhogādivāsanākṣaye kathaṃ śarīradhāraṇam| na ca yogasya saṃskārābhibhāvakatve kiṃ maunamiti vācyam| 'vyutthānanirodhasaṃskārayorabhibhāvaprādurbhāvau nirodhalakṣaṇacittānvayo nirodha pariṇāma' iti yogasūtratastatsiddheḥ| cirakālīnasya viṣayāntarāveśasya viṣayāntarasaṃskārābhibhāvakatayā loke'pyanubhavācceti tatrāha -- 
saṃskāraleśatastatsāddhiḥ || 83 ||
śarīradhāraṇahetavo ye viṣayasaṃskārāsteṣāmalpāvaśeṣāt tasya śarīradhāraṇasya siddhirityarthaḥ| atra cāvidyāsaṃskāraleśasya sattā nāpekṣyate| avidyāyā janmādirūpakarmavipākā''rambhamātre hetutvāt | yogabhāṣye vyāsaistathā vyākhyātatvāt | vītarāgajanmādarśanāditi nyāyācca| na tu prārabdhaphalakakarmabhoge'pīti | yatra ca niyamenāvidyāpekṣyate sa prayāsaviśeṣarūpo bhogo mūḍheṣvevāsti | jīvanmuktānāṃ tu bhogābhāsa eveti prāguktam| yattu kaścidavidyāsaṃskāraleśo'pi jīvanmuktasya tiṣṭhatītyāha| tanna | dharmādharmotpattiprasaṅgat andhaparamparāprasaṅgāt| avidyāsaṃskāraleśasattākalpane prayojanābhāvācca | etacca brahmamīmāṃsābhāṣye prapañcitamiti || 83 ||
śāstravākyārthamupasaṃharati -- 
vivekānniḥśeṣaduḥkhanivṛttau kṛtakṛtyatā
netarānnetarāt || 84 ||
uktāyā vivekasiddhitaḥ paravairāgyadvārā sarvavṛttinirodhena yadā niḥśeṣato bādhitābādhitasādhāraṇyenākhiladuḥkhaṃ nivartte tadaiva puruṣaḥ kṛtakṛtyo bhati | netarājjīvanmuktyāderapītyarthaḥ| netarāditi vīpsā'dhyāyasamāptau || 84 ||
atyantalayaparyantaḥ kāryo'vyaktasya nātmanaḥ||
prokta evaṃ viveko'tra paravairāgyasādhanam|| 1 ||
iti śrīvijñānabhikṣunirmite kāpilasāṅkhyapravacanasya bhāṣye
vairāgyādhyāyastṛtīyaḥ|| 3||
atha caturtho'dhyāyaḥ|| 4 ||
śāstrasiddhākhyāyikājātamukhenedānīṃ vivekajñānasādhanāni pradarśanīyānītyetadarthaṃ caturthādhyāya ārabhyate|
rājaputravat tattvopadeśāt || 1 ||
pūrvapādaśeṣasūtrasthaviveko'nuvarttate | rājaputrasyeva tattvopadeśādviveko jāyata ityarthaḥṣa atreyamākhyāyikā -- kaścidrājaputro gaṇḍarkṣajanmanāpurānniḥsāritaḥ śabareṇa kenacit poṣito'haṃ śabara ityabhimanyamāna āste| ta jīvantaṃ jñātvā kaścidamātyaḥ prabodhayati -- na tvaṃ śabaro rājaputro'sīti | sa yathā jhaṭityeva cāṇḍālābhimānaṃ tyaktvā tāttvikaṃ rājabhāvamevālambate rājāhamasmīti | evamevādipuruṣāt paripūrṇacinmātremābhivyaktādutpannastvaṃ tasyāṃśa iti kāruṇikopadeśāt prakṛtyabhimānaṃ tyaktvā brahmaputratvādahamapi brahmaiva, na tu tadvilakṣaṇaḥ saṃsārītyevaṃ svasvarūpamevālambata ityarthaḥ|
tathā gāruḍe -- 
yathākahemamaṇinā sarvaṃ hemamayaṃ jagat |
tathaiva jātamīśena jātenāpyakhilaṃ bhavet||
grahāviṣṭo dvijaḥ kaścicchūdro'hamiti manyate|
grahanāśāt punaḥ svīyaṃ brahmaṇyaṃ manyate yathā||
māyāviṣṭastathā jīvo deho'hamiti manyate|
māyānāśāt punaḥ svīyaṃ rūpaṃ brahmāsmi manyate|| iti || 1 ||
strīśūdrādayo'pi brāhmaṇena brāhmaṇasyopadeśaṃ śrutvā kṛtārthāḥ syuritye tadarthamākhyāyikāntaraṃ darśayati -- 
piśācavadanyārthopadeśe'pi || 2 ||
arjunārthaṃ śrīkṛṣṇena tattvopadeśe kriyamāṇe'pi samāpasthasya piśācasya vivekajñānaṃ jātamevamanyeṣāmapi bhavedityarthaḥ|| 2 ||
yadi ca sakṛdupadeśājjñānaṃ na jāyate tadopadeśāvṛttirapi karttavyetītihāsāntareṇāha -- 
āvṛttirasakṛdupadeśāt || 3||
upadeśāvṛttirapi kartavyā| chāndogyādau śvetaketvādikaṃ pratyāruṇiprabhṛtīnāsakṛdupadeśehāsādityarthaḥ|| 3 ||
vairāgyārthaṃ nidarśanapūrvakamātmasaṅghātasya bhaṅguratvādikaṃ pratipādayati -- 
pitāputravadubhayordṛṣṭatvāt|| 4 ||
svasya pitāputrayorivātmano'pi maraṇotpattyordṛṣṭatvādanumitatvādvairāgyeṇa viveko bhavatītyarthaḥ| taduktam -- 
ātmanaḥ pitṛputrābhyāmanumeyau bhavāpyayau || iti || 4 ||
itaḥ paramutpannajñānasya viraktasya ca jñānaniṣpattyaṅgānyākhyāyikoktadṛṣṭāntairdarśayati -- 
śyenavat sukhaduḥkhī tyāgaviyogābhyām|| 5 ||
parigrahe na kartavyo yato dravyāṇāṃ tyāgena lekaḥ sukhī viyogena ca duḥkhī bhavati śyenavadityarthaḥ| śyeno hi sāmiṣaḥ kenāpyapahatyā''miṣādviyojya duḥkhī kriyate | svayaṃ tyajati tadā duḥkhādvimucyate| taduktam
sāmiṣaṃ kuraraṃ jaghnurbalino'nye nirāmiṣāḥ|
tadāmiṣaṃ parityajya sa sukhaṃ samavidanta || iti ||
tathā manunā'pyuktam -- 
nadīkulaṃ yathā vṛkṣo vṛkṣaṃ vā śakuniryathā|
tathā tyajannimaṃ dehaṃ kṛcchrād grāhādvimucyate|| || 5 ||
ahinirlvāyinīvat[ atra cobhābhyāṃ sūtrābhyāṃ brahmopāsakasya dehatyāgasamaye ārabdhadehasya karmaṇaḥ bhogenaiva nāśāt tatra dehatyakturdaividhyam darśayati| ādyena ca yaḥ karmādhīnaṃ dehapatanamapekṣate saḥ 'nadīkūlaṃ yathā' ityādi dṛṣṭāntena darśitaḥ| dvitīyena ca jñānakarmasamuccayāt bhīṣmādaya iva svādhīnamṛtyurapi svecchayaiva dehaṃ tyajati tadeva 'vṛkṣaṃ vā śakuniryathā' ityādi dṛṣṭāntena pratipāditam | tadeva ca bhagavadgītāyāṃ pratipāditam -- 
vāsāṃsi jīrṇāni yathā vihāya navāni gṛhṇāti narā parāṇi|
tathā śarīrāṇi vihāya jīrṇānyanyāni saṃyāti navāni dehī ||] || 6 ||
yathāhirjīrṇāṃ tvacaṃ parityajyanāyāsena heyabudhyā, tathaiva mumukṣuḥ prakṛtiṃ bahukālopabhuktāṃ jīrṇāṃ heyabudhyā tyajedityarthaḥ| taduktam -- 'jīrṇātvacamivoraga' iti || 6 ||
tyaktaṃ ca prakṛtyādikaṃ punarna svīkuryādityatrāha -- 
ḍhinnahastavadvā|| 7 ||
yathā chinnaṃ hastaṃ punaḥ ko'pi nādatte tathaivaitat tyaktaṃ punarnābhimanyetetyarthaḥ| vāśabdo'pyarthe|| 7 ||
asādhanānucintanaṃ bandhāya bharatavat || 8 ||
vivekasya yadantaraṅgasādhanaṃ na bhavati , ceddharmo'pi syāt tathāpi tadanucintanaṃ tadanuṣṭhāne cittasya tātparyyaṃ na kartavyaṃ, yatastad bandhāya bhavati vivekavismārakatayā bhāratavat| yathā bharatasya rājarṣerdharmyamapi dīnānāthahariṇaśāvakasya poṣaṇamityarthaḥ| tathā ca jaḍabharataṃ prakṛtya viṣṇupurāṇe -- 
capalaṃ capale tasmin dūragaṃ dūragāmini |
āsīccetaḥ samāsaktaṃ tasmin hariṇapotake || iti || 8 ||
bahubhiryoge virodho rāgādibhiḥ kumārīśaṅkhavat || 9 ||
bahubhiḥ saṅgo na kāryaḥ| bahubhiḥ saṅge hi rāgādyabhivyaktyā kalaho bhavati yogabhraṃśakaḥ | yathā kumārīhastaśaṅkhānāmanyonyasaṅgena jhaṇatkāro bhavatītyarthaḥ|| 9 ||
dvābhyāmapi tathaiva || 10 ||
dvābhyāṃ yoge'pi tathaiva virodho bhavatyata ekākinaiva sthātavyamityarthaḥ| taduktam -- 
vāso bahūnāṃ kalaho bhavedvārtā dvayorapi |
eka eva caret tasmāt kumāryā iva kaṅkaṇam|| iti || 10 ||
āśā vai vaśyavirase citte santoṣavarjite |
mlāne vaktramivādarśe na jñānaṃ pratibimbati ||
iti vacanānnirāśatā yoginānuṣṭheyetyāha -- 
nirāśaḥ sukhī piṅgāvat || 11 ||
āśāṃ tyakatvā puruṣaḥ santoṣākhyasukhavān bhūyāt piṅgāvat| yathā piṅgalā nāma veśyā kāntārthinī kāntamalabdhvā nirviṇṇā satī vihāyāśāṃ sukhinī babhūva, tadvadityarthaḥ| taduktam -- 
āśā hi paramaṃ duḥkhaṃ nairāśyaṃ paramaṃ sukham|
yathā sañchidya kāntāśāṃ sukhaṃ suṣvāpa piṅgalā|| iti ||
nanvāśānivṛttyā duḥkhanivṛttiḥ syāt| sukhaṃ tu kutaḥ? sādhanābhāvāditi | ucyate | cittasya sattvaprādhānyena svābhāvikaṃ yat sukhamāśayā pihitaṃ tiṣṭhati tadevāśāvigame labdhavṛttikaṃ bhavati| tejaḥpratibaddhajalaśaityavaditi na tatra sādhanāpekṣā| etadeva cārthe sukhamityucyata iti || 11 ||
yogapratibandhakatvādārambho'pi bhogārthaṃ na kartavyo'nyathaiva tadupapatterityāha -- 
anārambhe'pi paragṛhe sukhī sarpavat || 12 ||
sukhī bhavediti śeṣaḥ| śeṣaṃ sugamam | taduktam -- 
gṛharambho hi duḥkhāya na sukhāya kathañcana|
sarpaḥparakṛtaṃ veśma praviśya sukhamedhate || 12 ||
śāstrebhyo gurubhyaśca sāra eva grāhyo'nyathābhyupagamavādādibhiraṃśato'sārabhāge'nyonyavirodhenārthabāhulyena caikāgratāyā asambhavādityāha -- 
bahuśāstragurupāsane'pi sārādānaṃ ṣaṭpadavat|| 13 ||
karttavyamiti śeṣaḥ| anyat sugamam| taduktam -- 
amubhyaśca mahadbhyaśca śāstrebhyaḥ kuśalo naraḥ|
sarvataḥ sāramādadyāt puṣyebhya iva ṣaṭpadaḥ|| iti ||
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe ca -- 
sārabhūtamupāsīta jñānaṃ yat svārthasādhakam|
jñānānāṃ bahutā yaiṣā yogāvidhnakarī hi sā |
idaṃ jñeyamidaṃ jñeyamiti yastṛṣitaścaret| asau kalpasahastreṣu naiva jñānamavāpnuyāt
|| iti || 13 ||
sādhanāntaraṃ yathā tathā bhavatu, ekāgratayaiva samādhipālanadvārā vivekasākṣātkāro niṣpādanīya ityāha -- 
iṣukāravannaikacittasya samādhihāniḥ|| 14 ||
yathā śaranirmāṇayaikacittasyeṣukārasya pārśve rājño gamanenā'pi na vṛttyantaranirodho hīyate evamekāgracittasya sravathā'pi samādhihānirvṛttyantaranirodhakṣitarbhavati | tataśca viṣayāntarasañcārābhāve dhyeyasākṣātkāro'pyavaśyaṃ bhavatītyekāgratāṃ kuryādityarthaḥ| taduktam -- 
tadaivamātmanyavaruddhacitto na veda kiñcidbahirantaraṃ vā|
yatheṣukāro nṛpatiṃ vrajantamiṣau gatātmā na dadarśa pārśve|| iti || 14 ||
satyāṃ śaktau jñānabalācchāstrakṛtaniyamo vṛthā laṅghyate | tadā jñānā niṣpattyānarthakyaṃ yogino bhavatītyāha -- 
kṛtanimalaṅghanādānarthakyaṃ lokavat || 15 ||
yaḥ śāstreṣu kṛto yogināṃ niyamastasyollaṃghane jñānaniṣpattyākhyo'rtho na bhavati | lokavat| yathā loke bhaiṣajyādau vihitapathyādīnāṃ laṅghane tattatsiddhirna bhavati, tadvidityarthaḥ| aśaktyā jñānarakṣārthaṃ vā laṅghane tu na jñānapratibandhaḥ.
apetavratakarmā tu kevalaṃ brahmaṇi sthitaḥ|
brahmabhūtaścaram̐lloke brahmacārīti kathyate||
iti mokṣadharmādibhyaḥ vasiṣṭādismṛtibhyaśca| ata eva viṣṇupurāṇādau vṛthā karmatyāgina eva pāṣaṇḍatayā ninditāḥ -- 'puṃsām̐ jaṭādhāraṇaṃ mauṇḍyavatāṃ vṛthaive'tyādineti|| 15 ||
niyamavismaraṇe'pyānarthakyamāha -- 
tadvismaraṇe'pi bhekīvat|| 16 ||
sugamam| bhekyāśceyamākhyāyikā -- " kaścid rājā mṛgayāṃ gato vipine sundarīṃ kanyāṃ dadarśa| sā ca rājñā bhāryābhāvāya prārthitā niyamaṃ cakre | yadā mahyaṃ tvayā jalaṃ pradarśyate tadā mayā gantavyamiti | ekadā tu krīḍayā pariśrāntā rājānaṃ papraccha, kutra jalamiti| rājāpi samayaṃ vismṛtya jalamadarśayat | tataḥ sā bhekarājaduhitā kāmarūpiṇī bhekī bhūtvā jalaṃ viveśa| tataśca rājā jālādibhiranviṣyāpi na tāmavindadi" ti || 16 ||
śravaṇavad guruvākyamīmāṃsāyā apyāvaśyakatve itihāsamāha -- 
nopadeśaśravaṇe'pi kṛtakṛtyatā parāmarśādṛte
virocanavat || 17 ||
parāmarśo guruvākyatātparyanirṇāyako vicāraḥ| taṃ vinā upadeśavākyaśravaṇe'pi tattvajñānaniyamo nāsti| prajñāpaterupadeśaśravaṇe'pīndravirocanayormadhye virocanasya parāmarśābhāvena bhrāntatvaśruterityarthaḥ| ato gurupadiṣṭasya mananamapi kāryamiti | dṛśyate cedānīmapyekasyaiva tattvamasyupadeśasya nānārūpairarthaiḥ sambhāvanā| akhaṇḍatvamavaidharmyalakṣaṇābhedo'vibhāgaśceti || 17 ||
ata eva ca parāmarśo dṛśyate ityāha -- 
dṛṣṭastayorindrisya || 18 ||
tacchabdenoktocyamānayoḥ parāmarśaḥ| tayorindravirocanayormadhye parāmarśaḥ indrasya dṛṣṭaścetyarthaḥ
|| 18 ||
tamyagajñānārthinā ca gurusevā bahukālaṃ karttavyetyāha -- 
praṇatibrahmacaryopasarpaṇāni kṛtvā siddhirbahukālāt
tadvat || 19 ||
tadvadindrisyevānyasyāpi gurau praṇativedādhyayanasevādīna kṛtvaiva siddhistattvārthasphūrttirbhavati, nānyathetyarthaḥ| tathā ca śrutiḥ -- 
yasya deve parā bhaktiryathā deve tathā gurau|
tasyaite kathitā hyarthāḥ prakāśante mahātmanaḥ|| iti || 19 ||
na kālaniyamo vāmadevavat || 20 ||
aihikasādhanādeva bhavatītyādirjñānodaye kālaniyamo nāsti| vāmadevavat | vāmadevasya janmāntarīyasādhanebhyo garbhe'pi yathā jñānodayastathānyasyāpītyarthaḥ| tathāca śrutiḥ -- 'tadvaitat paśyannṛṣirvāmadevaḥ pratipede'haṃ
manurabhavaṃ sabaryaśceti tadidamapyetarhi ya evaṃ vedā'haṃ brahmāsmīti sa idaṃ sarvaṃ bhavatī'tyādiriti | ahaṃ manurabhavamityādikamavaidharmyalakṣaṇabhedaparaṃ , sarvavyāpakatākhyabrahmatāparaṃ vā| 'sarvaṃ samāpnoṣi tato'si sarvaḥ' ityādismaraṇāt| sa idaṃ sarvaṃ bhavatīti tvaupādhikaparicchedasyātyantocchedaparamiti || 20 ||
nanusaguṇopāsanāyā api jñonahetutvaśravaṇāt tata eva jñānaṃ bhaviṣyati, kimarthaṃ duṣkarasūkṣmayogacaryeti ? tatrāha -- 
adhyastarūpopāsanāt pāramparyeṇa
yajñopāsakānāmiva || 21 ||
siddhirityanuṣajjyate| adhyastarūpaiḥ puruṣāṇāṃ brahmaviṣṇuharādīnāmupāsanāt pāramparyeṇa brahmādilokaprāptikrameṇa sattvaśuddhidvārā vā jñānaniṣpattirna sākṣāt | yathā yājñikānāmityarthaḥ|| 21 ||
brahmādilokaparamparayāpi jñānaniṣpattau nāsti niyama ityāha -- 
itaralābhe'pyāvṛttiḥ pañcāgniyogato janmaśruteḥ|| 22 ||
nirguṇātmana itarasyādhyastarūpasya brahmalokaparyantasya lābhe'pyāvṛttirasti| kutaḥ ? devayānapathena brahmalokaṃ gatasyāpi dyuparjanyadharā'marayoṣidrapāgnipañcake pañcāhutito janmaśravaṇāt| chāndogyapañcamaprapāṭhake 'asau vāvaloko gautamāgni' rityādinetyarthaḥ| yacca brahmalokādanāvṛttivākyaṃ, tat tatraiva prāyeṇotpannajñānapuruṣaviṣayakamiti || 22 ||
jñānaniṣpattirviraktasyaivetyatra nidarśanamāha -- 
viraktasya heyahānamupādeyopādānaṃ haṃsakṣīravat || 23 ||
viraktasyaiva heyānāṃ prakṛtyādīnāṃ hānamupādeyasya cātmana upādānaṃ bhavati | yathā dugdhajalayorekībhāvāpannayormadhye'sārajalatyāgena sārabhūtakṣīropādānaṃ haṃsasyaiva, na tu kākāderityarthaḥ|| 23 ||
siddhapuruṣasaṅgādapyetadubhayaṃ bhavatītyāha -- 
labdhātiśayayogādvā tadvat|| 24 ||
labdho'tiśayo jñānakāṣṭhā yena tatsaṅgādapyuktaṃ bhavati haṃsavadetretyarthaḥ| yatālarkasya dattātreyasaṅgamamātrādeva svayaṃ vivekaḥ prādurabhūditi || 24 ||
rāgisaṅgo na kārya ityāha -- 
na kāmacāritvaṃ rāgopahate śukavat|| 25 ||
rāgopahate puruṣe kāmataḥ saṅgo na kartavyaḥ| śukavat | yathā śukapakṣī prakṛṣṭarūpa iti kṛtvā kāmacāraṃ na karoti, rūpalolupairbandhanabhayāt, tadvadityarthaḥ|| 25 ||
rāgisaṅge tu doṣamāha -- 
guṇayogāt baddhaḥ śukavat || 26 ||
teṣāṃ saṅge tu guṇayogāt tadīyarāgādiyogād baddhaḥ syāt| śukavadeva | yathā śukapakṣī vyādhasya guṇai rajjabhirvaddho bhavati, tadvadityarthaḥ| athavā guṇitayā guṇalolupairbajaddho bhavati, śukavadityarthaḥ| atraivoktaṃ saubhariṇai -- 
sa me samādhirjalavāsamitramatsyasya sahgāt sahasaiva naṣṭaḥ|
parigrahaḥ saṅagakṛto mamāyaṃ parigrahotthāśca mahāvidhitsāḥ|| iti || 26 ||
vairāgyasyāpyupāyam avadhārayati dvābhyām -- 
na bhogādrāgaśāntirmunivat || 27 ||
yathā muneḥ saubharerbhogānna rāgaśāntirabhūt | evamanyeṣāmapi na bhavatītyarthaḥ| taduktaṃ saubhariṇaiva -- 
āmṛtyuto naiva manorathānāmanto'sti vijñātamidaṃ mayādya|
manorathāktiparasya cittaṃ na jāyate vai paramārthasaṅgi|| iti || 27 ||
api tu -- 
doṣadarśanādubhayoḥ || 28 ||
ubhayoḥ prakṛtitatkāryayoḥ pariṇāmitvaduḥkhātmakatvādidoṣadarśanādeva rāgaśāntirbhavati | munivadevetyarthaḥ| saubharerhi saṅgadoṣadarśanādeva saṅgevairāgyaṃ śrūyate -- 
duḥkhaṃ yadevaikaśarīrajanma tathārddhasaṃkhya tadidaṃ prasūtam|
parigraheṇa kṣitipātmajānāṃ sutairanakairbahulīkṛtaṃ tat|| iti || 28 ||
rāgādidoṣopahatasyopadeśagrahaṇe'pyanadhikāramāha -- 
na malinacetasyupadeśabījapraroho'javat|| 29 ||
upadeśarūpaṃ yajjñānavṛkṣasya bījaṃ tasyāṅkuro'pi rāgādimalinacitte notpadyate| ajavat | yathā'janāmni nṛpe bhāryāśokamalinacitte vasiṣṭhenoktasyā'pyupadeśabījasya nāṅkura utpanna ityarthaḥ|| 29 ||
kiṃ bahunā -- 
nābhāsamātramapi malinadarpaṇavat || 30 ||
āpātajñānamapi malinacetasyupadeśānna jāyate | viṣayāntarasañcārādibhiḥ pratibandhāt | yathā malaiḥ pratibandhanmalinadarpaṇe'rtho na pratibimbati tadvadityarthaḥ|| 30 ||
yadi vā kathañcijjñānaṃ jāyet,tathāpyupadeśānurūpaṃ na bhavedityāha -- 
na tajjasyāpi tadrūpatā paṅkajavat || 31 ||
tasmādupadeśājjātasyāpi jñānasyopadeśānurūpatā na bhavati | sāmagryeṇānavabodhāt | paṅkajavat | yathā bījasyottamatve'pi paṅkadoṣād bījānurūpatā paṅkajasya na bhavati, tadvadityarthaḥ| paṅkasthānīyaṃ śiṣyacittam|| 31 ||
nanu brahmalokādiṣvaiśvaryeṇaiva puruṣārthatāsiddhyā kimarthametāvatā prayāsena mokṣāya jñānaniṣpādanaṃ , tatrāha -- 
na bhūtiyoge'pi kṛtakṛtyatopāsyasiddhivadupāsya siddhivat || 32 ||
aiśvaryayoge'pi kṛtakṛtyatā kṛtārthatā nāsti, kṣayatiśayaduḥkhairanugamāt, upāsyasiddhivat | yathopāsyānāṃ brahmādīnāṃ siddhiyoge'pi na kṛtakṛtyatā | teṣāmapi yoganidrādau yogābhyāsaśravaṇāt| tathaiva tadupāsanayā prāptatadaiśvaryasyāpītyarthaḥ|| 32 ||
adhāyayatritayoktasya vivekasyāntaraṅgakam||
ākhyāyikābhiḥ samproktamatrādhyāye samāsataḥ|| 1||
iti śrīvijñānabhikṣunirmite kāpilasāṅkhyapravacanasya
bhāṣye ākhyāyikādhyāyaścaturthaḥ|| 4 ||
atha pañcamo'dhyāyaḥ|| 5 ||
svaśāstrasiddhāntaḥ paryāptaḥ | itaḥ paraṃ svaśāstre pareṣāṃ pūrvapakṣānapākartu pañcamādhyāya ārabhyate | tatrādāvādisūtre'thaśabdena yanmaṅgalaṃ kṛtaṃ tad vyarthamityākṣepaṃ samādhatte -- 
maṅgalācaraṇaṃ śiṣṭācārāt phaladarśanācchutitaśceti|| 1 ||
maṅgalācaraṇaṃ yat kṛtaṃ tasyaitaiḥ pramāṇaiḥ kartavyatāsiddhirityarthaḥ| iti śabdo hetvantarākāṅkṣānisārāsārthaḥ|| 1 ||
īśvarāsiddheriti yaduktaṃ tannopapadyate, karmaphaladātṛtayā tatsiddheriti ye pū5rvapakṣiṇastānnirākaroti -- 
neśvarādhiṣṭhate phalaniṣpāttiḥ karmaṇa tatsiddheḥ|| 2 ||
īśvarādhiṣṭhite kāraṇe karmaphalarūpapariṇāmasya niṣpattirna yuktā| āvaśyakena karmaṇaiva phalaniṣpattisambhavādityartaḥ|| 2||
īśvarasya phaladātṛtvamapi na ghaṭata ityāha sūtraiḥ -- 
svopakārādadhiṣṭhānaṃ lokavat || 3 ||
īśvarādhiṣṭhātṛtve svopakārārthameva lokavadadhiṣṭhānaṃ syādityarthaḥ||3 ||
bhavatvīśvarasyāpyupakāraḥ kā kṣatirityāśaṅkyāha -- 
laukikeśvaraditarathā || 4||
īśvarasyāpyupakārasvīkāre laukikeśvaravadeva so'pi saṃsārī syāt| apūrṇakāmatayā duḥkhādiprasaṅgādityarthaḥ|| 4 ||
tathaiva bhavatvityāśaṅkhyāha -- 
pāribhāṣiko vā || 5 ||
saṃsārasattve'pi cedīśvarastarhi sargādyutpannapuruṣe paribhāṣāmātramasmākamiva bhavatāmapi syāt | saṃsāritvāpratihatecchātvayorvirodhānnityaiśvaryānupapatterityarthaḥ|| 5 ||
īśvarasyādhiṣṭhātṛtve bādhakāntaramāha -- 
na rāgādṛte tatsiddhiḥ pratiniyatakāraṇatvāt|| 6 ||
kiñca rāgaṃ vinā nādhiṣṭhātṛtvaṃ siddhyati | pravṛttau rāgasya pratiniyatakāraṇatvādityarthaḥ|[ nanu tṛtīyasūtre yaduktaṃ svopakārārthameva lokavadadhiṣṭhānamiti tadeva cāsminsūtre 'na rāgādṛte tatsiddhi'riti uktaṃ tathā ca paunaruktyamata upakārarāgayorbhedaṃ darśayati|] upakāra iṣṭārthasiddhiḥ, rāgastūtkaṭeccheti na paunaruktyam || 6 ||
nanvevamastu rāgo'pīśvare, tatrāha -- 
tadyoge 'pi nityamuktaḥ|| 7 ||
rāgayoge'pi svīkriyamāṇe sa nityamukto na syāt | tataśca te siddhāntahānirityarthaḥ|| 7 ||
kiñca prakṛtiṃ pratyaiśvaryyaṃ prakṛtipariṇāmabhūtecchādinā na sambhavati, anyo'nyāśrayāt| nityeccādikaṃ
ca prakṛtau na yuktam, śrutismṛtisiddhasāmyāvasthānupapatteḥ| ataḥ prakāradvayamavaśiṣyate | tadyathā -- aiśvaryaṃ kiṃ pradhānaśaktitakvenāsmadabhimatānāmicchādīnāṃ sākṣādeva cetanasambandhāt? kiṃ vā'yaskāntamaṇivat sannidhisattāmātreṇa prerakatvāditi | tatrādyaṃ pakṣaṃ dūṣayati -- 
pradhānaśakteyogāccet saṅgāpattiḥ|| 8 ||
pradhānaśaktericchādeḥ puruṣe yogāt puruṣasyāpi dharmasaṅgāpattiḥ| tathāca ' sa yat tatra paśyatyananvāgatastena bhavatyasaṅgo hyayaṃ puruṣa' ityādiśrutivirodha ityarthaḥ || 8 ||
antye tvāha -- 
sattāmātrāccet sarvaiśvaryam|| 9 ||
ayaskāntavat sannidhisattāmātreṇa ceccetanaiśvaryaṃ, tarhi sarveṣāmeva tattatsargeṣu bhoktṛṇāṃ puṃsāmaviśeṣeṇaiśvaryamasmadabhipretameva siddham| akhilabhoktasaṃyogādeva pradhānena mahadādisarjanāditi | tataścaika eveśvara iti bhavatsiddhāntahānirityarthaḥ|| 9 ||
syādetat | īśvarasādhakapramāṇavirodhena te'sattarkā eva, anyathaiva vidhāsattarkasahasraiḥ pradhānamapi bādhituṃ śakyata iti | tatrāha -- 
pramāṇābhāvānna tatsiddhiḥ|| 10 ||
tatsiddhirnityeśvare tāvat pratyakṣaṃ nāstītyanumānaśabdāveva pramāṇe vaktavye| te ca na sambhavat ityarthaḥ|| 10 ||
asambhavameva pratipādayati sūtrābhyām‌ -- 
sambandhābhāvānnānumānam || 11 ||
sambandho vyāptiḥ abhāvo'siddiḥ| tathā ca mahadādikaṃ sakartṛkaṃ kāryatvādityādyanumāneṣvaprayojakatvena vyāpyatvāsiddhyā neśvare'numānamityartaḥ|| 11 ||
nā'pi śabda ityāha -- 
śrutirapi pradhānakāryatvasya || 12 ||
prapañce pradhānakāryatvasyaiva śrutirasti, na cetanakāraṇatve | yathā -- 
" ajāmekāṃ lohitaśuklakṛṣṇāṃ bahvīḥ prajāḥ sṛjamānāṃ sarūpāḥ|
taddhedaṃ tarhyavyākṛtamāsīt tannāmarūpābhyāṃ vyākriyate" tyādirityarthaḥ| yā ca 'tadaikṣata bahu syā' mityādiścetanakāraṇatāśrutiḥ sā sargādāvutpannasya mahattattvopādhikasya mahāpuruṣasya janyajñānaparā | kiṃ vā bahubhavanānurodhāt pradhāna eva kūlaṃ pipatiṣatītivad gauṇī | anyathā 'sākṣī cetā kevalo nirguṇaścetyādiśrutyuktāpariṇāmitvasya puruṣe'nupapatteriti | ayaṃ ceśvarapratiṣedha aiśvarye vairāgyārthamīśvarajñānaṃ vinā'pi mokṣapratipādanārthaṃ ca prauḍhavādamātramiti prāgeva vyākyātam| anyatā jīvavyāvṛttasyeśvaranityatvādergauṇatvakalpanāgauravam| aupādhikānāṃ nityajñāneccādīnāṃ mahadādipariṇāmānāṃ cāṅgīkāreṇa kaucasthyādyupapatterityādikaṃ brahmamīmāṃsāyāṃ draṣṭavyamiti || 12 ||
nāvidyāto bandha iti yat siddāntitaṃ prathamapāde, tatra paramataṃ vistarataḥ praghaṭṭakena dūṣayati -- 
nāvidyāśaktiyogo niḥsaṅgasya || 13 ||
pare prāhuḥ -- pradhānaṃ nāsti, kintu jñānanāśyānādyavidyā''kyā śaktiścetane tiṣṭhati| tata eva
cetanasya bandhastannāśe ca mokṣa -- iti | tatredamucyate| niḥsaṅgutayā cetanasyāvidyāśaktiyogaḥ sākṣānna sambhavatīti| avidyā hyanyasmim̐stadākāratā | sā ca vikāraviśeṣo'dhikārahetusaṃyogarūpaṃ saṅgaṃ vinā na sambhavatītyarthaḥ|| 13 ||
nanvavidyāvaśādevāvidyāyogo vaktavyaḥ, tathā cāpāramārthikatvānna tayā saṅga iti, tatrāha -- 
tadyoge tatsiddhāvanyonyāśrayatvam|| 14 ||
avidyāyogādividyāsiddhau cānyonyāśrayatvamātmāśrayatvam| anavasthā veti śeṣaḥ|| 14 ||
nanu bījāṅkuravadanavasthā na doṣāyetyāśaṅkyāha -- 
na bījāṅkuravat sādisaṃsāraśruteḥ|| 15 ||
bījāṅkuravadanavasthā na sambhavati, puruṣāṇāṃ saṃsārasyā'vidyādyakhilānartharūpasya sāditvaśruteḥ| pralayasuṣuptyādāvabhāvaśravaṇādityarthaḥ| 'vijñānaghana evaitebhyo bhūtebhyaḥ samutthāya tānyovānuvinaśyatī'tyādiśrutibhirhi pralayādau buddhivṛttyābhāvena tadaupādhikāvidyādyakhilasaṃsāraśūnyaṃ cinmātratvaṃ puruṣāṇāṃ siddhamiti | tasmādavidyāpyāvidyakīti vāṅmātram|| 15 ||
nanvasmākamavidyā pāribhāṣikī, na tu yogoktā'nātmanyātmabuddhyādirūpā, tathāca bhavatāṃ pradhānavadevāsmākamapi tasyā akhaṇḍānādi tayā puruṣaniṣṭhatve'pi nāsaṅgatāhānirityāśaṅkāyāṃ parikalpitamavidyāśabdārthaṃ vikalpya dūṣayati -- 
vidyāto'nyatve brahmabādhaprasaṅgaḥ || 16 ||
yadi vidyānyatvamevāvidyāśabdārthastarhi tasya jñānanāśyatathā brahmaṇa ātmano'pi bādho nāśaḥ prasajjyate, vidyābhinnatvādityarthaḥ|| 16 ||
abādhe naiṣphalyam|| 17 ||
yadi tvavidyārūpamapari vidyayā na bādhyeta tarhi vidyāvaiphalyam| avidyānivarttakatvābhāvādityarthaḥ|| 17 ||
pakṣāntaraṃ dūṣayati -- 
vidyābādhyatve jagato'pyevam|| 18 ||
yadi punarvidyayā cetane bādhyatvamevāvidyātvamucyate tathā sati jagataḥ prakṛtimahadādyakhilaprapañcasyāpyevamavidyātvaṃ syāt | 'atāta ādeśoneti netyasthūlamanaṇvi'tyādiśrutibhirmithyājñānasyeva prakṛtyāderapyātmani bodhitatvādityarthaḥ| tathā cākhilaprañcasyaivāvidyātve satyekasya jñānenāvidyānāśādanyairapi prapañco na dṛśyeteti bhāvaḥ| vidyānāśyatvaṃ cāvidyātvaṃ vaktuṃ na śakyate | vidyānāśyatvena vidyānāśyagrahāsambhavādātmāvidyātvaṃ vaktuṃ na śakyate | vidyānāśyatvena vidyānāśyagrahāsambhavādātmāśrayāditi|| 18 ||
tadrūpatve sāditvam|| 19 ||
