bhavatu vā yathākathañcidvidyābādhyatvamevāvidyāvam| tathāpi tādṛśavastunaḥ sāditvameva puruṣeṣu, na tvanāditvaṃ sambhavati| 'vijñānaghana eve' tyādyuktaśrutibhiḥ pralayādau puruṣasya cinmātratvasidderityarthaḥ| asmanmate ca pralaye puruṣasyā'saṃsāritve'pi svatantranityapradhānasaṃyogāt punarbandha upapāditaḥ | tathā pradhānasaṃyoge'pi prāgbhavīyāviveka eva sāvanādṛṣṭādidvārā nimittamityapyuktam| tasmādyogadarśanoktādanyā nāstyavidyā| sā ca buddhidharma eva, na puruṣadharma iti siddham|| 19 ||
atraivādhyāye karmanimittā pradhānapravṛttiriti yaduktaṃ, tatra parapūrvapakṣaṃ samādhatte praghaṭṭakena -- 
na dharmāpalāpaḥ prakṛtikāryavaicitryāt|| 20 ||
apratyakṣatayā dharmāpalāpo na sambhavati | prakṛtikāryeṣu vaicitryā'nyathānupapattyā tadanumānādityarthaḥ|| 20 ||
pramāṇāntaramapyāha -- 
śrutiliṅgādibhistatsiddhiḥ|| 21 ||
'puṇyo vai puṇyena bhavati pāpaḥ pāpene'tyādiśruteḥ 'svargakāmo'śvamedhena yajete' ti vidhyādirūpālliṅgādyogipratyakṣādibhiśca tatsiddhirityarthaḥ|| 21 ||
pratyakṣābhāvāddharmāsiddhiriti parasya hetuābhāsīkaroti -- 
naniyamaḥ pramāṇāntarāvakāśāt|| 22 ||
pratyakṣābhāvādvastvabhāva iti niyamo nāsti| pramāṇāntareṇāpi vastūnāṃ vi,yīkaraṇādityarthaḥ
|| 22 ||
dharmavadadharmamapi sādhayati -- 
ubhayatrāpyevam || 23 ||
dharmavadadharme'pyevaṃ pramāṇānītyarthaḥ|| 23 ||
nanu vidhyanyathānupapattirūpayā'rthāpattyā dharmasiddhiḥ, sā ca nāstyā dharma iti kathaṃ śrautaliṅgātideśo'dharma iti cennetyāha -- 
arthāt siddhiścet samānamubhayoḥ || 24 ||
yataḥ samānumubhayordharmādharmayoliṅgamasti 'paradārānna gacche' diti niṣedhavidhyāderevādharmarliṅgatvādityarthaḥ|| 24 ||
nanu dharmādikaṃ cet svīkṛtaṃ tarhi puruṣāṇāṃ dharmādimataktvena pariṇāmādyāpattirityāśaṅkāṃ pariharati -- 
antaḥ karaṇadharmatvaṃ dharmādīnām || 25 ||
ādiśabdena vaiśeṣikaśāstroktāḥ sarve ātmaviśeṣaguṇā gṛhyante | na caivaṃ pralaye'ntaḥkaraṇābhāvāddharmādikaṃ kva tiṣṭhatviti vācyam| ākāśavadantaḥkaraṇasyātyantavināśābhāvāt | antaḥkaraṇaṃ hi kāryakāraṇobhayarūpamiti prāgeva vyākyātam| ataḥ kāraṇāvasthe prakṛtyaṃśaviśeṣentaḥ -- karaṇe dharmādharmasaṃskārādikaṃ tiṣṭatīti|| 25 ||
syādetat| prakṛtikāryavaicitryācchrutyādeśca dharmādisiddhiriti yaduktaṃ, tadayuktam| triguṇātmakaprakṛtestatkāryāṇāṃ ca bhavatāṃ śrutyaiva bādhāt| ' sākṣī cetā kevalo nirguṇaśca' 'athāta ādeśo neti neti' -- 'aśabdamasparśamarūpamavyayaṃ tathā'rasaṃ nityamagandhavacca yad' ityādinā | 'na nirodho na cotpattiḥ' vācārambhaṇaṃ vikāro nāmadheyaṃ mṛtattiketyeva satya'mityādinā teci | tadetat pariharati -- 
guṇādīnāṃ ca nātyantabādhaḥ|| 26 ||
guṇānāṃ sattvādīnāṃ, taddharmāṇāṃ ca sukhādīnāṃ, tatkāryāṇāmapi mahadādīnāṃ svarūpato nāsti bādhaḥ | kintu saṃsargata eva cetane bādho'yasyauṣṇyabādhavat| tathā kālata evāvasthādibhirbādho guṇādyakhilapariṇāmina ityarthaḥ|| 26
||
kṛtaḥ punaḥ svarūpata eva bādho na bhavati, svapnamanorathādipadārthavadityākāṅkṣāyāmāha -- 
pañcāvayavayogāt sukhasaṃvittiḥ|| 27 ||
atra viśiṣya pakṣīkaraṇāya vivādaviṣayaikadeśasya sukhamātrasya grahaṇaṃ sarvaviṣayopalakṣakam| sukhādisaṃvittiriti pāṭhastu samī cīnaḥ| pañcāvayavāśya nyāyasya pratijñāhetūdāharaṇopanayanigamanāni| teṣāṃ yogānmelanāt sukhādyakhilapadārthasiddhirityarthaḥ| prayogaścāyam -- sukhaṃ sat arthakriyāritvāt| yaddarthakriyākāri tat sat, yathā cetanaḥ| pulakādirūpārthakriyākāri ca sukhaṃ , tasmāt saditi| cetanānāṃ cāvikāritve'pi viṣayaprakāśa evā'rthakriyeti| nāstikaṃ prati ca vyatirekyamumānaṃ kartavyam| tatra ca śaśaśrṛṅgādirdṛṣṭānta iti || 27 ||
pratyakṣātiriktaṃ pramāṇameva na bhavati, vyāpyatvādyasiddheriti cārvākaḥ punaḥ śaṅkate -- 
na sakṛdgrahaṇāt sambandhasiddhiḥ|| 28 ||
sakṛt sahacāragrahaṇāt sambandho vyāptirna liddhyati| bhūyastva cānanugatam| ato vyāptigrahāsambhavānnānumānenārthasiddhirityarthaḥ|| 28 ||
samādhatte -- 
niyatadharmasāhityamubhayorekatarasya vā vyāptiḥ|| 29 ||
dharmasāhityaṃ dharmatāyāṃ sāhityam| sahacāra iti yāvat | tathācobhayoḥ sādhyasādhanayorekatasya sādhanamātrasya vā niyataḥ avyabhicaritoyaḥ sahacāraḥ sa vyāptirityarthaḥ| ubhayoriti samavyāptipakṣe proktaṃ, niyamaścānukūlatarkeṇa grāhya iti na vyāptigrahā'sambhava iti bhāvaḥ|| 29 ||
vyāptirvakṣyamāṇaśaktyādirūpaṃ padārthāntaraṃ na bhavatītyāha -- 
na tattvāntaraṃ vastukalpanāprasakteḥ|| 30 ||
niyatadharmasāhityātiriktā vyāptirna bhavati | vyāptitvāśrayasya vastuno'pi kalpanāprasaṅgāt| asmābhistu siddhavastuna eva vyāptitvamātraṃ klṛptamityarthaḥ|| 30 ||
paramatamāha -- 
nijaśaktyudbhavamityācāryāḥ|| 31 ||
apare tvācāryā vyāpyasya svaśaktijanyaṃ śaktiviśeṣarūpaṃ tattvāntarameva vyāptirityāhuḥ| nijaśaktimātraṃ tu yāvaddravyasthāyitayā na vyāptiḥ| deśāntaragatasya dhūmasyāpi vahnyavyāpyatvāt | deśāntaragamanena ca sā śaktirnāśyata iti noktalakṣaṇe'tivyāptiḥ| svamate tūtpattikālāvacchinnatvena dhūmo viśeṣaṇīya ithi bhāvaḥ|| 31 ||
ādheyaśaktiyoga iti pañcaśikhaḥ|| 32 ||
buddhyādiṣu prakṛtyādivyāpyatāvyavahārādādhāratāśaktirvyāpakatā''dheyatāśaktimattvaṃ ca vyāpyamiti pañcaśikha ityarthaḥ|| 32 ||
nanvādheyaśaktiḥ kimarthaṃ kalpyate, vyāpyasya vsatunaḥ svarūpaśaktireva vyāptirastu | tatrāha -- 
na svarūpaśaktirniyamaḥ punarvādaprasakteḥ|| 33 ||
svarūpaśaktistu niyamo vyāptirna bhavati, paunaruktyaprasaṅgāt| ghaṭaḥkalaśa itivat buddhirvyāpyetyatrāpyarthābhedenetyarthaḥ| svarūpamiti vaktavye śaktipadopādānaṃ vyāptervyāpyadharmatopapādanāya || 33 ||
paunaruktyaṃ svayameva vivṛṇoti -- 
viśeṣaṇānarthakyaprasakteḥ|| 34 ||
pūrlasūtra eva vyākhyātaprāyamidam|| 34 ||
dūṣaṇāntaramāha -- 
pallavādiṣvanupapatteśca|| 35 ||
pallavādiṣu vṛkṣādivyāpyatāsti | svarūpaśaktimātraṃ tu tasya lakṣaṇaṃ na sambhavati, chinnapallave'pi svarūpaśakteranapāyena tadānīmapi vyāpyatāpatterityarthaḥ| ādheyadhaktistu chedakāle vinaṣṭheti na tadānīṃ vyāptiriti bhāvaḥ|| 35 ||
nanu kiṃ pañcaśikhena nijaśaktyudbhavo vyāptireva nocyate, tarhi dhūmasya vahnayodheyatvābhāvādvahnyavyāpyatāpattiriti | tatrāha -- 
ādheyaśaktisiddhau nijaśaktiyogaḥ samānanyāyāt|| 36 ||
ādheyaśaktervyāptitvasiddhau nijaśaktyudbhavo'pi vyāptitvena siddha eva, samānanyāyāt | yuktisāmyādityarthaḥ| ananugamastu nānārthaśabdāvanna doṣāya | evaṃ svamate'pi nānāvidhasahacārā eva vyāptayo bodhyāḥ| na caivamapyanumitihetutve vyāptīnāmananugamaḥ syāditi vācyam| tṛṇāraṇimaṇyādivat kāryagatavaijātyādyupapatteriti| pañcāvayavayogād guṇādisiddhiriti yaduktaṃ tadupapādanāya vyāptinirvacanenānumānaprāmāṇye bādhakamapāstam|| 36 ||
idānīṃ pañcāvayavarūpaśabdasya jñānajanakatvopapattaye śabdaśaktyādinirvacanena tadanupapattirūpaṃ śabdaprāmāṇye pareṇāṃ bādhakamapāsyate -- 
vācyavācakabhāvaḥ sambandhaḥ śabdārthayoḥ|| 37 ||
arthe vācyatākhyā śaktiḥ śabde vācakatākhyā śaktirasti saiva tayoḥ sambandho'nuyogitāvat | tajjñānācchabdenārthopasthitirityarthaḥ|| 37 ||
śaktigrāhakāṇyāha -- 
tribhiḥ sambandhasiddhiḥ|| 38 ||
āptopadeśo -- vṛddhavyavahāraḥ prasiddhapadasāmānādhikaraṇyam -- ityetaistribhiruktasambandho gṛhata ityarthaḥ|| 38 ||
na kārye niyama ubhayathā darśanāt || 39 ||
sa ca śaktigrahaḥ kārya eva bhavatīti niyamo nāsti loke kāryavadakārye'pi vṛddhavyavahārādidarśanādityarthaḥ| yathā hi gāmānayetyādikāryaparavākyād vṛddhasya navānayanādivyahāro dṛśyata iti , siddhārthaśabdaprāmāṇyasiddhau ca viveke vedāntaprāmāṇyaṃ siddhamityāśayaḥ || 39 ||
nanu bhavatu loke siddhe śaktigraho'rthapratyayādidarśanād vede tu kathaṃ bhaviṣyatyakāryabodhanavaiyarthyāditi| tatrāha -- 
loke vyutpannasya vedārthapratītiḥ|| 40 ||
loke śabdaśaktivyutpannasya puruṣasyatadanusāreṇaiva vedārthapratītiḥ| na hi loke śaktirbhinnā vede ca bhinnā, ya eva laukikāsta eva vaidikā iti nyāyāt| ato loke siddhārthaparatvasiddhau vede'pi tat siddhyatītyarthaḥ
|| 40 ||
atra śaṅkate -- 
na tribhirapauruṣeyatvādvedasya tadarthasyā'pyatīndriyatvāt || 41 ||
nanu tribhirāptopadeśādibhirvedaśabdena śaktigrahaḥ sambhavati| vedasyāpauruṣeyatvena tadartheṣvāptopadeśāsambhavāt| tathā vedārthasyā'tīndriyatayā tatra vṛddhavyavahārasya prasiddhapadasamānādhikaraṇyasya ca grahītumaśakyatvādityarthaḥ|| 41 ||
tatrātīndriyārthatvamādau nirākaroti -- 
na yajñādeḥ svarūpato dharmatvaṃ vaiśiṣṭyāt|| 42 ||
yaduktaṃ tanna| yato devatāddeśyakadravyatyāgādirūpasya yajñadānādeḥ svarūpata eva dharmatvaṃ vedavihitatvaṃ, vaiśiṣṭyat prakṛṣṭaphalakatvāt| yajñādikaṃ cecchādirūpatvānnātīndriyam| na tu yajñādiviṣayakāpūrvasyadharmatvaṃ, yena vedavihitasyātīndriyatā syādityarthaḥ| nanu tathāpi devatādyatīndriyārthaṃghaṭitatvamastīti cenna| atīndriyeṣvapi padārthatāvacchedakena sāmānyarūpeṇa pratītervakṣyamāṇatvāditi|| 42 ||
yaccoktamapauruṣeyatvenāptopadeśābhāva iti, tadapi nirākaroti -- 
nijaśaktirvyutpattyā vyavacchidyate|| 43 ||
apauruṣeyatve'pi vedānāṃ svābhāvikī yā'rtheṣu śaktirasti saivāptairvṛddhaparamparābhirvyutpattyā'sya śabdasyāyamartha ityevaṃrūpā vyavacchidyate śiṣyebhyorthāntarād vyāvartyopadiśyate, na tvādhunikaśabdavat svayaṃ saṅketyate, yena pauruṣeyatvāpekṣā syādityartaḥ|| 43 ||
nanutathāpyatīndriyadevatāphalādiṣu kathaṃ śaktigraho vaidikapadānāṃ syāt, tatrāha -- 
yogyāyogyeṣu pratītijanakatvāt tatsiddhiḥ|| 44 ||
pratyakṣāpratyakṣeṣu padārtheṣu sāmānyadharmapuraskāreṇa tatsiddhiḥ śakti graho bhavati| sādhāraṇyena padānāṃ pratītijanakatvasyānubhavasiddhatvāt| viśeṣastvatīndriyo'pūrva eva vākyārthaḥ| na ca tasya grahaṇaṃ prāgapekṣyate ityarthaḥ|| 44 ||
śabdaprāmāṇyaprasaṅgenaiva śabdagataṃ viśeṣamavadhārayati -- 
na nityatva vedānāṃ kāryatvaśruteḥ|| 45 ||
'sa tapo'tapyata tasmāt tapastepānāt trayo vedā ajāyante'tyādiśrutervedānāṃ na nityatvamityarthaḥ| vedanityatāvākyāni ca sajātīyānupūrvīpravāhānucchedaparāṇi|| 45||
tarhi kiṃ pauruṣeyā vedāḥ? netyāha -- 
na pauruṣeyatvaṃ tatkarttuḥ puruṣasyābhāvāt|| 46 ||
īśvarapratiṣedhāditi śeṣaḥ sugamam|| 46 ||
apara karttāṃ bhavatvityākāṅkṣāyāmāha -- 
na muktāmuktayorayogyatvāt|| 47 ||
jīvanmuktadhurīṇo viṣṇurviśuddhasattvatayā niratiśayasarvajño'pi vītarāgatvāt sahasraśākhavedanirmāṇāyogyaḥ| amuktastvasarvajñatvādevāyogya ityarthaḥ|| 47 ||
nanvevamapauruṣeyatvānnityatvamevāgataṃ, tatrāha -- 
nāpauruṣeyatvānnityatvamaṅkurādivat|| 48 ||
spaṣṭam|| 48 ||
nanvaṅkurādiṣvapi kāryatvena ghaṭādivat pauruṣeyatvamanumeyaṃ , tatrāha -- 
teṣāmapi tadyoge dṛṣṭabādhādiprasaktiḥ|| 49 ||
yat pauruṣeyaṃ taccarīrajanyamiti vyāptirloke dṛṣṭā| tasyā bādhādirevaṃ sati syādityarthaḥ|| 49 ||
nanvādipuruṣoccaritatvādvedā api pauruṣeyā evetyāha -- 
yasminnadṛṣṭe'pi kṛtabuddhirūpajāyate tat pauruṣeyam|| 50 ||
dṛṣṭa ivādṛṣṭe'pi yasmin vastuni kṛtabuddhirbuddhipūrvakatvabuddhirjāyate tadeva pauruṣeyamiti vyavahniyata ityarthaḥ| etaduktaṃ bhavati. na puruṣoccaritatāmātreṇa pauruṣeyatvam, śvāsapraśvāsayoḥ suṣuptikālīnayoḥ pauruṣeya tvavyavahārābhāvāt, vintu buddhipūrvakatvena | vedāstu niḥśvāsavadevādṛṣṭavaśādabuddhipūrvakā eva svayambhuvaḥ sakāśāt svayaṃ bhavanti| ato na te pauruṣeyāḥ| tathā ca śrutiḥ " tasyaitasya mahato bhūtasya niścasitametadyadṛgveda" ityādiriti|| 50 ||
nanvevaṃ yathārthavākyārthajñānāpūrvakatvācchukavākyasyeva vaidānāmapi prāmāṇyaṃ na syāt, tatrāha -- 
nijaśaktyābhivyakteḥ svataḥ prāmāṇyam|| 51 ||
vedānāṃ nijā svābhāvikī yā yathārthajñānajananaśaktistasyā mantrāyurvedādāvabhivyakterupalambhādakhiladevānāmeva svata eva prāmāṇyaṃ siddhyati, na vaktṛyathārthajñānamūlakatvādinetyarthaḥ| tathā ca nyāyasūtram "mantrāyurvedaprāmāṇyavacca tatprāmāṇyamiti " | guṇādīnāñca nātyantabādha iti pratijñāyaṃ nyāyena sukhādisiddherityeko heturūpanyastaḥ, prapañcitaśca|| 51 ||
samprataṃ tasyāmeva hetvantaramāha -- 
nāsataḥ khyānaṃ nṛśṛṅgavat|| 52 ||
āstāṃ tāvat pañcāvayavena sukhādisiddhaḥ| jñānamātrādapi tatsiddhiḥ| atyantāsattve sukhādīnāṃ jñānameva nopapadyate| naraśrṛṅgādīnāmabhānādityarthaḥ| tathāca brahmasūtram 'nābhāva upalabdheriti " | śuktirajatasvapnamanorathādau ca manaḥ pariṇāmarūpa evārthaḥ pratīyate, 'nātyantāsanniti' vakṣyati|| 52 ||
nanvevaṃ guṇādiratyantaṃ sanneva bhavatu | tathā ca nātyantabādha ityanta padavaiyarthyamit | tatrāha -- 
na sato bādhadarśanāt|| 53 ||
atyantasato'pi guṇāderbhānaṃ yuktam, vināśādikāle bādhadarśanāt| caitanyaṃ bhāsamānasya jagataścaitanya eva bādhadarśanācca| 'athāta ātetyādi' śrutibhirnyāyaiścetyarthaḥ|| 53 ||
nanvevamapi sadasadbhyāṃ bhinnameva jagadbhavatu| tathāpyatyantabādhapratiṣedhopapattiriti, tatrāha -- 
nānirvacanīyasya tadabhāvāt || 54 ||
sattvenāsattvena cānirvacanīyaṃ tādṛśasyāpi bhānaṃ na ghaṭate, tadabhāvāt| sadasadbhinnavastvaprasiddherityarthaḥ| dṛṣṭānusāreṇaiva kalpanāyā aucityāditi bhāvaḥ|| 54 ||
nanvevaṃ kimanyathākhyātireveṣṭā, netyāha -- 
nānyathākhyātiḥ svavacovyāghātāt|| 55 ||
anyadvastvanyarūpeṇa bhāsata ityapi na yuktam, svavacovyaghātāt| anyatrānyarūpasya nṛśrṛṅgatulyatvamanyathā śabdenocyate'vyathā ca tasya bhānamucyata iti svavaca eva vyāhatam. asato
bhānāsambhavasyānyathākhyātivādibhirapi vacanādityarthaḥ. purovarttinyasattve'nyatra tatsattāyā bhānāprayojakatvamiti bhāvaḥ| na ca sarvatrāsato bhāne samagrī na sambhavati, sannikarṣādyabhāvādityataḥ kvacit sattāmātramapekṣyata iti vācyam| anādivāsanādhārāyā eva bhramahetutsasambhavāditi|| 55 ||
nātyantabādha iti pūrvoktaṃ vivṛṇvānaḥ svasiddhāntamupasaṃhārati -- 
sadasatkhyātirbādhābādhāt || 56 ||
sadasatkhyātireva sarveṣāṃ guṇādīnām| kutaḥ? bādhābādhāt| tatra svarūpeṇābādhaḥ sarvavastūnāṃ nityatvāt | saṃsārgatastu bādhaḥ sarvavastūnāṃ caitanye'sti| yatā paṭādiṣu lauhityādestadvat| tathāvasthābhirapi bādho'khilapariṇāmināṃ kālādiṣvityarthaḥ| bādhaśca pratipannadharmiṇi niṣedhabuddhiviṣayatvam| asattvaṃ tvabhāvaḥ| so'pyadhikaraṇasvarūpa iti | na ca sadasattvayorvirodha iti vācyam| prakārabhedenāvirodhāt| yathāhi lauhityaṃ bimbarūpeṇa sat, sphaṭikagatapratibimbarūpeṇa cāsaditi dṛṣṭam| yatā vā rajataṃ vaṇigvīthīstharūpeṇa sat, śuktyadhyastarūpeṇa cāsaditi| taduktam -- 
arthe hyavidyāmāne'pi saṃsṛtirna nivartate|
dhyāyato viṣayānasya svapne'narthāgamo yathā|| iti ||
evamevāvasthābhedenāpi sadasattvamaviruddham | yathāhi vṛkṣādiḥ prarūḍhādyavasthābhiḥ sannapyaṅkurādyavasthābhirasan bhavati, tathaiva prakṛtyādikaṃ sadasadātmakamiti | taduktam -- 
avyaktaṃ kāramaṃ yat tannityaṃ sadasadātmakam|
pradhānaṃ prakṛtiśceti yadāhustattvacintakāḥ || iti ||
etaccāsmābhirbrahmamīmāṃsābhāṣye yogavārtike ca prapañcitamiti dik|| 56 ||
ayaṃ vitāraḥ paryāpta idānīṃ śabdavicāraḥ prasaṅgāgata āgantukatayānte prastūyate -- 
pratītyapratītibhyāṃ na sphoṭātmakaḥ śabdāḥ|| 57 ||
pratyekavarṇebhyo'tiriktaṃ kalaśa ityādirūpamakhaṇḍamekapadaṃ sphoṭa iti yogairabhyupagamyate | kambugrīvādyavayavebhyo'tirikto ghaṭādyavayavīva| sa ca śabdaviśeṣaḥ padākhyo'rthasphuṭīkaraṇāt sphoṭa ityucyate| sa śabdo'prāmāṇikaḥ| kutaḥ? pratītyapratītibhyām| sa śabdaḥ kiṃ pratīyate, na vā? ādye yena varṇasamudāyenānupūrvīviśeṣaviśiṣṭena so'bhivyajyate, tasyaivārthapratyāyakatvamastu, kimantargaḍunā tena | antye tvajñātasphoṭasya nāstyarthapratyāyanasaktiriti vyarthā sphoṭakalpanetyarthaḥ| 57 ||
pūrvaṃ vedānāṃ nityatvaṃ pratiṣiddhamidānīṃ varṇanityatvamapi pratiṣedhati -- 
na śabdānityatvaṃ kāryatāpratīteḥ|| 58 ||
sa evāyaṃ gakāra ityādipratyabhijñābalādvarṇanityatvaṃ na yuktam| utpanno gakāra ityādipratyayenānityatvasiddherityarthaḥ| pratyabhijñā ca tajjātīyatāviṣayiṇī | anyathā ghaṭāderapi pratyabhijñayā nityatāpatteriti || 58 ||
śaṅkate -- 
pūrvasiddhasattvasyābhivyaktirdīpeneva ghaṭasya || 59 ||
nanu pūrvasiddhasattākasyaiva śabdasya dhvanyādibhiryābhivyaktistanmātra mutpattiḥ pratīterviṣayaḥ | abhivyaktau dṛṣṭānto, dīpeneva ghaṭasyeti || 59 ||
pariharati -- 
satkāryasiddhāntaścet siddhasādhanam|| 60 ||
abhivyaktiryadyanāgatāvasthātyāgena vartamānāvasthālābha ityucyate tadā satkāryasiddhāntaḥ| tādṛśanityatvaṃ ca sarvakāryāṇāmeveti siddhasādhanamityarthaḥ| yadi ca varttamānatayā sata eva jñānamātrarūpiṇī abhivyaktirucyate tadā ghaṭādīnāmapi nityatāpattiḥ | kāraṇavyāpāreṇa jñānasyaivotpattipratītiviṣayatvaucityāditi bhāvaḥ
|| 60 ||
ātmādvaite pūrvānuktamapi bādhakamupanyasanūyamityetadarthamātmā'dvaitanirāsaḥ punarārabhyate -- 
nādvaitamātmano liṅgāt tadbhedapratīteḥ|| 61 ||
yadyapyātmanāmanyonyaṃ bhedavākyavadabhedavākyānyapi santi, tathāpi nādvaitaṃ nātyantamabhedaḥ| ajñādivākyasthaiḥ prakṛtityāgā'tyāgādiliṅgairbhedasyaiva siddherityarthaḥ| na hyatyantābhede tāni liṅgānyupapadyante | abhedavākyāni tu sāmyādiśrutyaikavākyatayā vaidharmyādilakṣaṇābhedaparatayopadyante | abhimānādinivṛttyanyatho'nupapattyāpi tatparatvāvadhāraṇācceti|| 61 ||
ātmanāmabhede liṅgaṃ bādhakamuktam| "ātmaivedaṃ sarvaṃ brahmaivedaṃ sarvamiti" śrutyātmano'nātmabhiradvaite tu pratyakṣamapi bādhakamastītyāha -- 
nānātmanāpi pratyakṣābādhāt|| 62 ||
anātmanāpi bhogyaprapañcenātmano nādvaitam| pratyakṣeṇāpi bādhāt| ātmanaḥ sarvabhogyābhede ghaṭapaṭayorapyabhedaḥ syāt | ghaṭādeḥ paṭādyabhinnātmābhedāt| sa ca bhedagrāhakapratyakṣabādhita ityarthaḥ|| 62 ||
nobhābhyāṃ tenaiva || 63 ||
abhyāsāṃ samuccitābhyāmapyātmānātmabhyāṃ nātyantābhedastenaiva hetudvayenetyartaḥ|| 63 ||
nanvevamātmaivedamityādiśratīnāṃ kā gatiriti| tatrāha -- 
anyaparatvamavivekānāṃ tatra || 64 ||
avivekānāmavivekipuruṣān prati tatrādvaite'nyaparatvamupāsanā'rthakānuvāda ityarthaḥ| loke hi śarīraśarīriṇorbhogyabhoktroścā'vivekenābhedo vyavahniyate'haṃ gaure mamotmā bhadrasena ityādiḥ| atastameva vyavahāramanūdya tāneva pati tathopāsānaṃ śrutirvidadhāti sattvaśuddhyādyarthamiti | ata eva paramārthadaśāyāmupāsyānāmātmatvaṃ pratiṣedhati śrutiḥ -- 
yanmanasā na manute yenāhurmano matam|
tadeva brahmatvaṃ viddhi nedaṃ yadidamupāsate || ityādineti || 64 ||
ekātmavādināṃ jagadupādānakāraṇamapi sambhavatītyāha -- 
nātmāvidyā nobhayaṃ jagadupādānakāraṇaṃ
niḥsaṅgatvāt || 65 ||
kevala ātmā ātmāśritā vā'vidyā samuccitaṃ vā kapāladvayavadubhayaṃ na jagadupādānaṃ sambhavati, āthmano'saṅgatvāt| saṅgākhyo hi yaḥ saṃyogaviśeṣastenaiva dravyāṇāṃ vikāro bhavati| ato'saṅgatvāt, asaṅgatvenāvidyāyogasya prāgeva nirastatvāt| pratyekopādanatvavadevobhayopādānatvamapyasaṅgatvādevā'sambhavītyarthaḥ| yadi cāvidyā dravyarūpā puruṣāśritā gagane vāyuvadiṣyate, tadātmādvaitahāniḥ| tathā prakṛtireva sati siddhasādhanaṃ ca tādṛśaṃ cāvibhāgenādvaitamasmākamapīṣṭameva| 'sadeva saumyedamagra āsīdekavādvitīyaṃ brahme'tyādiśrutyāpi cāvibhāgarūpamevādvaitaṃ pratipādyate| 'na tu taddviyīyamasti tato'nyadvibhaktaṃ yat paśye'diti śrutyantarāt| tathā coktam -- 
āsījjñānamayo'pyartha ekamevāvikalpitam|
tayorekataro hyarthaḥ prakṛtiścobhayātmikā||
jñānaṃ tvanyatamo bhāvaḥ puruṣaḥ sā'bhidhīyate|| iti ||
avikalpitavibhaktam| tasmādvedāntānāmakhaṇḍātmādvaitaṃ nārthaḥ tathāpyādhunikā vedāntinotratyapūrvapakṣajātameva brahmamīmāṃsāsiddhāntatayā kalpayanti|| tattu brahmasūtrānuktatvena pratyuta tadvirodhena na asmābhistatraiva nirākṛtamiti | atra ca brahmamīmāṃsāsiddhānto na duṣyate| apitu vedānteṣvāpātataḥ sambhāvito'rtha eva nirākriyata iti smartavyam| evamuttarasūtreṣvapi || 65 ||
prakāśasvarūpaṃ ātmeti svayaṃ siddhāntitam| tatra ',tyaṃ vijñānamānandaṃ brahme'ti śruterānando'pyātmanaḥ svarūpamiti pūrvapakṣaṃ nirākaroti -- 
naikasyānandacidrūpatve dvayorbhedāt || 66 ||
ekadharmiṇa ānandacaitanyobhayarūpatvaṃ na bhavati | duḥkhajñānakāle sukhānanubhavena sukhajñānayorbhedādityarthaḥ| na ca jñānaviśeṣaḥ sukhamiti vaktuṃ śakyate | āthmasvarūpajñānasyā'khaṇḍatvāt| atha eva caitanyānubhavakāle sapukhasyāvaraṇamapi vaktuṃ na śakyate| akhaṇḍatvenāndāvaraṇe duḥkhaṃ jānāmītyanupapatteḥ| na hyātmanoṃ'śabhedo'sti, yenānandāṃśāvaraṇe'pi caitanyāṃśo bhāyāditi| na ca śrutibalenaite'sattarkā iti vācyam| "nānandaṃ na nirānandamityādiśrutyā, 'aduḥkhamasukhaṃ brahma bhūtabhavyabhavātmaka' mityādismṛtyā cānandābhāvasyāpi pratipāditatvena tarkasyaivātrādartavyāditi|| 66 ||
nanvevamānandarūpatāśrupapateḥ kā gatistatrāha -- 
duḥkhanivṛttergauṇaḥ || 67 ||
duḥkhanivṛttyātmani śrauta ānandaśabdo gauṇa ityarthaḥ| taduktam -- 'sukhaṃ duḥkhasukhātyaya' iti | na nirānandamiti śrutistvaupādhikānandaparā| satyasaṅkalpatvādiśrutivaditi | yattu nirupādhipriyatvenātmanaḥ sukharūpatvānumānaṃ kaścidāha| tanna| dukhābhāvarūpatayāpi premopapatteḥ| sukhatvādivadātmatvasyāpi premaprayojakatvācca | anyathā parasukhe'pi premāpatteriti|| 67 ||
gauṇayoge bījamāha -- 
vimuktipraśaṃsā mandānām|| 68 ||
mandānajñān prati duḥkhanivṛttirūpāmātmasvarūpamukti sukhatvena śrutiḥ stauti prarocanārthamityarthaḥ
|| 68 ||
antaḥkaraṇopapatteḥ pūrvoktayā āñjasyenopapattaye manovaibhavapūrvapakṣamapākaroti -- 
na vyāpakatvaṃ manasaḥ karaṇatvadindriyatvādvā
vāsyādivaccakṣurādivat|| 69 ||
manaso'ntaḥkaraṇasāmānyasya na vibhutvam| karaṇatvāt| vāmyādivat| vāśabdo vyavasthitavikalpe| indriyatvādapyantaḥ karaṇaviśeṣasya tṛtīyasya na vibhutvamityarthaḥ| dehavyāpi jñānādikaṃ tu madhyamaparimāṇenaivopapadyata iti || 69 ||
atrāprayojakatvaśaṃkāyāmanukūlatarkamāha -- 
sakriyatvād gatiśruteḥ|| 70 ||
ātmano lokāntaragamanaśravaṇena tadupādhibhūtasyāntaḥkaraṇasya sakriyatvasiddherna vibhutvaṃ sambhavatītyarthaḥ|| 70 ||
kāryatvopapattaye manaso niravayavatvamapi nirākaroti -- 
na nirbhāgatvaṃ tadyogād ghaṭavat|| 71 ||
tacchabdaḥ pūrvasūtrasthendriyaṃ parāmṛśati| manaso na niravayavatvam| anekendriyeṣvekadā yogāt| kintu ghaṭavanmadhyamaparimāṇaṃ sāvayavamityarthaḥ| kāraṇāvasthaṃ cāntaḥkaraṇamaṇveveti bodhyam|| 71 ||
manaḥkālādīnāṃ nityatvaṃ pratiṣedhati -- 
prakṛtipuruṣayoranyata sarvamanityam|| 72 ||
sugamam| kāraṇāvasthaṃ cāntaḥkaraṇākāśīdikaṃ prakṛtirevocyate| na tu manādikaṃ vyavasāyādyasādhāraṇadharmābhāvāt|| 72 ||
nanu -- 
māyāṃ tu prakṛtiṃ vidyānmāyinaṃ tu maheśvaram|
asyā'vayavabhūtaistu vyāptaṃ sarvamidaṃ jagad||
ityādiśrutibhiḥ pumprakṛtyorapi sāvayavatvādanityatvamiti | tatrāha -- 
na bhāgalābho bhogināṃ nirbhāgatvaśruteḥ|| 73 ||
bheginaḥ puruṣasya pradhānasya cāvayavo na yujyate| niravayavatvaśruteḥ
niṣkriyaṃ śāntaṃ niravadyaṃ nirañjanam||
ityādinā ityarthaḥ| uktaśrutiścā'kāśajalayoriva pitīputracetanayoriva ca vibhāgamātreṇāṃśāṃśibhāvaṃ bodhayatīti || 73 ||
duḥkhanivṛttirmokṣa ityuktam| tadavadhāraṇāya tatra mokṣe pareṣāṃ matāni nirākareti -- 
nānandābhivyaktirmuktirnirdharmatvāt|| 74 ||
ātmanyānandarūpo'bhivyaktirūpaśca dharmo nāsti | svarūpaṃ ca nityameveti na sādhanasādhyam| ato nānandābhivyaktirmokṣa ityarthaḥ|| 74 ||
na viśeṣaguṇocchittistadvat|| 75 ||
aśeṣaviśeṣaguṇocchedo'pi na muktiḥ| tadvat| nirdharmatvādevetyarthaḥ| nanu tarhi duḥkhanivṛttireva kathaṃ mokṣa uktaḥ, duḥkhābhāvasyāpi dharmatvāditi cenna| asmābhirbhogyatāsambandhenaiva duḥkhābhāvasya puruṣārthatāvacanāditi
|| 75 ||
na viśeṣagatirniṣkriyasya || 76 ||
brahmalokagatirapi na mokṣaḥ| ātmano niṣkriyatvena gatyabhāvāt| liṅgaśarīrābhyupagame ca na mokṣo ghaṭata ityarthaḥ|| 76 ||
nākaroparāgocchittiḥ kṣaṇikatvādidoṣāt || 77 ||
kṣaṇikajñānamevātmā| tasya viṣayākāratā bandhaḥ | tadvāsanākhyoparāgasya nāśo mokṣa -- iti yannāstikamataṃ, tadapi na | kṣaṇikatvādidoṣeṇa mokṣasyāpuruṣārthatvādityarthaḥ|| 77 ||
nāstikasyaiva muktayantaraṃ dūṣayati -- 
nasravocchittirapuruṣārthatvādidoṣāt|| 78 ||
jñānarūpasyātmanaḥ sāmagryeṇaivocchittirapi na mokṣaḥ| ātmanāśasya loke puruṣārthatvādarśanādibhya ityarthaḥ|| 78 ||
evaṃ śūnyamapi || 79 ||
jñāne jñeyātmakākhilaprapañcanāśo'pyevamātmanāśenāpuruṣārthatvānna mokṣa ityarthaḥ|| 79 ||
saṃyogāśca viyogāntā iti na deśādilābho'pi || 80||
prakṛṣṭadeśadhanāṅganādisvāmyamapi na mokṣaḥ| yataḥ
saṃyogāśca viyogāntā manaṇāntaṃ ca jīvanam|
iti śrūyata ityarthaḥ| tathāca vināśitvāt svāmyaṃ na muktiriti|| 80 ||
na bhāgiyogo bhāgasya || 81 ||
bhāgasyāṃśasya jīvasya bhāginyaṃśini paramātmani layo na mokṣaḥ| saṃyogā hi viyogāntā ityuktahetoḥ īśvarānabhyupagamācca| tathā svalayasyāpuruṣārthatvāccetyarthaḥ|| 81 ||
nāṇimādiyogo'pyavaśyaṃ bhāvitvāt
taducchitteritarayogavat|| 82 ||
aṇimādyaiśvaryasambandho'pi na muktiḥ| aiśvaryāntarasambandhavadeva tasyāpyucchedaniyamādityarthaḥ
|| 82 ||
nendrādipadayogo'pi tadvat|| 83 ||
indrādyaiśvaryalābho'pi na muktiritaraiśvaryavat kṣayiṣṇutvādityarthaḥ|| 83 ||
indriyāṇāmāhaṅkārikatvaṃ yaduktaṃ, tatra paravipratipattiṃ nirākaroti -- 
na bhūtaprakṛtitvamindriyāṇāmāhaṅkārikatvaśruteḥ|| 84 ||
sugamā yojanā | pūrvaṃ caitad vyākhyātam|| 84 ||
śaktyādikamapi tattvamastītyāśayena pareṣāṃ padārthapratiniyamaṃ tanmātrajñānānmuktiṃ ca nirākaroti -- 
na ṣaṭpādārthaniyamastadbodhānmuktiḥ|| 85 ||
dravyaguṇakarmasāmānyaviśeṣasamavāyā eva padārthā iti yad vaiśeṣikāṇāṃ niyamo, yaśca tajjñānānmokṣa ityabhyupagamaḥ| so'prāmāṇikaḥ| śaktyādyatirekāt| pṛthivyādinavadravyebhyaḥ prakṛteratirekāccetyarthaḥ| gandhādimattvenaiva hi pṛthivyādivyavahāraḥ| gandhādiśca sāmyāvasthāyāṃ nāsti| ataḥ pṛthivītvādijātirapi ghaṭatvādivat kāryamātravṛttiriti| taduktam -- 
nāho na rātrirna nabho bhūmirnāsīt tamo jyotirabhūnna cānyat|
śabdādibuddhyādyupalambhamekaṃ prādhānikaṃ brahma pumāṃstadāsīt || iti || 85 ||
ṣoḍaśādiṣvapyevam|| 86 ||
nyāyapāśupatādimateṣu ṣoḍaśādiṣvapi na niyamo, na vā tanmātrajñānānmuktiḥ | uktarūpeṇa padārthādhikyādityarthaḥ| asmanmate tu nityaṃ padārthadvayameva | nityānityasādhāraṇāstu padārthāḥ pañcaviṃśatireveti niyamaḥ| pañcaviṃśatidravyeṣveva guṇakarmasāmānyaśaktyādīnāmantarbhāva iti || 86 ||
pañcabhūtānāṃ pūrvoktakāryatvopapattyarthaṃ vaiśeṣikādyabhyupagataṃ pārthivādyaṇinityatvamapākaroti -- 
nāṇunityatā tatkāryatvaśruteḥ|| 87 ||
pṛthivyādyaṇūnāṃ nityatā nāsti| teṣāmaṇūnāmapi kāryatvaśruterityarthaḥ| yadyapyasmābhiḥ sā śrutirna dṛśyate kālaluptatvādinā, tathāpyācāryavākyānmanusmaraṇāccānumeyā| yathā manuḥ -- 
aṇvyā mātrā vināśinyo daśārdhānāṃ ca yāḥ smṛtāḥ|
tābhiḥ sarddhamidaṃ sava sambhavatyanupūrvaśaḥ iti ||
daśārddhānāṃ pṛthivyādipañcabhūtānām| na cātra vākye'ṇuśabdena dvyaṇukādyeva grāhyamiti vācyam| saṅkoce pramāṇābhāvāditi| atrāṇuśabdobhūtaparamāṇupara eva vaiśeṣikādyabhimataṃ ca tasya nityatvamanena sūtreṇa nirākriyate| na tvaṇuparimāṇadravyasāmānyasya nityatvam| rajoguṇasya cāñcalyānurodhenāṇutvasiddheḥ| madhyamaparimāṇatve nityatvasya vibhutve ca kriyāyā anupapatteriti || 87 ||
nanu niravayavasya paramāṇoḥ kathaṃ kāyatvaṃ ghaṭate ? tatrāha -- 
na nirbhāgatvaṃ kāryatvāt|| 88 ||
śrutisiddhakāryatvānyathānupapattyā pṛthivyādyaṇūnāṃ na niravayavatvamityarthaḥ| ata eva tanmātrākhyasūkṣmadravyāṇyeva pārthivādyanāmavayavā iti pātañjalabhāṣye vyāsadevaiḥ pratipāditam| pṛthivyādīnāmapakarṣakāṣṭā'bhiprāyeṇaiva | ataḥ prakṛtiparyantamaṇutve'pi kṣatiriti | yadyapi tanmātreṣvapi gandhādyasti, tathāpi tasyāpratyakṣatayā na pṛthivītvādiniyāmakatvam| vyaṅgyagandhādereva pṛthivītvādisiddheḥ| ato na tanmātrāṇi pṛthivyādayaḥ| teṣu ca sūkṣmabhūtavyavahāro bhūtasākṣātkāraṇatvādinaivetyapi bodhyam|| 88 ||
prakṛtipuruṣasākṣātkāro na sambhavati rūpasya dravyasākṣātkārahetutvāditi nāstikākṣepaṃ nirākaroti -- 
na rūpanibandhanāt pratyakṣaniyamaḥ || 89 ||
rūpādeva nimittāt pratyakṣateti niyamo nāsti| dharmādinā'pi sākṣātkārasambhavādityarthaḥ| vyañjakāniyamasyoñjanādau dṛṣṭatvenādoṣatvāt| ato bahirdravyalokikapratyakṣaṃ pratyevodbhūtarūpaṃ vyañjakamiti bhāvaḥ|| 89 ||
nanvevaṃ kimaṇuparimāṇaṃ vastvasti, na vetyākāṅkṣāyāṃ parimāṇānirṇayaṃ karoti -- 
na parimāṇacāturvidhyaṃ dvābhyāṃ tadyogāt || 90 ||
aṇu mahaddīrghaṃ hrasvamiti parimāṇacāturvidhyaṃ nāsti| dvaividhyaṃ tu varttata eva | dvābhyāṃ tadyogāt| dvābhyāmevāṇumahatparimāṇābhyāṃ cāturvidhyasambhavādityarthaḥ| mahatparimāṇasyāvāntarabhedāveva hi hrasvadīrghau| anyathā cakrādirūpeḥ parimāṇānantyaprasaṅgāditi tatrāsmannaye'ṇuparimāṇamākāśasya kāraṇaṃ guṇaviśeṣaṃ varjayitvā bhūtendiyāṇāṃ mūlakāraṇeṣu sattvādiguṇeṣu mantavyam| anyatra yathāyogyaṃ madhyamādiparamamahattvāntaparimāṇāni | tāni ca mahattvasyaivāvāntarabhedā iti || 90 ||
puruṣaikatvaṃ sāmānyeneti kaṇṭhata evoktaṃ, prakṛterekatvaṃ sāmānyenetyarthāduktam| tadarthaṃ sāmānyeṣu nāstikavipratipattiṃ nirākaroti -- 
anityatve sthiratāyogāt pratyabhijñānaṃ
sāmānyasya || 91 ||
vyaktīnāmanityatve'pi sa evāyaṃ iti sthiratāyogena yat pratyabhijñānaṃ tat sāmānyasya | sāmānyaviṣayakameva tat pratyabhijñānamityarthaḥ|| 91 ||
tasmānna sāmānyāpalāpo yukta ityāha -- 
na tadapalāpastasmāt|| 92 ||
sugamam|| 92 ||
nanvatadvyāvṛttirūpeṇābhāvenaiva pratyabhijñopapādanīyā, saiva ca sāmānyaśabdārtho'stu tatrāha -- 
nānyanivṛttirūpatvaṃ bhāvapratīteḥ|| 93 ||
sa evāyamiti bhāvapratyayānivṛttirūpatvaṃ na sāmānyasyetyarthaḥ| anyathā hi nāyamaghaṭa ityeva pratīyate| kiñcānyavyāvṛttiśabdasyāghaṭavyāvṛttirityartho vācyaḥ| tatrāghaṭatvaṃ ghaṭasāmānyabhinnatvamiti sāmānyābhyupagama evāpatita iti || 93 ||
nanu sādṛśyanibandhanā pratyabhijñā bhaviṣyati| tatrāha -- 
na tattvāntaraṃ sādṛśyaṃ pratyakṣopalabdheḥ|| 94 ||
bhūyo'vayavādisāmānyādatiriktaṃ na sādṛśyamasti pratyakṣata eva sāmanyarūpatayopalambhādityarthaḥ
|| 94 ||
nanu svābhāvikī śaktireva sādṛśyamastu, na tu tat sāmānyamityāśaṅkāmapākaroti -- 
nijaśaktyabhivyaktirvā vaiśiṣṭyāttadupalabdheḥ|| 95 ||
vastunaḥsvābhāvikaśaktiviśeṣotpādo'pi na sādṛśyaṃ śaktyupalabdhitaḥ sādṛśyopalabdhorvilakṣaṇatvāt| śaktijñānaṃ hi nānyadharmijñānasāpekṣam| sādṛśyajñānaṃ punaḥ pratiyogijñānamapekṣate, abhāvajñānavaditi jñānayorvailakṣaṇyamityartaḥ| kiñca dharmiṇaḥ śaktisāmānyaṃ na sādṛśyam| bālyāvasthāyāmapi yuvasādṛśyāpatteḥ| kintu yuvādikālīnaḥ śaktiviśeṣo yuvādisādṛśyamiti vaktavyam| tathāca prativyaktyanantaśaktikalpanāpekṣayā sarvavyaktisādhāraṇaikasāmānyakalpanaiva yukteti || 95 ||
nanu tathāpi ghaṭādisaṃjñakatvameva ghaṭādivyaktīnāṃ sādṛśyamastutatrāha -- 
na saṃjñāsaṃjñisambandho'pi || 96 ||
yathoktaḥ saṃjñāsaṃjñinoḥ sambandho'pi na sādṛśyam| vaiśiṣṭyāt tadupalabdherevetyarthaḥ| saṃjñāsaṃjñibhāvamajānato'pi sādṛśyajñānāditi| 96 ||
api ca -- 
na sambandhanityatobhayānityatvāt || 97 ||
saṃjñāsaṃjñinoranityatvāttatsambandhasyāpi na nityatā | ataḥ kathaṃ tenātītavastusādṛśyaṃ vartamānavastuni syādityarthaḥ|| 97 ||
nanu sambandhyanityatve'pi sambandho nityaḥ syāt kimatra bādhakaṃ ? tatrāha -- 
nātaḥ sambandho dharmigrāhakapramāṇabādhāt|| 98 ||
kādācitkavibhāgo satyeva sambandhaḥ siddhyati| anyathā vakṣyamāṇasvarūpeṇaivopapattau sambandhakalpanānavakāśāt | sa ta kādācitkovibhāgo na sambandhanityatve sambhavati| ataḥ sambandhagrāhakapramāṇenaiva bādhānna nityaḥ sambandha ityarthaḥ|| 98 ||
nanvevaṃ nityayorguṇaguṇinornityaḥ samavāyo nopapadyeta| tatrāha -- 
na samavāyo'sti pramāṇābhāvāt || 99 ||
sugamam|| 99 ||
nanu naiśiṣṭyapratyakṣaṃ viśiṣṭabuddhyanyathānupapattiśca pramāṇam| tatrāha -- 
abhayatrāpyanyathāsiddherna pratyakṣanumānaṃ vā || 100 ||
abhayatrāpi vaiśiṣṭyapratyakṣe tadanumāne ca svarūpeṇaivānyathāsiddherna tadubhayaṃ samavāye pramāṇamityarthaḥ| ayaṃ bhāvaḥ yathā samavāyavaiśiṣṭya buddhiḥ samavāyasvarūpeṇaiveṣyate, anavasthābhayāditi| tatra pratyakṣānumāne anyathāsiddhe| evaṃ guṇaguṇiprabhṛtīnāṃ viśiṣṭabuddhirapi guṇādisvarūpeṇaiveṣyatām| atastatrāpi pratyakṣānumāne anyathāsiddhe iti | nanvevaṃ saṃyogo'pi na siddhyati| bhūtalādau ghaṭādipratyayasyāpi svarūpeṇaivānyathāsiddheriti cenna| viyogakāle'pi bhūtalaghaṭayoḥ svarūpatādāvasthyena viśiṣṭabuddhiprasaṅgāt| samavāyasthale ca samavetasya kadāpi svāśrayaviyogo nāstīti, nāyaṃ doṣaḥ| kaścittu tādātmyasambandhenātra samavāyasyānyathāsiddhimāha| tanna | śabdamātrabhedāt| tādātmyaṃ hyatra nātyantaṃ vaktavyam| guṇaviyoge'pi guṇisattvāt| vaiśiṣṭyapratyayācca| kintu bhedābhedabuddhiniyāmakaḥ sambandhaviśeṣa evāgatyā vaktavyaḥ| tathāca tasya samavāya iti vā tādātmyamiti vā nāmamātraṃ bhinnam| sambandhidvayātiriktaḥ sambandhastu siddha eveti | yadi ca tādātmyaṃ svarūpamevocyate tadāsmābhirapi tadevoktamiti śabdamātre bheda iti || 100 ||
prakṛteḥ kṣobhāt prakṛtipuruṣasaṃyogastasmāt kasyāpi kriyā, sarvaṃ vastu kṣaṇikaṃ yatrotpadyate tatraiva vinaśyatītyato na deśāntarasaṃyogonneyā kriyā siddhyatīti|| tatrāha -- 
nānumeyatvameva kriyāyā nediṣṭhasya tattadvato
revāparokṣapratīteḥ|| 102 ||
na deśāntarasaṃyogadādinā kriyāyā anumeyatvameva | yato nediṣṭasya nikaṭasthasya draṣṭuḥ kriyākriyavatoḥ pratyakṣeṇāpi pratītirasti vṛkṣaścalatītyādirityarthaḥ|| 101 ||
dvitīyādhyāye śarīrasya pañcabhautikatvādirūpairmatabhedā evoktā, na tu viśeṣo'vadhṛtaḥ| atrāparapakṣaṃ pratiṣedhati -- 
na pāñcabhautikaṃ bahunāmupādānāyogāt || 102||
bahūnāṃ bhinnajātīyānāṃ copādānatvaṃ ghaṭapadādisthale na dṛṣṭamiti sajātīmamevopādanama| itaracca bhūtacatuṣṭayamupaṣṭambhakamityāśayena pāñcabhautikavyavahāraḥ| ekopādānaktavaṃ'pi pṛthivyovopādānaṃ sarvaśarīrasyeti vakṣyati
|| 102 ||
sthūlameva śarīramiti kecit| tannirākaroti -- 
na sthūlamiti niyama ātivāhikasyāpi
vidyamānatvāt|| 103 ||
indriyāśrayatvaṃ śararīratvam||
yanmūrtyavayavāḥ sūkṣmāstasyomānyāśrayanti ṣaṭ|
tasmāccharīramityāhustasya mūrtiṃ manīṣiṇaḥ||
iti manuvākyāt| etādṛśaṃ ca śarīraṃ sthūlaṃ pratyakṣameveti na niyamaḥ| kutaḥ? ātivāhikasyāpratyakṣatayā sūkṣmasya bhotikasya śarīrāntarasyā'pi sattvādityarthaḥ| lokāllokāntaraṃ liṅgahedamativāhayatītyātivāhikam| bhūtāśrayatāṃ vinā citrādivadi gamanābāvasya prāgevoktatvāt| idaṃ ca sūtraṃ spaṣṭīkaraṇamātrārtham| liṅgasya ca śarīratvaṃ bhogāśrayatayā puruṣapratibimbāśrayatayā veti bodadhyam| ātivāhikaśarīre ca pramāṇam -- 
aṅguṣṭhamātraḥ puruṣo'ntarātmā
sadā janānāṃ hṛdaye sanniviṣṭaḥ|
aṅguṣṭhamātraṃ puruṣaṃ niścakarṣabalādyapaḥ|| iti śrutismṛtī | na hi liṅgaśarīrasya sakalaśarīravyāpinaḥ svato'ṅguṣṭhamātratvaṃ sambhavati | ata ādhārasyāṅguṣṭhamātramarthāt sidhyati | yathā dīpasya sarvagṛhavyāpitve'pi kalikākāratvaṃ tailavartyādisūkṣmāṃśasya daśopari sampiṇḍitasya pārthivabhāgasyakalikākāratayā , tathaiva liṃgadehasya dehavyāpitve'pyaṅguṣṭhaparimāṇatvaṃ sūkṣmabhūtasyāṅguṣṭhaparimāṇatvenānumeyamiti|| 103 ||
golakebhyo'tiriktānīndriyāṇi prāguktāni | tadupapādanāyendriyāṇāmaprāptaprakāśakatvaṃ nirākaroti -- 
nāprāptaprakāśakatvamindriyāṇāmaprāpteḥsarvaprāptervā || 104 ||
svāsambaddhārthānīndriyāṇi na prakāśayanti| aprāpteḥ | pradīpādīnāmaprāptakāśakatvādarśanāt| aprāptaprakāśakatve vyavahitādisarvavastuprakāśakatvaprasaṅgaccetyarthaḥ| ato dūrasthasūryādisambandhārthaṃ golakātiriktamindriyamiti bhāvaḥ| karaṇānāṃ cārthaprakāśakatvaṃ puruṣe'rthasamarpaṇadvāraiva, svato jaḍatvāt darpaṇasya mukhaprakāśakatvavat| atha vārthapratibimbod grahaṇamevārthaprakāśakatvamiti|| 104 ||
nanvevaṃ cakṣuṣastaijasatvameva yuktam, tejasa eva kiraṇarūpeṇāśu dūrāpasarpaṇadarśanāditi śaṅkāṃ nirākaroti -- 
na tejo'pasarpaṇāttaijasaṃ cakṣurvṛttitastatsiddheḥ|| 105 ||
tejaso'pasarpaṇaṃ dṛṣaśṭamiti kṛtvā taijasaṃ cakṣurna vācyam| kutaḥ? ataijasatve'pi prāṇavadeva vṛttibhedenāpasarpaṇāpapatterityarthaḥ| yathā hi prāṇaḥ śarīramantyajyaiva nāsāgrādvahiḥkiyaddūraṃ prāṇanā''khyavṛttyāpasarati| evamevātaijasadravyamapi cakṣurdehamasantyajyā'pi vṛttyākhyapariṇāmaviśeṣeṇa jhaṭityeva dūrasthaṃ kiṃ pramāṇaṃ tatrāha -- 
prāptārthaprakāśaliṅgād vṛttisiddhiḥ|| 106 ||
sugamam|| 106 ||
dehamaparityajyāpi gamanopapattaye vṛtteḥ svarūpaṃ darśayati -- 
bhāgaguṇābhyāṃ tattvāntaraṃ vṛttiḥ sambandhārthaṃ
sarpatīti || 107 ||
sambandhārthaṃ sarpatīti hetoścakṣurāderbhāgo visphuliṅgavadvibhaktāṃśorūpādivad guṇaśca na vṛttiḥ, kintu tadekadeśabhūtā bhāgaguṇābhyāṃ bhinnā vṛttiḥ,|vibhāge hi sati taddvārācakṣuśaḥ sūryādisambandho na ghaṭate| guṇatve ca sarpaṇākhyakriyānupapatterityarthaḥ| etena buddhivṛttirapi pradīpaśikhāvad dravyarūpa eva pariṇāmaḥ svacchatayārthākāratodgrāhī nirmalavasravaditi siddham|| 107 ||
nanvevaṃ vṛttīnāṃ dravyatve kathamiccādirūpabuddhiguṇena vṛttivyavahārastatrāha -- 
na dravyaniyamastadyogāt || 108 ||
vṛttirdravyameveti niyamo nāsti. kutaḥ? tadyogāt -- tatra vṛttau yogārthasattvāt | 'vṛttirvartanajīvane' iti hi yaugiko'yaṃ śabdaḥ| jīvanaṃ ca svasthitiheturvyāpāraḥ| 'jīva balaprāṇadhāraṇayo' rityanuśāsanāt| vaiśyavṛttiḥ śadravṛttirityādivyavahārācca | tatra yathā dravyarūpayāvṛttyā buddhirjīvati tathecchābhirapīti te'pi vṛttayaḥ sarvanirodhenaiva cittamaraṇādityarthaḥ|| 108 ||
indriyāṇāṃ bhotikatvasyāpi śravaṇāt kadācillokaviśeṣabhedena śrutivyavasthā śaṅkyeta, tatrāha -- 
na deśabhede'pyanyopādānatā'smadādivanniyamaḥ|| 109 ||
na brahmalokādideśabhedato'pīndriyāṇāmahaṅkārātiriktopādānakatvaṃ, kintvasmadādīnāṃ bhūlokasthānāmiva sarveṣāmevāhaṅkārikatvaniyamaḥ| deśabhedeneksayaiva liṅgaśarīrasya sañcāramātraśravaṇādityarthaḥ|| 109 ||
nanvevaṃ bhautiratvaśrutiḥ kathamupapadyatāṃ, tatrāha -- 
nimittavyapadeśāttadravyapadeśaḥ|| 110 ||
nimitte'pi prādhānyavivakṣayopādānatvavyapadeśo bhavāṃta| yathendhanādagniriti| ato bhūtopādānatvavyapadeśa ityartaḥ| tejādibhūtopaṣṭambhenaiva hi tadanugatāhaṅkārāccakṣurādīndriyāṇi sambhavanti| yathā pārthivopaṣṭambhena tadanugatāt tejaso'gnirbhavati| "annamayaṃ hi saumyaṃ manaḥ" ityādiśrutistaduktayuktiścātra pramāṇam
|| 110 ||
sthūlaśarīragataṃ viśeṣaṃ prasaṅgādavadhārayati -- 
ūṣmajāṇḍajajarāyujodbhijjasāṅkalpikasāṃsiddhikaṃ
ceti na niyamaḥ|| 111 ||
teṣāṃ khalveṣāṃ bhūtānāṃ trīṇyeva bījāni bhavanti| aṇḍajaṃ jīvajamudbhijjamiti śrutāvaṇḍajādirūpaṃ śarīratraividhyaprāyakābhiprāyeṇoktaṃ, na tu niyamaḥ, yata ūṣmajādi ṣaḍvidhameva śarīraṃ bhavatītyartaḥ| tatroṣmakāḥ dandaśūkādayaḥ | aṇḍajāḥ pakṣisarpādayaḥ| carāyudā manuṣyādayaḥ| udbhijjā vṛkṣādayaḥ| saṅkalpajāḥ sanakādayaḥ| sāṃsiddhikā mantratapa ādisiddhijāḥ | yathā raktabījaśarīrotpannaśarīrādayaḥ|| 111 |||
śarīrasyaikamātrabhūtopādānakatvaṃ pūrvoktamananaiva prasaṅgena viśiṣyāha -- 
sarveṣu pṛthivyupādānamasādhāraṇyāt tadvyapadeśaḥ
pūrvavat|| 112 ||
sarveṣu śarīreṣu pṛthivyevopādānam, asādhāraṇyāt -- ādhikyādibhirutkarṣāt| atrāpi pañcacaturādibhautikatvavyapadeśaḥ pūrvavat| indriyāṇāṃ bhautikatvavadupaṣṭhambhakatvamātreṇetyarthaḥ|| 112 ||
nanu prāṇasya śarīre prādhānyāt prāṇaṃ eva dehārambhako'stu, tatrāha -- 
na dehārambhakasya prāṇatvamindriyaśaktitastatsiddheḥ|| 113 ||
prāṇe na dehārambhakaḥ| indriyaṃ vinā prāṇānavasthānenānvayavyatirekābhyāmindriyāṇāṃ śaktiviśeṣādeva prāṇasiddheḥ prāṇotpatterityartaḥ| bhayaṃ bhāvaḥ| karaṇavṛttirūpaprāṇaḥ karaṇaviyoge na tiṣṭhati | ato mṛtadehe karaṇābhāvena prāṇābhāvānna prāṇo dehārambhaka iti || 113 ||
nanvevaṃ prāṇasya dehākāraṇatve prāṇaṃ vināpi deha utpadyetasa tatrāha -- 
bhokturadhiṣṭhānādbhogāyatananirmāṇamanyathā
pūtibhāvaprasaṅgāt|| 114 ||
bhoktuḥ prāṇino'dhiṣṭhānāha vyāpārādeva bhogāyatanasya śarīrasya nirmāṇaṃ bhavati| anyathā prāṇavyāpārābhāve śukraśoṇitayoḥ pūtibhāvaprasaṅgād mṛtadehavadityarthaḥ| tathā ca rasasañcārādivyāpāraviśeṣaiḥ prāṇo dehasya nimittakāraṇaṃ dhārakatvāditi bhāvaḥ|| 114 ||
nanu prāṇasyaivādhiṣṭhānatvaṃ sambhavati vyāpāravattvāt, na prāṇinaḥ kūṭasthatvād, nirvyāpārasyādhiṣṭhāne prayojanābhāvācceti, tatrāha -- 
bhṛtyadvārā svāmyadhiṣṭhitirnaikāntāt|| 115 ||
dehanirmāṇe vyāpārarūpamadhiṣṭhānaṃ svāminaścetanasyaikāntāt sākṣānnāsci, kintu prāṇarūpabhṛtyadvārā| yatā rājñaḥ puranirmāṇa ityarthaḥ| tathā ca prāṇasyādiṣṭhātṛtvaṃ sākṣāt puruṣasvādhiṣṭhātṛtvaṃ prāṇasaṃyogamātreṇeti siddham| kulālādīnāṃ ghaṭādinirmāṇeṣvapyevam| viśeṣastvayam| tatra cetanasya budhyādeścāpyupayogo'sti buddhipūrvakasṛṣṭitvāditi | yadyapi prāṇādhiṣṭhānādeva dehanirmāṇaṃ, tathāpi prāṇadvārā prāṇisaṃyogo'pyapekṣyate| puruṣārthameva prāṇena dehanirmāṇādityāśayena bhokturadhiṣṭhānādityuktam|| 115 ||
vimuktamokṣārtaṃ pradhānasyetyuktaṃ prāk| tatra kathamātmā nityamukto bandhadarśanāditi pareṣāmākṣepe nityamuktimupapādayitumāha -- 
samādhisuṣuptimokṣeṣu brahmarūpatā || 116 ||
samādhirasamprajñātāvasthā | suṣuptiścātra samagrasuṣuptiḥ| mokṣaśca videhakaivalyam| āsvavasthāsu puruṣāṇāṃ brahmarūpatā buddhivṛttivilayatastadaupādhikaparicchedavigamena svasvarūpapūrṇatayāvasthānam| yathā ghaṭadhvaṃse ghaṭākāśasya pūrṇatetyarthaḥ| tadetaduktam -- 'tannivṛttāvupaśāntoparāgaḥ svastha' iti | tathā ca brahmatvameva puruṣāṇāṃ svabhāvo naimittikatvābhāvāt, sphaṭikasya śauklyamiva | buddhivṛttisambandhakāle tu paricchinnacidrūpatvenābhivyaktyā paricchedābhimānaḥ tathā vṛttipratibimbavaśād duḥkhādimālinyamiva ca bhavatīti tat sarvamaupādhikameva| upādhyākhyanimittānvayavyatirekānuvidhānāt sphaṭikalauhityavaditi bhāvaḥ| tathā ca yogasūtram 'vṛttisārūpyamitaratreti' | asmaccāstre ca brahmaśabdaaupādhikaparicchedamālinyādirahitaparipūrṇacetanasāmānyavācī, na tu brahmamīmāṃsāyāmivaiśvaryopalakṣitapuruṣamātravācīti vivektavyam| atraite ślokāḥ śiṣyavyutpattyarthamucyante -- 
cidākāśe'nibhivyakte nānākārairitastataḥ|
dhīraṭantī saha vyāktyā cidaṭantīṃ pradarśayet||
vastutastu sadā pūrṇamekarūpaṃ ca cinnabhaḥ|
vṛttiśūnyapradeśeṣu dṛśyabhāvānna paśyati||
cakṣuṣā rūpavat puṃso dṛśyā vṛttirhi netarata|
samādhyādau ca sā nāstītyataḥ pūrṇaḥ pumāṃstadā|| 116 ||
tarhi kaḥ suṣuptisamādhibhyāṃ mokṣasya viśeṣastatrāha -- 
dvayoḥ sabījamanyatra taddhatiḥ|| 117 ||
dvayoḥ samādhisuṣuptyoḥ sabījaṃ bandhabījasahitaṃ brahmatvamanyatra mokṣe bījasyābhāva iti viśeṣa ityarthaḥ| nanu ceta samādhyājau bandhabījamastitarhi tenaiva paricchedāt kathaṃ barahmatvamiti cenna| bandhabījasya karmādestattadānīmupādhāvevāvasthānāt | na tu cetaneṣu puruṣe ca teṣāmapratibimbanāditi| jāgradādyavasthāyāṃ tu buddhivṛttipratibimbavaśādaupādhiko bandha ityāsakṛdāveditam||
nanu pātañjale tadbhāṣye cāsamprajñātayogo nirbīja uktaḥ, atra kathaṃ sabīja ucyata iti cenna| asamprajñāte krameṇa bījakṣayo bhavatītyāśayenaiva tatra nirbījatvavacanāt| anyathā sarvāsāmevāsamprajñātavyaktīnāṃ nirbījatve vyutthānānupapatteriti|| 117 ||
nanu samādhisuṣuptī dṛṣṭe staḥ mokṣe tu kiṃ pramāṇam? iti nāstikākṣepaṃ pariharati -- 
dvayoriva trayasyāpi dṛṣṭatvānna tu dvau || 118 ||
samādhisuṣuptidṛṣṭāntena mokṣasyāpi dṛṣṭatvādanumitatvānna tu dvau suṣuptyādau yo brahmabhāvastattyāgaścittāgatādrāgādidoṣavaśādeva bhavati| sa ceddoṣo jñānena nāśitastarhi suṣuptyādisadṛśasyevāvasthā sthirā bhavati, saiva mokṣa iti || 118 ||
nanu vāsanākhyabījasattve'pi vairāgyādinā vāsanākauṇṭhyādarthākārā vṛttiḥ samādhau mā bhavatu| suṣuptau brahmarūpatā yukteti. tatrāha -- vāsanayā na svārthakhyāpanaṃ doṣayoge'pi na nimittasya
pradhānabādhakatvam|| 119 ||
yathā vairāgye tathā nidrādoṣayoge'pi sati vāsanayā na svārthakhyāpanaṃ svaviṣayasmāraṇaṃ bhavati | yatona nimittasya guṇībhūtasya saṃskārasya balavattaranidrādoṣabādhakatvaṃ sambhavatītyarthaḥ| balavattara eva hi doṣo vāsanāṃ durbalāṃ svakāryakuṇṭhāṃ karotīti bhāvaḥ|| 119 ||
saṃskāraleśato jīvanmuktasya śarīradhāraṇamiti tṛtīyādhyāye proktam| tatrāyamokṣepaḥ -- 'jīvanmuktasya śaśvadekasminnapyarthe'smadādīnāmiva bhogo dṛśyate so'nupapannaḥ| prathamamutpādyaiva pūrvasaṃskāranāśāt saṃskārāntarasya ca jñānapratibandhena karmavadanudayāditi' | tatrāha -- 
ekaḥ saṃskāraḥ kriyānirvartako na tu pratikriyaṃ saṃskārabhedā bahukalpanāprasakteḥ|| 120 ||
yena saṃskāreṇa devādiśarīrabhoga ārabdhaḥ sa eka eva saṃkārastaccharīrasādhyasya prārabdhabhogasya samāpakaḥ| sa ca karmavadeva bhogasamāptināśyo, na tu pratikriyaṃ pratibhogavyaktisaṃskāranānātvam| bahuvyaktikalpanāgauravaprasaṅgādityartaḥ| kulālacakrabhramaṇasthale'pyevaṃ vegākhyaḥ saṃskāra eka eva bhramaṇasamāptiparyantasthāyī bodhyaḥ|| 120 ||
udbhijjaṃ śarīramastītyuktam| tatra bāhyabuddhyabhāvāccharīratvaṃ nāstīti nāstikākṣepamapākaroti -- 
na bāhyabuddhiniyamaḥ|| 121 ||
vṛkṣagulmalatauṣadhivanaspatitṛṇavīrudhādīnāmapi
bhoktṛbhogāyatanatvaṃ pūrvavat|| 122 ||
na bāhyajñānaṃ yatrāsti tadeva śarīramiti niyamaḥ, kintu vṛkṣādīnāmantaḥsaṃjñānāmapi bhoktṛbhogāyatanatvaṃ śarīratvaṃ mantavyam| yataḥ pūrvavat pūrvokto yo bhoktradhiṣṭhānaṃ vinā manuṣyādiśarīrasya pūtibhāvastadvadeva vṛkṣādiśarīreṣvapi śuṣkatādikamityarthaḥ| tathā ca śrutiḥ 'asya yadekāṃśākhāṃ jīvo jahotyatha sā śuṣyatī' tyādiriti |'na bāhyabuddhiniyama' ityaṃśasya pṛthaksūtratve'pi sūtradvayamekīkṛtya ityameva vyākyeyam| sūtrabhedastu dairdhyabhayāditi bodhyam|| 121 -- 122 ||
smṛteśca|| 123 ||
śarīrajaiḥ karmadoṣairyāti sthāvaratāṃ naraḥ|
vācikaiḥ pakṣimṛgatāṃ mānasairantyajātitām,||
ityādismṛterapi vṛkṣādiṣu bhoktṛbhogāyanatvamityarthaḥ|| 123 ||
nanu vṛkṣādiṣvapyevaṃ cetanatvena dharmādharmotpattiprasaṅgastatrāha -- 
na dehamātrataḥ karmādhikāritvaṃ vaiśiṣṭyaśruteḥ|| 124 ||
na dehamātreṇa dharmādharmotpattiyogyatvaṃ jīvasya. kutaḥ? vaiśiṣṭyaśruteḥ| brahmaṇādidehaviśiṣṭatvenaivādhikāraśravaṇādityarthaḥ|| 124 ||
dehabhedenaiva dharmādhikāraṃ darśayan dehatraividhyamāha -- 
tridhā trayāṇāṃ vyavasthā karmadehopabhogadehobhayadehāḥ|| 125 ||
trayāṇāmuttamādhamamadhyamānāṃ sarvaprāṇināṃ triprakāro dehavibhāgaḥ karmadehabhogadehobhayadehā itītyartaḥ| tatra karmadehaḥ paramarṣīṇāṃ, bhogadeha indrādīnāmubhayadehaśca rājarṣīṇāmiti | athra pradhānyena tridhā vibhāgaḥ| anyathā sarvasyaiva bhogadehatvāpatteḥ|| 125 ||
caturthamapi śarīramāha -- 
na kiñcidapyanuśayinaḥ|| 126 ||
vidyādanuśayaṃ dveṣyaṃ paścāttāpānutāpayoḥ|
iti vākyādanuśayo vairāgyam| viraktānāṃ śarīrametattrayavilakṣaṇamityarthaḥ| yathā dattātreyajaḍabharatādīnāmiti|| 126 ||
uktasyeśvarābhāvasya sthāpanāya parābhyupagataṃ jñānecchākṛtyādinityatvaṃ pratiṣedhati -- 
na buddhyādinityatvamāśrayāviśeṣe'pi vanhivat || 127 ||
buddhiratrādhyavasāyākhyā vṛttiḥ| tathā ca jñānecchākṛtyādīnāmāśrayaviśeṣe parairīśvaropādhitayābhyupagate'pi nityatvaṃ nāsti| asmadādibuddhidṛṣṭāntena sarveṣāmeva buddhīcchādīnāmanityatvānumānāt| yathā laukikavahnidṛṣṭāntenāvaraṇatejaso'pyanityatvānumānamityarthaḥ|| 127 ||
āstāṃ tāvajjñānecchādernityatvaṃ, tadāśraya īśvaropādhirevāsiddhā īśvarasyāsiddherityata āha -- 
āśrayāsiddheśca|| 128 ||
sugamam|| 128 ||
nanvevaṃ brahmāṇḍādisarjanasamarthaṃ sarvajñatvādikaṃ kathaṃ janyaṃ sambhāvyetā'pi loke tapādhibhirevamaiśvaryādarśanāditi, tatrāha -- 
yogasiddhayopyauṣadhādisiddhivannāpalapanīyāḥ|| 129 ||
auṣadhādisiddhidṛṣṭāntena yogajā apyaṇimā''disiddhayaḥ sṛṣṭyādyupayoginyaḥ sidhyantītyarthaḥ
|| 129 ||
puruṣasiddhipratikūlatayā bhūtacaitanyavādinaṃ pratyācaṣṭe -- 
na bhūtacaitanyaṃ pratyekādṛṣṭeḥ sāṃhatye'pi ca
sāṃhatye'pi ca || 130 ||
saṃhatabhāvāvasthāyāmapi pañcabhūteṣu caitanyaṃ nāsti| vibhāgakāle pratyekaṃ caitanyādṛṣṭerityarthaḥ| tṛtīyādhyāye cedaṃ svasiddhāntavidhayoktam|atra ca paramatanirākaraṇāyeti na paunaruktyaṃ doṣāyeti | vīpsādhyāya samāptau
|| 130 ||
svasiddhāntaviruddhārthabhāṣiṇo ye kuvādinaḥ||
pañcame tān nirākṛtya svasiddhānto dṛḍhīkṛtaḥ|| 1||
iti śrīvijñānabhikṣunirmite kāpilasāṅkyapvacanasya bhāṣye
parapakṣanirjayādhyāyaḥ pañcamaḥ || 5 ||
atha ṣaṣṭho'dhyāyaḥ|| 6 ||
adhyāyacatuṣkeṇa samastaśāstrārthaṃ pratijñāya pañcamādhyāye parapakṣanirākaraṇena prasādhyedānīṃ tameva sārabhūtaśāstrārthaṃ ṣaṣṭhādhyāyena saṅkalayannupasaṃharati| uktārthānāṃ hi punastantrākhye vistare kṛte śiṣyāṇāmasandigdhāviparyasto dṛḍhataro bodha utpadyata ityataḥ sthūṇānikhanananyāyādanuktayuktyādyupanyāsācca nātra paunaruktyaṃ doṣāya |
astyātmā nāstitvasādhanābhāvāt || 1 ||
jānāmītyevaṃ pratīyamānatayā puruṣaḥ sāmānyataḥ siddha evāsti bādhakapramāṇābhāvāt| atastadvivekamātraṃ karttavyamityarthaḥ|| 1 ||
tatra viveke pramāṇadvayamāha sūtrābhyām -- 
dehādivyatirikto'sau vaicitryāt || 2 ||
asāvātmā draṣṭā dehādiprakṛtyantebhyo'tyantaṃ bhinno vaicitryāt| pariṇāmitvāpariṇāmitvādivaidharmyādityarthaḥ| prakṛtyādayastāvat pratyakṣānumānāgamaiḥ pariṇāmitayaiva siddhāḥ puruṣasyā'pariṇāmitvaṃ tu sadājñātaviṣayatvādanumīyate | tathāhi -- yathā cakṣuṣo rūpameva viṣayo, na tu sannikarṣasāmyepyanyadvastviti phalabalāt kalṛptam| buddhivṛttyārūḍhatayaiva tvanyadbhogyaṃ bhavati puruṣasya., na svataḥ | sarvadā sarvabhānāpatteḥ| tāśca buddhivṛttyo nājñātāstiṣṭhanti jñānecchāsukhādīnāmajñātasattāsvīkāre teṣvapi ghaṭādāviva saṃśayādiprasaṅgādahamahaṃ jānāmi na vā, sukhī na vetyādirūpeṇa | atasteṣāṃ sadā jñātatvāt taddraṣṭā cetano'pariṇāmītyāyātam| cetanasya pāraṇāmitve kadācidāndhyapariṇāmena satyā api buddhivṛtteradarśanena saṃśayādyāpatteriti| evaṃ pārārthyāpārārthyādikamapi pūrvoktaṃ vaidharmyajātaṃ bodhyam|| 2||
ṣaṣṭhīvyapadeśādapi || 3 ||
mamedaṃ śarīraṃ, mameyaṃ buddhirityāderviduṣāṃ ṣaṣṭhīvyapadeśādapi dehādibhya ātmā bhinnaḥ | atyantābhede ṣaṣṭhyanupapatterityarthaḥ| taduktaṃ viṣṇupurāṇe -- 
tvaṃ kimetacchiraḥ kintu śirastava tathodaram|
kimu pādādikaṃ tvaṃ vai tavaitaddhi mahīpate!||
samastāvayavebhyastvaṃ pūthagbhūya vyavasthitaḥ|
ko'hamityatra nipuṇo bhūtvā cintaya pārthiva !|| iti ||
na ca sthūlo'hamityādirapi vidvadravyapadeśo'stīti vācyam| śrutyābādhitatayā, mamātmā bhadrasena itivad gauṇatvenaiva tadupapatteriti || 3 ||
nanu 'puruṣasya caitanyaṃ' 'rāhoḥśiraḥ, śilāputrasya śarīra' mityādivyapadeśavadayamapi bhavatu tatrāha -- 
na śilāputravaddharmigrāhakamānabādhāt || 4 ||
śilāputrasya śarīramityādivadayaṃ ṣaṣṭhīvyapadeśo na bhavati | śilāputrādisthale dharmigrāhakapramāṇena bādhādvikalpamātram| sama śarīramiti vyapadeśe tu pramāṇabādho nāsti| dehātmatāyā eva bādhādityarthaḥ| yastu śāstreṣu mamakārapratiṣedhaḥ sa svābhyasyā'nityatayā vācārasbhaṇamātratvenāsatyatāpara eveti bhāvaḥ| puruṣasya caitanyamityatrāpyasti dharmigrāhakamānabādhaḥ| anavasthābhayena lāghavājña dehādivyatiriktatayātmasiddhau cetanyasvarūpatāvagāhanāditi || 4 ||
dehādivyatiriktatayā puruṣamavadhārya tanmuktimavadhārayati -- atyantaduḥkhanivṛttyā kṛtakṛtyatā|| 5 ||
sugamam|| 5 ||
nanu duḥkhanivṛttyā sukhasyāpi nivarttanāt tulyāyavyayatvena na sa puruṣārtha iti, tatrāha -- 
yathā duḥkhāt kleśaḥ puruṣasya na tathā
sukhādabhilāṣaḥ || 6 ||
viṣayavidhayā hetutāyāṃ pañcabhyau | kleśaścātra dveṣaḥ| yathā duḥkhe dveṣo balavattaro, naivaṃ sukhe'bhilāṣo balavattaro'pi tu tadapekṣayā durbala ityarthaḥ| tathāca sukhābhilāṣaṃ bādhitvāpi duḥkhadveṣo duḥkhanivattāvevecchāṃ janayatīti na tulyāyavyayatvamiti | taduktam -- 
abhyarthanābhaṅgabhayena sādhirmādhyasthyamiṣṭe'pyabalambate'rthe|| iti ||
yā tu narakādiduḥkhadarśane'pi kṣudrasukhapravṛttiḥ sā rāgādidoṣavaśādeveti || 6 ||
sukhāpekṣayā duḥkhasya bahulatvādapi duḥkhanivṛttireva puruṣārtha ityāha -- 
kutrāpi ko'pi sukhīti || 7 ||
anantatṛṇavṛkṣapaśupakṣimanuṣyādimadhye svalpo manuṣyadevādireva sukhībhavatītyarthaḥ| iti hetau|| 7 ||
tadapi kādācitkaṃ kvācitkasukhaṃ madhuviṣasampṛktānnavaj vicārakāṇāṃ heyamevetyāha -- 
tadapi duḥkhaśabalamiti duḥkhapakṣe niḥkṣipante
vivecakāḥ|| 8 ||
tadapi pūrvasūtroktaṃ sukhamapi duḥkhamiśritamityato duḥkhamiśritamityato duḥkhakoṭau sukhaduḥkhavavecakā niḥkṣipanta ityarthaḥ| taduktaṃ yogasūtreṇa pariṇāmatāpasaṃskāraduḥkhairguṇavṛttivirodhācca sarvameva duḥkhaṃ vivekina iti ||
viṣṇupurāṇe'pi -- 
yadyat prītikaraṃ pumasāṃ vastu maitreya ! jāyate|
tadeva duḥkhavṛkṣasya bījatmavupagacchati || iti || 8 ||
kevalā duḥkhanivṛttirna puruṣārthaḥ, kintu sukhoparakteti matamapākaroti -- 
sukhalābhābhāvādapuruṣārthatvamiti cenna dvaividhyāt|| 9 ||
sukhalābhābhaāvānmokṣākhyaduḥkhābhāvasyāpuruṣārthatvamiti cenna | puruṣārthasya dvaividhyāt -- dviprakāratvāt| sukhatvaduḥkhābhāvatvābhyāmityarthaḥ| sukhī syāṃ, duḥkhī na syāmiti hi pṛthageva lokānāṃ prārthanā dṛśyata iti || 9 ||
śaṅkate -- nirguṇatvamātmano'saṅgatvādiśruteḥ|| 10 ||
nanvātmano nirguṇatvaṃ sukhaduḥkhamohādyakhilaguṇaśūnyatvaṃ nityameva siddham -- asaṅgatvaśruteḥ, vikārahetusaṃyogābhāvaśravaṇāt| taṃ vinā ca guṇākhyavikārāsambhavāt| ato na duḥkhanivṛttirapi puruṣārtho ghaṭata ityardhaḥṣa nanu saṃyogaṃ vinā svayameva vikāro bhavatviti cenna|
dāhāya nānalo vanhernāpaḥ klodāya cāmbhasaḥ|
tad dravyameva taddravyavikārāya na vai yataḥ||
kiñca svayaṃ vikāritve mokṣo naivopapadyate|
svayaṃ mohavikāreṇa punarbandhaprasaṅgataḥ|| iti ||
tathā coktaṃ kaurme -- 
yadyātmā malino'svaccho vikārī syāt svabhāvataḥ|
na hi tasya bhavenmuktirjanmāntaraśatairapi || iti || 10 ||
samādhatte -- 
paradharmatve'pi tatsiddhiravivekāt|| 11 ||
sukhaduḥkhādiguṇānāṃ cittadharmatve'pi prakṛtipuruṣasaṃyogadvāretyarthaḥ| etacca prathamādhyāye pratipāditam| 'nimittatvamavivekasya na dṛṣṭahāni'riti tṛtīyādhyāyasūtre ceti| tathā ca sphaṭike lauhityamiva puruṣe pratibimbarūpeṇa duḥkhasattvāt tannivṛttireva puruṣārthaḥ| pratibimbadvārakaduḥkhasambandhasyaiva bhogatayā pratibimbarūpeṇaiva duḥkhasya heyatvāditi|| 11 ||
avivekamūlaḥ puruṣe guṇabandho'vivekastu ityākāṅkṣāyāmāha -- 
anādiraviveko'nyathā doṣadvayaprasakteḥ|| 12 ||
agṛhītāsaṃsargakamubhayaviṣayakajñānamavivekaḥ| sa ca pravāharūpeṇānādiścittadharmaḥ pralaye vāsanārūpeṇa tiṣṭhati | anyathā tasya sāditve doṣadvayaprasaṅgāt | sāditve hi svata evotpāde muktasyāpi bandhāpattiḥ| karmādijanyatve ca karmādikaṃ pratyapi kāraṇatvenā'vivekāntarānveṣaṇe'na vasthetyarthaḥ| ayaṃ cāviveko vṛttirūpaḥ pratibimbātmanā puruṣadharma iva bhavatītyataḥ puruṣasya bandhaprayojaka iti prāgevoktaṃ vakṣyate|| 12 ||
nanu cedanādistarhi nityaḥ syāditi, tatrāha -- 
na nityaḥ syādātmavadanyathānucchittiḥ|| 13 ||
ātmavannityo'khaṇḍānādirna bhavati, kintu pravāharūpeṇā'nādiḥ| anyathānādibhāvasyocchedānupapatterityarthaḥ|| 13 ||
bandhakāraṇamuktvā mokṣakāraṇamāha -- 
pratiniyatakāraṇanāśyatvamasya dhvāntavat|| 14 ||
asya bandhakāraṇasyāvivekasya śuktirajatādisthale pratiniyataṃ yannāśakāraṇaṃ vivekastannāśyatvaṃ tamovat| andhakāro hi pritiniyatenālokenaiva nāśyate, nānyasādhanenetyarthaḥ| taduktaṃ viṣṇupurāṇo -- 
andhantama ivājñānaṃ dīpavaccendriyodbhavam|
yathā sūryastathā jñānaṃ yadviparṣe ! vivekajam|| iti || 14 ||
vivekenaivāviveko nāśyata iti pratiniyamasya grāhakamapyāha -- 
atrāpi pratiniyamo'nvayavyatirekāt || 15 ||
dhvāntālokayoriva prakṛte'pi pratiniyamaḥ śuktirajatādiṣvanvayavyatirekābhyāmeva grāhya ityarthaḥ| athavaivaṃ vyākhyeyam| nanu vivekasyāpi kiṃ pratiniyataṃ kāraṇaṃ? tatrāha -- atrāpi viveke'pi kāraṇaniyamo'nvayavyatirekābhyāmeva siddheḥ| śravaṇamanananididhiyāsanarūpameva kāraṇaṃ, natu karmādīti | karmādikaṃ tu bahiraṅgamevetyarthaḥ|| 15 ||
bandhasya svābhāvikatvādikaṃ na sambhavatīti prathamapādoktaṃ smārayati -- 
prakārāntarāsambhavādaviveka eva bandhaḥ|| 16 ||
bandho'tra duḥkhayogākhyabandhakāraṇam| śeṣaṃ sugamam|| 16 ||
nanu mukterapi kāryatayā vināśāpattyā punarbandhaḥ syāditi tatrāha -- 
na muktasya punarbandhayogo'pyanāvṛttiśruteḥ|| 17 ||
bhāvakāryasyaiva vināśitayā mokṣasya nāśo nāsti|'na sa punarāvarttata' iti śruterityarthaḥ| apiśabdaḥ pūrvasūtroktārthasamuccaye|| 17 ||
apuruṣārthatvamanyathā || 18 ||
anyathā muktasyāpi punarbandhe pralayavadeva mokṣasyāpuruṣārthatvaṃ, paramapuruṣārthatvābhāvo vā syādityarthaḥ|| 18 ||
apuruṣārthatve hetumāha -- 
aviśeṣāpattirubhayoḥ|| 19 ||
bhāvibandhatvasāmyenobhayormuktabaddhayorviśeṣo na syāt| tataścāpuruṣārthatvamityarthaḥ|| 19 ||
nanvevaṃ baddhamuktayorviśeṣā'bhyupagame nityamuktatvaṃ kathamucyate? tatrāha -- 
muktirantarāyadhvasterna paraḥ|| 20 ||
vakṣyamāṇāntarāyasya dhvaṃsādatiriktaḥ padārtho na muktirityarthaḥ| yathā hi svabhāvaśuklasya sphaṭikasya japopādhinimittaṃ raktatvaṃ śauklyāvarakarūpaṃ vidhnamātraṃ, na tu japopadhānena śauklya' naśyati, japāpāye cotpadyate| tathaiva svabhāvarduḥkhasya ātmano buddhayupādhikaṃ duḥkhapratibimbaṃ tadāvarakarūpaṃ vighnamātraṃ, na tu budhyupadhānena duḥkhaṃ jāyate, tadapāye ca naśyatīti | ato nityamukta āthmā, bandhamokṣau tu vyāvahārikāvityavirodha iti || 20 ||
nanvevaṃ bandhamokṣayormithyātve mokṣasya puruṣārthatāpratipādakaśrutyādivirodha ityāha -- 
tatrāpyavirodhaḥ || 21 ||
tatrāpyantarāyadhvaṃsasya mokṣatve'pi puruṣārthatvāvirodha ityarthaḥ| duḥkhayogaviyogāveva hi puruṣe kalpitau, na tu duḥkhabhogo'pi bhogaśca pratibimbarūpeṇa duḥkhasambandha ityataḥ pratibimbarūpeṇa duḥkhanivṛttiryathārthaiva puruṣārthaḥ. sa evāntarāyadhvaṃsaḥ| tādṛśaśca mokṣo yathārtha eveti bhāvaḥ|| 21 ||
nanvantarāyadhvaṃsamātraṃ cenmuktistarhi śravaṇamātreṇaiva tatsiddhiḥ syāt ajñānapratibaddhakaṇṭhacāmīkarasiddhivaditi| tatrāha -- 
adhikāritraividhyānna niyamaḥ|| 22 ||
uttamamadhyamādhamāstrividhā jñānādhikāriṇaḥ| tena śravaṇamātrānantarameva mānasasākṣātkāraḥ sarveṣāmiti na niyama ityarthaḥ| ato mandādhikāradoṣādvirocanādīnāṃ śravaṇamātrāccittavilāyanakṣamaṃ mānasajñānaṃ notpannam| na tu śravaṇamātraṃ jñānajananāsāmarthyāditi|| 22 ||
na kevalaṃ śravaṇamātraṃ jñāne dṛṣṭakāraṇamanyadapītyāha -- 
dārḍhyārthamuttareṣām|| 23 ||
śravaṇāduttareṣāṃ manananididhyāsanādīnāmantarāyadhvaṃsasyātyantikatvarūpadārḍhyārthaṃ niyama ityanuṣajjyate || 23 ||
uttarāṇyeva sādhanānyāha -- 
sthirasukhamāsanamiti na niyamaḥ|| 24 ||
āsane padmāsanādiniyamo nāsti| yataḥ sthiraṃ sukhaṃ ca yat tadevāsanamityarthaḥ|| 24 ||
mukhyaṃ sādhanamāha -- 
dhyānaṃ nirviṣayaṃ manaḥ|| 25 ||
vṛttiśūnyaṃ yadantaḥkaraṇaṃ bhavati tadeva dhyānaṃ yogaścittavṛttinirodharūpaityarthaḥ| etatsādhanatvena dhyānasya vakṣyamāṇatvāditi || 25 ||
nanu yogāyogayoḥ puruṣasyaikarūpyāt kiṃ yogenetyāśaṅkya samādhatte -- 
ubhayathāpyaviśeṣaścennaivamuparāganirodhādviśeṣaḥ|| 26 ||
uparāganirodhādvattipratibimbapagamādyogāvasthāyāmayogāvasthāto viśeṣaḥ puruṣasyeti siddhāntadalārthaḥ| śeṣaṃ vyākhyātaprāyam|| 26 ||
nanu niḥsaṅge kathamuparāgastatrāha -- 
niḥsaṅge'pyuparāgo'vivekāt|| 27 ||
niḥsaṅge yadyapi pāramārthika uparāgo nāsti, tathāpyuparāga iva bhavatīti kṛtvā pratibimba evoparāga iti vyavahriyate uparāgavivekibhirityarthaḥ|| 27 ||
etadeva vivṛṇoti -- 
japāsphaṭikayoriva noparāgaḥ kintvabhimānaḥ|| 28 ||
yatā japāsphaṭikayornoparāgaḥ, kintu japāpratibimbavaśāduparāgābhimānamātraṃ, raktaḥ sphaṭika iti| tathaiva buddhipuruṣayornoparāgaḥ| kintu buddhipratibimbavaśāduparāgābhimāno'vivekavaśādityarthaḥ| ata uparāgatulyatayā vṛttipratibimba eva puruṣoparāga iti sūtradvayaparyavasito'rthaḥ| sa eva ca duḥkhātmakavṛtteruparāgo duḥkhanivṛttyākhyamokṣasyāntarāyastasya ca dhvaṃsaścittalayāt| so'pi ca cittavṛttinirodhākhyenāsamprajñātayogenetyato yogādevāntarāyadhvaṃso bhavatīti yogaśāstrasyāpi siddhāntaḥ|| 28 ||
dhyānaṃ nirviṣayaṃ mana iti yoga uktastasya sādhanānyaicakṣāṇa eva yathoktoparāgasya nirodhopāyamāha -- 
dhyānadhāraṇābhyāsavairāgyādibhistannirodhaḥ|| 29 ||
samādhidvārā dhyānaṃ yogasya kāraṇaṃ dhyānasya ca kāraṇaṃ dhāraṇā| tasyāśca kāraṇamabhyāsaścittasthairyasādhanānuṣṭhānam, abhyāsasyāpi kāraṇaṃ viṣayavairāgyaṃ, tasyāpi doṣadarśanayamaniyamādikamiti pātañjaloktaprakriyayā tannirodha uparāganirodho bhavati cittavṛttinirodhākhyayogadvāretyarthaḥ|| 29 ||
cittaniṣṭadhyānādinā puruṣasyoparāganirodhe pūrnācāryasiddhaṃ dvāraṃ darśayati -- 
layavikṣepayorvyāvṛttyetyācāryāḥ|| 30 ||
dhyānādinā cittasya nidrāvṛtteḥ pramāṇādivṛtteśca nivṛttyā puruṣasyāpi vṛttyuparāganirodho bhavati | bimbanirodhe pratibimbasyāpi nirodhāditi pūrvācāryā āhurityarthaḥ| yathā patañjaliḥ, 'yogaścittavṛttinirodhaḥ, tadādraṣṭuḥsvarūpe'vasthānam, vṛttisārūpyamitaratre'ti sūtratrayeṇaitadevāha| tathā -- 
nityaḥ sarvatrago hyātpnā buddhisannidhimattayā|
yathā yathā bhaved buddhirātmā tadvidiheṣyate||
ityādismṛtayo'pyetadāhurita| tadevamasaṃprajñācayogādeva mokṣāntarāyadhvaṃsa iti praghaṭṭakārthaḥ||30||
dhyānādau guhādisthānaniyamo nāstītyāha -- 
na sthānaniyamaścittaprasādāt || 31 ||
cittaprasādādeva dhyānādikam| atastatra na guhādisthānaniyama ityarthaḥ| śāstraṃ tvautsargikābhiprāyeṇaivāraṇyagiriguhādisthānaṃ yogasyoddiṣṭamiti| ata eva brahmasūtramapi 'yatraikāgratā tatrāviśeṣā'diti
|| 31 ||
samāptau mokṣavicāraḥ| idānīṃ puruṣāpariṇāmitvāya jagatkāraṇaṃ vicārayati -- 
prakṛterādyopādānatānyeṣāṃ kāryatvaśruteḥ|| 32 ||
mahadādīnāṃ kāryatvaśravaṇāt teṣāṃ mūlakāraṇatayā prakṛtiḥ sidhyatītyarthaḥ|| 32 ||
puruṣa evopādānaṃ bhavatu, tatrāha -- 
nityatve'pi nātmano yogatvābhāvāt|| 33 ||
guṇavattvaṃ saṅgitvaṃ copādānayogyatā tayorabhāvāt puruṣasya nityatve'pi nopādānatvamityarthaḥ
|| 33 ||
nanu 'bahlīḥ prajāḥ puruṣāt sampasūtā' ityādiśruteḥ puruṣasya kāraṇatvāvagamādvivarttādivādā āśrayaṇīyā ityāśaṅkyāha -- 
śritivirodhānna kutarkāpasadasyātmalābhaḥ|| 34 ||
puruṣakāraṇatāyāṃ ye ye pakṣāḥ sambhāvitāste sarve śrutiviruddhā ityatastadabhyupagantṛṇāṃ kutarkikādyadhamānāmātmasvarūpajñānaṃ na bhavatītyarthaḥ| etenātmani sukhaduḥkhādiguṇopādānatvavādino'pi kutārkikā eva| teṣāmapyātmayathārthajñānaṃ nāstītyavagantavyam| ātmakāraṇatāśrutayaśca śaktiśaktimadabhedonopasānārthā eva | ajāmekāmityādiśrutibhiḥ pradhānakāraṇataḥsiddheḥ. yadi cākaśasthā'bhrādyadhiṣṭhānakāraṇatvamucyate tadā tanna nirākurmaḥ| pariṇāmasyaiva pratiṣedhāditi|| 34 ||
sthāvarajaṅgamādiṣu pṛthivyādīnāmeva kāraṇatvadarśanāt prakṛteḥ sarvopādānatvam? tatrāha -- 
pāramparye'pi pradhānānuvṛttiraṇuvat|| 35 ||
sthāvarādiṣu paramparayā kāraṇatve'pi teṣu pradhānasyā'numānādupādānatnamakṣatam| yathāṅkurādidvāraktve'pi sthāvarādiṣu pārtivādyaṇūnāmanugamādupādānatvamittyarthaḥ|| 35 ||
vananyoyena prakṛtervyāpakatve pramāṇamāha -- 
sarvatra kāryadarśānādvibhutvam|| 36 ||
avyavasthayā sarvatra vākāradarśanāt pradhānasya vibhutvam yatā'ṇordhaṭādivyāpitvamityarthaḥ. etacca prāgeva vyākyātam|| 36 ||
nanu paricchinnatve'pi yatra kāryamutpadyate tatra gacchatīti vaktavyaṃ, tatrāha -- 
gatiyoge'pyādyakāraṇatāniraṇuvat|| 37 ||
gatisvīkāre'pi paricchinnatayā mīlakāraṇatvābhāvaḥ pārthivādyaṇudṛṣṭāntenetyarthaḥ| athavetthaṃ vyākhyeyam| nanu triguṇātmakapradhānasyānyo'nyasaṃyogārthaṃ śrutismṛtiṣu kriyā kṣobhākhyā śrūyate kiyāvattvācca tantvādidṛṣṭāntena mūlakāraṇatvābhāva ityāśaṅkya pariharati| gatiyoge'pyādyakāraṇatāhāniraṇuvat| gatiḥ kiyā tatsattve'pi mūlakāraṇatāyā
ahāniryathā śeṣikamate pārthivādyaṇūnāmityarthaḥ|| 37 ||
nanupṛthivyādīnāṃ navānāmeva dravyāṇāṃ darśanāt kathaṃ pṛthivātvīdiśūnyaṃ pradhānākhyaṃ dravyaṃ ghaṭeta | na ca pradhānaṃ dravyameva mā'stviti vācyam| saṃyogavibhāgapariṇāmādibhirdravyatvasiddheriti, tatrāha -- 
prasiddhādhikyaṃ pradhānasya na niyamaḥ|| 38 ||
prasiddhanavadravyādhikyameva pradhānasyāto navaiva dravyāṇīti na niyama ityarthaḥ| aṣṭānāmeva kāryatvaśaṣravaṇaṃ cātra tarka iti bhāvaḥ|| 38 ||
kiṃ sattvādayo guṇā eva prakṛtirathavā guṇatrayarūpadravyādhārabhūtā prakṛtiriti saṃśaye'vadhārayati -- 
sattvādīnāmataddharmatvaṃ tadrūpatvāt|| 39 ||
sattvādiguṇānāṃ prakṛtidharmatvaṃ nāsti, prakṛtisvarūpatvādityarthaḥ| yadyapi śrutismṛtiṣūbhayameva śrūyate, tathāpi tarkataḥ svarūpatvamevāvadhāryate, na tu dharmatvam| tathāhi | sattvāditrayaṃ kiṃ prakṛteḥ kāryarūpo dharmo'thavā ākāśasya vāyuvat saṃyogamātreṇa nitya eva dharmaḥ syāt| ādye ekasyā eva prakṛterdravyāntarasaṅgaṃ vinā vicitraguṇatrayotpattyasambhavaḥ, dṛṣṭaviruddhakalpanā'nocityaṃ ca | antye nityebhya eva sattvādibhyo'nyānyasaṅgena vicitrasakalakāryopapattau tadatiriktaprakṛtikalpanāvaiyarthyamiti| sattvādīnāṃ prakṛtikāryatvādivacanāni yāṃśataḥ prakāśādikāryopahitatayābhivyaktyādikameva bodhayanti| yathā pṛtivīto dvīpotpattiriti || 39 ||
pradhānapravṛtteḥ prayodanamavadhārayati, niṣprayojanapravṛttyabhyupagame mokṣānupapatteriti -- 
anupabhoge'pi pumarthaṃ mṛṣṭiḥ pradhānasyoṣṭrakuṅkumavahanavat|| 40 ||
tṛtīyādhyāyasthe, pradhānasṛṣṭiḥ parārthetyādisūtre vyākhyātamidam|| 40 ||
vicitrasṛṣṭau nimittakāraṇamāha -- 
karmavaicitryāt sṛṣṭivaicitryam|| 41 ||
karma dharmādharmau| sugamamanyat || 41 ||
nanu bhavatu pradhānāt sṛṣṭiḥ? pralayastu kasmāt. na hyekasmāt kāraṇādviruddhakāryadvayaṃ ghaṭate| tatrāha -- 
sāmyavaiṣamyābhyāṃ kāryadvayam|| 42 ||
sattvādiguṇatrayaṃ pradhānam| teṣāṃ ca vaiṣamyaṃ nyūnātiriktabhāvena saṃhananam| tadabhāvaḥ sāmyam| tābhyāṃ hetubhyāmekasmādeva sṛṣṭipralayarūpaṃ viruddhakāryadvayaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ| sthitistu sṛṣṭimadhye praviṣṭetyāśayena tatkāraṇatvaṃ pradhānasya na pṛthagvicāritam|| 42 ||
nanu pradhānasya sṛṣṭisvābhāvyajjñānottaramapi saṃsāraḥ syāt, tatrāha -- 
vimuktabodhānna sṛṣṭiḥ pradhānasya lokavat || 43 ||
vimuktatayā puruṣasākṣātkārāddhetoḥ pradhānasya tatpuruṣārtha punaḥ sṛṣṭirna bhavati | kṛtārthatvāt | lokavat| yathā lokā amātyādayo rājño'rthaṃ sampādya kṛtārthāḥ santo na punaḥrājārthaṃ pravarttate. tathaiva pradhānamityarthaḥ| vimuktamokṣārthaṃ hi pradhānapravṛttirityuktam, sa ca jñānānniṣpanna iti bhāvaḥ|| 43 ||
nanu pradhānasya sṛṣṭyu paramo nāsti, ajñānāṃ saṃsāradarśanāt | tathāca pradhānasṛṣṭyā muktasyāpi punarbandhaḥ syāt, tatrāha -- 
nānyopasarpaṇe'pi muktopabhogau nimittābhāvāt|| 44 ||
kāryakāraṇasaṅghātādisṛṣṭyā'nyān prati pradhānasyopasarpaṇe'pi na muktasyopabhogo bhavati, nimittābhāvāt | upabhoge nimittānāṃ svopādhisaṃyogaviśeṣatatkāraṇāvivekādīnāmabhāvādityarthaḥ| idameva hi muktaṃ pratipradhānasṛṣṭyuparamo yat tadbhāgahetoḥ svopādhipariṇāmaviśeṣasya janmākhyasyānutpādanamiti|| 44 ||
nanviyaṃ vyavasthā tadā ghaṭeta yadi puruṣabahutvaṃ syāt, tadeva tvātmādvaitaśrutibādhitamityāśaṅkyāha -- 
puruṣabahutvaṃ vyavasthātaḥ|| 45 ||
'ye tadviduramṛtāste bhavantyathetare duḥkhamovāpiyantī'tyādiśrutyuktabandhamokṣavyavasthāta eva puruṣabahutvaṃ siddhyatītyarthaḥ|| 45 ||
nanūpādhiścet svīkriyate tarhyupādhisiddhyaiva punaradvaitabhaṅga ityarthaḥ| vsatutastūpādhibhede'pi vyavasthā na sambhavatīti prathamādhyāya eva prapañcitam|| 46 ||
nanūpādhayo'pyāvidyakā iti, -- na tairadvaitabhaṅga ityāśaṅkāyāmāha -- 
dvābhyāmapi pramāṇavirodhaḥ|| 47 ||
puruṣo'vidyeti dvābhyāmapyaṅgākṛtābhyāmadvaitapramāṇasya śrutervirodhastadavastha evetyarthaḥ|| 47 ||
aparamapi dūṣaṇadvayamāha -- 
dvābhyāmapyavirodhānna pūrvamuttaraṃ ca sādhakābhāvāt|| 48 ||
dvābhyāmapyaṅgīkṛtābhyāṃ hetubhyāṃ pūrvaṃ pūrvapakṣo bhavatāṃ na ghaṭate | asmābhirapi prakṛtiḥ puruṣaśceti dvayorevāṅgākārāt | vikārasyānityatayā vācārambhaṇamātratāyā asmābhirapīṣṭatvāt| nanu puruṣanānātvasvīkārāt prakṛternityatvasvīkārāccāstyevāsmadvigedha ityāśaṅya dūṣaṇāntaramāha -- uttaraṃ cetyādinā | advaitavādināmuttaraṃ siddhāntaśca na ghaṭate, ātmasādhakapramāṇasyābhāvāt | tadaṅgīkāre ca tenaivādvaitahāniriti jitaṃ tairātmyavādibhirityarthaḥ|| 48 ||
nanu svaprakāśata ātmā saṃtsyati, tatrāha -- 
prakāśatastatsiddhau karmakartṛvirodhaḥ|| 49 ||
caitanyarūpaprakāśataścaitanyasiddhau karmakartṛvirodha ityartaḥ| prakāśya prakāśasambandhe hi prakāśanamālokādiṣu dṛṣṭam| svasya sākṣāt svasmin sambandhaśca viruddha iti | asmanmate tu buddhivṛttyākhyapramāṇāṅgīkārāt taddvārā pratibimbarūpasya svasya bimbarūpe svasmina sambandho ghaṭate | yathā sūrye jaladvārā pratibimbarūpasvasambandha iti bhāvaḥ| ātmanaḥ svaprakāśatvaśrutistvananyopādhikaprakāśādiparā bodhyā|| 49 ||
nanu nāsti karmakartṛvirodhaḥ| svaniṣṭaprakāśadharmadvārā svasya svasambandhasambhavāt, yathā vaiśeṣikāṇāṃ svaniṣṭhajñānadvārā svasya svayaṃ viṣaya iti, tatrāha -- 
jaḍavyāvṛtto jaḍaṃ prakāśayati cidrūpaḥ || 50 ||
cetane prakāśarūpadharmaḥ sabaryādiṣviva nāsti, kintu citsvarūpa eva padārtho jaḍa prakāśayati | yatojaḍavyāvṛttimātreṇa cidityucyate, na tu jaḍavilakṣaṇadharmavattayetyarthaḥ| ata eva nirdharmatayā "sa eṣa neti netī"tyeva śrutyāpadiśyate, na tu vidhimukhatayeti | tathā ca smṛtirapi -- 
idaṃ taditi nirdeṣṭuṃ guruṇaāpi na śakyate|| iti ||
jaḍavyāvṛttāviti pāṭhe'pi hetau saptamyāmayamevārthaḥ| asmiṃmaśca sūtre jaḍameva prakāśayati cidrūpo na
tvātmānamiti nārthaḥ| tathā sati hi tasyājñeyatvena sādhakābhāvarūpaṃ bādhakaṃ pareṣūpanyāsānarham, svasyāpi tulyanyāyatvāditi|| 50 ||
nanvevaṃ pramāṇādyanurodhena dvaitasiddhāvadvaitaśruteḥ kā gatistatrāha -- 
na śrutivirodho rāgiṇāṃ vairāgyāya tatsiddheḥ|| 51 ||
advaitaśrutivirodhastu nāsti| rāgiṇāṃ puruṣātirikte vairāgyāyaiva śrutibhiradvaitasādhanāt| puruṣajñāna iva dvaitābhāvajñāne svatantraphalāntarāśravaṇāt| tacca vairāgyaṃ sadadvaitenaivopapadyate| sattvaṃ ca kūṭasthatvamityarthaḥ| ata eva śrutirapi sadadvaitameva chāndogye pratipāditavatīti bhāvaḥ|| 51 ||
na kevalamuktyaivādvaitavādino heyāḥ, api tu jagadasatyatāgrāhakapramāṇābhāvenā'pītyāha -- 
nidrādidoṣaduṣṭāntaḥkaraṇā''dijanyavād bādhakābhāvāt|| 52 ||
nidrādidoṣaduṣṭāntaḥkaraṇā''dijanyatvena svāpnaviṣayaśaṅkhapītimādīnāmasatyatvaṃ loke dṛṣṭam| tacca mahadādiprapañce nāsti| tatkāraṇasya prakṛterhiraṇyagarbhābuddheścāduṣṭatvāt 'yathāpūrvamakalpaya'diti śravaṇāt| nanu 'neha nānāstikiñcane'tyādiśrutyā bādhitatvenā'vidyādināmā kaścanānādirdoṣaḥ kalpanīyastatrāha bādhakābhāvāditi | ayaṃ bhāvaḥ. "neha nānāsti kiñcane" tyādiśrutayo yāḥ paraiḥ prapañcabādhakatayābhipreyante tāḥ prakāraṇānusāreṇa vibhāgādipratiṣedhikā eva, na tu prapañcātyantatucchatāparāḥ, svasyāpi bādhāpattyā svārthāsādhakatvaprasaṅgāt| na hi svapnakālīnaśabdasya bādhe tajjñāpito'pyarthaḥ punarna sandihyata iti | tasmādātmāvighātakatayā śrutayo na prapañcasyātyantabādhaparā iti | tatra 'neha nānāsti kiñcane'tyādiśruterbrahmavibhaktaṃ kimapi nāstītyarthaḥ, sarva samāpnoṣi tato'si sarva' ityādismṛtyaikavākyatvāt. 'vācārambhaṇaṃ vikāro nāmadheyaṃ mṛttiketyeva satya'mityādiśrutestu nityatārūpapāramārthikasattāviraho'rthaḥ, anyatā mṛttikādṛṣṭāntāsiddheḥ| na hi loke mṛttikāvikārāṇāmatyantatucchatvaṃ siddhaṃ, yena dṛṣṭāntatā syāditi||
na nirodho na cotpattirna baddho na ca syāditi||
na mumukṣurna vai mukta ityeṣa paramārthatā||
ityādiśrutestvātmā'tiriktasya kūṭasthanityatārūpātiparamārthasattāviraho'rthaḥ| kiñcā''tmano nirodhādyabhāvo'rthaḥ, anyathaitādṛṣajñānasya mokṣaphalakatvapratipādanavirodhāt| na hi mokṣo mithyeti pratipādya mokṣasya phalatvamapramattaḥ pratipādayatīti| yāścātmaikyaśrutayastāstu prathamodhyāya eva vyākhyātāḥ| brahmamīmāṃsābhāṣye caitā anyāsca śrutayo'smābhirvyākhyātā iti dik|| 52 ||
na kevalaṃ vartamānadaśāyāmeva prapañcaḥ sannapi tu sadaivetyāha -- 
prakārāntarāsambhavāt sadutpāttiḥ|| 53 ||
pūrvoktayuktivṛttibhirasadutpādāsambhavāt sūkṣmarūpeṇa sadevotpadyate'bhivyaktaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ|| 53 ||
kartṛtvabhoktṛtvayorvaiyadhikaraṇye'pi vyavasthāmupapādayati sūtrābhyām -- 
ahaṅkāraḥ kartā na puruṣaḥ|| 54 ||
hyabhimānavṛttikamantaḥkaraṇamahaṅkāraḥ| sa eva kṛtimān | abhimānottarameva prāyaśaḥ pravṛttidarśanāt | na tu puruṣaḥ, apariṇāmitvādityarthaḥ| pūrvaṃ ca dharmādikaṃ buddheriti yaduktaṃ tadekyaivāntaḥkaraṇasya vṛttimātrabhedāśayena|| 54 ||
cidavasānā bhuktistatkarmārjitatvāt|| 55 ||
ahaṅkārasya kartṛtve'pi bhogaścityeva paryavasanno bhavati| ahaṅkārasya saṃhatatvena parārthatvāt| nanvevamanyaniṣṭhakarmaṇā'nyasya bhoge puruṣaviśeṣaniyamo na syāt, tatrāha -- tatkarmārjitatvāditi| ahaṅkāreṇāsañjitaṃ tasyāścito
yat karma tajjanyatvādbhogasyetyarthaḥ| tathā ca yo'haṅkāro yaṃ puruṣamādāyācetane'haṃ mameti vṛttiṃ karoti tasyāhaṅkārasya karma tasyātmana ucyate | tenaiva ca karmaṇā tatrā''tmani bhogo'rjyata iti nātiprasaṅga ityāśayaḥ|| 55 ||
brahmalokāntagatibhirnāsti niṣkṛtiriti pūrvokte kāraṇaṃ darśayati -- 
candrādiloke'pyāvṛttirnimittasadbhāvāt|| 56 ||
nimittamavivekakarmādikam| sugamamanyat || 56 ||
nanu tattallokavāsijanopadeśādanāvṛttiḥsyāt, tatrāha -- 
lokasya nopadeśāt siddhiḥ pūrvavat|| 57 ||
yathā pūrvasya manuṣyalokasyopadeśamātrānna siddhirjñānaniṣpattirevaṃ tattallokasthalokasyopadeśamātrāttadgatānāṃ jñānaniṣpattirna bhavatītyarthaḥ|| 57 ||
nanvekaṃ 'brahmalokādanāvṛtti'riti śruteḥ kā gatistatrāha -- 
pāramparyeṇa tatsiddhau vimuktiśrutiḥ|| 58 ||
brahmalokādigatānāṃ śravaṇamananādiparamparayā prāyaśo jñānasiddhau satyāṃ vimuktiśravaṇaṃ na tu sākṣādgatimātreṇetyarthaḥ| prāyikatvādanyalokādviśeṣa iti || 58 ||
paripūrṇatve'pyātmano gatiśrutimupapādayati -- 
gatiśruteśca vyāpakatve'pyupādhiyogādbhogadeśakālalābho vyomavat || 59 ||
vyāpakatve'pyātmano gatiśravaṇānurodhena bhogadeśasya kālavaśāllābhaḥ siddhyati | vyomavadupādhiyogenetyarthaḥ| yathā hyākāśasya pūrṇatve'pi deśaviśeṣagatirghaṭādyupādhiyogād vyavahniyate | tathā ca śrutiḥ -- 
ghaṭasaṃvṛtamākāśaṃ nīyamāne ghaṭe yathā |
ghaṭo nīyate nākāśaṃ tadvajjīvo nabhopamaḥ|| iti || 59 ||
bhokturadhiṣṭhānādbhogāyatananirmāṇamiti yaduktaṃ tat prapañcayati sūtrābhyām -- 
anadhiṣṭhitasya pūtibhāvaprasaṅgānna tatsiddhiḥ|| 60 ||
bhoktranadhiṣṭhitasya śukrādeḥ pūtibhāvaprasaṅgānna pūrvoktabhogatanasiddhirityarthaḥ.| 60 ||
nanvadhiṣṭhānaṃ vinavādṛṣṭadvārā bhoktṛbhyo bhogāyatananirmāṇaṃ bhavatu, tatrāha -- 
adṛṣṭadvārā cedasambaddhasyatadasambhavājjalādivadaṅkure|| 61 ||
śukrādau sākṣādasambaddhasyādṛṣṭasya śarīrādinirmāṇe bhoktṛdvāratvāsambhavād bījāsambaddhānāṃ jalādīnātpattau karṣakādidvāratvavadityarthaḥ| athaḥ svāśrayasaṃyogasambandhaḥ śukrādiṣu vaktavyaḥ| tathā ca siddhamadṛṣṭavadātmasaṃyogarūpeṇā'dhiṣṭhānasya bhogopakaraṇanirmāṇahetutvamiti bhāvaḥ|| 61 ||
vaiśeṣikādinayenādṛṣṭasya sambandhaghaṭakatayātmano'dhiṣṭātṛtvaṃ sthāpitam, svasiddhānte tvadṛṣṭādīnāmātmadharmabhāvāt tadadvārā bhokturhetutvameva na sambhavatītyāha -- 
nirguṇatvāt tadasambhavādahaṅkāradharmā hyete || 62 ||
bhokturnirguṇatvenādṛṣṭāsambhavācca nādṛṣṭadvārakatvam| hi yasmādete'dṛṣṭādayo'haṅaghkārasyāntaḥkaraṇasāmānyasyaiva dharmā ityarthaḥ| tathā cāsmanmate dvāranairapekṣyeṇa saṃyogamātreṇa sākṣādeva bhokturadhiṣṭhānaṃ siddhyatīti bhāvaḥ|| 62 ||
nanu cet puruṣo vyāpakastarhi, -- 
bālāgraśatabhāgasya śatadhā kalpitasya ca|
bhogo jīvaḥ sa vijñeyaḥ sa cānantyāya kalpate||
iti śrutipracipāditaṃ jīvaparicchinnatvamanupapannam, tathośvarapratiṣedhāt puruṣāṇāṃ caikarūpyājjīvātmaparamātmavibhāgo'pi śāstrīyo'nupapanna iti, tāmimāmāśaṅkāṃ parihartumāha -- 
viśiṣṭasya jīvatvamanvayavyatirekāt|| 63 ||
'jāva balaprāṇanayo'riti vyutpattyā jīvatvaṃ prāṇitvam| taccāhaṅkāraviśiṣṭapuruṣasya dharmo, na tu kevalapuruṣasya | kutaḥ? anvayavyatirekāt| ahaṅkāravatāmeva sāmarthyātiśayaprāṇadhāraṇayordarśanāt| tacchūnyānāṃ ca cittavṛttinirodhasyaiva darśanāt| pravṛttiheturāgotpādakasya ahaṅkārasyābhāvādityarthaḥ| tathā cā'ntaḥkaraṇopādhikaṃ jīvasya paricchinnatvaṃ paramātmākhyāt kevalapuruṣādbhinnatvaṃ ceti bhāvaḥ| anena satreṇa viśiṣṭasya bhoktṛtvaṃ vā tvamahampratyayagocatvaṃ vā noktam, sākṣātkārarūpasya bhogasyā'ṅkāradharmatvābhāvāt| tvamahandharmipuraskāreṇa vivekānupapatteśca| kintu, -- 
yadā tvabhedavijñānaṃ jīvātmaparamātmanoḥ|
bhavet tadā muniśreṣṭhāḥ! pāśacchedo bhaviṣyati||
ātmānaṃ dvividhaṃ grāhuḥ parāparavibhedataḥ.
parastunirguṇaḥ prokta ahaṅkārayutto'paraḥ||
ityādivākyaśatokta jāvītmavibhāga eva pradarśitaḥ| thatra jīvatāyāmahaṅkāra upalakṣaṇamoveti|| 63 ||
idānīṃ mahadahaṅkārayoḥkāryabhedaṃ pratipādayiṣurādāvahaṅkārakāryamāha -- 
ahaṅkārakartradhīnā kāryasiddhirneśvarādhīnā
pramāṇabhāvāt || 64 ||
ahaṅkārarūpo yaḥ kartā tadadhīnaiva kāryasiddhiḥ sṛṣṭisaṃhāraniṣpattirbhavati, tādṛśabalasyāhaṅkārakāryatvāt| anahaṅkṛteṣu tatsāmarthyādarśanāt| na tu vaiśeṣikādyuktānahaṅkṛtaparameśvarādhīnā | anahaṅkṛtasraṣṭatve nityeśvare ca pramāṇā'bhāvādityarthaḥ| 'ahaṃ bahusyāṃ prajāyeyeti' hyahaṅkārapūrvikava sṛṣṭiḥ śrūyate tatrāhaśabdasyānukaraṇamātratve pramāṇābhāva iti | anena sūtreṇāhaṅkāropādhikaṃ brahmarudrayoḥ sṛṣṭisaṃhārakartṛtvaṃ śrutismṛtisiddhamapi pratipāditam|| 64 ||
nanubhavatvahaṅkāro'nyeṣāṃ kartā, ahaṅkārasya tu kaḥ kartā? tatrāha -- 
adṛṣṭodbhativat samānatvalam || 65 ||
yatā sargādiṣu prakṛtikṣobhakakarmābhivyaktiḥ kālaviśeṣamātrādbhavati tadudbodhakakarmāmtarasya kalpane'navasthāprasaṅgāt| tathaivā'haṅkuraḥ kālamātranimittādeva jāyate, na tu tasyāpi kartrantaramastīti samānatvabhāvayorityarthaḥ|| 65 ||
mahato'nyat || 66 ||
ahaṅkārakāryāt sṛṣṭyāderyadanyat pālanādikaṃ tanmahattattvādbhavati| viśuddhasattvatayā'bhimānarāgādyabhāvena parānugrahamātraprayojanakatvādityarthaḥ| anena ca sūtreṇa mahattattvopādhikaṃ viṣṇoḥ pālakatvamupāditam| mahattattvopādhikatvāt tu viṣṇurmahān parameśvaro brahmeti ca gīyate| taduktam -- 
yadāhurvāsudevākhyaṃ cittaṃ tanmahṛdātmakam|| iti||
atra śāstre kāraṇabrahma tu puruṣasāmānyaṃ nirguṇameveṣyate, īśvarānabhyupagamāt| tatra ca kāraṇaśabdaḥ svaśaktiprakṛtyupādhiko vā nimittakāraṇatāparo vā puruṣārthasya prakṛtipravartakatvāditi mantavyam|| 66 ||
avivekanimittakaḥ prakṛtipuruṣayorbhogyabhoktṛbhāva iti prāguktam, tatrāviveka eva kinnimittaka ityākāṅkṣāyāmavivekadhārakalpane'navasthāpattirityāśaṅkāyāḥ prāmāṇikatvena parihāraḥ sarvavādisādhāraṇa ityāha -- 
karmanimittaḥ prakṛteḥ svasvāmibhāvo'pyanādirbījāṅkuravat|| 67 ||
yeṣāṃ sāṅkhyaikadeśināṃ prakṛteḥ puruṣasya ca svasvāmibhāvo bhogyabhoktṛbhāvaḥ karmanimittakastanmate'pi sa pravāharūpeṇānādireva, bījāṅkuravat prāmāṇikatvādityarthaḥ| ākasmikatvaṃ muktasyāpi punarbhogāpatteriti|| 67 ||
avivekanimittakatvamate'pyetadanāditvaṃ samānamityāha -- 
avivekānimitto vā pañcaśikhaḥ|| 68 ||
avivekanimitto vā svasvāmibhāva iti pañcaśikha āha| tanmatepyanādirityarthaḥ. etadeva svamataṃ prāguktatvāt. avivekaśca pralaye'pi karmavadevāsti vāsanārūpeṇeti | vivekaprāgabhāvo'viveka iti mate tu bījāṅkuravadanāditvaṃ na ghaṭate| akhaṇḍaprāgabhāvasyaivākhilabhogahetutvāditi|| 68 ||
liṅgaśarīranimittaka iti sanandanācāryaḥ|| 69 ||
sanandanācāryastu liṅgaśarīranimittakaḥ prakṛtipuruṣayorbhogyabhoktṛbhāva ityāha -- liṅgaśarīradvāraiva bhogāditi | tanmate'pyanādiḥ sa ityarthaḥ| yadyapi pralaye liṅgaśarīraṃ nāsti tathāpi tatkāraṇamavivekakarmādikaṃ pūrvasargīyaliṅgaśarīrajanyamasti| taddvārā bījā'ṅkuratulyatvaṃ svasvāmibhāvaliṅgaśarīrayorityāśayaḥ|| 69 ||
śāstravākyārthamupasaṃharati -- 
yadvā tadvā taducchittiḥ puruṣārthastaducchittiḥ
puruṣārthaḥ|| 70 ||
karmanimitto vā'vivekādinimitto vā bhavatu prakṛtipuruṣayorbhogyabhoktṛbhāvaḥ| sarvathāpyanāditayā durucchedyasya tasyocchedaḥ paramapuruṣārtha ityarthaḥ| tetadādau pratijñānaṃ "trividhaduḥkhātyantanivṛttiratyantapuruṣārtha" iti | nanvatra sukhaduḥkhasādhāraṇabhoganivṛttipuruṣārtha ucyate, tatra duḥkhamātranivṛttiriti kathaṃ tatroktasyātnopasaṃhāra iti cenna| śabdabhede'pyarthābhedāt sukhaṃ hi tāvad duḥkhapakṣe nikṣiptamiti sukhabhogo'pi duḥkhabhoga eva| duḥkhabhogo'pi pratibimbarūpeṇa puruṣe duḥkhanivṛttirvivakṣitetyeka evārtha upakramopasaṃhārasūtrayoriti | bahulāṃśasya dvirāvṛttiḥ śāstrasamāptyarthā|| 70 ||
śasātramukhyārthavistārastantrākhye'nuktapūraṇaiḥ||
ṣaṣṭhādhyāye kṛtaḥ paścādvākyārthaścopasaṃhṛtaḥ|| 1 ||
tadidaṃ sāṅkhyaśāstraṃ kapilamūrtirbhagavān viṣṇurakhilalokahitāya prakāśitavān| yat tatra vedāntibruvaḥ kaścidāha, sāṅkyapraṇetā kapilo na viṣṇuḥ, kintvagnyavatāraḥ kapilāntaram -- 
agniḥ sa kapilo nāma sāṅkhyaśāstrapravarttakaḥ|
iti smṛteriti| tallokavyāmohanamātram|
etanme janmaloke'smin mumukṣūṇāṃ durāśayāt|
prasaṅkhyānāya tattvānāṃ sammatāyātmadarśanam||
ityādismṛtiṣu viṣṇvavatārasya devahūtiputrasyaiva sāṅkhyopadeṣṭṭatvāvagamāt kapiladvayakalpanāgoravācca| tatra cāgniśabdo'gnyākhyaśaktyāveśādeva prayuktaḥ| yathā, -- 
kālo'smi lokakṣayakṛt pravṛddhaḥ|
iti śrīkṛṣṇavākye kālaśaktyāveśādeva kālaśabdaḥ| anyathā viśvarūpapradarśakakṛṣṇasyāpi
viṣṇvavatārakṛṣṇād bhedāpatteriti dik||
sāṅkhyakulyāḥ samāpūrya vedāntamathitā'mṛtaiḥ||
kapilarṣirjñānayajña ṛṣīnāpāyayat purā|| 1||
tadvacaḥ śraddhayā tasmin gurau ca sthirabhāvataḥ||
tatprasādalavenedaṃ tacchāstraṃ vivṛtaṃ mayā|| 2 ||
iti śrīsāṃkhyācāryavijñānabhikṣuviracite kāpilasāṅkhyapravacanasya
bhāṣye tantrādhyāyaḥ ṣaṣṭhaḥ|| 6 ||
|| iti sāṅaakhyapravacanabhāṣyaṃ sampūrṇam ||
samāptañcedaṃ sāṃkhyadarśanaṃ pravacanabhāṣyaṃ ca ||
